### [1] [Eigenfunction asymptotics and nodal domain estimates for dumbbell domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31856)
*Thomas Beck,Yaiza Canzani,Jeremy L. Marzuola*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究平面哑铃区域上 Neumann 特征函数的节点结构：当连接两固定端域的颈部宽度趋于零时，在简单性与非退化假设下，细颈迫使节点域数目随极限特征值增大而增多；谱底部可达 Courant 上界（第三特征函数 Courant sharp），而端域上的节点亏损可持续到整个哑铃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哑铃域是研究几何与谱相互作用的典型模型。核心问题是：颈部收缩这一奇异极限如何影响 Neumann 特征函数的节点域数目与 Courant sharp 性质。难点在于奇异极限可能在端域上消失，但每个正颈宽下这些区域都贡献节点域，导致极限分析失效。

Method: 采用二维匹配渐近分析（含对数项），推导消失区域上首个非零剖面的精细渐近展开，并以颈部附着点为极点的 Neumann Green 函数表示；结合简单性与非退化假设分析特征值与节点域关系。

Result: 足够细的颈部迫使节点域数目随极限特征值增长而增加；在谱底部，这种迫使可达 Courant 上界，特别地第三 Neumann 特征函数恰有三个节点域且为 Courant sharp；同时端域特征函数的节点亏损可在整个哑铃上保持，产生非 Courant sharp 的特征函数。

Conclusion: 颈部收缩可同时产生 Courant sharp 与非 Courant sharp 两类 Neumann 特征函数，说明节点域数目与谱位置的对应关系在奇异极限下具有非平凡结构；所建立的对数型匹配渐近方法与 Neumann Green 函数表述是处理极限消失区域的关键工具。

Abstract: We study the nodal structure of Neumann eigenfunctions on planar dumbbell domains as the width of the neck joining two fixed end domains tends to zero. Under simplicity and non-degeneracy assumptions, we show that sufficiently thin necks force an increasing number of nodal domains as the limiting eigenvalue grows. At the bottom of the spectrum this forcing can attain Courant's upper bound: in particular, the third Neumann eigenfunction has exactly three nodal domains and is Courant sharp. At the same time, nodal deficiency of an eigenfunction on an end domain can persist on the full dumbbell, giving eigenfunctions that are not Courant sharp. The main analytic difficulty is that the singular limit may vanish on one or both end domains even though these regions contribute nodal domains for every positive neck width. We overcome this by deriving refined asymptotics for the first nonzero profiles on the vanishing regions, expressed through Neumann Green's functions with poles at the neck attachment points and obtained by a two-dimensional matched asymptotic analysis with logarithmic terms.

</details>


### [2] [Homogenization and Long Time Behavior of Wasserstein gradient flow for McKean-Vlasov Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31945)
*Yuan Gao,Nung Kwan Yip*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究含空间快速振荡的McKean-Vlasov方程，用变分与泛函不等式方法得到有效介质中的极限梯度流，并证明解以与振荡尺度一致的指数速率收敛到不变测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将作者此前关于线性Fokker-Planck方程的均匀化结果推广到非线性、非局部情形，同时刻画振荡对动力学与几何（势能与度规）的双重影响。

Method: 将方程表述为概率测度空间上赋予Wasserstein度规的梯度流；结合变分方法与泛函不等式（对数Sobolev不等式）进行均匀化分析。

Result: 识别出极限动力学，其同样为有效介质中的梯度流；证明解到不变测度的长时间指数收敛，收敛速率由对振荡长度尺度一致的对数Sobolev常数给出。

Conclusion: 该工作将线性Fokker-Planck的均匀化推广至非线性非局部的McKean-Vlasov框架，并给出尺度一致的收敛速率估计。

Abstract: We consider a McKean-Vlasov equation incorporating spatial fast oscillatory behavior into both the energetic and kinematic components of the model. The former is given in terms of a confining potential while the latter by an underlying metric. The equation is formulated as a gradient flow in the space of probability measures endowed with a Wasserstein metric. We identify the limiting dynamics which is also described as a gradient flow in an effective media. We further prove the long time exponential convergence of solutions to the invariant measure. The rate of convergence is expressed in terms of a Logarithmic Sobolev constant which is uniform in terms of the oscillatory length scale. Our approach is based on variational and functional inequalities. The homogenization result of the current paper extends the authors' previous work [GaoYip] on a linear Fokker-Planck equation to a nonlinear and nonlocal setting.

</details>


### [3] [A Fourier approach to the sharp stability of Heisenberg Uncertainty Principles of higher and fractional orders: a new perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31949)
*Anh Do,Nguyen Lam,Guozhen Lu,Van Hoang Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文揭示了傅里叶侧二阶Heisenberg不确定性原理(HUP)及其锐稳定性等价于对傅里叶变换施加的一阶$L^2$-Caffarelli–Kohn–Nirenberg不等式及其锐稳定性，从而给出锐稳定性估计的简短证明，并由此构造两族分数阶HUP，计算锐常数、刻画优化子、建立锐稳定性，并进一步说明亏损量可控制一系列余项链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者通过冗长论证建立了锐稳定性估计，本文希望找到更简洁、本质的证明，并揭示HUP与Caffarelli–Kohn–Nirenberg不等式之间的内在等价关系，同时推广到分数阶情形并挖掘亏损量所蕴含的更深层信息。

Method: 在傅里叶侧将二阶HUP及锐稳定性转化为一阶$L^2$-Caffarelli–Kohn–Nirenberg不等式及锐稳定性，作用于函数的傅里叶变换；据此构造两族单参数分数阶HUP（梯度由拉普拉斯分数次幂替换），对每族计算锐常数、刻画全部优化子并建立锐稳定性估计；并分析亏损量对逐次余项链的控制，涉及显式算子谱隙与合流超几何剖面。

Result: 给出了锐稳定性估计的简短新证明；构造了两族分数阶HUP并完全求解（锐常数、优化子、锐稳定性），其中第一族稳定性常数对任意非负阶数与维数均等于1，负阶数（Riesz势情形）亦处理；证明亏损量可控制显式余项链，其常数为显式算子的逐次谱隙；对经典HUP给出三个带最优显式常数的余项，对二阶HUP给出四个余项组成的链，由显式合流超几何剖面度量并经单组参数耦合；并将稳定性链应用于无旋向量场的HUP。

Conclusion: 二阶HUP的锐稳定性本质上是傅里叶侧Caffarelli–Kohn–Nirenberg不等式锐稳定性的等价表述，该观点统一并简化了证明，且自然推广出分数阶HUP族与更精细的余项链结构，表明亏损量所携带的信息远超单纯的优化子距离，为HUP稳定性理论提供了统一框架与新的应用。

Abstract: We show that, on the Fourier side, the sharp second order Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle (HUP) and its sharp stability for functions are nothing but the first order $L^{2}$-Caffarelli--Kohn--Nirenberg inequality and its sharp stability, applied to their Fourier transforms. This gives a proof in a few lines of the sharp stability estimate that we established recently by a much longer argument. The same observation then produces two one-parameter families of sharp fractional Heisenberg Uncertainty Principles, in which the gradient is replaced by a fractional power of the Laplacian: for each of them we compute the sharp constants, characterize all the optimizers, and establish the sharp stability estimates. The stability constant of the first family is equal to $1$ for every nonnegative order and every dimension. Negative orders, where the fractional Laplacian becomes a Riesz potential, are treated as well. Finally, we show that the deficit carries much more information than the distance to the optimizers alone: it controls an explicit chain of successive remainder terms whose constants are the successive spectral gaps of an explicit operator. For the classical Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle this gives three remainder terms with optimal explicit constants, and for the second order HUP a chain of four remainder terms measured against explicit confluent hypergeometric profiles and coupled through one single set of parameters. As applications, we also establish the chain of stability of the HUP for curl-free vector fields.

</details>


### [4] [Large time behaviour in the multidimensional nonlocal Fisher-KPP equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32051)
*Jean-Michel Roquejoffre,Mingmin Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究非球对称、非负紧支撑偶核的Fisher-KPP型反应扩散方程，证明水平集以Freidlin-Gärtner型公式给出的渐近速度扩张，并将展开精确到含对数延迟及方向依赖常数的项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多假设扩散核球对称，此时水平集扩张速度可由简单公式给出；当核不再球对称时，速度由Freidlin-Gärtner型公式刻画，但更高阶的渐近行为（尤其对数延迟及方向依赖修正）尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对扩散由非负、紧支撑、偶核给出的Fisher-KPP型反应扩散方程，分析其解的等位线（水平集）随时间的推进，利用Freidlin-Gärtner型渐近速度公式，将展开推进到随时间趋于无穷而消失的项。

Result: 得到水平集位置的主项速度与Freidlin-Gärtner型公式一致，并进一步给出高阶修正：一个渐近对数延迟项，加上一个时间上为常数、但依赖传播方向的函数。

Conclusion: 非球对称紧支撑核情形下，Fisher-KPP传播的水平集展开不仅包含对数延迟，还含有方向依赖的常数修正，推广了球对称假设下的经典结果。

Abstract: This work studies the expansion of the level sets of the solutions of a Fisher-KPP type reaction- diffusion equation, the diffusion being given by a nonnegative, compactly supported, even kernel. Because the latter is not assumed to be spherically symmetric, the level sets advance at an asymptotic speed given by a Freidlin-Gärtner type formula. The main contribution of this paper is to push the expansion up to terms that vanish as time goes to infinity, consisting in an asymptotically logarithmic delay corrected by an function that is constant in time, but depends on the direction of propagation.

</details>


### [5] [Isoperimetric clusters with a liquid phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32145)
*Jake Wellington*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将湿泡沫等周问题松弛化，识别出可解释塌缩膜的松弛能量，证明极小化序列极限是松弛能量的极小元，并给出广义极小元的正则性、塌缩膜上的压强平衡律，以及在液量趋于零时最小能量收敛于经典干泡沫的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿泡沫由被单一液体腔包裹界面的气室构成；在给定液体体积的等周问题中，液体腔可能塌缩为零厚度膜，导致极小元不一定存在，因此需要引入松弛框架来刻画塌缩现象并恢复极小元的存在性与规律性。

Method: 建立湿泡沫的等周变分问题，构造计入塌缩膜的松弛能量，并证明极小化序列的极限是松弛能量的极小元；进一步建立紧性与能量表示定理。

Result: 得到广义极小元的正则性，包括塌缩膜两侧压强满足的平衡律；并证明当液体体积趋于零时，最小能量收敛到经典干泡沫的最小能量。

Conclusion: 该工作通过松弛能量为湿泡沫等周问题提供了完整的变分与正则性理论，统一了含塌缩膜的广义极小元与经典干泡沫极限，并为相关界面问题提供了能量表示与紧性工具。

Abstract: A wet foam is a cluster of air chambers whose interfaces are coated by a single liquid chamber of prescribed volume. In the corresponding isoperimetric problem the liquid chamber may collapse onto films of zero thickness, so that minimizers need not exist. We identify the relaxed energy accounting for such collapsed films and prove that limits of minimizing sequences are its minimizers. The resulting compactness and energy representation theorems lead to the regularity of these generalized minimizers, including an equilibrium law for the pressures across collapsed films. Finally, as the liquid volume vanishes, the minimal energies converge to those of the classical dry foam.

</details>


### [6] [On elastic scattering behavior of corner domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32148)
*Pu-Zhao Kow,Mikko Salo,Henrik Shahgholian*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定波数下各向异性非均匀介质对弹性波的散射，证明在二维中具有分片$C^1$边界和角奇性的可穿透障碍物、以及高维中具有棱奇性的障碍物，均会散射满足适当假设的任意入射波。该工作将先前关于标量方程的结果推广到方程组情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广先前关于标量方程中“角总会散射”的结果到弹性波方程组（各向异性变系数介质），因为方程组情形需要更精细的爆破分析和更复杂的计算。

Method: 采用更精细的爆破分析（blowup analysis）与相关计算，研究固定波数下各向异性非均匀介质中弹性波被角/棱奇异障碍物散射的问题。

Result: 证明二维中具有分片$C^1$边界和角奇性的可穿透障碍物，以及高维中具有棱奇性的障碍物，均会散射满足适当假设的任意入射波。

Conclusion: 对于一般各向异性变系数介质，确立了“角总会散射”的普遍原理，并将该原理从标量方程扩展到方程组。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering of elastic waves by anisotropic inhomogeneous media at a fixed wave number. We prove that, in two dimensions, every penetrable obstacle with piecewise $C^{1}$ boundary and corner singularities, and, in higher dimensions, every obstacle with edge singularities, scatters every incident wave satisfying suitable assumptions. This work extends our previous result [J. Math. Anal. Appl. 2026, doi:10.1016/j.jmaa.2026.130630] from scalar equations to systems of equations, a generalization that requires a more delicate blowup analysis and more involved computations. Roughly speaking, our results establish the principle that \emph{corners always scatter} for general anisotropic media with variable coefficients.

</details>


### [7] [Convex counterexamples to the Schiffer and Pompeiu conjectures in dimensions three, four, six, eight, ten and fourteen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35419)
*Jizhou Guo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在维度 3,4,6,8,10,14 构造了具有实解析球面边界的有界凸非球区域，其上存在 Δu+u=0 的非平凡解且边界满足 u=1、∇u=0，从而在这些维度（甚至凸类内）否定 Schiffer 与 Pompeiu 猜想；并保留一个三维严格星形但非凸的独立例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schiffer 与 Pompeiu 猜想长期认为此类过定问题（Δu+u=0 且边界 u=1、∇u=0）只可能有球解。此前缺少维度≥3 的反例，凸类中更无已知反例。本文旨在构造反例以证伪这两个猜想。

Method: 利用 Green 恒等式使区域指示函数的 Fourier 变换在单位球面消失来证伪猜想。在 n=2m+2 (m=1,2,3,4,6) 时，区域取自秩二紧李代数 u(2)、so(4)、su(3)、so(5)、g_2，通过 Harish-Chandra 径向部分公式化归为平面 Helmholtz 问题，并用 Kostant 定理将凸性化归到 Cartan 截面。精确解在二进逼近附近由加权多项式系数空间中的计算机辅助压缩映射得到，有限块用区间界、所有无限尾部用解析界控制。三维情形为轴对称（对后两坐标 O(2) 不变、第一坐标反射不变），由子午截面的共形映射提升而得；另保留一个三维严格星形非凸例子。

Result: 在维度 3,4,6,8,10,14 构造出边界实解析的凸非球区域，均存在满足边界条件的非平凡解；据作者所知，除本文早期版本外，这是维度至少为三的首批反例，也是任意维度中首批凸反例。此外得到一个三维严格星形但非凸的独立反例。

Conclusion: Schiffer 与 Pompeiu 猜想在维度 3,4,6,8,10,14 被证伪，且在凸区域类内亦不成立；反例的构造结合了李代数约化、共形映射与计算机辅助解析估计。

Abstract: In dimensions $3,4,6,8,10,14$ we construct bounded convex non-ball domains with real-analytic spherical boundaries. Each admits a nonconstant solution of $Δu+u=0$ with $u=1$ and $\nabla u=0$ on the boundary. Green's identity makes their indicator Fourier transforms vanish on the unit sphere, disproving the Schiffer and Pompeiu conjectures even within the convex class in these dimensions. The three-dimensional domain is axisymmetric, invariant under $O(2)$ on the last two coordinates and reflection of the first, and is obtained by lifting a conformal map of its meridian section. In dimensions $n=2m+2$, $m=1,2,3,4,6$, the domains lie in the rank-two compact Lie algebras $\mathfrak{u}(2)$, $\mathfrak{so}(4)$, $\mathfrak{su}(3)$, $\mathfrak{so}(5)$ and $\mathfrak g_2$. Harish-Chandra's radial part formula reduces these cases to planar Helmholtz problems, and Kostant's theorem reduces convexity to the Cartan section. Exact solutions near dyadic approximations follow from computer-assisted contractions in weighted polynomial coefficient spaces, with interval bounds for finite blocks and analytic bounds for every infinite tail. A second, strictly star-shaped but nonconvex, example in dimension three is retained as an independent result. To our knowledge, apart from the earlier version of this work, these are the first counterexamples in dimensions at least three and the first known convex counterexamples in any dimension.

</details>


### [8] [Global-in-time inviscid limit of the Navier-Stokes equations for vortex-wave data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32233)
*Trinh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意指定有限时间区间上，二维 Navier-Stokes 方程的粘性消失极限收敛到 Marchioro-Pulvirenti 提出的涡旋-波系统，给出定量估计并在该初始数据类下提供了整体时间意义上的严格论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋-波系统自上世纪90年代由 Marchioro 与 Pulvirenti 提出，用以描述点涡与光滑涡量背景的相互作用，但其作为二维 Navier-Stokes 方程粘性消失极限的严格性局限于有限或局部时间，缺乏对任意给定时间区间的整体论证。

Method: 考察二维 Navier-Stokes 方程在粘性趋于零时的极限；初始涡量由一个点涡与光滑紧支撑背景组成，且背景在点涡附近为零。对正则分量证明其强收敛到由涡旋-波系统控制并在该点涡轨迹附近消失的背景涡量；对集中分量用位于极限点涡轨迹上的 Lamb-Oseen 涡进行逼近，并对两个分量给出定量误差估计，常数与粘性阈值依赖于所选时间区间。

Result: 正则分量强收敛于涡旋-波系统所控制的背景涡量，集中分量可由中心位于极限点涡轨迹的 Lamb-Oseen 涡逼近；两个分量均获得定量估计，其常数及所需粘性阈值仅依赖于预先选定的时间区间，从而在所给初始数据类下实现了整体时间意义上的收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为涡旋-波系统作为二维 Navier-Stokes 方程粘性消失极限提供了严格证明，并推广到任意有限时间区间，实现了这一初始数据类下的整体时间验证。

Abstract: We justify the vortex-wave system introduced by Marchioro and Pulvirenti in the 1990s as the vanishing-viscosity limit of the two-dimensional Navier-Stokes equations on every prescribed finite time interval. The initial vorticity consists of a point vortex and a smooth, compactly supported background that vanishes near the vortex. The regular component converges strongly to the background vorticity governed by the vortex-wave system, while the concentrated component is approximated by a Lamb-Oseen vortex centered on the limiting point-vortex trajectory. We obtain quantitative estimates for both components, with constants and a viscosity threshold depending on the chosen time interval, thereby providing a global-in-time justification of the vortex-wave system for this class of initial data.

</details>


### [9] [Scattering for the quadratic NLS system in $\mathbb{R}^{5}$ without mass resonance or radial symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32364)
*Takahisa Inui,Kuranosuke Nishimura*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究二维耦合非线性薛定谔方程组（NLS系统）在非质量共振条件 $\kappa\neq1/2$ 下、非径向解的整体散射问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作在质量共振条件 $\kappa=1/2$ 时（Hamano）或仅对径向解（Hamano--Inui--Nishimura, 2021）证明了散射。本文旨在去掉径向对称假设，在 $\kappa\neq1/2$ 的一般情形下证明基态以下的散射，填补非径向情形的研究空白。

Method: 采用Kenig--Merle（2006）的集中紧性（concentration compactness）与刚性（rigidity）方法。刚性论证部分，借鉴Pausader（2010）的做法，使用沿垂直于动量方向的局部化virial恒等式。

Abstract: We consider the quadratic NLS system \begin{equation*}
  \begin{cases}
  i\partial_tu+Δu=v\bar{u},\newline
  i\partial_tv+κΔv=u^2,
  \end{cases}
  \qquad (t,x)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^5, \end{equation*} where $κ>0$. If $κ=1/2$, which is called mass resonance condition, then scattering below the ground state is shown by Hamano. Moreover, when $κ\neq 1/2$, scattering of radial solutions is proved in Hamano--Inui--Nishimura (2021). In the present paper, we prove scattering below the ground state for $κ\neq1/2$ without radial symmetry. Our proof is based on the concentration compactness and rigidity method by Kenig--Merle (2006). For the rigidity argument, following Pausader (2010), we use a localized virial argument in the direction orthogonal to the momentum.

Abstract: In the nonlinear source of the pressure Poisson equation the fluctuating enstrophy and strain nearly cancel. We ask where the cancellation is complete, which we call silent, and how the source escapes it. Pointwise the source depends only on the eigenvalues of the velocity gradient. Pure shear has none, so it is silent. It is the origin of the normalised invariant plane, whose second-invariant coordinate is the normalised source. Let $f$ be the shear fraction of the gradient and $m$ its distance from that origin. We prove that $m\le1-f\le3m$ at every point of every incompressible flow. Wherever the source nearly vanishes and the third invariant is small, the gradient is nearly pure shear. Otherwise the cancelling gradient is a balanced vortex, a swirl its own strain cancels at any strength. At a no-slip wall the silent object is exact: the wall shear-stress fluctuation, a rank-one sheet. Away from the wall we assume the same sheet, dressed with a small residual, and derive how it escapes. The escape passes through one element of the residual, the along-sheet variation of the sheet-normal velocity. Its amplitude is the geometric mean of sheet and residual, and its sign selects swirl or strain. A rank-two silent object has the same source through the same gates but balanced eigenvalues, so the assumption fixes the escape's topology, not its source. The shear fraction is thus a pointwise proxy for cancellation by shear, and a model of the source must be built on what escapes.

</details>

Abstract: We formalise in Lean 4, on top of Mathlib, the solvability of the Dirichlet problem for second-order linear elliptic operators in divergence form. The machine-verified results, with no sorry in the development, include the Poincaré inequality, the existence of weak solutions by the Lax-Milgram theorem, Rellich-Kondrachov compactness, the Fredholm alternative, the spectral theorem, interior regularity estimates, and the Sobolev embedding theorem. From these results we obtain a formalisation of classical solvability for sufficiently regular coefficients and data. Our Lean library includes a self-contained theory of Sobolev spaces developed independently of existing formalisations. Throughout the paper we associate each prose statement with the named machine-checked Lean declaration that discharges it.

Abstract: We prove that, in phenotype-structured populations under uniform competition, the long-term phenotypic distribution does not depend on the proliferation rates: it is the stationary distribution of the switching dynamics alone. The density u(x,t) of cells with phenotype x evolves by advection and diffusion in phenotype space (switching) and by proliferation at a phenotype-dependent rate r(x,t), modulated by a factor g(U) common to all phenotypes, where U is the total population. This extends to continuous phenotypes a recent result for compartmental models. On a bounded interval with no-flux boundary conditions, the total population converges monotonically to the carrying capacity U*, the density converges in L1 to U*psi, where psi is the stationary density of the switching dynamics, and the nonlinear model is asymptotically equivalent to the linear one; by an explicit estimate, the imprint of fitness decays at the spectral gap of the switching dynamics. On the whole real line we prove the same convergence under two natural conditions, confinement and no escape to infinity, covering Ornstein-Uhlenbeck dynamics, multi-well landscapes, and heavy-tailed densities. If g'(U*) < 0 and psi satisfies a Poincare inequality, convergence is exponential in a weighted L2 norm, while no uniform L1 rate holds for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck dynamics. The proof follows the compartmental one: under uniform competition the reaction term has one sign, so its size is the growth rate of the total population, integrable in time, and the switching dynamics contracts the zero-mass perturbations it produces. Abstract: We study the fractional perimeter per period of planar sets with prescribed horizontal period and area per period. For period one, a half-width of at least $14$ suffices for every fractional exponent $s\in(0,1)$. The proof first replaces each vertical section by a centered interval of the same length. We then establish a quantitative lower bound for the perimeter excess in terms of the deviation of the resulting half-width profile from its mean. The main difficulty is that the perimeter of a strip is a concave function of its width. We show that, at large mean width, the interaction cost of unequal sections dominates the corresponding concavity deficit and leaves a positive remainder for every nonconstant profile. Abstract: In this paper, we provide a rigorous study of the direct scattering problem proposed by Kodama, Ablowitz and Satsuma for the complete integrability theory of the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation. Abstract: We determine the small-diffusion limit of the principal eigenvalue for a one-dimensional cooperative elliptic system with component-dependent drifts and Neumann boundary conditions. For scalar equations, this limit is determined by local data at the drift equilibria and boundary points. We show that such localization fails for systems: switching between components can make some intervals spectrally relevant. We first prove that the logarithmic transforms of all component eigenfunctions converge to the same function, which solves a scalar Hamilton-Jacobi equation. The associated Aubry set determines the asymptotic behavior of the principal eigenvalue. Under nondegeneracy assumptions, this set decomposes into isolated points, regular intervals, and composite intervals containing common drift zeros. We then identify the effective value of each class through local Ornstein-Uhlenbeck spectra and interval transport problems, and establish that the limit of the principal eigenvalue is the minimum of these class values. Result: 线性化基本解有显式表示与尖锐双侧逐点估计；传播阈值恰为β > α(√q - 1)^2；在此基础上确定临界速度c_*和衰减率λ_*；并在该区域证明对任意固定θ∈(0,1)，最外层θ-水平集满足κ_θ(t) = c_* t - (3/(2λ_*))log t + O(1)（t→∞）。

In this regime, we prove that, for every fixed \(θ\in(0,1)\), the outermost \(θ\)-level set satisfies \[ κ_θ(t) =

Abstract: Since the pioneering work of Taylor and Goldstein in 1931 laid the foundation for the study of stratified shear flows, non-parallel configurations have received comparatively little attention. In this article, we study the stability of the three-dimensional Boussinesq system near the plane Couette flow $V^*=(y,0,0)$ under the vertically stratified background $η^*=αz$. The shear and stratification directions are perpendicular. This geometry produces two structural effects that are absent in the parallel configuration: an additional nonlocal coupling in the nonzero-mode system, which prevents the system from having a valid symmetrization structure without reformulation, and a non-parallel/wave-shear coupled lift-up mechanism for the zero modes. The $L^p$ transient growth due to the lift-up effect can be suppressed either by sufficiently strong stratification (when $p>2$), or under suitable assumptions on the initial data (when $p\geq 2$). To study the nonzero-mode system, we introduce a reformulation based on the linearized potential vorticity (PV), a fundamental quantity in geophysical fluid dynamics, to recover a better energy structure. We establish the inviscid damping of $u_{1,\neq}$ and $u_{2,\neq}$. We further apply this method to study the linear stability of the tilted Couette flow. Based on the new formulation through PV, we also investigate the nonlinear stability of the plane Couette flow for Sobolev perturbations on $\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{T}$. We consider initial data with finite weighted Sobolev norm, where the weight is chosen to suppress the lift-up effect at the linear level, so that transient growth arises only through nonlinear interactions and the nonlinear lift-up can be suppressed by strong stratification.

Abstract: Motivated by a class of three-component Fourier ODE systems consisting of a conserved mode coupled to a pair of oscillatory modes, we develop a "potential-vorticity-wave" (PV-Wave) decomposition strategy for constructing adapted energy variables in the stability analysis of Couette flows.   \qquad\text{for some }2<r\leq\infty. \] Second, for every $2<p<\infty$, if $u_0\in\dot H^{1/2}(\mathbb R^3)$ and, for some fixed unit vector $\boldsymbol e\in\mathbb S^2$, \[
  u\cdot\boldsymbol e
  \in
  L^p\bigl(0,T;\dot H^{1/2+2/p}(\mathbb R^3)\bigr), \] then the corresponding Fujita--Kato solution also extends beyond $T$, without any additional low-integrability assumption on the initial vorticity.

  \dot B^{3/2}_{2,r},
  \qquad 2<r\leq\infty, \] with the endpoint case $r=\infty$ closed by an Osgood-type argument. Abstract: For the fractional heat equation $\partial_t u + (-Δ)^{β/2}u=0$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $β\in(0,2)$, Weber and Zacher introduced the optimal constant $C_{\rm LY}(β,d)$ in the Li--Yau type estimate $$(-Δ)^{β/2}\log u(t,\cdot)\leq \frac {C_{\rm LY}(β,d)}{t},\quad t>0.$$ They asked whether $C_{\rm LY}(β,d)$ converges to the classical Li--Yau constant $d/2$ as $β\uparrow2$. More precisely, for every fixed $d\geq1$ there exists a constant $K_d>0$ such that $$\frac{d}β\leq C_{\rm LY}(β,d)\leq\frac{d}β+K_d(2-β)\log\frac{e}{2-β},\quad 1\leqβ<2.$$ Consequently, $\lim_{β\uparrow2}C_{\rm LY}(β,d)=d/2$. A terminal representation of the signal separates two complementary mechanisms: consumption excludes normalized density concentration at positive-signal points, while a localized weighted estimate improves density integrability at zero-signal points. Together, these mechanisms yield decay of a scale-invariant density quantity and allow a local $\varepsilon$-regularity criterion to be applied without a separate smallness assumption on the signal gradient.

Abstract: We study the uniform null-controllability, in the vanishing viscosity limit, of the transport--diffusion equation $u_t + M u_x = \varepsilon u_{xx}$ on $[0,L]$ with a Dirichlet boundary control at $x=0$. We determine this time exactly for both signs of the transport velocity: $T_{\mathrm{unif}} = 2 L / M$ in the positive-speed case $M>0$, and $T_{\mathrm{unif}} = (2 + 2 \sqrt{2}) L / |M|$ in the negative-speed case $M<0$. The positive-speed result disproves the conjecture $T_{\mathrm{unif}}=L/M$ suggested by Coron and Guerrero. For negative speed, the conjectured threshold had already been disproved by Lissy. Our result determines the exact threshold. We establish the lower bounds by constructing adjoint solutions that violate uniform observability below the respective thresholds. In the positive-speed case, the construction exploits the asymptotic structure of the Blaschke products and model spaces associated with the exponential family generated by the adjoint spectrum. The same model-space structure is used to reduce the upper-bound problems for both signs of $M$ to infinite-time observability inequalities, which are proved through a representation of the boundary-to-interior map and estimates of its Hilbert--Schmidt norm.

</details>

Method: 研究在共形度量 $g=\omega|dz|^2$ 下，其中 $\omega\in C^2(\Omega)\cap L^\infty(\Omega)$ 正且可在边界趋于零，假设高斯曲率 $K_g\leq K$（$K>0$）且 $KM<4\pi$。通过分析Neumann特征值 $\mu_2,\mu_3$ 与常曲率曲面上的测地圆盘 $D_K(M)$ 的特征值关系，证明倒数和的比较不等式，并利用等号条件分析刚性。

Result: 证明了 $\frac{1}{\mu_2(\Omega;\omega)}+\frac{1}{\mu_3(\Omega;\omega)}\geq\frac{2}{\mu_2(D_K(M))}$，其中 $D_K(M)$ 是常曲率 $K$ 曲面中面积为 $M$ 的测地圆盘。等号成立当且仅当区域内部等距。特别地，模型圆盘在该度量类中最大化第一正Neumann特征值。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb C$ be a bounded simply connected Lipschitz domain with conformal metric $g=ω|dz|^2$, where $ω\in C^2(Ω)\cap L^\infty(Ω)$ is positive in the interior and may tend to zero at the boundary. If the Gaussian curvature satisfies $K_g\leq K$, $K>0$, and $KM<4π$, where $M=\int_Ωω\,dA$, then the Neumann eigenvalues, numbered by $0=μ_1(Ω;ω)<μ_2(Ω;ω)\leqμ_3(Ω;ω)\leq\cdots$, satisfy $\frac{1}{μ_2(Ω;ω)}+\frac{1}{μ_3(Ω;ω)}\geq\frac{2}{μ_2(D_K(M))}$. Here $D_K(M)$ is the geodesic disk of area $M$ in the surface of constant curvature $K$. We prove that equality holds exactly when the interiors are isometric. Abstract: We investigate the local stability of direct Mach configurations for the two-dimensional steady compressible Euler equations. Given a piecewise constant Mach configuration that consists of an incident shock, a reflected shock, a Mach stem and a slip line, with subsonic downstream states, we prove the following: under a transversality condition on the shock polar loops and a suitable condition on the truncation segment, every sufficiently small perturbation of the incoming flow gives rise to a Mach configuration that is a small perturbation of the background one, in the sense that the reflected shock, the Mach stem and the slip line, together with the downstream subsonic flow, stay close to their background counterparts; moreover, the solution is unique in the class of solutions satisfying the a priori estimate (for incoming flows that are small in a slightly stronger norm). Lagrangian coordinates are employed to transform the unknown contact discontinuity curve into a fixed boundary and to reduce the full Euler system to a first-order elliptic system for the flow direction and the pressure. The key idea in dealing with the contact discontinuity is to solve a mixed boundary value problem, in divergence form with discontinuous coefficients, for a single elliptic equation, so that the contact discontinuity conditions are naturally preserved as compatibility conditions for the solutions of the elliptic problem.

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the Ginzburg-Landau functional among $\mathbb{R}^n$-valued maps from the unit ball $B \subset \mathbb{R}^n$ with vortex boundary condition $u(x) = x$ on $\partial B$. Abstract: We study the sharp-interface limit of the fractional Allen--Cahn equation in $\R^n$, $n\geq 2$, in the strongly nonlocal regime $s\in(0,\frac12)$, for initial data consisting of finitely many nested transition layers. We identify a coupled geometric law for the motion of the resulting fronts: each velocity contains fractional mean curvature and a nonlocal interaction potential generated by the other fronts. These interactions persist while the fronts remain separated, in contrast to the independent motion in the critical regime $s=\frac12$. Abstract: In this work, we establish a sharp logarithmic Choquard inequality by combining Beckner's entropy inequality with the sharp Pitt-type inequality. Motivated by this estimate, we classify the positive classical solutions, in a suitable integrability class, of the critical logarithmic Choquard problem \[ \mathcal{L}_Δu = σu + \frac{1}{\|u\|_2^2} \left(G_{\ln}(u)-\frac{4}{N}\int_{\mathbb{R}^N}u^2\ln u\,dx\right)u \qquad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^N, \] where $σ\in\mathbb{R}$, $N\geq 1$, $\mathcal{L}_Δ$ is the logarithmic Laplacian, and $G_{\ln}(u)=\ln(1/|x|^4)*u^2$. We construct explicit solutions as limits of critical fractional Choquard bubbles and prove that every positive classical solution in the prescribed class has the form \[ u_{σ,t}(x) = e^{\frac{N}{4}(σ-B_{N,\mathcal{L}})} B_{N,0}\left(\frac{t}{t^2+|x-x_0|^2}\right)^{N/2}, \qquad t>0,\quad x_0\in\mathbb{R}^N, \] where \[ B_{N,0} =\left(\frac{Γ(N)}{π^{N/2}Γ(N/2)}\right)^{1/2}, \qquad B_{N,\mathcal{L}} =2\ln 2+4ψ(N/2)-2ψ(N)+\frac{4}{N}\ln B_{N,0}. \]

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在欧氏系数球面上赋予归一化曲面测度，将Bohnenblust--Hille比值视为随机变量，研究其在任意给定单项式支撑、全复多项式空间及实多项式空间上几乎必然收敛与球面测度下的渐近尺度。

Result: 对任意指定单项式支撑，该比值几乎必然最终不超过1，且当单项式个数趋于无穷时球面测度趋于零；在全复多项式空间得到关于维数一致的典型渐近尺度，在临界情形n_m/m→1时为2√2/√(m log m)，与不趋于零的极值尺度形成对比；对实多项式，比值球面测度趋于零当且仅当变量数趋于无穷。

Conclusion: Bohnenblust--Hille比值的典型行为与决定经典常数的极值行为存在尖锐反差，通常远小于1并趋于零，表明经典常数由测度零的极值方向所主导。

Abstract: The polynomial Bohnenblust--Hille inequality controls the coefficient $\ell_{2m/(m+1)}$-norm of an $m$-homogeneous polynomial by its supremum norm, with a constant independent of the dimension. We study the associated Bohnenblust--Hille ratio from a probabilistic point of view, by placing normalized surface measure on the Euclidean coefficient sphere. Our results reveal a sharp contrast between the extremal behavior governing the classical Bohnenblust--Hille constants and the typical scale seen in coefficient directions. For arbitrary prescribed monomial supports, the ratio is eventually at most $1$ almost surely, and it tends to zero in spherical measure exactly when the number of monomials tends to infinity. On the full complex polynomial spaces we determine its typical asymptotic scale uniformly in the dimension; in the critical regime $n_m/m\to1$ this gives $2\sqrt2/\sqrt{m\log m}$, in contrast with the nonvanishing extremal scale. For real polynomials, the corresponding ratio tends to zero in spherical measure exactly when the number of variables tends to infinity.

</details>


### [69] [Inverse-Limit Detection Envelopes as Calkin Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32330)
*Rui Liu,Jie Shen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文引入有界检测包络 $\widehat A=\varprojlim^{b}(A/I_r,\pi_r^s)$，并证明任一此类包络等距同构于某可分 Banach 空间 $X$ 的 Calkin 代数 $\mathcal B(X)/\mathcal K(X)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画哪些单位 Banach 代数可作为 Banach 空间的 Calkin 代数，并统一处理各类算子代数与卷积代数的实现问题。

Method: 结合向量值 Bourgain–Delbaen 扩张、oracle 图灵机递归与 Argyros–Haydon 算子分析，构造可分 Banach 空间 $X$；并以等距前对偶、可数联合忠实弱星到范数连续有限维单位表示给出包络的代数特征。

Result: 对每个由有限余维真闭双侧理想递减序列决定的包络 $\widehat A$，存在可分 $X$ 使 $\mathcal B(X)/\mathcal K(X)\simeq\widehat A$ 等距同构；并给出包络的完备代数刻画及多类具体实例的实现。

Conclusion: 有界检测包络精确对应于具有范一单位的、具备等距前对偶与可数联合忠实有限维表示的单位 Banach 代数，从而统一实现有限维代数、$\ell_\infty$、Tsirelson 空间单位化、解析算子代数及多种经典与量子卷积代数为 Calkin 代数。

Abstract: For a unital Banach algebra $A$ with $\|1_A\|=1$ and a decreasing sequence $(I_r)$ of proper closed two-sided ideals of finite codimension, we introduce the bounded detection envelope $$\widehat A:=\operatorname{Env}^{b}_{(I_r)}(A)=\varprojlim^{b}(A/I_r,π_r^s),\qquad \|(a_r)_r\|=\sup_r\|a_r\|.$$ Combining vector-valued Bourgain--Delbaen extensions, oracle Turing-machine recursion, and Argyros--Haydon operator analysis, we construct a separable Banach space $X$ such that, isometrically as unital Banach algebras, $$\operatorname{Cal}(X)=\mathcal{B}(X)/\mathcal{K}(X)\simeq\widehat A. $$ We also characterize these envelopes as the unital Banach algebras with norm-one units admitting an isometric predual and countably many jointly faithful weak-star-to-norm continuous finite-dimensional unital representations.
  Applications include arbitrary finite-dimensional unital Banach algebras with norm-one units and their countable $\ell_\infty$-sums, as well as unitized coordinatewise algebras over normalized $1$-unconditional boundedly complete Schauder bases. These include incidence algebras, $\ell_\infty$, and the unitization of Tsirelson's original space. Further applications concern analytic operator algebras, Fourier--Stieltjes and Fourier multiplier algebras of countable discrete groups, and classical and quantum convolution algebras. In particular, we realize $H^\infty(\mathbb D)$, $\operatorname{Mult}(H_2^2)$, $\mathcal L_2$, $M_{\mathrm{cb}}A(\mathbb F_2)$, and $C^u(O_3^+)^*$ as Calkin algebras, with their canonical norms and products.

</details>

*Rui Liu,Jie Shen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文利用 oracle-相对的两类有限扩张构造，将任意可分 Banach 空间的对偶空间等距嵌入 Calkin 代数，刻画其对偶空间为某 Banach 空间的对偶。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对每个可分 Banach 空间 V，构造一个 oracle-相对的两类（two-sorted）有限扩张构造，其 oracle 记录 V 的局部有理结构，从而构造可分 Banach 空间 X_V。再计算 Cal(X_V)，并利用对偶与 Jacobson 根的关系来识别 V*。

Result: 得到 Cal(X_V)=B(X_V)/K(X_V) ≅ [[K,0],[V*,K]]（上三角 2×2 矩阵代数），且 V* 与 Jacobson 根的识别是等距的。由此，每个具有可分前对偶的非零对偶 Banach 空间，经等价重范数后，可赋予一个与某可分 Banach 空间的 Calkin 代数同构的单位 Banach 代数结构。此外，存在一个可分 Banach 空间 X，使得每个可分 Banach 空间都等距同构于 B(X) 的某闭双侧理想 J 模去 K(X) 的商 J/K(X)，且子空间-理想对应保持典范序。

Conclusion: Calkin 代数的可能形式比预期更丰富：任何具有可分前对偶的非零对偶空间的对偶都可作为 Calkin 代数的 Jacobson 根等距出现，且所有可分 Banach 空间可统一实现为某 B(X) 的理想商，并保持序结构。

Abstract: For $\mathbb{K}=\mathbb{R}$ or $\mathbb{C}$ and every separable Banach space $V$, we use an oracle-relative two-sorted finite-extension construction, whose oracle records the local rational structure of $V$, to construct a separable Banach space $X_V$ such that $$ \operatorname{Cal}(X_V)=\mathcal{B}(X_V)/\mathcal{K}(X_V)\simeq \begin{pmatrix} \mathbb{K}&0\\ V^*&\mathbb{K} \end{pmatrix} $$ as Banach algebras. The identification of $V^*$ with the Jacobson radical is isometric. Consequently, every nonzero dual Banach space with a separable predual admits, after an equivalent renorming, a unital Banach-algebra structure isomorphic to the Calkin algebra of a separable Banach space. Moreover, there is a separable Banach space $X$ such that every separable Banach space is isometric to $\mathcal{J}/\mathcal{K}(X)$ for a closed two-sided ideal $\mathcal{J}$ of $\mathcal{B}(X)$, and the subspace--ideal correspondence preserves the canonical order.

</details>

Result: 主要结果有三：(i) 当n>1时，正、单位、保迹的线性变换是V上唯一保持e-双随机性的线性变换；(ii) 对e-双随机矩阵A，线性组合∑r_i a_i的特征值向量被系数向量(r_1,…,r_n)^T优超；(iii) 当p为完备时，由（广义）Jordan框架诱导的e-双随机V-矩阵是所有e-双随机V-矩阵构成的紧凸集的极点。

Abstract: In the setting of a hyperbolic (polynomial) system $(\mathcal{V}, p, e)$ of degree $n$, an $n$-tuple $\mathbf{A} = \big[ a_1, a_2, \dots, a_n \big]$ is said to be an $e$-doubly stochastic $\mathcal{V}$-matrix if each $a_i$ belongs to the hyperbolicity cone, has trace $1$, and $a_1 + a_2 + \dots + a_n = e$. In this article, we characterize linear preservers of such $\mathcal{V}$-matrices, describe some connections between $e$-doubly stochasticity and majorization, and study extreme points of the set of all $e$-doubly stochastic $\mathcal{V}$-matrices. We show, for example, that $(i)$ when $n>1$, positive, unital, and trace-preserving transformations are (the only) linear transformations on $\mathcal{V}$ that preserve $e$-doubly stochasticity; $(ii)$ when $\mathbf{A}$ is $e$-doubly stochastic, the eigenvalue vector of the linear combination $\sum_{i=1}^{n} r_ia_i$ is majorized by the coefficient vector $(r_1, r_2, \dots, r_n)^{T} \in \mathbb{R}^n$; and $(iii)$ when $p$ is complete, $e$-doubly stochastic $\mathcal{V}$-matrices induced by (generalized) Jordan frames are extreme points of the compact convex set of all $e$-doubly stochastic $\mathcal{V}$-matrices.

Main category: math.FA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 \(g\)-函数（\(\alpha=1\)）的 \(L^p\) 有界性已有丰富理论，但分数阶Poisson核对应的 \(g_\alpha\) 在维数依赖性与精确常数方面尚待刻画；特别是弱型 \((1,1)\) 常数随维数的定量渐近行为缺乏明确结果。

Result: 对所有 \(1<p<\infty\) 建立了 \(g_\alpha\) 与维数无关的 \(L^p\) 界；证明其精确 \(L^2\) 范数为 \(\sqrt{\alpha/2}\)；确定了当 \(n\to\infty\) 时弱型 \((1,1)\) 常数的精确渐近行为，并给出了以维数表示的定量上界与下界。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了分数阶 Littlewood-Paley 函数 \(g_\alpha\) 在 \(L^p\)、\(L^2\) 及弱型 \((1,1)\) 意义下的定量性质，表明其 \(L^p\) 界与维数无关，且给出了弱型常数随维数增长的精确刻画。

Abstract: Let \(0<α\leq1\), and let \(g_α\) be the Littlewood-Paley function associated with the fractional Poisson kernel, which reduces to the classical \(g\)-function when \(α=1\). We establish dimension-free \(L^p\) bounds for \(g_α\) for every \(1<p<\infty\) and identify its exact \(L^2\) norm as \(\sqrt{α/2}\). We further determine the precise asymptotic behavior of the weak-type \((1,1)\) constant as \(n\to\infty\), yielding quantitative upper and lower bounds in terms of the dimension.

</details>


### [73] [Multiplicativity of the weak Haagerup constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35211)

Method: 通过证明弱Haagerup常数关于直积的乘性等式（即同时给出反向不等式），从而建立Λ_WH(G×H)=Λ_WH(G)Λ_WH(H)。

Abstract: We prove that the weak Haagerup constant is multiplicative with respect to direct products, namely $Λ_{\mathrm{WH}}(G \times H)=Λ_{\mathrm{WH}}(G) Λ_{\mathrm{WH}}(H)$ for any locally compact groups $G$ and $H$. Knudby previously proved the submultiplicative inequality and asked whether equality always holds.

</details>


### [74] [Almost norming vertices for homogeneous polynomials on the cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32526)
*Damián Pinasco,Ignacio Zalduendo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了固定次数的实齐次多项式在 Bombieri 高斯测度下，其单位立方体顶点上的最大值能否渐近逼近整个立方体上的最大值，并证明了相对损失以概率不超过 \(n^{-1/2}\) 阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于 \(m\) 次齐次多项式 \(P\)，立方体 \([-1,1]^n\) 上的最大值 \(M(P)\) 与顶点上的最大值 \(V(P)\) 之间的关系是经典问题。本文关注“典型”多项式，即在 Bombieri 范数诱导的高斯测度 \(\gamma_{m,n}\) 下随机选取的多项式，其顶点是否渐近成为范数决定集。这一定量刻画有助于理解高维齐次多项式空间中的范数集中现象。

Method: 在 \(\mathcal{P}_m(\mathbb{R}^n)\) 上赋予与 Bombieri 范数对应的标准高斯测度 \(\gamma_{m,n}\)；对随机多项式 \(P_n\sim\gamma_{m,n}\)，比较 \[ M(P)=\max_{x\in[-1,1]^n}|P(x)|,\qquad V(P)=\max_{\varepsilon\in\{-1,1\}^n}|P(\varepsilon)|, \] 并估计相对损失 \(1- V(P_n)/M(P_n)\) 的概率上界。

Result: 证明相对损失 \(1- V(P_n)/M(P_n)\) 依概率至多为 \(n^{-1/2}\) 阶。因此对任意 \(0<\beta<1/2\)，有 \[ \gamma_{m,n}\{P\in\mathcal{P}_m(\mathbb{R}^n): V(P)\ge (1-n^{-\beta})M(P)\}\to 1,\qquad n\to\infty. \]

Conclusion: 在 Bombieri 高斯测度下，对于固定次数的齐次多项式，超立方体顶点渐近构成范数决定集：典型多项式的顶点最大值可逼近其在整立方体上的最大值，相对误差趋于零且具有 \(n^{-1/2}\) 阶概率上界。

Abstract: Let $m\geq1$ be fixed. We consider the space $\mathcal{P}_m(\mathbb{R}^n)$ of real $m$-homogeneous polynomials on $\mathbb{R}^n$, endowed with the standard Gaussian measure $γ_{m,n}$ associated with the Bombieri norm. We study how well the norm of a typical polynomial on the unit ball of $\ell_\infty^n$, namely the cube $[-1,1]^n$, can be recovered from its values at the vertices.
  For $P\in\mathcal{P}_m(\mathbb{R}^n)$, set \[ M(P)=\max_{x\in[-1,1]^n}|P(x)|, \qquad V(P)=\max_{\varepsilon\in\{-1,1\}^n}|P(\varepsilon)|. \] If $P_n$ is chosen according to $γ_{m,n}$, we prove that the relative loss \[ 1-\frac{V(P_n)}{M(P_n)} \] is of order at most $n^{-1/2}$ in probability. Consequently, for every $0<β<1/2$, \[ γ_{m,n}\left\{ P\in\mathcal{P}_m(\mathbb{R}^n): V(P)\geq (1-n^{-β})M(P) \right\} \longrightarrow1 \] as $n\to\infty$. Thus, with respect to the Bombieri Gaussian measure, the vertices of the cube are asymptotically norming for homogeneous polynomials of fixed degree.

</details>

Method: 在有限离散环面上构造坐标对径差的标量线性分解（通过符号增量）及其伴随散度提升；利用商空间对偶将最优非线性提升常数等同于相应Banach值差分常数；结合与Cheng、Xiang合作建立的sharp \(L_1\)度量余型不等式进行分析。

Result: 在边长\(2m\)（\(m\)偶数）的环面上，两种分解的最优正则范数均为\(m\)；所得\(\ell_\infty^N\)值非线性提升在sharp度量余型尺度下具有一致界，而归一化标量线性提升的最优正则范数随环面维数增长。

Conclusion: 非线性提升可达到与维数无关的一致界，而标量线性提升的最优正则范数随维数发散，表明线性与非线性方法在高维离散环面上存在本质的量化差距。

Abstract: We study scalar linear factorizations of coordinate antipodal differences through sign increments on finite discrete tori and the corresponding adjoint divergence liftings. On tori of side length \(2m\) with \(m\) even, the optimal regular norms of both factorizations are \(m\). Quotient-space duality identifies the optimal nonlinear lifting constant with the corresponding Banach-valued difference constant. A sharp \(L_1\) metric-cotype inequality established jointly with Cheng and Xiang then yields \(\ell_\infty^N\)-valued nonlinear liftings with a uniform bound at the sharp metric-cotype scales, whereas the optimal regular norms of the normalized scalar linear liftings grow with the dimension of the torus.

</details>


### [76] [A Quaternionic Hansen--Pedersen Inequality and its Application to $S_r$-Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32623)
*M. Fashandi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文建立了右四元数Hilbert空间上实算子凸函数的四元数Hansen–Pedersen不等式，并应用于p-亚正规算子变换的亚正规性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Hansen–Pedersen算子不等式推广到四元数Hilbert空间，并借助四元数Jensen型算子不等式研究极分解相关变换的亚正规性保持问题。

Method: 利用自伴四元数算子的连续实泛函演算、直和泛函演算恒等式以及分块酉构造完成不等式证明；应用中对单射p-亚正规算子T=U|T|的变换S_r(T)=U|T|^r U进行分析。

Result: 对0<r≤1/2，当0<p≤1/2时S_r(T)为2p-亚正规，当1/2<p≤1时为亚正规；此外S_r(T)对任意r>0保持对数亚正规性。

Conclusion: 结果展示了四元数Jensen型算子不等式在与极分解相关变换研究中的应用价值，推广了Hansen–Pedersen不等式并给出亚正规性保持的新结论。

Abstract: We establish a quaternionic Hansen--Pedersen inequality for real operator-convex functions on right quaternionic Hilbert spaces. The proof is based on the continuous real functional calculus for selfadjoint quaternionic operators, a direct-sum functional-calculus identity, and a block-unitary construction. As an application, let \(T = U|T|\) be an injective \(p\)-hyponormal operator. We prove that, for \(0 < r \leq 1/2\), the transform \(S_{r}(T) = U|T|^{r}U\) is \(2p\)-hyponormal when \(0 < p \leq 1/2\) and hyponormal when \(1/2 < p \leq 1\). We also show that \(S_{r}(T)\) preserves log-hyponormality for \(r > 0\). These results illustrate the use of quaternionic Jensen-type operator inequalities in the study of transforms associated with the polar decomposition.

</details>


### [77] [Hypercyclic Bergman-Toeplitz operators with some harmonic symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32650)
*Qianrui Leng,Xiaoyan Zhang,Xianfeng Zhao*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Bergman空间上符号形如 $a\bar{z}+p(z)$ 的Toeplitz算子的动力学性质，给出了其超循环、弱混合、混合、混沌及频繁超循环的若干必要与充分条件，并完整刻画了调和线性多项式符号情形的超循环性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toeplitz算子在Bergman空间上的动力学行为（尤其是超循环等性质）尚不完全清楚，本文旨在系统刻画一类具有代表性符号 $a\bar{z}+p(z)$ 的Toeplitz算子的动力学性质，并比较其与Hardy空间上的差异。

Method: 针对符号 $a\bar{z}+p(z)$（其中 $a\neq 0$，$p$ 在闭单位圆盘上解析），通过分析算子结构，分别建立超循环、弱混合、混合、混沌与频繁超循环的必要条件和充分条件；进一步对调和线性多项式符号作完整分类。

Result: 获得了该类Toeplitz算子上述各类动力学性质的必要与充分条件；完整刻画了调和线性多项式符号Toeplitz算子的超循环性；并给出同符号Toeplitz算子在Hardy空间与Bergman空间上超循环行为可能不同的例子。

Conclusion: 符号为 $a\bar{z}+p(z)$ 的Toeplitz算子的动力学性质可由符号参数刻画，且调和线性多项式情形可被完全分类；同一符号在不同函数空间（Hardy与Bergman）上的超循环行为可能不一致。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the dynamical properties of Toeplitz operators with symbols of the form $a \bar{z} + p (z)$ on the Bergman space, where $a\neq 0$ and $p$ is analytic on the closed unit disk. Some necessary conditions and some sufficient conditions for such a class of operators to be hypercyclic, weakly mixing, mixing, chaotic, or frequently hypercyclic are obtained. As an application, we obtain a complete characterization of hypercyclicity for Toeplitz operators with harmonic linear polynomial symbols. Additionally, we show that Toeplitz operators with the same symbol may exhibit different hypercyclicity behavior on the Hardy and Bergman spaces.

</details>


### [78] [Rademacher Type and Enflo Type Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32666)
*Nika Areshidze*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在离散立方体上建立无维数依赖的向量值Poincaré型不等式，由此证明Rademacher型与Enflo型一致，并将定量估计常数改进为$p$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rademacher型与Enflo型的一致性已知，但已有定量估计中常数较大（$\pi/\sqrt{2}$），需要更精确的常数和更简洁的证明。

Method: 对具有Rademacher型$p$（$1\le p\le2$）的Banach空间，在离散立方体上建立无维数依赖的向量值Poincaré型不等式。

Result: 得到$T_p^R(X)\le T_p^E(X)\le p\,T_p^R(X)$，从而给出Rademacher型与Enflo型一致的替代证明，并将常数从$\pi/\sqrt{2}$改进为$p$。

Conclusion: 该工作以更优常数$p$统一并强化了Rademacher型与Enflo型关系的定量结果。

Abstract: We establish a dimension-free vector-valued Poincaré-type inequality on the discrete cube for Banach spaces with Rademacher type $p$, $1\le p\le2$. As a consequence, we obtain $$ T_p^R(X)\le T_p^E(X)\le p\,T_p^R(X). $$ This gives an alternative proof that Rademacher type and Enflo type coincide, while improving the constant $π/\sqrt{2}$ in the quantitative estimate of Ivanisvili, van Handel, and Volberg \cite{IVV} to $p$.

</details>


### [79] [Mean size and Schauder-basis properties of Daubechies wavelet packets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32904)
*Morten Nielsen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明：对长度≥4的任意Daubechies滤波器（及所有具有Daubechies幅频响应的实谱因子），其移位小波包系统在1<p<∞, p≠2时不是L^p(R)的Schauder基，且p>2时过渡矩阵族的p-范数联合谱半径超过Haar值4^{1/p}，从而证实Nielsen–Zhou两个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2002年Nielsen与Zhou提出猜想：长度≥4的Daubechies滤波器对应的移位小波包系统在1≤p≤∞, p≠2时不是L^p(R)的Schauder基，且p>2时各尺度小波包在p-平均意义下不一致有界。此前仅长度4通过显式计算验证了1<p<∞, p≠2的基障碍，完整的p-平均断言及一般长度的情形仍悬而未决。

Method: 核心是矩阵压力(pressure)的结构性分析：证明矩阵压力中的一个仿射部分迫使谱半径为常数；借助Protasov–Voynov定理，常数谱半径意味着过渡矩阵同时正交，而这与高通符号的双零点不相容。由此导出不等式ρ_p ρ_{p'}>2 (1<p<∞, p≠2)，并估计p>2时全族过渡矩阵的p-范数联合谱半径。方法适用于任意长度≥4的Daubechies滤波器，并推广到具有相同幅频响应的所有实谱因子（含最不对称滤波器）。

Result: 对每个长度≥4的Daubechies滤波器：当1<p<∞, p≠2时，ρ_p ρ_{p'}>2，故移位小波包系统不构成L^p(R)的Schauder基；当p>2时，全族过渡矩阵的p-范数联合谱半径严格大于Haar值4^{1/p}，即小波包在各尺度上p-平均不一致有界。结论同样适用于具Daubechies幅频响应的所有实谱因子，包括最不对称滤波器。两个猜想均被完整证实。

Conclusion: 本文完整证实了Nielsen–Zhou的两个猜想，把长度4的显式计算提升为对一般长度Daubechies滤波器的通用证明，并揭示其深层机理——矩阵压力中仿射项导致常数谱半径，进而由Protasov–Voynov定理推出与高通双零点矛盾的矩阵同时正交性。

Abstract: In 2002 Nielsen and Zhou conjectured that the shifted wavelet packet system associated with a Daubechies filter of length at least four fails to be a Schauder basis of $L^p(\mathbb{R})$ for $1\le p\le\infty$, $p\ne2$, and that the packets are not uniformly bounded in $p$-mean across scales for any $p>2$. The basis obstruction throughout the range $1<p<\infty$, $p\ne2$, was previously established for length four, via an explicit computation; the full $p$-mean assertion remained open. We prove both for every Daubechies filter of length at least four: the high-pass transition matrices satisfy $ρ_pρ_{p'}>2$ for $1<p<\infty$, $p\ne2$, and for $p>2$ the full family of transition matrices has $p$-norm joint spectral radius exceeding the Haar value $4^{1/p}$. The key step is structural: an affine piece of the matrix pressure forces constant spectral radius, which by a theorem of Protasov and Voynov forces simultaneous orthogonality of the transition matrices, incompatible with the double zero of the high-pass symbol. The results extend to every real spectral factor with the Daubechies magnitude response, including the least-asymmetric filters.

</details>


### [80] [The strong reproducing kernel thesis for Hankel operators and the Garsia norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33242)
*Konstantin M. Dyakonov*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文提出强再生核论题（SRKT）概念，并提出两个问题：确定哪些Hankel算子满足SRKT，以及研究BMO单位球在Garsia范数下的几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在再生核Hilbert空间上，算子范数通常难以直接计算，而通过规范化再生核来估计范数（再生核论题）是一个自然且重要的问题。作者希望以SRKT这一更强的等式形式刻画算子范数，并借此深入理解Hankel算子与BMO空间的结构。

Method: 作者引入强再生核论题（SRKT）的概念：对再生核Hilbert空间H上的有界算子T，若满足‖T‖=sup_k‖Tk‖（k取遍规范化再生核），则称T满足SRKT。随后针对Hardy空间H^2上的Hankel算子以及BMO空间（配备Garsia范数）的单位球几何展开分析，提出两个具体问题。

Result: 论文提出两个待解决问题：一是刻画哪些H^2上的Hankel算子满足SRKT；二是描述BMO单位球在Garsia范数下的几何结构。文中未给出最终结论，而是将问题形式化并提出。

Conclusion: SRKT为研究再生核Hilbert空间上算子范数提供了新的视角，围绕Hankel算子与BMO几何的两个问题为进一步研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Given a reproducing kernel Hilbert space $\mathcal H$ and a bounded operator $T$ on $\mathcal H$, we say that $T$ obeys the strong reproducing kernel thesis (SRKT) if $\|T\|=\sup_k\|Tk\|$, where $k$ ranges over the normalized reproducing kernels in $\mathcal H$. Two problems are posed. One of these is to determine which Hankel operators on the Hardy space $H^2$ obey the SRKT. The other concerns the geometry of the unit ball of $BMO$ with respect to the Garsia norm.

</details>


### [81] [Riesz transforms on the Vicsek set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33858)
*Fabrice Baudoin,Aobo Chen,Li Chen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究无界Vicsek集上Laplacian分数幂的Riesz与逆Riesz不等式，关键分数阶为γ_p=(d_f+p-1)/(p d_w)，其中d_f=log_3 5、d_w=d_f+1。建立了弱型逆Riesz不等式(1≤p≤2)，并刻画了Riesz变换R_p在各类L^p与L^{p,q}空间间的有界性，其中R_1不是弱(1,1)型、R_p仅当p=2时L^p(m)→L^p(ν)有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分形（无界Vicsek集）设定下，Hausdorff测度m与奇异长度测度ν并存，梯度∂仅在骨架上定义，经典欧氏意义下1/2阶的Riesz理论不再适用，需确定正确的分数阶γ_p并刻画相应不等式与变换的有界性。

Method: 定义骨架上的弱梯度∂，以关于m奇异的长度测度ν度量；利用d_f=log_3 5、d_w=d_f+1引入临界阶γ_p=(d_f+p-1)/(p d_w)；分别研究逆Riesz不等式在强型、弱型下的成立范围，以及Riesz变换R_p=∂(-Δ)^{-γ_p}在L^p、L^{p,∞}、L^{p,1}与对应ν端点间的映射性质。

Result: 对p∈[1,2]，弱型逆Riesz不等式‖(-Δ)^{γ_p}f‖_{L^{p,∞}(m)}≲‖∂f‖_{L^p(ν)}成立；相应强型估计在1≤p<2失败，弱型估计在2<p<∞失败（即使经热正则化）。R_1不是弱(1,1)型；R_p对p∈(1,2)由L^p(m)到L^{p,∞}(ν)有界，对p∈(2,∞)由L^{p,1}(m)到L^p(ν)有界；但对p≥1，仅当p=2时R_p由L^p(m)到L^p(ν)有界。

Conclusion: 无界Vicsek集上分数阶Laplacian的Riesz理论具有临界指数γ_p，逆Riesz不等式仅以弱型形式在p∈[1,2]内成立，而Riesz变换的有界性在端点附近退化；L^p(m)→L^p(ν)的有界性仅在p=2成立，凸显测度奇异性与分形维数对临界指数的本质影响。

Abstract: We study Riesz and reverse Riesz inequalities for the fractional powers of the Laplacian $Δ$ on the unbounded Vicsek set. The space carries its Hausdorff measure $m$. The gradient $\partial$ is a weak gradient defined on the skeleton, and it is measured with respect to a length measure $ν$ that is singular with respect to $m$. The relevant fractional order for the Riesz inequalities is not $1/2$ but $γ_{p}=\frac{d_f+p-1}{pd_w}$, where $d_f=\log_{3}5$ and $d_w=d_f+1$. For $p\in[1,2]$, we prove the weak-type reverse Riesz inequality $\|(-Δ)^{γ_p}f\|_{L^{p,\infty}(m)}\lesssim\|\partial f\|_{L^p(ν)}$. The corresponding strong estimate fails for $1\le p<2$, whereas the weak estimate fails for $2<p<\infty$, even after heat regularization. For the Riesz transform $\mathcal{R}_p=\partial(-Δ)^{-γ_p}$, we show that $\mathcal{R}_1$ is not of weak type $(1,1)$ but that $\mathcal{R}_p$ is bounded from $L^p(m)$ to $L^{p,\infty}(ν)$ for $p\in(1,2)$, and from $L^{p,1}(m)$ to $L^p(ν)$ for $p\in(2,\infty)$. However, if $p \ge 1$, $\mathcal{R}_p$ is bounded from $L^p(m)$ to $L^p(ν)$ only for $p=2$.

</details>


### [82] [Bourgain--Brezis selection maps for Hodge systems in Besov and Triebel--Lizorkin spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33876)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bourgain--Brezis问题关于选择映射的可解范围此前已知结果有限，作者希望将其推广到更广的Besov与Triebel--Lizorkin空间参数范围，并揭示无迹Beurling--Ahlfors变换在该问题求解中的关键作用。

Method: 方法上将无迹Beurling--Ahlfors变换与非线性对偶方法相结合；为控制集中现象，采用了Stolyarov在研究Maz'ya Φ-不等式时发展的工具。

Conclusion: 结果虽与部分早期工作有重叠，但显著扩展了Bourgain--Brezis问题已知解的范围，并凸显了无迹Beurling--Ahlfors变换在解决该问题中的重要性。

Abstract: We establish Bourgain--Brezis selection maps for Hodge systems in new ranges of Besov and Triebel--Lizorkin spaces. Our approach combines the trace-free Beurling--Ahlfors transform with a nonlinear duality method. To control concentrations, we use tools developed by Stolyarov in the study of Maz'ya's $Φ$-inequalities. These methods yield selection maps in Besov spaces $\dot B^{n/p}_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ for every $1\leq q\leq p\leq 2$, and in Triebel--Lizorkin spaces $\dot F^{n/p}_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ for every $1<p\leq q\leq 2$. The theorems hold for every dimension $n\geq 2$ and every form degree $1\leq l\leq n-1$. Although our results overlap with some earlier work, they considerably extend the known range of solutions to the Bourgain--Brezis problem and underscore the importance of the trace-free Beurling--Ahlfors transform in its resolution.

</details>


### [83] [Modulation spaces: from Feichtinger's original definition to modern Gabor and symplectic characterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33894)
*Antonio Caputo,Elena Cordero,Gianluca Giachi,Luigi Rodino*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于调制空间（modulation spaces）的综述性论述，从Feichtinger在局部紧阿贝尔群上的原始构造出发，追溯其局部-全局原理在多种工具中的体现，并延伸至近年来的辛（symplectic）表述与metaplectic Wigner分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理调制空间理论的发展脉络，厘清诸多等价描述与各种函数空间推广之间的区别，并将近期出现的辛几何框架自然地纳入Feichtinger调制空间理论体系之中。

Method: 采用综述性（expository）论述方式，沿"局部-全局原理"这一主线，串联有界一致单位分解、短时傅里叶变换、coorbit方法与Gabor系数等工具；同时考察加权、拟Banach以及Gelfand-Shilov等函数设定下的推广，并分析Gabor矩阵、卷积与嵌入估计，最后借助metaplectic Wigner分布$W_{\mathcal A}$的shift-invertible情形进行结构刻画。

Result: 指出在shift-invertible情形下，metaplectic Wigner分布$W_{\mathcal A}$本质上（相差chirp与相空间变量的线性变换）即为重标度后的短时傅里叶变换；并给出Gabor矩阵、卷积与嵌入估计的相关结果以及一个非线性色散方程的应用。

Conclusion: 上述结构性事实统一解释了$W_{\mathcal A}$对调制空间与Wiener amalgam空间的刻画及其由metaplectic Gabor框架实现的离散化，从而将近期辛理论自然地置于Feichtinger的调制空间框架之内。

Abstract: We present an expository excursus on modulation spaces, from Feichtinger's original construction on locally compact abelian groups to recent symplectic formulations. We trace the same local-global principle through bounded uniform partitions of unity, the short-time Fourier transform, coorbit methods, and Gabor coefficients, while distinguishing equivalent descriptions from the weighted, quasi-Banach, and Gelfand-Shilov extensions of the underlying functional setting. We also discuss Gabor matrices, convolution and embedding estimates, and an application to a nonlinear dispersive equation. The final part is devoted to metaplectic Wigner distributions $W_{\mathcal A}$. In the shift-invertible case these representations are, up to chirps and a linear change of phase-space variables, rescaled short-time Fourier transforms. This structural fact explains both their characterization of modulation and Wiener amalgam spaces and their discretization by metaplectic Gabor frames, and places the recent symplectic theory naturally within Feichtinger's modulation-space framework.

</details>


### [84] [Tensor Products of Almost \(f\)-Algebras: A Counterexample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33963)
*Mohamed Amine Ben Amor*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 两个Archimedean几乎f-代数的自然环境张量积乘法一般不能延拓到其Fremlin张量积；反例基于同一底空间C[0,1]上的显式正、三阶幂零乘法，障碍是(s,t)↦e^{st}不属于C[0,1]的Fremlin张量积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中对几乎f-代数的Riesz投影张量积与Fremlin投影张量积曾有若干论断，作者旨在检验这些断言；核心问题是两个Archimedean几乎f-代数各自的乘法能否自然地延拓到它们的Fremlin张量积上。

Method: 构造反例：取两个具有相同底层向量格C[0,1]的Archimedean几乎f-代数，其乘法为显式的、正的且指数为三的幂零乘法；通过考察函数(s,t)↦e^{st}是否属于C[0,1]的Fremlin张量积来刻画乘法延拓的障碍；该构造还可调整以得到Banach几乎f-代数。

Result: 两个几乎f-代数的自然张量积乘法一般不延拓到Fremlin张量积；障碍在于e^{st}不在C[0,1]⊗̄C[0,1]（Fremlin张量积）中，而非仅仅是不在代数张量积中。

Conclusion: 乘法延拓到Fremlin张量积并非普遍成立，此前关于几乎f-代数Riesz与Fremlin投影张量积的部分已发表结论因此需要修正；该反例同时推广至Banach情形。

Abstract: We show that the natural tensor product multiplication of two Archimedean almost \(f\)-algebras does not, in general, extend to their Fremlin tensor product. The two algebras in the counterexample have the same underlying vector lattice \(C[0,1]\). Their multiplications are explicit, positive and nilpotent of index three. The obstruction is the fact that the function \((s,t)\mapsto e^{st}\) does not belong to \(C[0,1]\ftensor C[0,1]\). The same construction gives Banach almost \(f\)-algebras and has consequences for previously published assertions on Riesz and Fremlin projective tensor products of almost \(f\)-algebras.

</details>


### [85] [Endpoint-Safe Direct Characterisations of Weighted Bilinear Hardy Inequalities on Arbitrary Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34110)
*Saikat Kanjilal*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究了任意实区间上同向乘积 $H_I f H_I g$ 的加权双线性 Hardy 不等式，在权重可取 $0$ 与 $+\infty$ 的一般框架下，借助定向紧限制 $J\Subset I$ 及算子型局部特征，给出了最优常数的完整刻画，并在正则内部情形下恢复经典 $A_1,\ldots,A_7$ 条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典加权 Hardy 不等式及其多参数、双线性推广的刻画通常依赖较强正则性假设，难以统一处理 $1\le p_i\le\infty$、权重退化或发散等边界情形。作者希望建立一个不依赖权重正则性的统一刻画框架，把端点原子、内部紧性以及全局常数与局部特征之间的联系精确化，并说明经典 $A_1,\ldots,A_7$ 条件只是该框架在正则内部区域的特例。

Method: 以定向紧限制 $J\Subset I$ 为技术核心：在每个紧区间上引入端点安全的输入剖面，将 $p_i=1$ 的边界贡献保留为 Stieltjes 原子；通过下三角约化把闭 Hardy 部分与严格 Copson 部分分离，使公共原子恰好计一次。上标与混合区域利用精确冻结和紧线性 Hardy 估计处理；下标区域通过幂提升与测度值 Hardy–Copson 定理处理；对权重同时正则化后，将紧最优常数与一个可操作的局部特征以仅含指数常数的方式等价起来，并证明全局常数满足 $C_I\asymp\sup_{J\Subset I} A_J^{\mathrm{op}}$。

Result: 在权重可取 $0$ 和 $+\infty$ 的一般情形下，得到了同向乘积加权双线性 Hardy 不等式的最优常数刻画： $C_I\asymp\sup_{J\Subset I} A_J^{\mathrm{op}}$，其中 $A_J^{\mathrm{op}}$ 为紧区间上的算子型局部特征，等价关系仅含依赖于指数的常数。端点 $p_i=1$ 情形被完整纳入 Stieltjes 原子框架，公共原子不重复计数。在正则内部范围内，所得条件恰好恢复经典的 $A_1,\ldots,A_7$ 刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作为加权双线性 Hardy 不等式提供了一个统一、可操作且对权重正则性要求极弱的刻画理论，将端点原子、紧限制局部化与算子型特征有机结合，并明确了与经典 $A_1,\ldots,A_7$ 理论的关系。其方法对含乘积结构与退化权重的加权不等式研究具有推广价值。

Abstract: We study the weighted bilinear Hardy inequality for the same-direction product $H_I f H_I g$ on an arbitrary real interval $I$, with $0<q<\infty$, $1\le p_1,p_2\le\infty$, and measurable weights allowed to take the values $0$ and $+\infty$. The characterisation is formulated through directed compact restrictions $J\Subset I$. On each compact interval, endpoint-safe input profiles retain the $p_i=1$ boundary contribution as a Stieltjes atom, while the lower-triangle reduction separates a closed Hardy part from a strict Copson part so that common atoms are counted exactly once. Upper and mixed regimes follow from exact freezing and compact linear Hardy estimates. The lower regimes are obtained by power lifting and a measure-valued Hardy-Copson theorem. After simultaneous regularisation of the weights, the compact optimal constant is equivalent, with exponent-only constants, to an operational local characteristic, and the global constant satisfies $C_I\asymp\sup_{J\Subset I} A_J^{\mathrm{op}}$. In the regular interior range, the resulting conditions recover the classical $A_1,\ldots,A_7$ characterisations.

</details>


### [86] [phi-semi contractible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34434)

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究Banach代数的φ-半可收缩性，给出其完全刻画、遗传性质及近似版本的等价刻画，并证明投影张量积A⊗B关于φ⊗ψ半可收缩当且仅当A、B分别关于各自特征半可收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半可收缩性是Banach代数同调代数中的重要概念，但针对具体特征的细化研究尚不充分。作者希望引入φ-半可收缩性这一新概念，以便更精细地刻画Banach代数在特定特征下的结构性质。

Method: 通过构造满足特定代数条件的元素，对φ-半可收缩性进行完全刻画；研究其在闭理想、稠密值域连续同态及投影张量积下的遗传性质；进一步引入近似φ-半可收缩性并给出等价刻画。

Result: 建立了φ-半可收缩性的完全刻画；证明了投影张量积A⊗B关于φ⊗ψ半可收缩当且仅当A和B分别关于各自特征φ、ψ半可收缩；给出了近似φ-半可收缩性的等价刻画。

Conclusion: φ-半可收缩性为Banach代数的同调性质提供了新的研究视角，其张量积刻画与近似版本结果进一步丰富了半可收缩性理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce and investigate the notion of phi-semi contractibility for Banach algebras. We establish a complete characterization of phi-semi contractibility through the existence of specific elements satisfying particular algebraic conditions. We thoroughly examine the hereditary properties of this concept, including its behavior under closed ideals, continuous homomorphisms with dense range, and projective tensor products. Notably, we prove that the projective tensor product A tensor B is phi tensor psi-semi contractible precisely when both A and B are semi contractible with respect to their respective characters, where phi belongs to the character space of A and psi belongs to the character space of B. Furthermore, we introduce and analyze the approximate version of phi-semi contractibility, providing equivalent characterizations.

</details>


### [87] [Vector-valued Littlewood--Paley--Rubio de Francia Inequalities on Vilenkin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34689)
*Deyu Chen,Guixiang Hong*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 论文研究任意Vilenkin系统上等长LPR不等式的两种表述：规范区间表述在Schatten类中失效，而Vilenkin平移表述等价于UMD且type 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等长Littlewood–Paley–Rubio de Francia不等式在环面已有研究，但在Vilenkin系统上"等长区间"有两种自然定义，二者关系不明。论文旨在厘清这两种表述的性质差异及其与Banach空间几何性质的联系。

Method: 针对规范表述，通过对角化构造反例证明Schatten类S^p对任意2<p<∞均不满足等长LPR性质；针对Vilenkin表述，结合从环面到有限循环群的transference论证与将Vilenkin区间嵌入数字矩形包络的技术，证明充分性，必要性与UMD、type 2相关。

Result: 规范表述下，对每个2<p<∞，Schatten类S^p不满足等长LPR性质，与环面情形形成鲜明对比；Vilenkin表述下，对每个2≤p<∞，Banach空间X满足等长LPR性质当且仅当X具有UMD性质且type 2，且所得界与生成序列无关。

Conclusion: 两类等长区间的定义在Vilenkin系统上性质截然不同：规范区间导致Schatten类失败，而Vilenkin平移区间给出的性质精确刻画了UMD∧type 2这一空间几何条件，且估计与生成序列无关。

Abstract: We investigate two natural formulations of equal-length Littlewood--Paley--Rubio de Francia (LPR) inequalities on arbitrary Vilenkin systems \(\Gm\). The canonical formulation uses canonical intervals in \(\mathbb N\) of equal cardinality, while Vilenkin's formulation uses translates of a common initial interval under the group operation on the dual of \(\Gm\).
  In this paper, our main results show that the definitions of these two types of intervals exhibit dramatically different properties. More precisely, for the canonical formulation, we construct a counterexample showing that, for every \(2<p<\infty\), the Schatten class \(S^p\) fails the equal-length LPR property, in sharp contrast to the corresponding result on the torus; for Vilenkin's formulation, we prove that, for every \(2\le p<\infty\), a Banach space \(X\) has the equal-length LPR property if and only if it is UMD and has type~\(2\). The sufficiency proof combines a transference argument from the torus to finite cyclic groups with an embedding of Vilenkin intervals into digital rectangular envelopes, yielding bounds independent of the generating sequence.

</details>


### [88] [Vector-Valued Sub-Bergman Spaces: Range Inclusions and Nonrigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34694)
*Shuaibing Luo,Jiming Shen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在向量值加权Bergman空间上证明了关于Toeplitz算子子Bergman空间的一个猜想，并构造反例说明有限维刚性定理不能推广到无穷维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Toeplitz算子T_B所关联的子Bergman空间A_α(B)与A_α(B*)的结构与包含关系，验证文献[GLM26]提出的猜想，并探讨其有限维刚性定理在无穷维系数空间情形下是否成立。

Method: 设E为可分Hilbert空间，B∈H^∞_1(L(E))，α>-1，在向量值加权Bergman空间A^2_{α,E}上研究Toeplitz算子T_B及其子Bergman空间；通过包含关系证明与显式构造无穷维双侧内函数B的方法进行研究。

Result: 证明了A_α(B)⊃A^2_{α-1,H(B(0))}以及A_α(B*)⊃A^2_{α-1,H(B(0)*)}，从而证实了[GLM26]的猜想；并构造出一个无穷维双侧内函数B，满足‖B(0)‖<1且A_α(B)≈A_α(B*)≈A^2_{α-1,E}，但B不是有限Blaschke-Potapov乘积。

Conclusion: 所构造的例子表明[GLM26]的有限维刚性定理不能推广到无穷维系数空间的情形。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal E$ be a separable Hilbert space, $B\in H^\infty_1(L(\mathcal E))$, and $α>-1$. Associated with the Toeplitz operator $T_B$ on the vector-valued weighted Bergman space $A^2_{α,\mathcal E}$ are the sub-Bergman spaces $\mathcal A_α(B)$ and $\mathcal A_α(B^*)$. In this paper, we prove that \[
  \mathcal A_α(B)\supset
  A^2_{α-1,\mathcal H(B(0))}
  \quad\text{and}\quad
  \mathcal A_α(B^*)\supset
  A^2_{α-1,\mathcal H(B(0)^*)}. \] This confirms a conjecture of \cite{GLM26}. We also construct an infinite-dimensional two-sided inner function $B$ such that $\|B(0)\|<1$ and \[
  \mathcal A_α(B)\approx
  \mathcal A_α(B^*)\approx
  A^2_{α-1,\mathcal E}, \] but $B$ is not a finite Blaschke-Potapov product. This example shows that the finite-dimensional rigidity theorem of \cite{GLM26} does not extend to infinite-dimensional coefficient spaces.

</details>


### [89] [Some applications of binary numeration systems with nonzero redundancy to the theory of locally complex functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34746)
*Svitlana Vaskevych,Yuliia Vovk,Oleksandr Pratsiovytyi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究非整数基 $a>1$ 在二进制字母表上的数制系统 $\Delta^r_a$，刻画其表示几何、柱重叠结构及度量性质，并分析由二进制到该数制的映射函数 $f$ 的函数方程、分形水平集、自仿射图像及积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非整数基数制（如 $\beta$-展开）是数论与分形几何的经典研究对象，但其在二进制字母表约束下的 $r_a$-表示及其对应映射函数的解析与分形性质尚未被充分刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 定义区间 $[0, r/(a-1)]$ 上的 $r_a$-表示 $\Delta^r_a$，分析数字的几何解释、柱集重叠结构与度量性质；构造映射 $f$（将二进制展开的数字序列保持系数、把基 $2$ 换成基 $a$），推导 $f$ 满足的二元函数方程组，并利用自仿射迭代函数系方法研究其图像与水平集。

Result: 证明 $f$ 满足二元函数方程组；$f$ 为无处单调且具有无界变差的函数；其水平集为分形集，图像为自仿射集；并给出 $f$ 在 $[0,1]$ 上积分的显式求值。

Conclusion: 该 $r_a$-数制与映射 $f$ 提供了连接二进制展开与非整数基展开的桥梁，其无处单调、无界变差、分形水平集与自仿射图像等性质丰富了非整数基数制与分形函数理论，并给出了积分的精确结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a numeration system with a non--integer base $a>1$ over the binary alphabet $A=\{0,1\}$: $$\left[0;\frac{r}{a-1}\right]\ni x=\sum\limits_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{α_n}{a^n} \equiv Δ^{r_a}_{α_1α_2\ldotsα_n\ldots}, \quad α_n\in A.$$ We investigate the geometry of $r_a$--representation of numbers, including the geometric interpretation of digits, the structure of cylinder overlaps, and the associated metric properties.
  The main object of our study is a nowhere monotone function of unbounded variation defined by $$f\left(x=\sum\limits_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{α_n}{2^n}\right) =\sum\limits_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{α_n}{a^n},$$ where the classical binary representation of the argument is assumed not to end with the infinite period $(1)$. We derive a system of two functional equations satisfied by $f$. We prove that the function has fractal level sets and a self--affine graph. We also evaluate the integral of $f$ over the interval $[0,1]$.

</details>


### [90] [A BMO-type representation formula for the total variation of SBV functions in Heisenberg groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35577)
*Marco Di Marco,Sebastiano Don,Davide Vittone*

### [92] [Improvements of Classical Eigenvalue Inequalities I: Quantitative Hersch-deficit and the Joint Spectral Range on the Two-Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32443)
*Zuoqin Wang,Qingwei Zeng*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文在二维球面面积归一化度量下，对经典拉普拉斯特征值不等式做定量改进，研究前两个特征值的联合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典特征值不等式（Hersch、Nadirashvili等）多为定性或最优常数形式，缺乏对偏离极值情形及多特征值联合范围的定量刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 综合运用Petrides的显式谱族、度理论论证，以及改进的有限维矩阵方法。

Result: 得到Hersch倒数和不等式关于第一特征值的尖锐二次改进、前三个特征值的同步二次估计、可允许测度的几何稳定性结果，以及具有尖锐指数尺度的Nadirashvili第二特征值不等式的定量改进，并给出(λ1,λ2)联合范围的非平凡内外界。

Conclusion: 该工作将球面上经典谱不等式提升到定量与联合谱层面，所得估计具尖锐尺度。

Abstract: For area-$4π$ metrics on the two-sphere, we obtain quantitative refinements of classical eigenvalue inequalities and study the joint behavior of the first two Laplace eigenvalues. We prove a sharp quadratic improvement of Hersch's reciprocal-sum inequality in terms of the first eigenvalue, together with a simultaneous quadratic estimate for the first three eigenvalues and a geometric stability result for admissible measures. We also establish a quantitative refinement of Nadirashvili's second-eigenvalue inequality with sharp exponential scale. Finally, using explicit spectral families due to Petrides, a degree argument, and a refined finite-dimensional matrix method, we obtain nontrivial inner and outer bounds for the joint range of $(λ_1,λ_2)$.

</details>


### [93] [Large eigenvalues of the Connes-Moscovici operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32639)
*Kouichi Taira,Max Willems,Michał Wrochna*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Connes-Moscovici预测的对数Weyl定律，针对算子P=-∂_x(x²-1)∂_x-4π²x²在实轴上特定自伴延拓的大正特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Connes-Moscovici曾预测该算子特定自伴延拓的大正特征值应满足对数Weyl定律，但此前缺乏严格证明，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用WKB方法，建立Bohr-Sommerfeld量子化条件来刻画充分大的特征值；针对h依赖的Lagrangian子流形非紧致性，引入作用量积分的重整化处理；针对系数在x=±1处的退化，运用基于径向估计和Lagrangian正则性的准模匹配论证。

Result: 证明了所预测的对数Weyl定律，并通过Bohr-Sommerfeld量子化条件成功刻画了充分大的特征值。

Conclusion: WKB方法与准模匹配技术可有效处理非紧致Lagrangian子流形及系数退化问题，为对数Weyl定律提供了严格数学证明。

Abstract: We prove the logarithmic Weyl law predicted by Connes and Moscovici for large positive eigenvalues of the distinguished self-adjoint extension of the operator $P=-\partial_x(x^2-1)\partial_x-4π^2x^2$ on the real line. More precisely, we use the WKB method and show a Bohr-Sommerfeld quantization rule characterizing sufficiently large eigenvalues. A particular difficulty is that the associated $h$-dependent Lagrangian submanifolds are non-compact, which requires a renormalization of the action integral. Furthermore, to deal with the degeneracy of the coefficients at $x =\pm 1$, we use a quasimode matching argument based on radial estimates and Lagrangian regularity.

</details>


### [94] [Off-diagonal bounds for spectral projector kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33957)
*Panagiotis Dimakis*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究黎曼流形上Laplace-Beltrami算子谱函数在几乎处处意义下的增长估计，n≥3时给出对数修正的Λ^{n/2}界，n=2时给出更强的Λ^{5/6}界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱函数（局部Weyl律余项）的增长阶是谱几何与调和分析的核心问题，Sogge等人已建立L^∞意义下的最优型估计；本文旨在揭示对几乎所有点（而非一致估计）谱函数能否获得更精细的增长阶。

Method: 在n维黎曼流形M上考察Laplace-Beltrami算子的谱函数E_Λ(x,y)；对固定点x，构造M中具有满测度的例外集Z_x，利用调和分析中的局部Weyl律、特征函数估计与测度论手段，对不在Z_x中的"好点"y给出E_Λ(x,y)的精细上界。

Result: n≥3维时：对任意x∈M，存在满测度子集Z_x⊂M，使得对所有y∈Z_x，E_Λ(x,y)=O_{M,x,y,ε}(Λ^{n/2}(logΛ)^{3/2}(loglogΛ)^{1/2+ε})。n=2维时结论更强：对任意x∈M，存在满测度子集Z_x⊂M，使得对所有y∈Z_x，E_Λ(x,y)=O_{M,x,y,ε}(Λ^{5/6}(logΛ)^{1/6}(loglogΛ)^{1/6+ε})。估计中的常数依赖M,x,y,ε，ε>0任意小。

Conclusion: 在几乎处处意义下谱函数的增长阶显著低于一致估计的界，说明例外点集可为满测度且不影响增长控制；二维情形因可应用更强的plancherel型/平方根消去技巧而获得Λ^{5/6}的更优阶，这一现象具有维度相关的定量特征。

Abstract: We study the growth of the spectral function of the Laplace-Beltrami operator on a Riemannian manifold $M$ of dimension $n$. For $n\geq 3$ we show that for any $x\in M$ there is a full measure subset $Z_x\subset M$ such that for all $y\in Z_x$ \[E_Λ(x,y) = O_{M,x,y,\varepsilon}(Λ^{n/2}(\logΛ)^{3/2}(\log\logΛ)^{1/2+\varepsilon}). \] For $n=2$ we prove a stronger statement. For any $x\in M$ there is a full measure subset $Z_x\subset M$ such that for all $y\in Z_x$ \[E_Λ(x,y) = O_{M,x,y,\varepsilon}(Λ^{5/6}(\logΛ)^{1/6}(\log\logΛ)^{1/6+\varepsilon}) .\]

</details>

### [95] [Hahn-Type Orthogonality for the Resolvent Delta Operator $\mathscr L=D(I-D)^{-1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31805)
*Youssef Esstafa,Ridha Sfaxi*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究移位不变预解 delta 算子 L=D(I-D)^{-1} 下的正交性，证明其 Appell 情形唯一对应平移 Laguerre 序列，并给出对角族 S_β 满足精确 Hahn 关系及多项刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Appell 多项式与 delta 算子下的正交性刻画多集中于导数算子；预解算子 L=D(I-D)^{-1} 作为移位不变 delta 算子，能否提供新的正交性结构、并揭示 delta 算子降阶与半经典正交性之间的联系，尚不清晰，因此需要系统的刚性刻画与结构描述。

Method: 以移位不变预解 delta 算子 L=D(I-D)^{-1} 为工具：先研究首一正交多项式序列的 L-Appell 性质；再构造对角族 S_β=β/(β+1)L^0+1/(β+1)δ；通过 Hahn 型降阶关系、单点关系 P_{n+1}(c)=θ L P_{n+1}(c) 的刚性分析，并推导连接与结构关系、半经典 Pearson 方程、微分刻画及生成函数。

Result: (1) 刚性定理一：首一正交多项式序列为 L-Appell 当且仅当其为平移 Laguerre 序列；(2) 对角族 S_β 满足精确 Hahn 关系 L B_{n+1}(x;β)=(n+1)B_n(x;β+1)；(3) 刚性定理二：单点关系 P_{n+1}(c)=θ L P_{n+1}(c) 已迫使正交泛函为平移 Laguerre 泛函或对角族的平移成员；(4) 得到连接与结构关系、半经典 Pearson 方程、微分刻画及生成函数。

Conclusion: 预解算子 L 提供了一个显式框架，使 delta 算子降阶与半经典正交性由精确结构恒等式相联系。

Abstract: We investigate orthogonality under the shift-invariant resolvent delta operator $\mathscr L=D(I-D)^{-1}$. First, we prove a rigidity theorem: a monic orthogonal polynomial sequence is $\mathscr L$-Appell if and only if it is a translated Laguerre sequence. We then move beyond the Appell setting and show that the diagonal family associated with $$\mathcal S_β=\fracβ{β+1}\mathcal L^0+\frac{1}{β+1}δ$$ satisfies the exact Hahn relation $$\mathscr L B_{n+1}(x;β)=(n+1)B_n(x;β+1).$$ A second rigidity result shows that a single pointwise relation $P_{n+1}(c)=θ\,\mathscr L P_{n+1}(c)$ already forces the orthogonality functional to be a translated Laguerre functional or a translated member of the diagonal family. Connection and structure relations, a semiclassical Pearson equation, a differential characterization, and a generating function complete the description. Thus the resolvent operator provides an explicit setting in which delta-operator lowering and semiclassical orthogonality are linked by exact structural identities.

</details>


### [96] [A component-wise inequality for permutation matches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31979)
*Felipe Gonçalves*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文将有限域矩曲线端点延拓不等式的证明推广为关于置换匹配的分量式不等式，后者可推出原结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期关于有限域矩曲线端点延拓不等式（通过两点对称化论证）工作的启发，希望给出更一般的结论。

Method: 采用分量式不等式方法处理置换匹配，作为两点对称化论证的推广与强化。

Result: 证明了关于置换匹配的更一般的分量式不等式。

Conclusion: 该分量式不等式蕴含原论文的尖锐端点延拓不等式，具有更强的普适性。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent paper [Sharp endpoint extension inequalities for the moment curve on finite fields II: an extremal property of the uniform distribution, arXiv:2609.29882], which proves sharp extension inequalities in finite fields via a two-point symmetrization argument, we prove here a more general component-wise inequality for permutation matches that implies theirs.

</details>


### [97] [Characterization of the boundedness of the segment multiplier on weighted Lebesgue spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32039)
*Miguel F. Barea-Fernández,Jan Lang,Javier Soria*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了分段乘子在加权 L^p 空间上有界性等价于"大尺度 Muckenhoupt 条件"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分段乘子在 L^p(w) 上的有界性此前缺乏简洁而准确的权特征；经典 A_p 条件对高频/低频成分不敏感，本文试图给出恰当的"大尺度"加权条件。

Method: 将经典 A_p 条件限制到长度不小于 1 的区间上得到大尺度 Muckenhoupt 条件；证明通过离散化，将问题转化为某个相关联的加权序列空间上的离散 Hilbert 变换。

Result: 对 1<p<∞，证明了分段乘子在 L^p(w) 上有界当且仅当权 w 满足大尺度 Muckenhoupt 条件。

Conclusion: 建立了分段乘子有界性的完整加权刻画，揭示了其与大尺度 A_p 条件及离散 Hilbert 变换的联系。

Abstract: For $1<p<\infty$, we characterize the boundedness of the segment multiplier on $L^p(w)$ by a large-scale Muckenhoupt condition, obtained by restricting the classical $A_p$ condition to intervals of length at least one. The proof uses a discretization involving the discrete Hilbert transform on an associated weighted sequence space.

</details>


### [98] [Sharp Bernstein--Nikolskii inequalities for polynomials and entire functions of exponential type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32072)
*D. V. Gorbachev*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文综述了多项式与指数型整函数的Bernstein–Nikolskii不等式，重点关注最优常数与极值函数，涵盖经典结果与最新进展并给出若干公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein–Nikolskii不等式的最优常数与极值函数问题具有核心地位，且p=1情形尤为困难；同时该主题与逼近论、数论、设计理论及堆球等多个领域存在深刻联系，亟需系统梳理。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理经典结果与近期发展，包括多项式与指数型整函数Nikolskii常数的极限关系、加权与多维版本、受限函数类上的不等式；重点采用再生核、对偶与极值逼近方法处理p=1情形。

Result: 总结了最优常数与极值函数的相关结果，阐明了与逼近论、Riemann zeta函数、球面设计、Turán–Delsarte型问题及堆球的联系，并提出若干公开问题。

Conclusion: 该综述整合了Bernstein–Nikolskii不等式在多方向的研究成果，指出p=1及多维推广等方向仍存在关于最优常数与极值函数的待解决问题。

Abstract: We survey Bernstein--Nikolskii inequalities for polynomials and entire functions of exponential type, with particular emphasis on sharp constants and extremal functions. We discuss both classical results and recent developments, including limit relations between Nikolskii constants for polynomials and entire functions of exponential type, weighted and multidimensional versions, and inequalities on constrained classes of functions. Special attention is given to the difficult case $p=1$ and to methods based on reproducing kernels, duality, and extremal approximation. We also describe connections of sharp Bernstein--Nikolskii inequalities with approximation theory, the Riemann zeta function, spherical designs, Turán--Delsarte type problems, and sphere packing. Several open problems concerning sharp constants, extremal functions, and multidimensional extensions are formulated.

</details>


### [99] [Sharp lower bounds for the periodic maximal Schrödinger operator in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32090)
*Inbo Gottlieb Fenves,Jiahao Tan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了环面 T^d (d≥2) 上 Schrödinger 极大函数的尖锐下界，并由此得到周期 Schrödinger 方程逐点收敛的尖锐正则性条件，结合 Compaan-Lucá-Staffilani 的充分性结果，完整解决了 d≥2 时周期情形的 Carleson 问题（至端点），同时推翻了 Miao-Yuan-Zhao 猜测的正则性条件，并给出高维环面上发散集维数的尖锐估计。方法上使用了阿贝尔簇上的复乘法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期 Schrödinger 方程逐点收敛的 Carleson 问题，特别是确定环面 T^d (d≥2) 上必要的正则性条件，以补足已有充分性结果并检验 Miao-Yuan-Zhao 的猜想，同时揭示周期情形与欧氏空间 R^d 情形的本质差异。

Method: 通过对阿贝尔簇使用复乘法，证明环面 T^d 上 Schrödinger 极大函数的尖锐下界，并据此推导逐点收敛的必要正则性条件；进一步利用该方法获得高维环面上发散集维数的尖锐估计。

Result: 获得 T^d (d≥2) 上 Schrödinger 极大函数的尖锐下界及逐点收敛的尖锐正则性条件；与 Compaan-Lucá-Staffilani 的充分性结果结合，完整解决 d≥2 时周期 Schrödinger 方程的 Carleson 问题（至端点）；推翻 Miao-Yuan-Zhao 的猜想；并给出高维环面上发散集维数的尖锐估计。

Conclusion: 周期 Schrödinger 方程的 Carleson 问题在 d≥2 时已被完整解决（至端点），且其正则性条件与 R^d 情形存在本质差异，Miao-Yuan-Zhao 的猜想不成立；所发展的基于阿贝尔簇复乘法的方法对研究周期色散方程的收敛性问题及发散集结构具有广泛意义。

Abstract: We prove sharp lower bounds for the Schrödinger maximal function on the torus T^d for all dimensions d at least 2, and as a corollary obtain sharp regularity conditions for pointwise convergence of the periodic Schrödinger equation. Combined with sufficiency results established by Compaan-Lucá-Staffilani, this yields a full resolution of Carleson's problem up to endpoint for the periodic Schrödinger equation in all dimensions at least 2, and disproves the conjectured regularity condition of Miao-Yuan-Zhao, in contrast to the corresponding question in R^d. We also prove sharp estimates on the dimensions of divergence sets for the equation for high dimensional tori. Our approach uses complex multiplication on abelian varieties.

</details>


### [100] [Rigidity of reflectionless Riesz measures and Sobolev estimates for Riesz transforms on Lipschitz graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32179)
*Lu Chen,Haolin Liu,Hongyu Liu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中 $n$ 维 Riesz 变换的正反射测度刚性，在多项式上增长与单点大尺度下质量界条件下，证明了两类平坦性结果：全局 Lipschitz 图测度情形与紧集外为平坦测度的情形，均推出测度为仿射 $n$-平面上 $n$ 维 Hausdorff 测度的常数倍；并给出 $H^s$ ($s>n/2$) 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进 Xavier Tolsa 提出的正反射测度刚性猜想，理解反射测度在何种正则性与增长假设下必为平坦测度。

Method: 在 $1\leq n<d$、多项式上增长及单点大尺度下质量界假设下，对 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中 $n$ 维 Riesz 变换的反射测度，分别研究有限 $\dot H^{1/2}$ 能量的全局 Lipschitz 图测度（参数密度有界、一致正且偏差属 $L^2$），以及紧集外与平坦测度重合的反射测度。

Result: 两类情形下测度均为仿射 $n$ 平面上的 $n$ 维 Hausdorff 测度的常数倍；对整数 $s>n/2$，在斜率与密度扰动 $L^\infty$ 小的条件下，用归一化 Riesz 场控制图梯度与密度扰动，得到 $H^s$ 估计，常数与支撑集无关。

Conclusion: 在适当增长与单点下界假设下，反射测度呈现强刚性，只能为平坦测度的常数倍，且给出支撑集无关的定量 $H^s$ 稳定性估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we focus on the positive reflectionless measures rigidity problem proposed by Xavier Tolsa in [16]. Let $1\leq n<d$, we consider positive reflectionless measures for the $n$-dimensional Riesz transform in $\mathbb R^d$. Under polynomial upper growth and a lower mass bound at large scales about one point, we obtain two flatness results for reflectionless measures. The first concerns globally defined Lipschitz graph measures with finite $\dot H^{1/2}$ energy and bounded, uniformly positive parameter densities whose deviations from a constant belong to $L^2$. The second case concerns a reflectionless measure that coincides with a flat measure outside a compact set. In both cases, the measure is a constant multiple of the $n$-dimensional Hausdorff measure on an affine $n$-plane.
