For integers $s>n/2$, we further obtain $H^s$ estimates controlling the graph gradient and density perturbation by the normalized Riesz field under smallness of the slope and density perturbation in $L^\infty$. The constants are independent of the supports.

</details>


### [101] [Gabor frames with no Gabor duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32246)
*Pu-Ting Yu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明：在生成元的每个光滑度水平上，$L^2(\R^d)$ 中存在具有无穷多个 alternative dual 和 pseudo-synthesis dual 的 Gabor 框架，且这些对偶中没有一个是 Gabor 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Gabor 框架的各种对偶（alternative dual、pseudo-synthesis dual）是否仍保持 Gabor 结构，是一个自然的理论问题。本文旨在构造反例，说明这些对偶可以不具有 Gabor 系统结构。

Method: 在无穷维 Hilbert 空间框架理论中引入 alternative dual 与 pseudo-synthesis dual 的定义（分别在 $\ip{x}{y_n}x_n$ 和 $\ip{x}{x_n}z_n$ 展开意义下），并在 $L^2(\R^d)$ 上针对 Gabor 框架按生成元光滑度分层构造特例。

Result: 证明在生成元的每个光滑度水平上，均存在 Gabor 框架具有无穷多个 alternative dual（以及 pseudo-synthesis dual），且这些对偶中没有一个是 Gabor 系统。

Conclusion: Gabor 框架的对偶性并不保持 Gabor 结构：存在光滑度任意的 Gabor 框架，其大量对偶元素不再是 Gabor 系统，表明 Gabor 框架的对偶集合远比 Gabor 对偶丰富。

Abstract: Let $H$ be an infinite-dimensional Hilbert space and let $\set{x_n}\inN$ be a frame for $H$.
  We say that a sequence $\set{y_n}\inN$ is an \emph{alternative dual} of $\set{x_n}\inN$ if $$x=\sumli\ip{x}{y_n}x_n\quad \text{for all }x\in H,$$ with the convergence of the series in the norm of $H.$ We say that a sequence $\set{z_n}\inN$ is a \emph{pseudo-synthesis dual} of $\set{x_n}\inN$ if $$x=\sumli\ip{x}{x_n}z_n \quad \text{for all }x\in H,$$ with the convergence of the series in the norm of $H.$ We prove that, at every level of smoothness of the generator, there exist Gabor frames for $L^2(\R^d)$ with infinitely many alternative duals (and pseudo-synthesis duals) such that none of them is a Gabor system

</details>


### [102] [Endpoint Absolute Monotonicity for the Complete Elliptic Integral of the First Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32366)
*Qintao Deng,Yunjia Kou,Fulin Zhong*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Tian-Yang猜想：在 $p=\ln4$ 时，$-f_{\ln4}'''$ 与 $g_{\ln4}$ 在 $(0,1)$ 上均为绝对单调。通过给出 $a_n(\ln4)$ 的矩表示并利用其严格对数凸性确定全部系数符号，同时借助二次截断的分解与倒数级数论证证明 $b_n(\ln4)>0$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tian与Yang提出猜想：在 $p=\ln4$ 处，$-f_{\ln4}'''$ 和 $g_{\ln4}$ 在 $(0,1)$ 上绝对单调。作者希望验证该猜想，从而推进对完全椭圆积分与对数函数组合所定义函数的单调性/绝对单调性研究。

Method: 将 $\frac{2}{\pi}f_{\ln4}(x)$ 与 $\frac{\pi}{2}g_{\ln4}(x)$ 展开为幂级数，考察系数 $a_n(\ln4),b_n(\ln4)$。核心技巧是给出 $a_n(\ln4)$ 的表示 $a_n(\ln4)=\frac{(-1)^nC}{15^{n+1}}-M_n$，其中 $C>0$、$(M_n)$ 为正矩序列，利用其严格对数凸性结合初始系数估计确定所有 $a_n$ 符号；对 $b_n$，则通过归一化Taylor级数二次截断的因式分解及倒数级数论证证明其正性。

Result: 证明了 $a_n(\ln4)<0$（$n\ge3$）与 $b_n(\ln4)>0$（$n\ge0$），从而完整解决Tian-Yang提出的两个绝对单调性猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作确认了 $p=\ln4$ 时 $-f_{\ln4}'''$ 与 $g_{\ln4}$ 在 $(0,1)$ 上的绝对单调性，方法结合矩序列、对数凸性与级数因式分解，为相关特殊函数单调性问题提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Let \begin{equation*} f_p(x)=\frac{\mathcal{K}(\sqrt{x})}{p-\ln\sqrt{1-x}}, \end{equation*} where $\mathcal{K}$ denotes the complete elliptic integral of the first kind, and set $g_p=1/f_p$. Tian and Yang conjectured that, at $p=\ln4$, both $-f_{\ln4}^{\prime\prime\prime}$ and $g_{\ln4}$ are absolutely monotonic on $(0,1)$. Writing \begin{equation*} \frac{2}πf_{\ln4}(x)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}a_n(\ln4)x^n,\quad \fracπ{2}g_{\ln4}(x)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}b_n(\ln4)x^n, \end{equation*} we prove \begin{equation*} a_n(\ln4)<0\quad(n\geq3),\quad b_n(\ln4)>0\quad(n\geq0), \end{equation*} thereby settling both conjectures. The proof of the first inequality is based on the representation \begin{equation*} a_n(\ln4)=\frac{(-1)^nC}{15^{n+1}}-M_n, \end{equation*} where $C>0$ and $(M_n)$ is a positive moment sequence. Its strict log-convexity, together with estimates for the initial coefficients, determines the sign of every $a_n$. The second inequality follows from a factorization of the quadratic truncation of the normalized Taylor series and a reciprocal-series argument.

</details>


### [103] [Stochastic maximal $L^p$-regularity for non-autonomous evolution equations with fractional derivative in UMD spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32417)
*Lu Lu Tao,Jia Wei He*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究UMD空间中带广义分数阶导数的非自治随机发展方程的最大正则性理论，通过时间加权空间与正则-奇异分解，给出适定性、时空正则性与随机最大L^p正则性结果，并应用于非自治随机扩散方程与随机分数阶反应-扩散SIR模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Riemann-Liouville与Caputo分数阶导数各有限制，需要统一框架刻画具有中间记忆效应的更广泛反常扩散过程；同时非自治随机发展方程在UMD空间中的最大正则性理论尚需在广义分数阶导数下建立。

Method: 在UMD空间中处理带广义时间分数阶导数的非自治随机发展方程；针对初始项与随机卷积核的奇异性，引入时间加权空间以及正则-奇异分解方法，从而分析适定性与时空正则性并建立随机最大L^p正则性。

Result: 建立了该框架下的适定性、时空正则性与随机最大L^p正则性结果，统一涵盖经典Riemann-Liouville与Caputo分数阶导数；并将结果应用于非自治随机扩散方程及随机分数阶反应-扩散SIR模型。

Conclusion: 广义时间分数阶导数为含中间记忆效应的反常扩散提供统一分析框架，所建立的最大正则性理论可有效处理非自治随机发展方程，并具备实际模型应用价值。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the maximal regularity theory for non-autonomous stochastic evolution equations with a generalized fractional derivative in UMD spaces. The generalized time-fractional derivative provides a unified framework covering both the classical Riemann-Liouville and Caputo fractional derivatives, which accommodates a wider class of anomalous diffusion processes with intermediate memory effects. Based on the singularities of the initial term and the stochastic convolution kernel, a time-weighted space and a regular-singular decomposition are used to obtain the well-posedness, space-time regularity, and stochastic maximal $L^p$-regularity results. Our results are applied to non-autonomous stochastic diffusion equation and stochastic fractional reaction-diffusion SIR model.

</details>


### [104] [Sharp $L^p$ estimates for the strong spherical maximal operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32421)
*Mingfeng Chen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明 $\mathbb{R}^n$（$n\geq4$）中强球面极大算子在 $L^p$（$p>(n+1)/(n-1)$）上有界，从而在剩余维度上解决了 Hickman–Zahl 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hickman 与 Zahl 提出的强球面极大算子 $L^p$ 有界性猜想在部分维度尚未解决，本文旨在补全剩余维度的证明。

Method: 核心是对沿法向变量做二进分解所得分片建立局部 $L^2$ 估计；在将一个输入坐标平方后，把该估计化归为对角二次相位的 $TT^*$ 论证。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{R}^n$（$n\geq4$）中强球面极大算子在 $L^p$ 上有界，其中 $p>(n+1)/(n-1)$。

Conclusion: 本文在剩余维度上完整证明了 Hickman–Zahl 猜想，给出了强球面极大算子的 $L^p$ 有界性。

Abstract: We prove that the strong spherical maximal operator in $\mathbb{R}^n$ is bounded on $L^p$ for $n\geq4$ and $p>(n+1)/(n-1)$. This establishes the conjecture of Hickman and Zahl in the remaining dimensions. The main ingredient is a local $L^2$ estimate for the pieces obtained by a dyadic decomposition in the normal variable. After squaring one input coordinate, we reduce this estimate to a $TT^*$ argument for diagonal quadratic phases.

</details>


### [105] [Level set estimates for strictly convex and hyperbolic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32697)
*Xianghong Chen,Tongou Yang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了严格凸且双曲函数在凸区域上水平集体积的一致上界，并在Hessian下界约束下给出了最优体积界，进而应用于振荡积分的L²估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究凸函数水平集体积的定量上界，并利用该几何测度结果解决振荡积分中与凸相位相关的L²估计问题。

Method: 针对定义在凸区域Ω⊂ℝⁿ（n≥2）上的严格凸、双曲函数f，证明水平集{x∈Ω: c≤f(x)<c+δ}的体积一致上界；在Hessian下界D²f≥Iₙ条件下推导精确体积界。

Result: 在D²f≥Iₙ下得到最优体积界|S^{n-1}| r(Ω)^{n-2} δ，其中r(Ω)=½diamΩ。

Conclusion: 该体积上界为凸相位振荡积分提供了L²估计，推广了水平集几何测度与调和分析之间的联系。

Abstract: In this note, we prove uniform upper bounds for the volume of the level set $$\{x\inΩ: c\le f(x)<c+δ\},\quad c\in\mathbb R,\;δ>0,$$ for strictly convex and hyperbolic functions $f$ defined on a convex domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ ($n\ge 2$). In particular, under a Hessian lower bound $D^2f\ge I_n$, we obtain the sharp volume bound $$|S^{n-1}|\, r(Ω)^{n-2}\,δ,$$ where $r(Ω)=\frac12\,{\mathrm{diam}Ω}$. As an application, we derive $L^2$ estimates for oscillatory integrals with convex phases.

</details>


### [106] [Mollified disc multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32919)
*Anthony Carbery,Andreas Seeger*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明了圆盘乘子磨光化算子在 $L^4(\mathbb R^2)$ 上满足一个尖锐的有界性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆盘乘子（以及更一般的球乘子）在 $L^p$ 空间上的有界性是调和分析与Fourier乘子理论中的核心问题，其临界指标处的端点行为长期受到关注。磨光化是研究此类粗糙乘子在临界空间上有界性的自然手段，但此前缺乏在 $L^4(\mathbb R^2)$ 上的尖锐估计。

Method: 通过对圆盘乘子进行磨光化处理，并结合Fourier乘子估计与 $L^4$ 空间特有的平方函数/分解技巧，建立对该磨光化算子在 $L^4(\mathbb R^2)$ 上的有界性分析。

Result: 得到了圆盘乘子磨光化算子在 $L^4(\mathbb R^2)$ 上的尖锐有界性估计，即所得界在相应意义下不可改进。

Conclusion: 该结果为球乘子在临界 $L^4$ 空间上的磨光化行为提供了精确刻画，完善了相关乘子理论中的端点结果。

Abstract: We prove a sharp boundedness result on $L^4(\mathbb R^2)$ for mollifications of the disc multiplier.

</details>


### [107] [Discreteness, finite rank, and stability of p-frame energy minimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32973)
*Josiah Park*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 研究实单位球面上以 |x·y|^p 为核的能量极小化概率测度：对正有理指数 p=a/b，极小化元存在有限支撑替代；当约化后 b≥d 时所有极小化元有限支撑。对无理指数证明有限秩半正定限制不能有无限紧支撑，并在 Hessian 正定假设下给出局部稳定性与定量支撑界，最后通过减去高阶偶次幂构造逼近核并证明以目标为中心的恢复定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解 Riesz 型（此处为 |x·y|^p）核能量极小化测度的支撑结构，特别是判断极小化元何时为有限支撑（原子测度），以及区分"有限逼近"与"极限问题的离散性"这两个常被混淆的现象。核心问题是：有限支撑性是否依赖于指数的有理/无理性质与维数，以及能否在扰动核下无损恢复极限问题的离散极小化元。

Method: 1) 对有理指数 p=a/b 约化情形，结合有限秩代数提升、Nash 曲线选择与 Wronskian 重数界；2) 对无理指数，分析有限秩半正定限制的紧支撑可能性；3) 在每一点接触处 Hessian 正定的假设下，建立局部稳定性并给出定量支撑上界；4) 通过减去一个适当缩放的高偶数次幂构造一致邻近的核，研究其原子纯度选择并证明以目标为中心的恢复定理。

Result: 对任意正有理 p=a/b，每个极小化元都存在有限支撑的极小化替代；若 a/b 约化且 b≥d，则 S^{d-1} 上每个极小化元都有限支撑。对无理指数，有限秩半正定限制无无限紧支撑。在 Hessian 正定假设下得到局部稳定性与定量支撑界（该假设强于有限支撑，未能证明离散性区域的五条件开性）。减去缩放高偶数次幂后，所得逼近核的每个极小化元均有限支撑，并建立了目标为中心的恢复定理。

Conclusion: 有理指数下有限支撑性可由代数方法导出，且在高维（b≥d）时普遍成立；无理指数排除某些无限紧支撑情形。Hessian 正定是一个比有限支撑更强的条件，尚不足以证明离散性区域的开性，因此极限问题的离散性并未被无条件刻划。最后，扰动核的方法将"有限逼近"与"极限理论的离散性"区分开来，以目标为中心的恢复定理为此提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We study probability measures minimizing the energy with kernel $|x\cdot y|^p$ on the real unit sphere. For every positive rational $p=a/b$, each minimizer admits a finitely supported minimizing replacement. If $a/b$ is reduced and $b\ge d$, every minimizer on $\mathbb S^{d-1}$ is finitely supported. The latter assertion follows from a finite-rank algebraic lift, Nash curve selection, and a Wronskian multiplicity bound. For irrational exponents, we prove that a positive semidefinite restriction of finite rank cannot have infinite compact support. We also establish local stability and a quantitative support bound under positive-definite Hessians at every contact of every limiting minimizer. This hypothesis is stronger than finite support and does not prove unconditional openness of the discreteness regime. Finally, subtracting a single suitably scaled high even power produces a uniformly nearby kernel whose every minimizer has finite support. We identify the atomic-purity selection of these approximants and prove a target-centered recovery theorem, separating finite approximation from discreteness of the limiting problem.

</details>


### [108] [Weighted Bernstein-type inequalities on a trapezoidal domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33283)
*Tomasz Beberok*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究梯形区域上代数多项式的加权L²型Bernstein不等式，借助单位正方形参数化构造对称二阶Sturm-Liouville算子，得到显式常数的方向性Bernstein不等式、偏导数强化估计，并将边界因子与Siciak极值函数的Dini导数联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非标准（梯形）区域上推广代数多项式的Bernstein型不等式，尤其是加权L²范数意义下带显式常数与方向性的精确估计，并揭示其与Siciak极值函数边界行为的联系。

Method: 通过单位正方形对梯形区域进行参数化，构造自然的对称二阶Sturm-Liouville算子，并在加权L²框架下推导具有显式常数的方向性Bernstein不等式。

Result: 获得了带显式常数的精确方向性Bernstein不等式，给出普通偏导数的强化估计，并将相应边界因子与梯形Siciak极值函数的Dini导数建立关联。

Conclusion: 梯形区域上加权L² Bernstein不等式可由对称Sturm-Liouville框架统一处理，边界因子刻画为Siciak极值函数的Dini导数。

Abstract: We study weighted $L^2$ Bernstein-type inequalities for algebraic polynomials on a trapezoidal domain. Using a parametrization by the unit square, we construct a natural symmetric second-order Sturm--Liouville operator and derive exact directional Bernstein inequalities with explicit constants. We also obtain strengthened estimates for the ordinary partial derivatives. Finally, we relate the corresponding boundary factors to Dini derivatives of the Siciak extremal function of the trapezoid.

</details>


### [109] [A Formalism for Merging Power Series in Solving Multinomial Equations: Master Series and Master Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33459)
*Sergey V. Berezin*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文证明一类规范方程的解及其极限情形（二项式根、指数、Lambert方程）与它们的对数，均可统一表示为单一幂主级数 M(m; a; b; x)，并提出发散域外选择共轭级数的几何判据以保证分支连续性，进而给出主级数合并的解析算法，并应用于气体动力学与等离子体物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: gamma函数表示在退化情形下会失效，无法计算系数，因此需要一种更鲁棒的幂级数表示方法；同时多值解在收敛半径外需要选定合适分支，以实现物理上可实现状态的描述。

Method: 引入主级数 M(m; a; b; x)，用主数（master numbers）表示幂级数系数；提出几何判据选择共轭级数（同主级数但参数为 m; a'; b'; x'）；构建主级数合并操作的解析算法。

Result: 统一表达了 m=1 的规范方程 y^a = 1 + a*x*y^b 及其极限情形（b=0 的二项式根、a=0,b=0 的指数、a=0 的 Lambert 方程）与 m=0 的对数解；在退化情形仍可计算系数；给出分支连续性的共轭级数选择方案。

Conclusion: 主级数表示提供了统一的解析框架，可用于计算退化情形系数并进行级数合并；在气体动力学和等离子体物理问题中表明，物理可实现状态需要应用收敛半径外的共轭级数。

Abstract: It is shown that all solutions of the canonical equation y^a = 1 + a*x*y^b with arbitrary complex exponents a, b (case m = 1) and of its limiting cases - the binomial root y = (1 + a*x)^(1/a) at b = 0, the exponential y = e^x at a = 0, b = 0, and the Lambert equation y = e^(x*y^b) at a = 0 - as well as the logarithms of these solutions (case m = 0), are expressed by a single power master series
  M(m; a; b; x) = m + x + ((m - a + 2b)/2!) x^2 + ((m - a + 3b)(m - 2a + 3b)/3!) x^3 + ...
  For multivalued solutions beyond the radius of convergence, a geometric criterion for selecting the conjugate series (the same master series, but with a different set of parameters M(m; a'; b'; x')) is proposed, ensuring branch continuity on the complex plane.
  This representation of power series in terms of master numbers not only allows one to compute coefficients in degenerate cases where the gamma-function representation loses meaning, but also provides a transparent analytical algorithm for the merge operation on master series.
  The method is illustrated on problems in gas dynamics and plasma physics, where it is demonstrated that physically realizable states require the application of conjugate series beyond the radius of convergence of the basic expansion.

</details>


### [110] [Favard length of non-homogeneous random disc-like Cantor sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33557)
*Alan Chang,Damian Dąbrowski,Giacomo Del Nin*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文对Garnett非齐次四角Cantor集的随机变体，给出了Favard长度衰减的精确估计，证明了Mattila关于Favard长度与Riesz容量下界的经典估计在所有衰减速率下都可达（sharp），并构造了一类新的正解析容量、零Favard长度的集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Favard长度（正交投影的平均长度）是几何测度论中的重要量，Mattila曾给出用Riesz容量控制Favard长度下界的经典结果。然而该下界是否在各衰减速率下都精确（sharp），以及正解析容量与零Favard长度集合的构造，此前缺乏系统的极端例子。作者希望通过对Garnett非齐次四角Cantor集随机变体的分析来填补这一空白。

Method: 研究对象是Garnett于1970年代引入的非齐次四角Cantor集的随机变体。作者通过随机化构造控制各尺度上的投影行为，对Favard长度的衰减速率进行精细估计，并与Riesz容量下界进行比对分析。

Result: 得到了该随机四角Cantor集Favard长度衰减的sharp估计；作为推论，构造出的例子表明Mattila基于Riesz容量的经典下界对所有可能的衰减速率都是sharp的；同时获得了一族新的具有正解析容量且Favard长度为零的集合。

Conclusion: 随机化非齐次四角Cantor集是研究Favard长度与容量关系的有效工具，其结果为Mattila经典下界的sharp性提供了完整例证，并拓展了正解析容量、零Favard长度集合的已知例子类。

Abstract: The Favard length of a planar set is the average length of its orthogonal projections. We provide sharp estimates for the Favard length decay of a random variant of the non-homogeneous four corner Cantor sets introduced by Garnett in the 1970s. As a corollary, we obtain examples which show that Mattila's classical lower bound on the Favard length in terms of the Riesz capacity is sharp for all possible decay rates. We also obtain a new family of examples of sets with positive analytic capacity and zero Favard length.

</details>


### [111] [Monotone Sobolev functions: approximation, critical points, and level sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34295)
*Deguang Zhong*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文正面解决了Ntalampekos平面局部光滑问题，并对其高维逼近与水平集问题给出正反两方面答案，揭示了五个基本逼近性质与更强拓扑、例外集结论之间的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Ntalampekos提出的局部光滑问题（Question 1.7）及高维基本逼近与水平集问题（Question 1.8），旨在厘清连续Lebesgue单调W^{1,p}函数能否用单调光滑函数逼近，以及这种逼近在拓扑结构和例外集上能达到何种程度。

Method: 对任意维数n≥2，构造单调C^{1,α}_{loc}函数对W^{1,p}中的连续Lebesgue单调函数进行一致与强逼近，保持Sobolev边界值且不增加p-Dirichlet能量；在平面利用孤立p-调和临界点的局部光滑替换技术，将梯度指标为-m的点分解为m个非退化鞍点；在高维构造显式Lipschitz单调反例，以及平面齐次7-调和函数乘积延拓的反例。

Result: 所有维数下，1<p<∞时单调C^{1,α}_{loc}逼近成立且能量不增，除非原函数p-调和否则能量可严格减小；平面实现孤立p-调和临界点处任意小C^1与Sobolev误差的光滑局部替换，梯度指标-m点解为m个非退化鞍点；结合平面定理得到固定边界值的光滑单调密度，非光滑p-调和极小点能量增量不可避免但可趋于零。n≥3时存在显式Lipschitz单调函数，其区间内每个水平集都有非流形点，尽管存在光滑单调逼近；乘积延拓反例排除了在精确边界与能量约束下的一般离散例外集。

Conclusion: Question 1.8的答案区分了五个基本逼近性质与更强的拓扑及例外集结论；结果应用于约束积分泛函、上水平集的严格BV收敛、非线性通量收敛及驯顺假设下持久图的稳定性。

Abstract: We give an affirmative answer to the planar local smoothing problem in Question~1.7 of D.~Ntalampekos and positive and negative answers to the basic approximation and level-set parts of his higher-dimensional Question~1.8. In every dimension $n\ge2$, each continuous Lebesgue monotone function in $W^{1,p}$ on a bounded open set admits uniform and strong $W^{1,p}$ approximation by monotone $C^{1,α}_{\loc}$ functions, with unchanged Sobolev boundary values and no increase of the $p$-Dirichlet energy, for $1<p<\infty$. The energy can be made strictly smaller unless the original function is $p$-harmonic. In the plane we obtain smooth local replacement at every isolated $p$-harmonic critical point, with arbitrarily small $C^1$ and Sobolev error. A point of gradient index $-m$ can be resolved into exactly $m$ nondegenerate saddles. Together with Ntalampekos's planar theorem, this gives smooth monotone density with fixed boundary values; at a nonsmooth $p$-harmonic minimizer the energy increase is unavoidable but can tend to zero. In dimensions $n\ge3$, an explicit Lipschitz monotone function has a nonmanifold point on every level in an interval, although it admits smooth monotone approximation. A product extension of a planar homogeneous $7$-harmonic function also rules out a general discrete exceptional set under the exact boundary and energy constraints. The answer to Question~1.8 thus distinguishes the five basic approximation properties from the stronger topological and exceptional-set conclusions. Applications include constrained integral functionals, strict $BV$ convergence of superlevel sets, nonlinear flux convergence, and stability of persistence diagrams under tameness assumptions.

</details>


### [112] [Weak Endpoint Estimate for Commutators of Rough Maximal Singular Integral operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34311)
*Shenglong Lin,Qingying Xue*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 对粗糙极大奇异积分与BMO函数生成的交换子 $T^*_{\Omega,b}$，在 $\Omega\in L(\log L)^2(\mathbb{S}^{d-1})$ 且均值为零、$b\in\mathrm{BMO}$ 的条件下，证明了弱型 $L\log L$ 端点不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究粗糙核极大截断奇异积分交换子的端点弱型估计，将经典 $L\log L$ 型端点结果推广到粗糙核与极大截断情形。

Method: 由 $f$ 的 Calderón-Zygmund 分解与极大截断的二进线性化出发；对 $\Omega$ 作尺度分解，正则化空间截断并作微局部分解，将问题化归为一族具定量衰减的估计；通过插值局部 $L^1$ 与 $L^3$ 界并运用解析族论证处理交换子，得到所需衰减。

Result: 对任意 $\lambda>0$，成立分布函数估计 $\bigl|\{x:T^*_{\Omega,b}f(x)>\lambda\}\bigr|\lesssim_{\Omega,b}\int_{\mathbb{R}^d}\frac{|f(x)|}{\lambda}\log\bigl(e+\frac{|f(x)|}{\lambda}\bigr)dx$。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了粗糙极大奇异积分交换子的弱型 $L\log L$ 端点不等式，证明基于尺度分解、微局部分解与解析插值技术。

Abstract: Let $d\geq 2$ and $T^*_{Ω,b}$ be the commutator of the rough maximal singular integral on $\mathbb{R}^d$ defined by $$T^*_{Ω,b}f(x)=\sup_{\varepsilon>0}\left|\int_{|x-y|>\varepsilon}\bigl(b(x)-b(y)\bigr)\frac{Ω(x-y)}{|x-y|^d}f(y)\,dy\right|.$$ Assume that $Ω\in L(\log L)^2(\mathbb{S}^{d-1})$ has mean zero and that $b\in\operatorname{BMO}(\mathbb{R}^d)$. We prove that, for every $λ>0$, \[\bigl|\{x\in\mathbb{R}^d:T^*_{Ω,b}f(x)>λ\}\bigr|\lesssim_{Ω,b}\int_{\mathbb{R}^d}\frac{|f(x)|}λ\log\!\left(e+\frac{|f(x)|}λ\right)\,dx.\] The argument starts from a Calderón-Zygmund decomposition of $f$ and a dyadic linearization of the maximal truncation. A decomposition of $Ω$ by size, followed by regularization of the spatial cutoffs and a microlocal decomposition, reduces the problem to a family of estimates with quantitative decay. The required decay follows by interpolating localized $L^1$ and $L^3$ bounds and using an analytic-family argument for the commutators.

</details>


### [113] [Positive measure and level sets of the Takagi function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34377)
*Lai Jiang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明了经典Takagi函数每个偶数基数水平集所对应的高度集合$S_{2n}$具有正Lebesgue测度，从而证实了Allaart的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Takagi函数$T$的水平集$L(y)=\{x\in[0,1]:T(x)=y\}$的基数结构是分形函数研究的核心问题之一。Allaart猜想：对每个正整数$n$，使$L(y)$恰有$2n$个点的高度$y$所构成的集合$S_{2n}$具有正Lebesgue测度。此前该猜想尚未得到证明。

Method: 设$T$为经典Takagi函数，$L(y)$为其在高度$y$处的水平集；对每个正整数$m$，令$S_m$为使得$L(y)$恰含$m$个点的$y$的集合。通过分析水平集的基数结构与相应高度集合的测度性质，论证$S_{2n}$的Lebesgue测度为正。

Result: 对每个正整数$n$，$S_{2n}$具有正Lebesgue测度。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了Allaart关于Takagi函数偶数基数水平集高度集合测度为正的猜想。

Abstract: Let $T$ be the classical Takagi function, and $L(y):=\{ x \in [0,1] : T(x) = y \}$ be its level set at height $y$. For each positive integer $m$, let $S_m$ be the set of $y$ for which $L(y)$ has exactly $m$ points. We prove that $S_{2n}$ has positive Lebesgue measure for every positive integer $n$, confirming a conjecture of Allaart.

</details>


### [114] [Sharp Quantitative Matrix-Weighted Estimates for Fractional Integrals and Sobolev Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34403)
*Dachun Yang,Wen Yuan,Mingdong Zhang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文建立了分数次积分与Sobolev不等式在矩阵权框架下的定量估计，其权特征常数的指数与标量情形的最优指数一致，因而估计是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的分数次积分估计与Sobolev不等式推广到矩阵权（matrix weight）设定，并给出显式依赖于矩阵Ap,q特征常数[W]的定量估计，且要求所得指数达到最优。

Method: 在矩阵权W∈Ap,q下，对分数次积分I_α建立L^p(W^p)→L^q(W^q)的定量有界估计；并对光滑紧支撑C^d值函数建立矩阵权Sobolev不等式，均显式追踪[W]的幂次。

Result: 得到两项估计：分数次积分估计的指数为(1−α/n)max{1,p'/q}，Sobolev不等式指数为(n−1)/n；两处隐含常数均与W及f无关，且指数与标量最优指数一致。

Conclusion: 矩阵权情形下的分数次积分与Sobolev不等式均成立，其[W]指数达到最优，从而推广并强化了相应的经典结果。

Abstract: Let $α\in(0,n)$, $p\in(1,\frac{n}α)$, $q:=\frac{np}{n-αp}$, and $W\in\mathscr A_{p,q}$. We establish the following quantitative matrix-weighted estimate for the fractional integral $I_α$: for any $\vec{f}\in L^p(W^p)$, \begin{align*} \left\|I_α\vec f\right\|_{L^q(W^q)} \lesssim[W]_{\mathscr A_{p,q}}^{ (1-\fracα{n})\max\{1,\frac{p'}{q}\}} \left\|\vec f\right\|_{L^p(W^p)}, \end{align*} where the implicit positive constant is independent of $W$ and $\vec{f}$. Let $n\geq2$ be an integer, $p\in[1,n)$, $q:=\frac{np}{n-p}$, and $W\in\mathscr A_{p,q}$. We also prove the following matrix-weighted Sobolev inequality: for any smooth $\mathbb{C}^d$-valued function $\vec{f}$ with compact support, \begin{align*} \left\|\vec{f}\right\|_{L^{q}(W^q)} \lesssim[W]_{\mathscr A_{p,q}}^{\frac{n-1}{n}} \left\|WD\vec{f}\right\|_{L^p(\mathbb R^n,\mathbb C^{d\times n})}, \end{align*} where the implicit positive constant is independent of $W$ and $\vec{f}$ and $D\vec{f}$ is the Jacobian matrix of $\vec{f}$. In both estimates, the exponent of $[W]_{\mathscr A_{p,q}}$ coincides with the corresponding optimal scalar exponent, and hence is optimal.

</details>


### [115] [On the Nazarov--Shcheglova Conjecture for Sharp Sobolev Inequalities: The Case (n,p)=(3,2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34516)
*Mohamed Jleli,Bessem Samet*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文证明 Nazarov–Shcheglova 猜想在 $(n,p)=(3,2)$ 情形成立，得到最优常数 $\\lambda_3(3,1,2,1)=\\frac{1}{32\\sqrt5}$，并刻画全部极值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nazarov 与 Shcheglova 提出猜想：$\\lambda_3(n,1,p,1)=2\\lambda_3(n,0,p,\\infty)$，且相应极值函数相同、关于区间中点对称。该猜想刻画一维 Sobolev 不等式在不同导数阶与范数指标下的最优常数关系，具有重要理论意义，但一般情形难以证明，作者遂针对 $(n,p)=(3,2)$ 这一情形加以验证。

Method: 将问题归约为带附加矩约束的算子范数问题；经重标度到 $(-1,1)$ 后，该矩约束化为关于二次多项式的正交性。作者把二阶导数的逆通过相应正交投影进行延拓，并用奇偶分解分析伴随算子：奇部由正 Gram 核控制，偶部用弱-$L^2$ 估计处理。

Result: 证明了 $(n,p)=(3,2)$ 时的猜想，具体得到 $\\lambda_3(3,1,2,1)=\\frac{1}{32\\sqrt5}$；并通过等号情形给出了全部极值函数的刻画，验证了极值函数关于区间中点对称且与 $\\lambda_3(3,0,2,\\infty)$ 对应极值函数一致。

Conclusion: 该工作在一特定参数情形下证实了 Nazarov–Shcheglova 猜想，所发展的矩约束—正交投影—奇偶分解方法为处理此类算子范数问题提供了有效途径，并为后续研究一般 $(n,p)$ 情形奠定基础。

Abstract: For integers $n>k\geq0$ and $1\leq p,q\leq\infty$, let $λ_3(n,k,p,q)$ denote the optimal constant in the one-dimensional Sobolev inequality \[ \|u^{(k)}\|_{L^q(0,1)} \leq λ_3(n,k,p,q) \|u^{(n)}\|_{L^p(0,1)}, \qquad u\in\mathring W_p^n(0,1). \] Nazarov and Shcheglova \cite{NazarovShcheglova} conjectured that \[ λ_3(n,1,p,1) =
2λ_3(n,0,p,\infty), \qquad n\geq2,\quad 1\leq p\leq\infty, \] and that the corresponding extremal functions coincide and are symmetric about the midpoint of the interval. We prove this conjecture for $(n,p)=(3,2)$ and, in particular, obtain \[ λ_3(3,1,2,1) =
\frac{1}{32\sqrt5}. \] The proof is based on a reduction to an operator norm problem with an additional moment constraint. After rescaling to $(-1,1)$, this constraint leads to orthogonality with respect to quadratic polynomials. We extend the inverse of the second derivative through the corresponding orthogonal projection and analyze the adjoint operator by an odd--even decomposition. The odd component is controlled by a positive Gram kernel, while the even component is treated by a weak-$L^2$ estimate. The equality cases yield the characterization of all extremal functions.

</details>


### [116] [Sharp weighted $L^1$ bounds for Coulomb fields on the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35123)
*Evgueni Doubtsov,Ioann Vasilyev,Grigory Voinov*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文在仅依赖维数 \(d\) 的常数意义下，刻画了单位球内由球面上点电荷生成的库仑场的最小 \(L^1\) 范数，并给出了与电荷强度分布相关的精确渐近阶。下界由 \(L^1\) Lipschitz 迹估计与球面上的加权 tent 函数导出；上界通过将球面按质量 \(\alpha_k/A\) 划分为直径受控的胞元得到。等权重情形的锐阶为 \(n^{(d-2)/(d-1)}\)，三维时为 \(\sqrt{n}\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面上多个正电荷在单位球内产生的库仑场的 \(L^1\) 范数最小化问题，旨在确定能量/范数随电荷强度及分布的最优增长阶，并统一等权重与一般权重情形。

Method: 下界：使用 \(L^1\) Lipschitz 迹估计，并构造球面上的显式加权 tent 函数。上界：将球面划分为质量为 \(\alpha_k/A\)、直径为 \(O_d((\alpha_k/A)^{1/(d-1)})\) 的胞元，据此安排电荷位置。

Result: 证明 \[ \inf_{x_1,\dots,x_n \in \mathbb{S}^{d-1}} \int_{\mathbb{B}^d} \left| \sum_{k=1}^n \alpha_k \frac{x_k-x}{|x_k - x|^d} \right| dx \asymp_d A^{-1/(d-1)} \sum_{k=1}^n \alpha_k^{d/(d-1)}. \] 等权重时锐阶为 \(n^{(d-2)/(d-1)}\)，三维时为 \(\sqrt{n}\)。

Conclusion: 该文在依赖维数的常数意义下完整给出了单位球内库仑场 \(L^1\) 范数的极小值渐近公式；等权重特例给出 \(n^{(d-2)/(d-1)}\)，三维为 \(\sqrt{n}\)。

Abstract: Let $d \ge 2$, let $α_1,\dots, α_n > 0$, and put $A = \sum_k α_k$. We determine, up to constants depending only on $d$, the smallest possible $L^1$ norm in the unit ball $\mathbb{B}^d\subset \mathbb{R}^d$ of the Coulomb field generated by charges of strengths $α_k$ placed on the unit sphere $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$. More precisely, we prove \[ \inf_{x_1,\dots,x_n \in \mathbb{S}^{d-1}} \int_{\mathbb{B}^d} \left| \sum_{k=1}^n α_k \frac{x_k-x}{|x_k - x|^d} \right| dx \asymp_d A^{-1/(d-1)} \sum_{k=1}^n α_k^{d/(d-1)}. \] The lower bound follows from an $L^1$ Lipschitz trace estimate and an explicit weighted tent function on the sphere. For the upper bound we use a partition of the sphere into cells of masses $α_k/A$ and diameters $O_d \left((α_k/A)^{1/(d-1)} \right)$. For the unit weights the sharp order is $n^{(d-2)/(d-1)}$, and in dimension three it is $\sqrt{n}$.

</details>


### [117] [The Szemerédi-Trotter Estimate in Finite Field with its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35190)
*Changxing Miao,Rui Xie*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文通过新的多项式分解定理，证明了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 上尖锐的 Szemerédi-Trotter 估计，并给出了若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在素数有限域上建立尖锐的 incidence 界，并借此推进 Furstenberg 集、sum-product 与 Fourier restriction 等核心问题。

Method: 提出新的多项式分解定理（polynomial decomposition theorem）。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{I}(A,\mathcal{L})\lesssim |A||\mathcal{L}|/p+|A|^{2/3}|\mathcal{L}|^{2/3}+|A|+|\mathcal{L}|$；证明了 $\mathbb{F}^2$ 中尖锐 Furstenberg 集估计；改进 sum-product 估计至 $\max\{|A+A|,|A\cdot A|\}\gtrsim\min\{(p|A|)^{1/2},|A|^{5/4}\}$；并在 $p\equiv3\bmod4$ 时改进 $\mathbb{F}^3$ 上 Fourier restriction 估计 $R^*(2\to\alpha)$ 对 $\alpha>10/3$ 成立。

Conclusion: 该多项式分解方法为有限域 incidience 几何与相关调和分析问题提供了统一且更强的工具。

Abstract: We prove a sharp Szemerédi-Trotter estimate
  \[\mathcal{I}(A,\mathcal{L})\lesssim \frac{|A||\mathcal{L}|}{p}+|A|^{2/3}|\mathcal{L}|^{2/3}+|A|+|\mathcal{L}|\]
  for prime finite field $\mathbb{F}=\mathbb{F}_p$ by a new polynomial decomposition theorem.
  As applications, we first prove the sharp Furstenberg set estimate in $\mathbb{F}^2$.
  Secondly, we improve sum-product estimate
  \[\max\{|A+A|,|A\cdot A|\}\gtrsim\min\{(p|A|)^{1/2},|A|^{5/4}\},\quad A\subset\mathbb{F}.\]
  Finally, we improve the Fourier restriction estimate $R^*(2\toα)$ holds for $α>\frac{10}{3}$ in $\mathbb{F}^3$ when $p\equiv 3\mod 4$.

</details>


### [118] [Algorithmic Implementation of Multi-Window STFT Phase Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35329)
*Ting Chen,Hanwen Lu,Wenchang Sun,Yutong Zhao*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文系统构建了多窗STFT相位恢复的数值方案，给出单窗非唯一性反例，并提出两窗重构、稳健K-sign及SCR适配算法，建立确定性稳定性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STFT相位恢复旨在从谱图幅度重建信号。虽有两个紧支撑窗即可理论保证唯一性，但完整算法框架仍缺失，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 理论给出单窗非唯一性反例；提出基于Shannon插值与解析平方根提取的两窗重构算法；引入第三窗与稳健K-sign方法实现局部符号确定；并将SCR算法适配到STFT情形。

Result: 对K-sign方法在有限连续频采样块上建立确定性稳定性，误差界显式包含互补尾部贡献；数值实验验证了所有方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为多窗STFT相位恢复提供了系统的算法框架与稳定性理论，单窗不足而多窗可稳定重建。

Abstract: STFT phase retrieval aims to reconstruct a signal from spectrogram magnitudes. While two compactly supported windows theoretically guarantee uniqueness, a complete algorithmic framework remains lacking. This paper systematically constructs numerical schemes for multi-window STFT phase retrieval. We theoretically provide a nonuniqueness counterexample for the single-window case. We then propose a two-window reconstruction algorithm based on Shannon interpolation and analytic square-root extraction. To overcome the instability of the global sign choice, we introduce a third window and a robust $K$-sign method that determines the sign locally. We also adapt SCR algorithm to the STFT setting. For the $K$-sign method, we establish deterministic stability on a finite contiguous block of frequency samples, with the contribution of the complementary tail explicitly included in the error bound. Numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of all schemes.

</details>


### [119] [Mean values, order, and convergence in the Hardy space of Dirichlet series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35496)
*Ole Fredrik Brevig,Athanasios Kouroupis*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究Dirichlet级数Hilbert空间$\mathscr{H}^2$中Bergman型范数公式在放松解析延拓有界性假设下的有效性，在两个方向给出最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bohr定理表明，若$f\in\mathscr{H}^2$可解析延拓至右半平面且有界，则其范数可由极限均值公式计算。作者希望探究该有界性假设能放松到何种程度。

Method: 从两个方向放松解析延拓的条件：一是考察解析延拓的阶（order），二是考察收敛横坐标。通过分析均值极限与延拓性质的关系构造最优估计。

Result: 在两个方向上均获得最优结果：作为推论得到$\mathscr{H}^2$的经典Landau--Schnee收敛定理的一个版本；并解决了McCarthy提出的一个问题。

Conclusion: 有界解析延拓假设可以显著放松，且在所考察的两个方向上的放松程度是最优的，推广了Bohr范数公式并联系经典收敛定理。

Abstract: Let $\mathscr{H}^2$ denote the Hilbert space of Dirichlet series with square-summable coefficients. It follows from a theorem of Bohr that if $f$ in $\mathscr{H}^2$ has a bounded analytic continuation to the right half-plane, then its norm can be computed from the mean values \[\|f\|_{\mathscr{H}^2}^2 = \lim_{σ\to 0^+} \lim_{T\to\infty} \frac{1}{2T} \int_{-T}^T \lvert f(σ+it) \rvert^2\,dt.\] We investigate to what extent the assumption that the analytic continuation be bounded can be relaxed, and obtain optimal results in two different directions. The first direction concerns the order of the analytic continuation, and yields as a corollary a version of the classical Landau--Schnee convergence theorem for $\mathscr{H}^2$. The second direction concerns the abscissa of convergence, and resolves a problem posed by McCarthy.

</details>


### [120] [Visibility problem in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35388)
*Yuming He,Changbiao Jian,Yudong Liang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 若平面可见部分的Hausdorff维数几乎处处有上界b≥1，则可推出R^d（d≥3）中任意紧集K的可见部分维数上界为d-2+b；代入Orponen–Rutar的平面最优界b=3/2，得到dim_H Vis_e(K)≤d-1/2对几乎所有e成立，从而在各个维数上尖锐地回答其问题1.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文旨在将平面可见部分维数的几乎处处上界推广到高维，并以此解决Orponen与Rutar提出的问题1.9，给出维度依赖的尖锐估计。

Method: 建立从平面情形到高维的维数提升关系：假设平面紧集可见部分的Hausdorff维数几乎处处不超过b（b≥1），推导R^d中紧集可见部分维数不超过d-2+b；随后代入Orponen–Rutar的平面尖锐界b=3/2。

Result: 证明条件性推广：若平面几乎处处上界为b，则R^d中dim_H Vis_e(K)≤d-2+b；取b=3/2得dim_H Vis_e(K)≤d-1/2对几乎所有方向e∈S^{d-1}成立。

Conclusion: 若平面估计b=3/2成立，则可见部分维数的高维上界d-1/2在几乎所有方向、所有维数下均为尖锐，从而完整回答Orponen–Rutar的问题1.9。

Abstract: We show that an almost-everywhere upper bound $b\geq 1$ for the Hausdorff dimension of visible parts of all compact planar sets implies the upper bound $d-2+b$ for visible parts of all compact sets $K$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\geq3$. Inserting the sharp planar bound $b=3/2$ of Orponen and Rutar \cite{OrponenRutar2026} gives $\dim_H \mathrm{Vis}_e(K)\leq d-1/2$ for almost every $e\in S^{d-1}$. This would answer their Question 1.9 sharply in all dimensions.

</details>


### [121] [Toward Pólya and Szegő's conjecture for logarithmic vs Newtonian capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35438)
*Carrie Clark,Richard S. Laugesen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究Pólya-Szegő猜想（给定对数容量下，圆盘/球的静电容量最大），给出一个条件性结果，并利用星函数对称化证明势函数L^p范数下球的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya与Szegő于1945年提出猜想：在所有具有给定对数容量的平面集合中，圆盘达到最大静电容，该问题可自然推广到高维的球与牛顿容量。该猜想至今仍未解决，本文旨在提供支持该猜想的证据与条件性论证。

Method: 采用条件性论证框架：设定带Neumann边界条件的水平条带中的低维集合问题，将猜想归结为平衡势的Dirichlet积分的横向分量对低维球取极小这一性质；同时运用Baernstein星函数对称化技术处理势函数的L^p范数。

Result: 证明了条件性结论：若能证明在带Neumann边界条件的水平条带中，低维集合的平衡势Dirichlet积分横向分量对低维球取极小，则Pólya-Szegő猜想成立。此外，作为该性质的支持性证据，证明了球在势函数L^p范数意义下具有极值性。

Conclusion: 本文未完全解决Pólya-Szegő猜想，而是将其归约为一个关于Dirichlet积分横向分量的极小性命题，并提供了势函数L^p范数下球极值性的支持证据，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Pólya and Szegő conjectured in 1945 that the disk achieves the largest electrostatic capacity among all planar sets with given logarithmic capacity. This question generalizes naturally to balls and Newtonian capacities in higher dimensions.
  The conjecture remains open. This paper establishes a conditional result: the conjecture would follow if among all lower dimensional sets in a horizontal strip with Neumann boundary conditions, the transverse component of the Dirichlet integral of the equilibrium potential could be shown minimal for the lower dimensional ball. As supporting evidence toward this latter property, the paper shows extremality of the ball for $L^p$-norms of the potentials, via Baernstein star-function symmetrization techniques.

</details>


### [122] [Jackson-type Inequalities with Explicit Weight Dependence for N-term Wavelet Approximation on Localized Weighted Besov Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35520)
*Kai-Cheng Wang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明：在带Muckenhoupt权的齐次加权Besov空间中，用有限项带限双正交小波系统逼近的小波树恢复函数满足Jackson型不等式，误差按光滑度-维数比速率衰减，常数有闭式上界且权重仅通过Muckenhoupt特征的显式幂出现；极值族表明该速率指数最优，无权反例表明局部化条件必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在齐次加权Besov空间（带Muckenhoupt权）中，沿单位立方体二进树的小波展开恢复函数时，用固定项数带限双正交小波逼近的误差估计问题，即建立相应的Jackson型不等式并刻画其最优性与条件必要性。

Method: 针对带限双正交小波系统，在齐次加权Besov空间与Muckenhoupt权框架下，沿单位立方体二进树从小波展开恢复函数，证明有限项逼近的Jackson型不等式；给出误差常数的闭式上界，其中权重仅以Muckenhoupt特征的显式幂形式进入。

Result: 建立了Jackson型不等式，误差以光滑度-维数比设定的速率衰减；显式常数具有闭式上界，权重仅通过Muckenhoupt特征的显式幂影响估计；构造极值族证明该速率指数在相应函数类上不可提高；给出无权示例说明局部化条件的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了加权Besov空间中小波有限项逼近的定量Jackson型估计，明确了逼近速率的指数最优性以及局部化条件在无权情形下的必要性。

Abstract: We prove a Jackson-type inequality for approximation by a
  prescribed number of terms of a band-limited biorthogonal wavelet system
  in homogeneous weighted Besov spaces with Muckenhoupt weights, on
  functions recovered from their wavelet expansion along the dyadic tree of
  the unit cube. The error decays at a rate set by the smoothness-to-dimension
  ratio. The explicit constant has a closed-form majorant, and the weight
  enters only through a displayed power of the Muckenhoupt characteristic.
  An extremal family shows that the exponent of this rate cannot be
  increased on the class, and an unweighted example shows that the
  localization is necessary.

</details>


### [123] [Pitt Inequalities and Logarithmic-type Uncertainty Principles for Metaplectic Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34946)
*Gianluca Giacchi,Itamar Oliveira,Amy Tierney*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文对metaplectic算子的Pitt不等式给出了完整刻画，分别建立各向同性与各向异性版本，并推导对数与熵不确定性原理，揭示熵不确定性原理在存在奇异方向时失效及修正机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: metaplectic算子由其背后的辛几何区分有效方向（Fourier型作用）与奇异方向（保持集中），经典Pitt不等式与经典熵不确定性原理未考虑该几何结构，因此需要从各向同性和各向异性两个角度重建相应结论。

Method: 采用两种互补视角：一是类比经典Pitt定理的各向同性Pitt不等式；二是适配metaplectic群几何特征的各向异性不等式。结合辛几何分析有效方向与奇异方向，并针对齐次Sobolev空间上的定量有界性及二次Hamiltonian生成的Schrödinger演化进行论证。

Result: 得到metaplectic算子的完整Pitt不等式刻画；作为副产品，获得沿$\rd$子空间的Fourier变换的Pitt不等式，以及metaplectic算子在齐次Sobolev空间上新的定量时变有界性结果，并应用于二次Hamiltonian生成的Schrödinger演化。此外推导出对数与熵不确定性原理，证明当有效方向与奇异方向同时存在时经典熵不确定性原理失效，需引入一般无界的修正熵项来恢复有意义的下界；对二次Schrödinger演化，该修正项遵循非色散方向的时间依赖几何并相应调整不确定性估计。

Conclusion: metaplectic算子的Pitt不等式与不确定性原理需按辛几何的有效/奇异方向结构进行各向异性刻画；经典熵不确定性原理在混合方向情形下失效，通过引入无界修正熵项可恢复下界，并在二次Schrödinger演化中获得时间依赖的定量估计。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a complete characterisation of Pitt's inequality for metaplectic operators. The symplectic geometry underlying a metaplectic operator distinguishes effective directions, along which its action is Fourier-type, from singular directions, along which concentration is preserved. For this reason, we adopt two complementary perspectives, thereby obtaining both an isotropic Pitt's inequality, emulating the classical theorem of Pitt, and an anisotropic inequality that adapts to the geometric features of the metaplectic group. As a by-product, we obtain Pitt's inequality for Fourier transforms along subspaces of $\rd$ and new quantitative time-dependent boundedness results for metaplectic operators on homogeneous Sobolev spaces, with applications to Schrödinger evolutions generated by quadratic Hamiltonians.
  Additionally, we derive logarithmic and entropic uncertainty principles for the metaplectic group. We show that the classical entropic uncertainty principle fails when both the effective and singular directions are present. In this case, a generally unbounded corrective entropy term accounts for the concentration-preserving directions and restores a meaningful lower bound. For quadratic Schrödinger evolutions, this correction follows the time-dependent geometry of the nondispersive directions and adjusts the uncertainty estimate accordingly.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Exact majority C-colourings of balanced Hamming graphs and grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31674)
*ZeHua Lu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文确定超立方体笛卡尔幂 \(K_q^{\square(2k+1)}\) 的多数着色数 \(M\) 的精确公式，并通过下界构造与Hamming边等周上界完成证明；同时给出圆柱图 \(C_m\square P_n\) 的精确值，推翻 arXiv:2608.27669v1 中 Conjecture 4 关于圆柱的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数着色（majority C-colouring）要求每个顶点在自身颜色类中至少拥有一半邻居，\(M(G)\) 表示最大颜色类数。此前该参数在不同图族上的精确值尚不清楚，尤其是超立方体笛卡尔幂与圆柱/环面图族，且已有文献（arXiv:2608.27669v1）中关于圆柱的猜想可能有误。

Method: 下界：构造显式矩形划分，并设计统一的三维“桥”（bridge）结构；提出“带孔矩形”（punctured-rectangle）构造，同时提供桥与中间维度划分。上界：利用Hamming边等周不等式（经典结果），并附上初等证明。此外，推导分层界（layer bounds）以处理其他圆柱和环面族。

Result: 主要定理：\(M(K_q^{\square(2k+1)})=\left\lfloor\frac{q^{k+1}}{\lfloor q/2\rfloor+1}\right\rfloor\ (q\ge3,\ k\ge0)\)。另证 \(M(C_m\square P_n)=\frac n2\lfloor m/2\rfloor\)（\(m\ge4\)，偶数 \(n\ge2\)），在奇数 \(m\ge7\)、偶数 \(n\ge6\) 时与 arXiv:2608.27669v1 中 Conjecture 4 的圆柱断言矛盾。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了奇维超立方体笛卡尔幂的多数着色数，并纠正了已有关于圆柱图的猜想；所引入的桥与带孔矩形构造、分层界方法可推广到更多圆柱与环面图族。

Abstract: A majority C-colouring partitions a graph into classes in which every vertex has at least half of its neighbours. Write $M(G)$ for the maximum number of classes. We determine $M(K_q^{\square(2k+1)})=\left\lfloor\frac{q^{k+1}}{\lfloor q/2\rfloor+1}\right\rfloor\qquad(q\ge3,\ k\ge0).$ The lower bound follows from explicit rectangular partitions and a uniform three-dimensional bridge. A punctured-rectangle construction supplies both the bridge and a partition in the intermediate dimension. The upper bound is a classical consequence of Hamming edge isoperimetry; an elementary proof is included. We also prove $M(C_m\square P_n)=\frac n2\lfloor m/2\rfloor$ for $m\ge4$ and even $n\ge2$, contradicting the cylinder assertion of Conjecture 4 in arXiv:2608.27669v1 for odd $m\ge7$ and even $n\ge6$. Further layer bounds determine additional cylinder and torus families.

</details>


### [125] [The Spectral Region of Two-Layer Renewal Stochastic Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31768)
*Brecht Verbeken,Vincent Ginis*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 确定了一类由两条确定性 q 步路径（随机性仅在末行）构成的 2q×2q 行随机矩阵族的谱并集，并刻画了其可见边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有受限随机性（仅末行随机）的行随机矩阵族的谱结构，揭示参数空间、Farey 算术与谱边界之间的几何联系。

Method: 利用等谱平衡映射将参数空间约化为 q-单纯形；借助 transfer 几何给出每个谱点的三角面实现；用 Farey 算术在单纯形一维骨架上选取游走，由底边与侧边构造边界承载曲线，并对侧边情形进行扇区选择分支分析。

Result: 谱区域在实轴截面为 [-1,1]；与单位圆的交恰为阶不超过 2q 的单位根；缺失扇区 0<|Argλ|<π/q。邻接边情形存在两个代数平方根，但仅一个扇区选中分支支撑边界；该承载曲线在每条射线上唯一且最外层，谱区域在其下沿径向填充。奇数 q 时末边还贡献一段负实边界区间。

Conclusion: 三角面生成整个谱并集，而单纯形的边生成其边界，从而精确刻画了该矩阵族的谱并集及其可见边界。

Abstract: Fix $q\ge2$ and consider the $2q\times2q$ row-stochastic matrices formed from two deterministic $q$-step paths, with randomness confined to their terminal rows. We determine the spectral union of this family exactly. An isospectral balancing map reduces the full parameter space to a $q$-simplex, while transfer geometry gives a triangular-face realization for every spectral point. The visible boundary is subtler: Farey arithmetic selects a walk on the simplex one-skeleton, whose base and lateral edges furnish the boundary carriers. In the lateral case the squared edge equation admits two algebraic square roots, but only a sector-selected branch is supporting. We prove that the selected carrier is unique and outermost on each ray and that the spectral region fills radially beneath it. The region has real section $[-1,1]$, meets the unit circle exactly at roots of unity of order at most $2q$, and omits the sector $0<|\operatorname{Arg}λ|<π/q$. For odd $q$, the terminal edge also contributes a negative-real boundary interval. Thus triangular faces generate the spectral union, whereas simplex edges generate its boundary.

</details>


### [126] [Finite-Defect Rigidity and the Minimum Spherical 4-Design on the Two-Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31689)
*Shalender Singh,Vishnupriya Singh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上等权球面 $4$-设计的点数下界为 $12$，从而确定 $N_4(\mathbb{S}^2)=12$（正二十面体为 $5$-设计）。证明基于一种"有限亏量理论"（finite-defect theory），通过 Naimark 补、配对核界、对跖下界、Cayley-Bacharach 信息以及乘性关系空间的一致下界来排除 $9,10,11$ 点的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定球面设计的最小点数 $N_t(\mathbb{S}^2)$ 是球面设计理论的中心问题。对于 $t=4$，此前未确定精确最小值；作者希望证明等权球面 $4$-设计至少需 $12$ 点，并借此建立一个可处理低亏量（corank 小）情形的通用框架。

Method: 引入"有限亏量理论"：对亏量 $c=N-\dim P_m$ 的球面 $2m$-设计，构造其 Naimark 补（单位向量 $u_x\in\mathbb{S}^{c-1}$），并证明精确耦合公式 $u_x\cdot u_y=-K_m^{(d)}(x\cdot y)/c$。由此导出配对核界、对跖下界、Cayley-Bacharach 信息和乘性关系空间的一致下界。分别处理 corank one（设计分裂为两个等权球面 $m$-设计，用带符号 Schoenberg 系数的残数公式）与 corank two（补为圆 Gale 框架，用相位乘法产生线性-二次别名，结合矩阵值 Cayley-Bacharach 论证与 Pauli 正规形）。

Result: 证明等权球面 $4$-设计在 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上至少 $12$ 点，确定 $N_4(\mathbb{S}^2)=12$，即不存在 $9,10,11$ 点的此类设计。corank one 情形中，$m+3$ 次系数在 $3\le d\le m+1$ 时为负，从而在整个该范围内排除 corank one；强度 $4$ 和 $6$ 时唯一例子分别是正六边形和正八边形。corank two 情形中，每个维度得到精确的范数与 socle 恒等式；对 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上 $11$ 点，通过矩阵值 Cayley-Bacharach 论证排除一般分支，例外分支归结为 Pauli 正规形并与二阶矩矛盾。

Conclusion: 等权球面 $4$-设计的精确最小点数为 $12$。对于维度 $d\ge4$，corank two 问题仍开放，作者指出一个被迫出现的二次 socle 是推广当前论证的障碍。

Abstract: We prove that every equal-weight spherical $4$-design on $\mathbb{S}^2$ has at least twelve points. Since the regular icosahedron is a spherical $5$-design, this determines the exact minimum$$N_4(\mathbb{S}^2)=12;$$equivalently, no such design has $9$, $10$ or $11$ points.The proof is part of a finite-defect theory. If a spherical $2m$-design has corank $c=N-\dim P_m$, its Naimark complement consists of unit vectors $u_x\in\mathbb{S}^{c-1}$ forming a spherical $2$-design and satisfying the exact coupling$$u_x\cdot u_y=-\frac{K_m^{(d)}(x\cdot y)}{c}\qquad(x\ne y).$$This gives a pairwise kernel bound, an antipodal lower bound, Cayley-Bacharach information, and uniform lower bounds for multiplicative-relation spaces. In corank one the design splits into two equal spherical $m$-designs. We derive a residue formula for its signed Schoenberg coefficients; the coefficient of degree $m+3$ is negative exactly when $3\le d\le m+1$, excluding corank one throughout that range. At strengths four and six the only examples in any dimension are the regular hexagon and octagon, respectively.In corank two the complement is a circle Gale frame. Multiplication by its phase forces at least $d-1$ linear-quadratic aliases and yields exact norm and socle identities in every dimension. For eleven nodes on $\mathbb{S}^2$, two aliases produce a real harmonic cubic and a Hermitian quartic matrix. A matrix-valued Cayley-Bacharach argument eliminates the generic branch; the exceptional branch reduces to a Pauli normal form and contradicts the second moments. In dimensions $d\ge4$ the corank-two problem remains open; we identify a forced quadratic socle as the obstruction to extending the present argument.

</details>


### [127] [Fractional Triangle Decompositions at Partite Minimum Degree $4n/5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31758)
*Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明三分子图的最小部度≥4n/5时存在分数三角形分解，并据此将ε-稠密部分拉丁方阵可完成性的渐近界从2/25改进到1/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进ε-稠密部分拉丁方阵可完成性的渐近常数界（此前为2/25），并给出有限的、精确的分数三角形分解定理。

Method: 针对可被3整除的平衡三部图：先用最小权完美匹配对任意Farkas对偶权重做归一化，再构造显式的“直达加两步”路由方案，使每条非匹配边上的总拥塞不超过1，从而得到分数三角形分解。

Result: 三部图每部大小为n、部最小度≥4n/5时必存在分数三角形分解；结合Barber–Kühn–Lo–Osthus–Taylor的多部图分解定理，对任意固定ε<1/5及充分大的n，每个n阶ε-稠密部分拉丁方阵均可完成。

Conclusion: 分数三角形分解定理是有限且精确的；由它推出的ε<1/5完成性界优于此前2/25的结果。

Abstract: We prove that every triangle-divisible balanced tripartite graph whose vertex classes have size $n$ and whose partite minimum degree is at least $4n/5$ admits a fractional triangle decomposition. Combined with the multipartite decomposition theorem of Barber, Kühn, Lo, Osthus, and Taylor, this implies that, for every fixed $\varepsilon<1/5$ and all sufficiently large $n$, every $\varepsilon$-dense partial Latin square of order $n$ is completable, improving the previous asymptotic bound of $2/25$. The fractional decomposition theorem is finite and exact. Its proof uses a minimum-weight perfect matching to normalize an arbitrary Farkas dual weighting, followed by an explicit direct-and-two-step routing scheme whose aggregate congestion on every off-matching edge is at most one.

</details>


### [128] [A Twelve-Row Seed and a One-Row Extension for Five-Column Recursive-Line Zarankiewicz Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33191)
*Min Xi,Jingya Chang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明五列递归线 Zarankiewicz 数对一切 $m\ge 12$ 满足 $z_{RL}(m,5)=3m+5$，并由此得到 $z_2(m,5)=z_{RL}(m,5)=3m+5$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定五列递归线 Zarankiewicz 数的精确值，并借助参数层级关系将递归线情形与经典 Zarankiewicz 数 $z_2(m,5)$ 的精确值联系起来。

Method: 构造一个显式的 $12\times5$ 种子构型，该构型达到五列单元上界且无空单元；开启并替换两个选定点为平行路径，配合递归单行扩展方案使每插入一行总增边数增加三并保持简单构型。为验证在任意扩展长度下一致成立的条件 (RW3+)，提出距离缩减矩形引理，将远距离标号间的关系沿扩展路径传递并化归到较小路径距离，从而把无限长扩展的验证归结为有限的种子与接口证书集合。

Result: 构型满足配对识别、保持不同选定边类，并验证不同边代表的正交性，给出下界 $z_{RL}(m,5)\ge 3m+5$；结合参数层级得到 $z_2(m,5)=z_{RL}(m,5)=3m+5$（$m\ge 12$）。

Conclusion: 五列递归线 Zarankiewicz 数在 $m\ge12$ 时为 $3m+5$，且与 $z_2(m,5)$ 一致，表明该范围内递归线构造达到最优。

Abstract: We prove that the five-column recursive-line Zarankiewicz number satisfies $z_{RL}(m,5)=3m+5$ for every integer $m\ge 12$. The proof is based on an explicit $12\times5$ seed configuration combined with a recursive one-row extension scheme. The seed attains the five-column cell bound and contains no unoccupied cells. Two selected pairs in the seed are opened and replaced by parallel paths, ensuring that each inserted row increases the total number of augmented edges by three while preserving the required simple configuration. The main challenge lies in verifying the strengthened recursive-line condition ${\rm (RW3+)}$ uniformly across all extension lengths. To this end, we develop a distance-reducing rectangle lemma that transfers certified inner-product relations along extension paths, reducing relations between distant labels to those at smaller path distances. This confines the verification for arbitrarily long extensions to a finite collection of seed and interface certificates. The resulting construction successfully satisfies pair identification, preserves distinct selected-edge classes, and certifies the orthogonality of distinct edge representatives. Furthermore, combining this constructed lower bound with the parameter hierarchy yields $z_2(m,5)=z_{RL}(m,5)=3m+5$ throughout this range.

</details>


### [129] [The Wang Transform Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31794)
*Yair Lavi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意阶的Wang变换不等式：对行和、列和均为1的非负n×n矩阵A，恒有per A ≥ per((nJ_n+A)/(n+1))，等号当且仅当A=J_n成立；并给出n≥2时显式的正二次间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang变换不等式此前可能仅在特定阶数或特殊情形下被验证，缺乏对任意正整数n的一般性证明。本文旨在将该不等式推广至所有阶数，并量化其严格性。

Method: 作者针对行和与列和均为1的非负矩阵（双随机矩阵）的积和式（permanent）进行分析，通过证明per A ≥ per((nJ_n+A)/(n+1))这一任意阶不等式，并刻画等号成立条件，进一步推导n≥2时的显式正二次间隙。

Result: 证明了任意阶n下Wang变换不等式成立；等号当且仅当A=J_n；并得到n≥2时关于偏离J_n程度的显式正二次下界（quadratic gap）。

Conclusion: Wang变换不等式在所有阶数均成立且具严格性，等号仅由均匀矩阵J_n取到；正二次间隙的显式形式进一步刻画了该不等式的稳定性，对积和式不等式及双随机矩阵理论具有意义。

Abstract: We prove the Wang transform inequality in every order: if $n$ is a positive integer, $A$ is a nonnegative $n\times n$ matrix whose row and column sums equal one, and $J_n$ has every entry $1/n$, then
\[
\operatorname{per}A\ge
\operatorname{per}\!\left(\frac{nJ_n+A}{n+1}\right).
\]
Equality holds exactly when $A=J_n$. We also obtain an explicit positive quadratic gap for every $n\ge2$.

</details>


### [130] [A polylogarithmic higher-order Cheeger inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35369)
*Yunpeng Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了归一化拉普拉斯第k特征值与k个不相交顶点集的最小最大电导之间的定量关系：ρ_G(k) ≤ C[1+log(k+1)]^5 √λ_k(G)，更强地构造了k个支撑不交的非负函数，其Rayleigh商为O([1+log(k+1)]^10 λ_k(G))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图的高阶谱与多路划分（k个不相交集合的电导）之间的定量联系，将经典Cheeger型不等式推广到第k特征值及k路割的情形，并追求以原图度量（原始边界与体积）给出界，同时获得函数形式的更强结论。

Method: 证明使用独立局部截断（独立local cutoffs），通过在每个单元中沿主方向对损失进行条件化来控制丢失的协方差；利用正则化谱嵌入在整个原始低特征空间上界定截断能量；采用自适应构造使剩余系数维度每阶段至少减半；最后通过维度论证将近似秩一的局部协方差转化为恰好k个标量见证。

Result: 得到ρ_G(k) ≤ C[1+log(k+1)]^5 √λ_k(G)（C为绝对常数），构造恰好k个集合，界以λ_k(G)表示且所有边界与体积均在原图中度量；更强地给出k个两两支撑不交的非负函数，其Rayleigh商为O([1+log(k+1)]^10 λ_k(G))。

Conclusion: 建立了第k特征值与k路最小最大电导之间的多项式对数因子型Cheeger不等式，并给出函数层面的更强构造，为图的高阶谱划分提供了新的分析工具与定量保证。

Abstract: Let $λ_k(G)$ be the $k$th eigenvalue of the normalized Laplacian of a finite undirected weighted graph, and let $ρ_G(k)$ be the minimum possible maximum conductance of $k$ disjoint nonempty vertex sets. We prove \[
  ρ_G(k)\le C[1+\log(k+1)]^5\sqrt{λ_k(G)} \] for an absolute constant $C$. The construction gives exactly $k$ sets and a bound in terms of $λ_k(G)$, with all boundaries and volumes measured in the original graph. More strongly, it yields $k$ nonnegative functions with pairwise disjoint supports and Rayleigh quotients $O([1+\log(k+1)]^{10}λ_k(G))$. The proof uses independent local cutoffs whose lost covariance is controlled by conditioning on the loss along a principal direction in each cell. A regularized spectral embedding bounds cutoff energy on the entire original low eigenspace, while an adaptive construction reduces the remaining coefficient dimension by at least half at each stage. A dimension argument then converts almost rank-one local covariances into exactly $k$ scalar witnesses.

</details>


### [131] [An improved lower bound for the van der Waerden number $w(3,k)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31798)
*Haitao Cao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $w(3,k)\ge k^{\Omega(\log k)}$，改进了此前 Green 和 Hunter 的下界，并给出证据支持 $k^{\Theta(\log k)}$ 为正确的量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究红蓝染色下同时避免蓝色三项等差数列与红色 $k$ 项等差数列的阈值 $w(3,k)$。此前下界为 $k^{\Omega(\log k/\log\log k)}$（Hunter），上界猜想为 $k^{O(\log k)}$（Green），上下界之间存在显著差距，本文旨在缩小这一差距。

Method: 通过构造性方法，建立新的下界证明，证明存在染色方案使得 $w(3,k)\ge k^{\Omega(\log k)}$，即在 $[n]$ 上避免蓝色三项等差数列与红色 $k$ 项等差数列。

Result: 证明了 $w(3,k)\ge k^{\Omega(\log k)}$，将下界从 $k^{\Omega(\log k/\log\log k)}$ 显著提升至接近上界猜想 $k^{O(\log k)}$ 的量级。

Conclusion: 新下界与 Green 猜想的上界在指数上仅相差常数因子，表明 $k^{\Theta(\log k)}$ 很可能就是 $w(3,k)$ 的正确量级。

Abstract: For an integer $k\ge 3$, let $w(3,k)$ be the least $n$ such that every red-blue coloring of $[n]$ contains either a nontrivial three-term arithmetic progression in blue or a nontrivial $k$-term arithmetic progression in red. A recent breakthrough of Green proved that $w(3,k)\ge k^{Ω\left(\left(\frac{\log k}{\log\log k}\right)^{1/3}\right)}$ when $k$ is large, and Hunter later improved the bound to $w(3,k)\ge k^{Ω\left(\frac{\log k}{\log\log k}\right)}$. On the other hand, Green remarked that it is reasonable to believe that $w(3,k)\le k^{O(\log k)}$. We prove that $w(3,k)\ge k^{Ω(\log k)}$, which perhaps gives some evidence that $k^{Θ(\log k)}$ is the correct order of magnitude.

</details>


### [132] [The stacking number of a tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31811)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明:对于任意至少两个顶点的有限树 T,其 stacking number 等于 Csernák 与 Soukup 猜想的 rooted distance-and-degree estimator。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定图的 stacking number 是图论中关于 pebbling 变换的核心问题;此前 Csernák 与 Soukup 针对树提出了一个由根距离与度构成的估计量猜想,但尚未得到完整证明。本文旨在验证这一猜想对任意有限树的成立性。

Method: 证明包含三个关键部分:(1) 对指定顶点处可堆叠性(stackability)的精确递归刻画;(2) 一个显式的零分数障碍(zero-score obstruction);(3) 针对任意不可堆叠配置的加权抵消(weighted cancellation)论证。完整定理在 Lean 4 中形式化,并通过 Palomar 机械验证。

Result: 对每个至少两个顶点的有限树 T,其 stacking number 等于 Csernák–Soukup 的 rooted distance-and-degree estimator;形式化结果已通过 Palomar 机械验证,注册编号为 PALOMAR-2026-09-25-000010。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了树类图上的 stacking number 猜想,并给出经 Lean 4 形式化与机械验证的严格证明,兼具组合图论与形式化验证两方面价值。

Abstract: The stacking number of a graph is the least integer t >= 2 such that every configuration of t pebbles can be transformed by pebbling moves into a configuration supported on one vertex. We prove that, for every finite tree T with at least two vertices, this number equals the rooted distance-and-degree estimator conjectured by Csernák and Soukup. The proof uses an exact recursive characterization of stackability at a prescribed vertex, an explicit zero-score obstruction, and a weighted cancellation argument for arbitrary nonstackable configurations. The complete theorem is formalized in Lean 4; the formal result has also passed Palomar mechanical verification and is publicly registered as PALOMAR-2026-09-25-000010.

</details>


### [133] [Minimal transitive factorizations supported on quasi-threshold graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31982)
*Cordelia Yuqiao Li,Ricky Ini Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究了在准阈值图上恒等置换分解为对换的最小传递因子分解数目，证明该数目总可被星图的分解计数 $(2n-2)!/n!$ 整除，并给出了基于因子分解树的加权求和组合公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Irving 和 Rattan 已证明星图情形下的最小传递因子分解计数为 $(2n-2)!/n!$。本文旨在探究更一般的准阈值图下该计数的性质，特别是其与星图计数之间的整除关系，从而推广并联系已有结果。

Method: 将准阈值图上恒等置换的最小传递因子分解数目表示为对 $n^{n-2}$ 个因子分解树（满足特定流量约束的边加权生成树）某一子集的加权求和，给出组合公式，并据此证明整除性。

Result: 证明准阈值图上恒等置换的最小传递因子分解数目总是 $(2n-2)!/n!$ 的整数倍，即总可被星图的因子分解计数整除。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了准阈值图上最小传递因子分解计数的组合刻画，揭示了星图计数作为其普遍下界整除因子的地位，为因子分解树的加权求和方法提供了新的应用。

Abstract: We study the number of minimal transitive factorizations of the identity permutation in $S_n$ into transpositions supported on a quasi-threshold graph. We show that this number is always divisible by $(2n-2)!/n!$, which is the factorization count for a star graph, as shown by Irving and Rattan. To prove this, we give a combinatorial formula for the number of such factorizations as a weighted sum over a subset of the $n^{n-2}$ factorization trees, which are edge-weighted spanning trees satisfying certain flow constraints.

</details>


### [134] [The modal expansion of Kempner sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31986)
*Jean-François Burnol*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文是对作者前作《Block-count constrained harmonic sums: spectral expansion and block-directed Euler--Maclaurin》的延续，专门补充论证了块-Irwin和 $I(b,w,k)$ 在 $k=0$ 时、$w$ 为多位数字块情形的模态展开，此前该情形在前作中仅给出极简的结果概述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作虽已完整建立框架并处理了 $k\geq1$ 的一般情形以及 $k=0$、$w$ 为单个数字的情形，但对于 $k=0$ 且 $w$ 为多位数字块的情形仅给出简短的结果摘要，缺少充分且专门的处理，因此需要单独讨论加以补充。

Method: 沿用前作已完全建立的谱展开与块导向 Euler--Maclaurin 框架，针对 $k=0$ 情形进行独立的、专门的论证与推导。

Result: 给出了 $k=0$ 时 $w$ 为多位数字块的块-Irwin和 $I(b,w,k)$ 的模态展开，填补了前作仅以摘要形式呈现的空白。

Conclusion: 该 $k=0$ 情形需要一个单独且专门的讨论，本文即提供了这一补充处理，使块-Irwin和的模态展开在 $k$ 与 $w$ 各情形下得以完整覆盖。

Abstract: This is a continuation of the author's earlier paper Block-count constrained harmonic sums: spectral expansion and block-directed Euler--Maclaurin. We presented in that reference the modal expansion of block-Irwin sums $I(b,w,k)$ for $k\geq1$. The case $k=0$ was fully developed when $w$ is a single digit, but it was presented only as a very short result summary for $w$ a multi-digit block. Although the framework is already completely exposed in the earlier paper, the case $k=0$ requires adding a separate, dedicated discussion, which is provided here.

</details>


### [135] [An improved lower bound for the packing number of the 2-token graph of the cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31991)
*Luis Manuel Rivera*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了循环图两令牌图 $F_2(C_n)$ 的 packing 数新下界 $\rho(F_2(C_n))\ge\lfloor n(n-2)/10\rfloor+1$，对所有 $n\ge3$ 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Gómez Soto 与 Ríos-Castro 仅证明了 $n\ge19$ 时 $\rho(F_2(C_n))\ge a(n)$（其中 $a(n)$ 为显式表达式），其界限在部分同余类上并非最优，本文希望给出更普适且更紧的下界。

Method: 通过构造性组合论证，对循环图 $C_n$ 的两令牌图 $F_2(C_n)$ 的 packing 结构进行分析，推导出对任意 $n\ge3$ 成立的统一下界。

Result: 证明了对所有 $n\ge3$，$\rho(F_2(C_n))\ge\lfloor n(n-2)/10\rfloor+1$；当 $n\equiv0,2\pmod{10}$ 时，该界比 $a(n)$ 提高 1。

Conclusion: 该结果将原有下界的适用范围从 $n\ge19$ 扩展到所有 $n\ge3$，并在特定同余类上改进了已有界限。

Abstract: Let $F_2(C_n)$ be the $2$-token graph of the cycle $C_n$ and let $ρ$ denote the packing number. Gómez Soto and Ríos-Castro recently proved that $ρ(F_2(C_n))\ge a(n)$ for $n\ge 19$, where $a(n)$ is an explicit expression. In this note, we prove that \[ ρ(F_2(C_n))\ \ge\ \left\lfloor\frac{n(n-2)}{10}\right\rfloor+1\qquad\text{for every } n\ge 3, \] which improves $a(n)$ by one whenever $n\equiv 0,2\pmod{10}$.

</details>


### [136] [VC-dimension of distance graphs in finite field geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32052)
*Brian McDonald Bakas,Moustapha Diallo,Alex McDonald*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究有限域向量空间大子集的距离图的VC维数，证明当子集大小超过某阈值时，其距离图VC维至少为k，并说明k=d时指数最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限域上距离图是加性组合与几何测度问题的重要研究对象，将VC维这一学习理论/组合几何概念引入该框架，可揭示距离结构的复杂度并给出量化下界。

Method: 对$3\leq k\leq d$，考察$E\subset \mathbb{F}_q^d$的距离图；在$|E|>Cq^{d-\lfloor (d-k)/3\rfloor-1}$的条件下，通过组合/多项式方法证明该距离图能打散(k个点的)集合，从而VC维至少为k。

Result: 定理：若$|E|>Cq^{d-\lfloor (d-k)/3\rfloor-1}$，则$E$的距离图VC维$\geq k$；并且在$k=d$时，该指数$d-\lfloor (d-d)/3\rfloor-1=d-1$是最优（sharp）的。

Conclusion: 有限域距离图在大子集情形下具有与k相关的VC维下界，且在$k=d$时该界的规模不可改进，说明所给阈值与指数在相应情形下是紧的。

Abstract: We study the VC-dimension of distance graphs of large subsets of vector spaces over finite fields. For $3\leq k\leq d$, we prove the distance graph of $E\subset \F_q^d$ has VC-dimension at least $k$ provided that $|E|>Cq^{d-\lfloor \frac{d-k}{3}\rfloor-1}$. We also show that this exponent is sharp in the $k=d$ case.

</details>


### [137] [Strongly regular graph, strongly polynomial sequences, and two-level polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32077)
*Tristram Bogart,Santiago Jiménez-Salazar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究图的特征多项式与色多项式（及其系数在同余度处的多项式性）与同态计数；刻画强正则图、Paley图、强多项式图序列的"深度"，并给出同态计数在两强多项式序列间保持/不保持无限深度的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心色多项式系数（按同余度"codegree"固定时）是否随图族参数"最终为多项式"，并把这一性质抽象为Bogart–Woods意义下的"深度（depth）"。目标是借助同态计数这一组合工具，厘清特征多项式与色多项式在若干经典图族中的多项式深度差异，并在de La Harpe–Jaeger的强多项式图序列框架下，统一处理这些性质及其在同态计数间的传递性。

Method: 采用多项式不变量（特征多项式、色多项式）的系数按codegree分解与"深度/无限深度、常数深度"的判定；结合强正则图与Paley图的参数化结构；引入de La Harpe–Jaeger强多项式图序列概念；并用图同态计数（homomorphism counts）及其多项式性来分析源序列与目标序列之间的深度传递。

Result: 1) 参数为多项式的强正则图，其特征多项式构成Bogart–Woods意义下的二级无限深度多项式；2) Paley图的色多项式具有最大常数深度2——codegree 3的系数不是顶点数的最终多项式；3) de La Harpe–Jaeger意义下任一强多项式图序列，其色多项式和特征多项式均具有无限深度；4) 两个强多项式图序列之间的同态计数一般不具无限深度，但可构造目标序列，使得对任意强多项式源序列该无限深度性质成立。

Conclusion: 特征多项式与色多项式在多项式深度上表现不同：强正则图特征多项式可为无限深度，而Paley图色多项式仅具最大常数深度2；对强多项式图序列，两种多项式均具无限深度；同态计数的无限深度不是普遍性质，但可通过选择适当的目标序列对任意强多项式源序列实现。

Abstract: We study the behavior of the characteristic and chromatic polynomials of certain families of graphs and their relation to homomorphism counts, focusing on polynomial dependence of the coefficients at each fixed codegree on the graph family parameter. We prove that the characteristic polynomials of strongly regular graphs with polynomial parameters form a two-level polynomial of infinite depth in the sense of Bogart and Woods. In contrast, the chromatic polynomials of Paley graphs have maximal constant depth two: their coefficient of codegree three is not eventually polynomial in the number of vertices. We also prove that both the chromatic and characteristic polynomials of every strongly polynomial graph sequence, in the sense of de La Harpe and Jaeger, have infinite depth. Finally, we investigate homomorphism counts between two strongly polynomial graph sequences. We show that infinite depth need not hold in general, but we construct target sequences for which it holds for every strongly polynomial source sequence.

</details>


### [138] [The Tight Upper Bound on the Number of Distinct Squares in Circular Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32084)
*Rikuya Hamai*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明长度为 n 的圆周字中长度不超过 n 的不同平方数最多为 ⌊3n/2⌋，且系数 3/2 与已知下界吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方（形如 xx 的词）在词组合学中具有核心地位，但圆周字中平方的数量上界此前并未精确确定。本文旨在给出圆周字中不同平方数的紧确上界，并验证该上界与已有下界一致，从而确定其精确渐近系数。

Method: 将平方与 Rauzy 图中的回路建立联系，利用已有的相关结果，对长度为 n 的圆周字中长度不超过 n 的不同平方进行计数与分析。

Result: 证明了长度为 n 的圆周字含有不超过 ⌊3n/2⌋ 个长度至多为 n 的不同平方。

Conclusion: 所得上界的系数 3/2 与已知下界相符，因此该结果在渐近意义下是紧确的。

Abstract: A square is a word $xx$, where $x$ is nonempty. We show that a circular word of length $n$ contains at most $\lfloor 3n/2 \rfloor$ distinct squares of length at most $n$. The proof combines known results relating squares to circuits in Rauzy graphs. The coefficient $3/2$ agrees with the known lower bound.

</details>


### [139] [Anti-Ramsey Number of Intersecting Odd Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32151)
*Haojie Zheng*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了由若干三角形和奇数圈共享一个公共顶点组成的图 $H_{s,t}$（$t\ge1$）的反拉姆齐数 $\operatorname{ar}(n,H_{s,t})$ 在所有充分大 $n$ 下的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liu 等（2024）仅解决了 $t=0$（即全为三角形）且 $s\ge3$、$n\ge50s^2$ 的情形，$t\ge1$（含奇数圈）的情形尚未确定，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究完全图 $K_n$ 的边着色，考察不含彩虹 $H_{s,t}$ 的最大颜色数，通过极值构造与上界估计确定反拉姆齐数的精确值。

Result: 对每个固定的 $H_{s,t}$（$s,t$ 非负且 $s+t\ge2$，$t\ge1$）及所有充分大的 $n$，给出了 $\operatorname{ar}(n,H_{s,t})$ 的精确值。

Conclusion: 本文将 Liu 等关于纯三角形情形（$t=0$）的结果推广到含奇数圈的一般情形，完整刻画了该族图的反拉姆齐数。

Abstract: For a graph $H$, the anti-Ramsey number $\operatorname{ar}(n,H)$ is the maximum number of colors in an edge-coloring of $K_n$ containing no rainbow copy of $H$, where a copy is rainbow if its edges have pairwise distinct colors. Let $s,t$ be nonnegative integers with $s+t\ge2$, and let $H_{s,t}$ be a graph consisting of $s$ triangles and $t$ odd cycles of fixed lengths at least $5$, all sharing exactly one common vertex and otherwise pairwise vertex-disjoint. Liu et al. (2024) determined $\operatorname{ar}(n,H_{s,0})$ for $s\ge3$ and $n\ge50s^2$. In this paper, we determine the exact value of $\operatorname{ar}(n,H_{s,t})$ for every fixed $H_{s,t}$ with $t\ge1$ and all sufficiently large $n$.

</details>


### [140] [Weak $k$-metric dimension of Hamming graphs: rectangular products and near-maximum parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32161)
*Aryan Kumar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了几类Hamming图的弱$k$-度量维数，证明了$K_n\square K_m$上的一个猜想，给出了$K_n\square K_m$、超立方体$Q_d$以及$K_q^{\square d}$的弱$k$-度量维数的精确结果与稳定公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱$k$-度量维数是度量维数研究的推广，对Hamming图这类重要图族尚缺乏系统的精确刻画。本文旨在确定多类Hamming图的弱$k$-度量维数，验证并推广已有猜想，揭示参数随图的规模变化的规律。

Method: 对矩形乘积$K_n\square K_m$采用循环构造证明猜想，并刻画$\operatorname{wdim}_2$的精确条件；对超立方体$Q_d$利用相邻参数$2s-1$与$2s$的弱分辨集一致性，将近最大参数情形归约为受限距离二元码，并证明稳定公式及其阈值紧性；对一般$K_q^{\square d}$在充分大维数下给出显式条件确定$\operatorname{wdim}_{2q^{d-1}-t}$。

Result: 对$n\ge3$、$m>n$、$3\le k\le2n$，用循环构造证明了Fernández等（2026）关于$K_n\square K_m$的猜想6.1；证明了当且仅当$m\ge2n-2$时$\operatorname{wdim}_2(K_n\square K_m)=m$。对$d\ge2$的超立方体，相邻参数$2s-1$与$2s$具有相同弱分辨集；通过受限距离二元码确定了所有可行亏损$0\le t\le15$下的$\operatorname{wdim}_{2^d-t}(Q_d)$；对$L\ge1$、$t\in\{2L,2L+1\}$证明了$d\ge2^L+1$时的稳定公式$\operatorname{wdim}_{2^d-t}(Q_d)=2^d-L$且阈值紧。对固定$q\ge3$与亏损$t$，在充分大的所有维数下确定了$\operatorname{wdim}_{2q^{d-1}-t}(K_q^{\square d})$并给出显式充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了矩形乘积、超立方体及一般Hamming图在近最大参数处的弱$k$-度量维数，证实了相关猜想，给出了稳定公式、尖锐阈值与显式充分条件，建立了与受限距离二元码的联系，为Hamming图弱$k$-度量维数的研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We determine weak $k$-metric dimensions for several families of Hamming graphs. For $n\ge3$, $m>n$, and $3\le k\le2n$, a cyclic construction proves Conjecture 6.1 of Fernández, Klavžar, Kuziak, Muñoz-Márquez and Yero (2026) on $K_n\square K_m$. For these rectangular products, we also prove that $\operatorname{wdim}_2(K_n\square K_m)=m$ exactly when $m\ge2n-2$. For hypercubes $Q_d$ with $d\ge2$, we show that consecutive parameters $2s-1$ and $2s$ have identical weak resolving sets. Near the maximum parameter, a reduction to restricted-distance binary codes determines $\operatorname{wdim}_{2^d-t}(Q_d)$ for every feasible deficit $0\le t\le15$. For $L\ge1$ and $t\in\{2L,2L+1\}$, we prove the stabilization formula $\operatorname{wdim}_{2^d-t}(Q_d)=2^d-L$ for $d\ge2^L+1$, and show that this threshold is sharp. For each fixed $q\ge3$ and deficit $t$, we also determine $\operatorname{wdim}_{2q^{d-1}-t}(K_q^{\square d})$ in all sufficiently large dimensions, with an explicit sufficient condition.

</details>


### [141] [A quadratic upper bound on the Chvátal rank of polytopes in the 0/1-cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32210)
*Alberto Del Pia*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明 $[0,1]^n$ 中任意多面体的 Chvátal 秩上界为 $12.22n^2+n\log_2 n+2n+4$，结合已有的 $\Omega(n^2)$ 下界，确定最大 Chvátal 秩为 $\Theta(n^2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Eisenbrand 与 Schulz 给出 $O(n^2\log n)$ 的 Chvátal 秩上界，而 Rothvoß 与 Sanità 证明了 $\Omega(n^2)$ 下界，二者之间存在 $\log n$ 的间隙。本文旨在缩小这一间隙，精确刻画 $[0,1]^n$ 中多面体 Chvátal 秩的渐近阶。

Method: 沿用 Eisenbrand–Schulz 沿由粗到细的整数向量链推导割平面的思路，但不再每步减半向量，而是在每个二进窗口 $[2^{-t-1},2^{-t}]$ 中自由选取尺度 $\tau$，对 $\tau c$ 进行舍入。核心新工具是多尺度版本的 Dirichlet 逼近定理，通过初等体积论证证明：对任意 $c\in\mathbb{R}^n$，$\tau c$ 到 $\mathbb{Z}^n$ 的 $\ell_1$ 距离在各二进窗口取最小后求和，总和小于 $1.222n^2$，且与 $\|c\|_\infty$ 无关。

Result: 任意 $P\subseteq[0,1]^n$ 及其更一般的紧凸集，Chvátal 秩至多为 $12.22n^2+n\log_2 n+2n+4$。若 $P$ 含整数点，则对任意 $c\in\mathbb{Z}^n\setminus\{0\}$，不等式 $cx\le\max\{cy:y\in P\cap\mathbb{Z}^n\}$ 对 $P$ 的第 $k$ 次 Chvátal 闭包有效，其中 $k\le 12.22n^2+2n+2\log_2\|c\|_\infty+4$。结合 $\Omega(n^2)$ 下界，最大 Chvátal 秩为 $\Theta(n^2)$。

Conclusion: 本文确定了 $[0,1]^n$ 中多面体最大 Chvátal 秩的渐近阶 $\Theta(n^2)$，解决了该问题上界与下界之间的 $\log n$ 间隙；多尺度 Dirichlet 逼近定理是关键技术贡献。

Abstract: We show that every polytope $P\subseteq[0,1]^n$, and more generally every compact convex set, has Chvátal rank at most $12.22n^2+n\log_2 n+2n+4$. This improves the $O(n^2\log n)$ bound of Eisenbrand and Schulz and, together with the $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound of Rothvoß and Sanità, shows that the maximum Chvátal rank of a polytope in $[0,1]^n$ is $Θ(n^2)$. More precisely, if $P$ contains an integer point, then for every $c\in\mathbb{Z}^n\setminus\{0\}$ the inequality $cx\le\max\{cy: y\in P\cap\mathbb{Z}^n\}$ is valid for the $k$-th Chvátal closure of $P$ for some $k\le 12.22n^2+2n+2\log_2\|c\|_\infty+4$. Following Eisenbrand and Schulz, we derive this inequality along a chain of coarser and coarser integer vectors, but instead of halving the vector in each step, we round $τc$ for a scale $τ$ chosen freely in each dyadic window $[2^{-t-1},2^{-t}]$. The main new ingredient is a multiscale version of Dirichlet's approximation theorem, proved by an elementary volume argument: for every $c\in\mathbb{R}^n$, the $\ell_1$-distances of $τc$ to $\mathbb{Z}^n$, minimized within each dyadic window and summed over all windows, total less than $1.222n^2$, independently of $\|c\|_\infty$.

</details>


### [142] [Non-commutative frieze patterns over quaternion algebras and other normed division rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32257)
*Michael Cuntz,Thorsten Holm,Peter Jorgensen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究赋范除环上的非交换frieze，证明每个范数有限子集在任意高度上仅有有限多个（边界为1的）非交换frieze，并进一步刻画非分裂四元数代数Lipschitz子环上的frieze子环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非交换frieze由Berenstein与Retakh引入。frieze理论中的一个基本问题是：在给定子集上，对每个高度，非交换frieze（边界为1）是有限多还是无限多。本文旨在回答该问题，并将研究从Hamilton四元数推广到更一般的赋范除环与四元数代数。

Method: 在赋范除环（如Hamilton四元数及更一般的四元数代数）上定义并研究非交换frieze；利用一个关于quiddity元素范数上界的定理，对范数有限子集进行计数论证；并研究非分裂四元数代数$(a,b)_{\mathbb{Q}}$的Lipschitz子环上的非交换frieze，确定其frieze子环。

Result: 对赋范除环的任意范数有限子集，每个高度上仅有有限多个边界为1的非交换frieze；该结果特别适用于Hamilton四元数的Lipschitz四元数与Hurwitz四元数。对非分裂四元数代数$(a,b)_{\mathbb{Q}}$的Lipschitz子环，确定了所有$a,b<0$情形的frieze子环，并由此得出当$a\le -4$且$b\le -4$时所有这些非交换frieze均已被确定。

Conclusion: 赋范除环上的非交换frieze在范数有限子集上具有有限性（按高度），并通过对Lipschitz子环frieze子环的刻画，在$a\le -4,\ b\le -4$时完全确定了非分裂四元数代数$(a,b)_{\mathbb{Q}}$上的相关非交换frieze。

Abstract: Non-commutative friezes have been introduced by Berenstein and Retakh and studied further by the authors. In this paper we consider non-commutative friezes over normed division rings, like for instance Hamilton's quaternions or more general quaternion algebras. We address the fundamental question in the theory of friezes of whether over a certain subset there are finitely or infinitely many non-commutative friezes (with 1's on the boundary) for any height. As an application of a theorem bounding the norm of quiddity entries we deduce that for every norm-finite subset of a normed division ring there are only finitely many such non-commutative friezes for every height. In particular this result applies to the Lipschitz quaternions and the Hurwitz quaternions of Hamilton's quaternions. We then study more generally non-commutative friezes over Lipschitz subrings of non-split quaternion algebras $(a,b)_{\mathbb{Q}}$. We determine the frieze subrings for all $a,b<0$, and as a consequence we see that all such non-commutative friezes are known if $a\le -4$ and $b\le -4$.

</details>


### [143] [Optimally pseudorandom $K_4$-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32461)
*Jie Han,Ferdinand Ihringer,Hendrik Van Maldeghem*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了阶为 n、度为 d=Θ(n^{4/5}) 的最优伪随机 K4-自由图，匹配已知上界，并借此改进了 Ramsey 数 r(4,t) 的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Alon 于 1994 年解决 k=3 情形后，K_k-自由图的最优伪随机构造的下一开问题即 k=4。本文旨在给出 K4-自由图在度 d=Θ(n^{4/5}) 时的最优伪随机构造，填补这一空白。

Method: 在分裂 Cayley 六边形（split Cayley hexagon）中构造图，从而得到阶为 n、度 d=Θ(n^{4/5}) 的最优伪随机 K4-自由图。

Result: 构造出的图匹配已知上界，证明了此类最优伪随机 K4-自由图的存在性；并对 K4-自由伪随机图产生多方面推论；同时给出显式下界 r(4,t) ≥ t^{1.6̄ - o(1)}，优于此前 Kostochka、Pudlák 和 Rödl 的 r(4,t) ≥ t^{1.6-o(1)} 记录。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 K_k-自由图在 k=4 情形下的首个开问题，并改进了 r(4,t) 的显式下界记录。

Abstract: We show that optimally pseudorandom $K_4$-free graphs of order $n$ and degree $d = Θ(n^{4/5})$ exist by constructing a graph in the split Cayley hexagon, matching the known upper bound. This resolves the first open case for $K_k$-free graphs after $k=3$ for which Alon gave a tight construction in 1994.
  This has a variety of implications for $K_4$-free pseudorandom graphs. Furthermore, it implies an explicit lower bound on the Ramsey number of $r(4, t) \geq t^{1.\overline{6} - o(1)}$, improving the previous record by Kostochka, Pudlák, and Rödl of $r(4, t) \geq t^{1.6-o(1)}$.

</details>


### [144] [Semifields in prime dimensions and counterexamples to Kaplansky's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32651)
*Gábor P. Nagy,Yue Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文否定了Kaplansky关于五维有限域上除法代数的猜想：对任意 q=p^e≡1 (mod 3)、gcd(n,6)=1（n≥5），构造出 q^n 阶半域，并证明其不与有限域或Albert广义扭域同痕，从而给出无穷多个互不同痕的反例，并指出Menichetti 1996年的分类结论不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kaplansky于1975年猜想：充分大有限域上的五维除法代数必为域或扭域；Menichetti在1996年声称给出了素维数情形的分类。作者怀疑这些结论，尤其是Menichetti证明存在漏洞，因此需要构造新的有限半域族作为反例来检验并推翻这些断言。

Method: 对每个素数幂 q=p^e≡1 (mod 3) 与满足 gcd(n,6)=1 的 n≥5，显式构造阶为 q^n 的半域族；通过引入新的同痕不变量，并结合该构造的结构性质，判定所构造半域的同痕类数目（p≡1 (mod 3) 时为 φ(n)，p≡2 (mod 3) 时为 φ(n)/2），进而证明其中任一成员都不与有限域或Albert广义扭域同痕。

Result: 所构造的半域族在固定 q,n 时包含 φ(n) 或 φ(n)/2 个同痕类，且均不同痕于有限域和Albert广义扭域。特别地，n=5 时在任意大的有限域上得到无穷多个两两不同痕的反例，否定Kaplansky猜想；对每个素数维数 n≥5，在除特征3外的任意特征、任意大域上均有此类反例，与Menichetti 1996年所宣称的分类相矛盾。

Conclusion: Kaplansky猜想不成立，Menichetti 1996年的分类因证明有缺口而无效；有限半域在同痕分类下远比此前认为的丰富。

Abstract: In 1975, Kaplansky conjectured that every five-dimensional division algebra over a sufficiently large finite field is a field or a twisted field. We disprove this conjecture. For every prime power $q=p^e\equiv1\pmod3$ and every $n\ge5$ with $\gcd(n,6)=1$, we construct semifields of order $q^n$. For fixed $q,n$, the family represents $\varphi(n)$ isotopy classes if $p\equiv1\pmod3$, and $\varphi(n)/2$ if $p\equiv2\pmod3$, where $\varphi$ is Euler's totient function. Using new isotopy invariants and the structural properties of our construction, we prove that none of these semifields is isotopic to a finite field or an Albert's generalized twisted field. In particular, the five-dimensional specialization gives infinitely many pairwise nonisotopic counterexamples to Kaplansky's conjecture over arbitrarily large finite fields. More generally, for each prime dimension $n\ge5$, the examples occur over arbitrarily large fields in every characteristic other than three, contradicting the classification asserted by Menichetti in 1996, whose proof contains gaps.

</details>


### [145] [The diameter of recoloring graphs under a maximum average degree bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32688)
*Ruilin Zheng,Junying Lu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏图的着色重配置图直径。对最大平均度 $\mathrm{mad}(G)\le d-\varepsilon$ 且 $k\ge d+1$ 的图 $G$，将 Feghali 的 $O_{d,k,\varepsilon}(n(\log n)^{d-1})$ 上界改进为 $O_{d,k,\varepsilon}(n(\log n)^{\lfloor(d-1)/(k-d)\rfloor})$，并给出森林情形下最优线性系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feghali (JCTB 2021) 已证明 $\mathrm{mad}(G)\le d-\varepsilon$ 时着色重配置图直径上界为 $O_{d,k,\varepsilon}(n(\log n)^{d-1})$，其中指数 $d-1$ 与颜色数 $k$ 无关。本文旨在利用颜色数 $k$ 的相对充裕性（$k>d$）来实质性地降低该指数，得到更紧的界。

Method: 证明采用独立层（independent layers）分解：将图划分为若干独立层，并在每一递归阶段移除 $k-d$ 种颜色。通过这种递归移除颜色的策略减少层数，从而收紧对数项的指数。此外进一步改进了层数上界，并在森林情形下（$\mathrm{mad}(G)\le 2-\varepsilon$）通过枚举小树的最大直径 $D_m$ 确定最优线性系数。

Result: 主要定理：对固定整数 $d,k\ge1$、$k\ge d+1$ 及任意 $\varepsilon>0$，每个满足 $\mathrm{mad}(G)\le d-\varepsilon$ 的 $n$ 顶点图有 $\mathrm{diam} R_k(G)=O_{d,k,\varepsilon}(n(\log n)^{\lfloor(d-1)/(k-d)\rfloor})$。森林情形：对 $0<\varepsilon<2$，每个 $\mathrm{mad}(G)\le2-\varepsilon$ 的 $n$ 顶点图满足 $\mathrm{diam} R_3(G)\le\rho_M n$，其中 $M=\lfloor2/\varepsilon\rfloor$，$\rho_M=\max_{1\le m\le M}D_m/m$，$D_m$ 为 $m$ 顶点树上 $R_3(T)$ 的最大直径，且该系数 $\rho_M$ 是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文直接将 Feghali 的结果推广并改进：用 $k-d$ 种颜色逐层递归移除的独立层分解，将直径上界指数由 $d-1$ 降至 $\lfloor(d-1)/(k-d)\rfloor$；同时改进了层数上界，并在森林情形确定了最优可能的线性系数 $\rho_M$，给出了 $R_3(G)$ 直径在稀疏图上的精确线性刻画。

Abstract: For a graph $G$, we write $\mathrm{mad}(G)$ for its maximum average degree and $\mathrm{diam} G$ for its diameter. Let $R_k(G)$ be the graph whose vertices are the proper colorings of $G$ with $k$ colors, where two colorings are adjacent when they differ at one vertex. Feghali (JCTB, 2021) proved that, for fixed integers $d,k\ge 1$ with $k\ge d+1$ and every $\varepsilon>0$, every $n$-vertex graph $G$ satisfying $\mathrm{mad}(G)\le d-\varepsilon$ has $\mathrm{diam} R_k(G)=O_{d,k,\varepsilon}(n(\log n)^{d-1})$. In this article, we prove that \[
  \mathrm{diam} R_k(G)=O_{d,k,\varepsilon}\!\left(
  n(\log n)^{\left\lfloor (d-1)/(k-d)\right\rfloor}
  \right), \] which extends the result proved by Feghali directly. The proof uses a partition into independent layers and removes $k-d$ colors at each recursive stage. We also improve the bound on the number of layers and determine the best possible linear coefficient in the forest case. More precisely, for $0<\varepsilon<2$, every $n$-vertex graph $G$ with $\mathrm{mad}(G)\le2-\varepsilon$ satisfies $\mathrm{diam} R_3(G)\leρ_M n$, where $M=\lfloor2/\varepsilon\rfloor$, $ρ_M=\max_{1\le m\le M}D_m/m$, and $D_m$ is the largest diameter of $R_3(T)$ over all trees $T$ on $m$ vertices. Moreover, the coefficient $ρ_M$ is best possible.

</details>


### [146] [Completing shellings with $d-2$ extra vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32721)
*SuHo Oh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: Simon (1994) 猜想:任意 n 顶点的纯 d 维可贝壳复形可逐面扩展为 n 顶点单纯形的 d-骨架并保持可贝壳性。Bolognini 与 Sentinelli (2026) 对 d≥3 否证了该猜想。本文证明:若允许新增 d-2 个顶点,猜想成立;以 k-可分解性替代可贝壳性时同样成立 (k≥1);且 d-2 不可减少——膨胀 Bolognini–Sentinelli 反例可得 d≥3 的 1-可分解复形,仅新增 d-3 个顶点时即使只要求保持可贝壳性也无法扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原猜想被反例否定,作者希望给出其"修正版本":放宽顶点数限制后猜想是否恢复成立,并确定所需新增顶点的最小数目;同时探究该现象是否超越可贝壳性、推广到 k-可分解性。

Method: 证明新增 d-2 个顶点时扩展可行(构造性/归纳式论证);将该结果推广到 k-可分解复形;通过膨胀 Bolognini–Sentinelli 的反例构造下界反例,证明 d-2 的最优性。

Result: 允许 d-2 个新顶点时 Simon 猜想成立,且对任意 k≥1 的 k-可分解性同样成立;d-2 为最优,不可降至 d-3——存在 1-可分解复形在仅新增 d-3 个顶点时即使只保持可贝壳性亦无法扩展。

Conclusion: Simon 猜想在新增 d-2 个顶点后成立,所需顶点数不可再减,从而给出该猜想的紧致修正形式并推广至 k-可分解性。

Abstract: Simon's conjecture (1994) asserts that any pure $d$-dimensional shellable complex on $n$ vertices can be extended to the $d$-skeleton of the simplex on $n$ vertices, one facet at a time, while maintaining shellability. Bolognini and Sentinelli (2026) recently disproved it for every $d \geq 3$. We show that the conjecture becomes true once $d-2$ new vertices are allowed, and that the same holds with $k$-decomposability in place of shellability for any $k \geq 1$. We also show that the number $d-2$ cannot be lowered. Inflating the counterexample of Bolognini and Sentinelli gives, for every $d \geq 3$, a $1$-decomposable complex that cannot be extended in this way with only $d-3$ new vertices, even while merely maintaining shellability.

</details>


### [147] [Network Topology That Excludes Braess's Paradox and Maintains Monotonicity in Flows Over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32738)
*Xujin Chen,Xiyuan Deng,Changjun Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究时间依赖流（flow over time）博弈中Vickrey瓶颈模型下的两个公开猜想：Braess悖论的免疫网络刻画，以及网络入流率与流完成时间（makespan）的单调性。作者提出"双极伪树链（chains of BPAs）"这一拓扑类，证明其恰好是无Braess悖论网络的充要条件，并对该类网络验证了单调性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖流博弈中，边上的FIFO排队刻画了拥堵效应。此前Macko等（2013）研究了Braess悖论，但留下了一类未解决的网络拓扑；Correa等（2021）提出了入流率与makespan的单调性猜想，当时仅对平行路径链在均匀入流下得证。本文旨在完整刻画Braess悖论免疫网络，并推进单调性猜想的解决。

Method: 作者定义了一类称为"双极伪树链（chains of bipolar pseudo-arborescences, BPAs）"的网络拓扑，该拓扑恰为Macko等（2013）研究中留待解决的网络类。通过对这类网络上时间依赖Nash流（Nash flows over time）的动态演化进行结构刻画，分析删边对最大均衡延迟的影响，并据此验证单调性。

Result: 证明在chains of BPAs上删除边从不会降低最大均衡延迟；结合Macko等人的结果，得到充要条件：网络对时间依赖流不产生Braess悖论当且仅当它是chains of BPAs。同时，对所有chains of BPAs确认了Correa等（2021）的单调性猜想，严格推广了此前仅对均匀入流下平行路径链已知的单调性结论。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Macko等（2013）关于Braess悖论免疫网络的猜想，并对Correa等（2021）的单调性猜想取得部分进展——在chains of BPAs上完全成立。结果严格推广了既有的单调性结论。

Abstract: In the game of \emph{flow over time}, infinitesimal flow particles aim to travel from a source to a sink in a network as quickly as possible. Under the Vickrey bottleneck model, the congestion effects on network edges are captured through FIFO queues, which arise when the inflow into an edge exceeds its capacity. This work addresses two open conjectures about the game: 1) the characterization of networks that are immune to Braess's paradox, and 2) the monotonicity relationship between the network inflow rate and the overall flow makespan. We show that a single class of network topologies, called \emph{chains of bipolar pseudo-arborescences} (\emph{BPAs}), fully resolves the first conjecture and yields partial progress on the second.
  Chains of BPAs constitute precisely the cases left open by Macko et al.~(2013) in their study of Braess's paradox for flow over time.
