By structurally characterizing the dynamic evolution of Nash flows over time on such networks, we prove that removing edges from these networks never decreases the maximum equilibrium latency. Combined with the results of Macko et al., this establishes a necessary and sufficient condition: \emph{a network does not admit Braess's paradox for flow over time if and only if it is a chain of BPAs}, thereby resolving their conjecture. Furthermore, we confirm the monotonicity conjecture of Correa et al.~(2021) for all chains of BPAs. This result strictly generalizes the previously known monotonicity for chains of parallel paths under uniform inflows.

</details>


### [148] [The automorphism group of the derangement graph of $\operatorname{PGL}_{2}(q)$ acting on the projective line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32969)
*Andriaherimanana Sarobidy Razafimahatratra*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了射影直线 $\operatorname{PG}_1(q)$ 上自然作用的 $\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$ 所对应的错排图 $\Gamma_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}$ 的自同构群，其等于 $\left(L_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}\times R_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}\right) \rtimes \left(\langle \psi\rangle \times \gamma_{\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)}\right)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错排图（derangement graph）是代数图论与置换群理论交叉的重要研究对象，其自同构群的刻画有助于理解图结构与群结构之间的联系。对于 $\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$ 在射影直线上的自然作用这一经典传递置换群，其错排图自同构群的精确描述此前尚需完善，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设 $G\leq \operatorname{Sym}(\Omega)$ 为有限传递群，错排图 $\Gamma_G$ 以 $G$ 为顶点集，$g$ 与 $h$ 相邻当且仅当 $h^{-1}g$ 是无固定点置换。本文针对 $G=\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$ 在 $\operatorname{PG}_1(q)$ 上的自然作用，通过分析其错排图的结构与自同构，确定了完整的自同构群。

Result: $\operatorname{Aut}(\Gamma_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}) = \left(L_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}\times R_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}\right) \rtimes \left(\langle \psi\rangle \times \gamma_{\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)}\right)$，其中 $L$、$R$ 分别为左、右正则表示，$\gamma_{\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)}$ 为 $\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)$ 元素共轭作用生成的群，$\psi(x)=x^{-1}$。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了 $\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$ 在射影直线上自然作用所对应错排图的自同构群，其结果由左右正则表示、逆映射 $\psi$ 以及域自同构诱导的共轭群共同构成，为传递群错排图自同构的进一步研究提供了具体范例。

Abstract: Given a finite transitive group $G\leq \operatorname{Sym}(Ω)$, the derangement graph $Γ_G$ is the graph whose vertex set is $G$, and two vertices $g$ and $h$ are adjacent if the ratio $h^{-1}g$ is a fixed-point-free permutation. In this paper, we show that the automorphism group of the derangement graph of the transitive permutation group corresponding to the natural action of $\operatorname{PGL}_{2}(q)$ on the projective line $\operatorname{PG}_{1}(q)$ is \begin{align*}
  \operatorname{Aut}(Γ_{\operatorname{PGL}_{2}({q})}) = \left(L_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}\times R_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}\right) \rtimes \left(\langle ψ\rangle \times γ_{\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)}\right), \end{align*} where $L_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}$ is the left-regular representation of $\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$, $R_{\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)}$ is the right-regular representation of $\operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$, $γ_{\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)}$ is the group of conjugation by elements of $\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{F}_q)$, and $ψ: \operatorname{PGL}_2(q) \to \operatorname{PGL}_2(q)$ such that $ψ(x) = x^{-1}$.

</details>


### [149] [Staircase diagrams, pattern avoidance, and smooth permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33015)
*Faqruddin Ali Azam,Edward Richmond*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究楼梯图（staircase diagrams）的偏序结构与光滑置换（smooth permutations）的模式避免之间的联系，并据此枚举若干由模式避免刻画的、光滑置换的子类（包括 polished permutations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lakshmibai-Sandhya 定理刻画了 A 型光滑 Schubert 簇；后续工作表明单链 Dynkin 图上的楼梯图与对应类型的光滑 Schubert 簇一一对应。作者希望揭示楼梯图的偏序结构如何与光滑置换上的模式避免相联系，从而借助该组合结构刻画并枚举光滑置换的子类。

Method: 通过建立楼梯图的偏序结构与置换上的模式避免之间的对应关系，利用该组合框架分析并刻画特定的光滑置换子类。

Result: 建立了楼梯图偏序结构与光滑置换上模式避免之间的联系，并据此枚举了若干由模式避免刻画的光滑置换子类，其中包括 Gaetz 和 Gao 研究过的 polished permutations 类。

Conclusion: 楼梯图的偏序结构为理解和枚举光滑置换中的模式避免类提供了有效工具，推广了对光滑 Schubert 簇的刻画。

Abstract: A well known result due to Lakshmibai and Sandhya states that smooth Schubert varieties of type A correspond to permutations that avoid the patterns 3412 and 4231. It was later shown by the second author and Slofstra that staircase diagrams over a simply-laced Dynkin diagram are in bijection with smooth Schubert varieties of the corresponding type. We explore how the poset structure of staircase diagrams is connected with pattern avoidance on smooth permutations. As an application, we enumerate several subclasses of smooth permutations which are characterized by pattern avoidance. These subclasses include the class of polished permutations which were studied by Gaetz and Gao.

</details>


### [150] [There are no nontrivial uniquely C_9-saturated graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33035)
*Fayzan Khan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 t=9 时的不存在性猜想：不存在非平凡的 uniquely C_9-saturated 图，从而在 t≥9 的方向上取得首个突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wenger 与 West 证明了 t=6、7 时不存在非平凡 uniquely C_t-saturated 图，并猜想对一切 t≥6 均成立；t=8 已被验证但未发表，t≥9 长期悬而未决。

Method: 对最长偶环长度 L∈{4,6,8,10,12} 分情形讨论：L=12、10、4 手工解决；L=6、8 通过对环外分量的手工分类，将问题归约为有限构型族，再用可复现的计算机枚举排除（含已知答案对照与零 cap hit 的完备性认证）。同时给出 Wenger–West 某结构引理在 t=9 时的加强形式。

Result: 证明 t=9 时猜想成立：不存在非平凡 uniquely C_9-saturated 图。

Conclusion: t=9 情形得证，推进了对 Wenger–West 猜想（t≥6 时无非平凡 uniquely C_t-saturated 图）的理解，并补充了相关结构引理的证明。

Abstract: A graph G is uniquely C_t-saturated if G contains no cycle of length t and, for every edge e of the complement, G+e contains exactly one cycle of length t; it is nontrivial if it has at least t vertices. Wenger and West proved that no nontrivial uniquely C_6- or C_7-saturated graphs exist, and conjectured the same for every t >= 6; the case t=8 was verified but never published, and t >= 9 has remained open. We prove the conjecture for t=9: there is no nontrivial uniquely C_9-saturated graph. The proof is a case analysis on the length L of a longest even cycle of length at most 12 (L is 4, 6, 8, 10, or 12). The cases L=12, L=10, and L=4 are settled by hand; for L=6 and L=8, hand classifications of the components outside the cycle reduce each case to a bounded finite family of configurations, eliminated by a short, replayable computer enumeration with known-answer controls and completeness certified by zero cap hits. Along the way we prove, in sharpened form, the t=9 instance of a structural lemma Wenger and West stated without proof.

</details>


### [151] [Permutation automorphism groups of cyclic codes I: cyclotomic association schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33050)
*Yanni Wu,Ziqing Xiang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将循环码的自同构群理论与分圆结合方案（等价于循环群上的Schur环）理论建立联系，证明在特定码长下$q$-仿射群并非正确的预期自同构群，并用算术条件刻画这类码长，证明其密度为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berger-Charpin猜想断言循环码的置换自同构群通常为$q$-仿射群，但文献中"通常（generally）"一词并未被精确化。本文作为系列论文的第一部分，旨在明确研究Berger-Charpin猜想及其变体何时成立。

Method: 建立循环码理论与分圆结合方案（等价地，循环群上的Schur环）之间的自然联系，并借助该联系分析$q$-仿射群作为置换自同构群的适用性。

Result: 证明在某些码长下，$q$-仿射群并非正确的预期置换自同构群；用算术条件刻画了这些码长，并证明其密度为零。

Conclusion: Berger-Charpin猜想的表述需要修正：$q$-仿射群仅在除一组密度为零的码长之外才普遍适用，而这一例外码长集合可由明确的算术条件刻画。

Abstract: The Berger-Charpin conjecture predicts that the permutation automorphism group of a cyclic code is generally the $q$-affine group, which is generated by the shift and the Frobenius multiplier on the index set. The word {\em generally} is not made precise in the literature, and the goal of this series of papers is to study when the Berger-Charpin conjecture or its variants hold.
  In this first part, we establish a natural connection between the theory of cyclic codes and the theory of cyclotomic association schemes (equivalently, Schur rings over cyclic groups). Using it, we show that the $q$-affine group is not the correct group to expect for certain code lengths. We characterize these lengths by an arithmetic condition, and show that they have density zero.

</details>


### [152] [Arithmetic Progressions in a Random Binary Subset-Sum Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33067)
*Norbert Hegyvári,Thang Pham,Boqing Xue*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 论文研究二进制序列生成的递推集合 $A_u$ 中算术级数在 $[1,N]$ 内的最大长度 $L_u(N)$，给出对所有二进制序列的下界、随机序列的几乎必然上界以及最终周期序列的渐近下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是刻画由二进制序列通过仿射递推 $X_{n+1}=2X_n+u_n$ 生成的有限子集和集合 $A_u$ 的加法结构，特别是其中算术级数的最大长度随 $N$ 的增长规律，并揭示一般序列、随机序列与最终周期序列之间的差异。

Method: 定义 $X_0=1$、$X_{n+1}=2X_n+u_n$ 与 $A_u$ 为 $(X_n)$ 的非空有限子集和集合，令 $L_u(N)$ 为 $A_u\cap[1,N]$ 中算术级数的最大长度；随后分别采用组合/解析方法证明统一下界，用概率方法处理独立均匀二进制序列的几乎必然上界，并对最终周期序列作特殊结构分析。

Result: 存在绝对常数 $c>0$ 与 $N_0\ge1$，使得任意二进制序列 $u$ 满足 $L_u(N)\ge \exp(c\sqrt{\log N/\log\log N})$；当 $u_n$ 独立且均匀分布于 $\{0,1\}$ 时，几乎必然 $L_u(N)\ll_u N^{2/3}\exp(C\sqrt{\log N\log\log N})$；每个最终周期二进制序列满足 $L_u(N)\gg_u N^{1/2}$。

Conclusion: 该文建立了 $A_u\cap[1,N]$ 中算术级数最大长度的多尺度界：一般序列至少达到次多项式型的指数下界，随机序列几乎必然具有接近 $N^{2/3}$ 的上界，而最终周期序列则具有 $N^{1/2}$ 阶下界，刻画了二进制序列结构对子集和集合算术级数长度的影响。

Abstract: Let $u=(u_n)_{n\ge0}$ be a binary sequence, and define \[ X_0=1,\qquad X_{n+1}=2X_n+u_n \qquad(n\ge0). \] Let $A_u$ be the set of all nonempty finite subset sums of the sequence $(X_n)$, and let $L_u(N)$ denote the maximum length of an arithmetic progression contained in $A_u\cap[1,N]$. We prove that there are absolute constants $c>0$ and $N_0\geq 1$ such that, for every binary sequence $u$, \[ L_u(N)\ge \exp\!\left(c\sqrt{\frac{\log N}{\log\log N}}\right) \] for all $N\geq N_0$. Moreover, if the random variables $u_n$ are independent and uniformly distributed on $\{0,1\}$, then, almost surely, \[ L_u(N)\ll_u N^{2/3}\exp\!\left(C\sqrt{\log N\log\log N}\right), \] where $C>0$ is an absolute constant. Furthermore, every eventually periodic binary sequence satisfies $L_u(N)\gg_u N^{1/2}$.

</details>


### [153] [Association schemes from vectorial generalized Maiorana-McFarland bent functions with non-weakly regular components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33107)
*Rumi Melih Pelen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb{F}_r$ 值广义 Maiorana-McFarland 函数 $H(x,y,z)=P^{(z)}(x)+\operatorname{Tr}_s^k(yz)$，证明其水平集划分在温和条件下是 Fourier-自反的，从而诱导出具有 $\frac{q-1}{r-1}(r+1)+r$ 类的平移结合方案，并构造融合得到 $(2r+1)$ 与 $(3r+2)$ 类方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结合方案多由标量 bent 函数或弱正则分量的向量ial bent 函数构造；本文旨在突破弱正则性限制，利用向量ial 对偶-bent 函数（其分量可全部非弱正则）构造新的平移结合方案，并统一与推广近期标量构造。

Method: 取奇素数 $p$、$q=p^k$、$r=p^s$（$s\mid k$），构造 $\mathbb{F}_r$ 值函数 $H(x,y,z)=P^{(z)}(x)+\operatorname{Tr}_s^k(yz)$。要求 $P^{(z)}$ 为向量ial 对偶-bent 且分量弱正则，在 $\mathbb{F}_q^*$ 关于 $\mathbb{F}_r^*$ 的陪集上为常值，且 $z\ne 0$ 时关于 $t\mapsto t^{-1}$ 为反演型；对 $P^{(0)}$ 加温和条件。通过分析 $H$ 的 $\mathbb{F}_r$ 值水平集并按相关坐标的 $\mathbb{F}_r$-线细化，证明该划分 Fourier-自反，进而用 Fourier-自反划分构造平移结合方案；再沿 $\mathbb{F}_r$-子空间构造融合。

Result: 在温和条件下，该细化划分是 Fourier-自反的，诱导出平移结合方案，类数为 $\frac{q-1}{r-1}(r+1)+r$（退化情形少一类），当各原料为偶函数时方案对称。$s=1$ 时恢复近期标量构造；$s=k$ 时得到 $(2q+1)$ 类方案，其来源向量ial bent 函数的分量可全部非弱正则。沿 $\mathbb{F}_r$-子空间融合得到 $(2r+1)$ 类与 $(3r+2)$ 类方案，并建立不同层级间的联系。反例表明反演性、陪集常值性、子空间条件与弱正则性假设一般不可省略。

Conclusion: 本文给出了由向量ial bent 函数（分量可全部非弱正则）构造平移结合方案的首批结果，推广并统一了标量情形，并通过融合获得低类数方案与层级联系；所依赖的反演、陪集常值、子空间与弱正则条件在一般情况下是必要的。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, $q=p^k$, and $r=p^s$ with $s\mid k$. We study $\mathbb{F}_r$-valued generalized Maiorana-McFarland functions $H(x,y,z)=P^{(z)}(x)+\operatorname{Tr}_s^k(yz)$ on $V_n\times\mathbb{F}_q\times\mathbb{F}_q$. The ingredients $P^{(z)}$ are vectorial dual-bent functions with weakly regular components, constant on the cosets of $\mathbb{F}_r^*$ in $\mathbb{F}_q^*$, and, for $z\ne 0$, of inversion type with respect to $t\mapsto t^{-1}$. Under a mild condition on $P^{(0)}$, we prove that the partition into the $\mathbb{F}_r$-valued level sets of $H$, refined according to the $\mathbb{F}_r$-lines of the relevant coordinates, is Fourier-reflexive. It therefore induces a translation association scheme with $\frac{q-1}{r-1}(r+1)+r$ classes (one fewer in a degenerate case), symmetric when the ingredients are even. For $s=1$ this recovers recent scalar constructions, while for $s=k$ it yields $(2q+1)$-class schemes from vectorial bent functions whose components may all be non-weakly regular. We also construct fusions along $\mathbb{F}_r$-subspaces, obtaining $(2r+1)$- and $(3r+2)$-class schemes and connections between different levels. Counterexamples show that the inversion, coset-constancy, subspace, and weak-regularity hypotheses cannot in general be omitted. To our knowledge, these are the first association schemes obtained from vectorial bent functions with non-weakly regular components.

</details>


### [154] [Vizing's theorem for signed multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33108)
*You Lu,Jingru Zhao,Li Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了无环有限带号多重图的染色指数满足 χ'(Σ) ≤ Δ(G)+μ(G)，该上界是紧的，统一推广了Vizing多重图定理与Behr带号简单图定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带号图的染色指数问题此前主要针对简单图（Behr定理）或普通多重图（Vizing定理）分别研究，缺少一个同时涵盖正负边且允许重边的统一上界。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立带号多重图的一般性Vizing型界。

Method: 针对任意无环有限带号多重图 Σ=(G,σ)，研究其染色指数 χ'(Σ)，结合最大度 Δ(G) 与最大重数 μ(G) 作为参数，通过结构性分析（如边着色、开关/换色等技巧）证明 χ'(Σ) ≤ Δ(G)+μ(G)。

Result: 证明了 χ'(Σ) ≤ Δ(G)+μ(G) 对所有无环有限带号多重图成立；该界是紧的，即使在正负边同时存在的情形下也无法改进。

Conclusion: 该结果将 Vizing 关于普通多重图的定理和 Behr 关于带号简单图的定理统一为一个更一般的定理，并给出紧界，为带号多重图边着色理论提供了基础性结论。

Abstract: We prove that every finite loopless signed multigraph $Σ=(G,σ)$ satisfies $χ'(Σ)\leqΔ(G)+μ(G)$, where $χ'(Σ)$ is its chromatic index, and $Δ(G)$ and $μ(G)$ are the maximum degree and maximum multiplicity of $G$, respectively. This bound is sharp, even when both positive and negative edges are present, and generalizes both Vizing's theorem for ordinary multigraphs and Behr's theorem for signed simple graphs.

</details>


### [155] [Proper circular arc graphs are $e$-positive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33124)
*Aarush Vailaya*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了正常圆弧图的色对称函数具有e-正展开式，从而解决了Ellzey猜想中q=1的情形，并借此给出单位区间图e-正性、Stanley–Stembridge猜想的间接新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ellzey猜想中q=1情形尚未解决；同时希望为已知的e-正性及Stanley–Stembridge猜想提供新证明路径。

Method: 定义颜色矩阵（计数正常染色）与 tableau 矩阵（元素为非负有理数及初等对称函数之比），证明二者通过一族换基矩阵相联系，限制到有限颜色数后可逆；并将正常圆弧图的色对称函数表示为这些矩阵的迹。

Result: 得到正常圆弧图色对称函数的e-正公式；结合Hikita tableaux，给出该色对称函数作为tableaux加权和（首尾各k个顶点位于同列）的显式公式。

Conclusion: 解决了Ellzey猜想q=1情形，并给出单位区间图e-正性及Stanley–Stembridge猜想的新证明。

Abstract: We prove an $e$-positive formula for the chromatic symmetric function of proper circular arc graphs solving the $q=1$ case of Ellzey's conjecture. In doing so, we provide a new proof of the $e$-positivity of unit interval graphs, which alongside Guay-Paquet's reduction gives a new proof of the Stanley--Stembridge conjecture. We define color matrices, which count proper colorings, and tableau matrices, whose entries are nonnegative rational numbers and ratios of elementary symmetric functions. We prove the two matrices are related by a single family of change of basis matrices, which become invertible after restricting to finitely many colors, and we show the chromatic symmetric function of proper circular arc graphs comes from taking the trace of these matrices. Using Hikita's tableaux, this gives an explicit formula for the chromatic symmetric function of a proper circular arc graph as a weighted sum over tableaux whose first and last $k$ vertices lie in the same columns.

</details>


### [156] [Optimal and minimal $p$-ary linear codes from generalized order ideals of hierarchical posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33128)
*Rumi Melih Pelen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将偏序集序理想编码推广到奇特征情形，引入带重数的广义序理想，并给出相关码的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作基于两级层次偏序集的序理想构造了最优、极小二元线性码；奇特征下仅有下集与支撑闭子集两种推广，尚无序理想本身的推广。

Method: 引入偏序集元素的{0,…,p-1}重数，构造阶为p-1的广义序理想，研究两级层次偏序集上的两类自然序理想定义，计算补码的权重分布，并利用广义序理想的示性函数构造码。

Result: 当理想触及上层时，定义集既非下集亦非支撑闭集；确定了补码的权重分布，给出上层元素具任意重数的一族Griesmer码，并得到长度p^n-1、维数n+1的无穷族极小p元码，违反Ashikhmin–Barg条件。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了奇特征下偏序集序理想编码推广的空白，为构造最优与极小p元码提供了新框架。

Abstract: Hyun, Kim, Wu and Yue constructed optimal and minimal binary linear codes from order ideals of hierarchical posets with two levels. Two different generalizations of the underlying antichain (simplicial complex) setting to odd characteristic are known: down-sets of $\mathbb{F}_p^n$ under the componentwise order, and support-closed subsets of $\mathbb{F}_q^m$. No generalization of the poset setting itself has appeared. We introduce generalized order ideals of a poset of order $p-1$, obtained by attaching multiplicities in ${0,\dots,p-1}$ to the elements of a poset, and study the two natural notions of order ideal that arise for hierarchical posets with two levels. Whenever the ideal meets the upper level, the resulting defining sets are neither down-sets nor support-closed. We determine the weight distributions of the associated complement codes, exhibit a family of Griesmer codes in which the upper element carries an arbitrary multiplicity, and, via the characteristic function of a generalized order ideal, obtain an infinite family of minimal $p$-ary codes of length $p^n-1$ and dimension $n+1$ violating the Ashikhmin--Barg condition.

</details>


### [157] [Maximal Lehmer Codes for Permutations with Classical and Consecutive 321-Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33137)
*Andrew Beveridge,Yufan Hu,Yucheng Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Lehmer码将321经典模式与321连续模式避让排列对应为加权偏序集，刻画其极大元数量与最大权元结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 321经典模式避让与321连续模式避让是两类重要的排列避让类，已有研究多关注计数，但两者在Lehmer码下的偏序结构与逆序数极值行为缺乏统一刻画，本文旨在建立加权偏序集框架并比较两类避让的极大元与最大权元。

Method: 定义S_n(321)与S_n(下划线321)两类避让排列集合，通过Lehmer码双射为码集；以码的逆序数为权重构造加权偏序集L_n(321)与L_n(下划线321)；分析其极大元的结构与计数，并确定最大权元素在n奇偶下的个数。

Result: L_n(321)的极大元个数为2^{n-2}；L_n(下划线321)的极大元由Padovan数枚举；当n为偶数时，两个偏序集各有唯一最大权元，当n为奇数时各有两个最大权元。

Conclusion: 最大权Lehmer码恰好对应各自避让类中逆序数最大的排列，从而以偏序与权重的视角统一揭示了经典模式321与连续模式321避让的极值结构。

Abstract: Let ${S}_n(321)$ and ${S}_n(\underline{321})$ denote the sets of $n$-permutations avoiding the classical pattern $321$ and the consecutive pattern $\underline{321}$, respectively. Permutations are in bijection with Lehmer codes, a type of inversion sequence. Using Lehmer codes, we create the corresponding weighted posets ${L}_n(321)$ and ${L}_n(\underline{321})$, where the weight of a code is the inversion number of its permutation. We show that there are $2^{n-2}$ maximal elements of ${L}_n(321)$, while the maximal elements of ${L}_n(\underline{321})$ are enumerated by the Padovan numbers. We also show that when $n$ is even, each of these posets has a unique maximum weight element, and that when $n$ is odd, there are two maximum weight elements. These maximum weight Lehmer codes correspond to the pattern avoiding permutations with maximum inversion number.

</details>


### [158] [A product inequality and its application to cross-intersecting families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33187)
*Toshihiro Shimizu,Norihide Tokushige*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于 $r$ 个 $(0,1)$ 区间实数的乘积不等式，并据此给出了 $r$-cross $t$-intersecting 族测度的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立一个新的乘积型不等式，作为工具来界定量测意义下 $r$-cross $t$-intersecting 族的测度，推进相交族（intersecting families）方向的经典问题。

Method: 对整数 $r\geq 3$ 及 $a_1,\ldots,a_r\in(0,1)$，令 $a=a_1\cdots a_r$，通过不等式证明技术比较 $\prod_{i=1}^r(a_i+\cdots+a_i^r)$ 与 $\prod_{i=1}^r(a_i+\cdots+a_i^{r-1}+a)$ 的大小，进而把该不等式应用于 $r$-cross $t$-intersecting 族的测度估计。

Result: 证明了 $\prod_{i=1}^r(a_i+a_i^2+\cdots+a_i^r)\geq \prod_{i=1}^r(a_i+a_i^2+\cdots+a_i^{r-1}+a)$，并利用该不等式得到了 $r$-cross $t$-intersecting 族测度的界。

Conclusion: 该乘积不等式为相交族测度问题提供了有效的分析工具，可用于推导 $r$-cross $t$-intersecting 族的测度上界。

Abstract: For an integer $r\geq 3$ and real numbers $a_1,\ldots,a_r\in(0,1)$, let $a=a_1\cdots a_r$. We show that \[ \prod_{i=1}^r(a_i+a_i^2+\cdots+a_i^r)\geq \prod_{i=1}^r(a_i+a_i^2+\cdots+a_i^{r-1}+a). \] This inequality enables us to bound the measure of $r$-cross $t$-intersecting families.

</details>


### [159] [A planar algebraic Zarankiewicz theorem over prime fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33327)
*Le Quang-Ham*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了由有界次多项式方程布尔组合定义的二部图关联上界，将Lewko的插值与接触重数方法从直线推广到代数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联几何中经典的Szemerédi–Trotter型上界主要针对直线或简单代数曲线。本文希望在有限域上，对由多项式方程布尔组合刻画的一般二部图（以及点与代数族分支的关联）建立统一的关联上界，并处理奇异、非约化成员等困难情形。

Method: 推广Lewko的插值与接触重数（contact-multiplicity）方法：由直线情形扩展到两参数多项式族与一般代数族，结合有界描述复杂度t的控制，处理特征p（p=0时取1/p=0）及奇异、非约化成员。

Result: 若图是K_{k,k}-free且两侧顶点数分别为m、n，则边数为O_{t,k}((mn)^{2/3}+m+n+mn/p)；对点与两参数多项式族的互异几何不可约分支之间的关联，同样的界成立。

Conclusion: 该上界统一并推广了有限域上的关联结果，应用包括丰富分支（rich components）、差集上的多项式取值以及多项式扩张。

Abstract: We prove an incidence bound for bipartite graphs on finite subsets of $\mathbb{F}^2\times \mathbb{F}^2$ defined by Boolean combinations of polynomial equations of bounded degree. If such a graph is $K_{k,k}$-free and its vertex classes have sizes $m$ and $n$, then it has $O_{t,k}((mn)^{2/3}+m+n+mn/p)$ edges, where $t$ bounds the description complexity, $p$ is the characteristic of $\mathbb{F}$, and $1/p=0$ in characteristic zero. We also prove this bound for incidences between points and distinct geometrically irreducible components of a two-parameter polynomial family, allowing singular and nonreduced members. The proof extends Lewko's interpolation and contact-multiplicity method from lines to algebraic families. Applications include rich components, polynomial values on difference sets, and polynomial expansion.

</details>


### [160] [The minimum spectral radius of maximal outerplanar graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33333)
*Suil O*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 确定了极大外平面图中谱半径的最小值图：$n$ 个顶点的极大外平面图 $G$ 满足 $\rho(G)\ge\rho(F_n)$，等号成立当且仅当 $G=F_n$，其中 $F_n$ 是 $n$ 边形的锯齿三角剖分（即 $n$ 个顶点路径的平方）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lin 和 Ning 已确定谱半径最大的外平面图，且极值图为极大外平面图；本文转向确定谱半径最小的情形，即刻画极小化图。

Method: 对极大外平面图使用三种局部操作，每种都严格减小谱半径：一是反转某块沿弦的附着方式，二三是在弦对面的孪生顶点没有携带耳或仅携带单个耳时，把一块从一个顶点移到其孪生处。证明无操作可用的图或者是 $F_n$，或者谱半径大于 $4$，或者是由中心三角形带三个各含至少三个三角形的锯齿叶片组成且顶点数至多 $15$；最后一种情形包含两个 $12$ 顶点显式图之一，其谱半径超过 $F_{15}$。途中用到的数值不等式由小整数分量的显式整数向量验证。

Result: 证明了每个 $n$ 顶点极大外平面图 $G$ 满足 $\rho(G)\ge\rho(F_n)$，且等号成立当且仅当 $G=F_n$。由于对所有 $n$ 有 $\rho(F_n)<4$，从而完成极小值的确定。

Conclusion: $F_n$（$n$ 边形锯齿三角剖分，即路径的平方）是极大外平面图中谱半径的唯一极小化图，谱半径下界为 $\rho(F_n)$ 且小于 $4$。

Abstract: An outerplanar graph is \emph{maximal} if no edge can be added without losing outerplanarity. Lin and Ning determined the outerplanar graph with the largest spectral radius, and the maximizer is a maximal outerplanar graph. We determine the minimizer. In this paper, we prove that every $n$-vertex maximal outerplanar graph $G$ satisfies $ρ(G)\geρ(F_n)$, where $F_n$ is the zig-zag triangulation of the $n$-gon, that is, the square of the path on $n$ vertices, with equality if and only if $G=F_n$. The proof uses three local operations on maximal outerplanar graphs, each of which strictly decreases the spectral radius: the first reverses the way a piece is attached along a chord, and the second and third move a piece from one vertex to its twin across a chord when the twin carries nothing or a single ear, respectively. A graph at which no operation applies is $F_n$, or has spectral radius greater than $4$, or consists of a central triangle with three zig-zag blades of at least three triangles each and has at most $15$ vertices; in the last case it contains one of two explicit graphs on $12$ vertices whose spectral radius exceeds that of $F_{15}$. Since $ρ(F_n)<4$ for all $n$, this completes the proof. The numerical inequalities used along the way are certified by explicit integer vectors with small entries.

</details>


### [161] [A combinatorial approach to point-flat incidences over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33365)
*Ben Lund,Tao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在有限射影空间 PG(n+d,q) 中建立了点集 P 与 n-平坦族 L 之间的关联上界，给出了随 log_q|L| 变化的显式分段线性指数，并在若干参数区间内改进了 Haemers 的经典估计与 Kong-Tamo 界；匹配构造证明在多个范围内达到常数因子下的紧性。证明为组合方法，避免谱与傅里叶分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的关联估计（如 Haemers 界、Kong-Tamo 界）在有限射影空间中并不在所有平坦数量区间内最优，且既有证明常依赖谱或傅里叶分析方法。作者希望获得更精细、在不同 |L| 区间自适应变化的关联界，并据此改进丰富平坦、例外正交投影及 Furstenberg 集等相关问题的估计。

Method: 采用纯组合方法，构造点集 P 与 n-平坦族 L 在 PG(n+d,q) 中的关联上界，其误差项指数为关于 log_q|L| 的分段线性函数。证明不依赖谱方法或傅里叶分析，并辅以匹配构造来验证界在若干参数范围内的紧性，上级应用于丰富平坦、例外正交投影和 Furstenberg 集的下界。

Result: 对固定维度，得到关联误差的显式分段线性指数界，在指定 |L| 范围内改进 Haemers 估计与 Kong-Tamo 界；匹配构造表明在若干参数区间内该界在常数因子意义下是紧的。作为应用，获得丰富平坦与例外正交投影的更优估计，并在规定方向比例较小的某些范围内强化了 Furstenberg 集的下界。

Conclusion: 有限射影空间中点-平坦关联可在无需谱与傅里叶分析工具的情况下获得分段线性、依 |L| 自适应的改进界，且该界在多个范围内最优。其方法可推广至丰富平坦、例外正交投影及 Furstenberg 集等问题，带来更强结论。

Abstract: We establish new bounds for incidences between a point set P and a family L of n-flats in PG(n+d,q). For fixed dimensions, our bound on the incidence discrepancy has an explicit piecewise-linear exponent in $\log_q|L|$, improving the classical estimate of Haemers and the Kong-Tamo bound in specified ranges of the number of flats. Matching constructions establish sharpness up to constant factors in several parameter ranges. The proof is combinatorial and avoids spectral and Fourier analytic methods. As applications, we obtain improved estimates for rich flats and exceptional orthogonal projections, together with stronger lower bounds for Furstenberg sets in certain ranges where the fraction of prescribed directions is small.

</details>


### [162] [Rank Stabilization for Sparse Coordinate Completion of Unit-Norm Tight Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33388)
*Dongwei Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文确定了单位范数紧框架的通用补全纤维维数，针对每列恰好缺失两个坐标的情形：缺失对构成标记多重图，其 Jacobian 拟阵在长度 $R\ge N_d=\binom{d+1}{2}-1$ 时为显式子空间配置的 Rado 拟阵，秩由图论最小公式给出且与 $R$ 无关，余秩亏即为通用纤维维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究部分观测列（每列恰缺失两个坐标）下单位范数紧框架的补全问题，需要刻画解空间的通用纤维维数，并揭示其与缺失模式（多重图）之间的代数—组合联系，同时确定长度阈值与稳定化临界长度。

Method: 在实数框架方程定义的复代数簇上工作，将缺失坐标对编码为标记多重图。核心是在长度 $N_d=\binom{d+1}{2}-1$ 处证明一个实现定理，结合横截—图的拟阵划分、零坐标矩浸没以及通用剩余矩阵的相容分解；再利用基扩张与完全观测列下的稳定性将秩公式推广到更大长度，并给出四维显式亏格公式与光滑实框架轨迹上的局部实对应结果。

Result: 对每个 $d\ge4$ 与 $R\ge N_d$，缺失对 Jacobian 拟阵是显式子空间配置的 Rado 拟阵，其秩由图论最小值公式给出且与 $R$ 无关，余秩亏为通用纤维维数；给出四维显式亏格公式和光滑实框架轨迹上的局部实对应；通过公共旋转障碍证明最小一致稳定化阈值下界为 $2d-1$。

Conclusion: 缺失对 Jacobian 拟阵的秩具有与 $R$ 无关的图论刻画，通用补全纤维维数由此确定；实现定理与拟阵划分是核心工具，四维情形可显式给出亏格，且公共旋转障碍给出稳定化阈值 $2d-1$ 的下界。

Abstract: We determine generic completion-fiber dimensions for unit-norm tight frames when each partially observed column has exactly two missing coordinates. We work on the complex algebraic variety defined by the real frame equations. The missing coordinate pairs form a labelled multigraph. For every $d\ge4$ and every frame length $R\ge N_d:=\binom{d+1}{2}-1$, we prove that the missing-pair Jacobian matroid is the Rado matroid of an explicit subspace arrangement. Its rank is therefore given by a graph-theoretic minimum formula and is independent of $R$; the complementary rank defect is the generic fiber dimension. The main step is a realization theorem at length $N_d$. It combines a transversal--graphic matroid partition with a zero-coordinate moment submersion and a compatible decomposition of a generic residual matrix. Basis extension and stability under fully observed columns then yield the rank formula at every larger length. We also give an explicit defect formula in dimension four and a local real counterpart on the smooth real frame locus. A common-rotation obstruction bounds the smallest uniform stabilization threshold from below by $2d-1$.

</details>


### [163] [A Curved-Path Refinement of the Kalai--Kleitman Diameter Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33434)
*Tianhao Liu,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过曲线路径编码迭代Kalai–Kleitman递推，改进了指向多面体图直径Δ_u(d,n)的拟多项式上界，将指数中的对数因子再降低一层，并给出超额型互补界、若干渐近区域的更优指数及该框架内主项的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带一个标记顶点的d维多面体在其n个面数下的最大图直径Δ_u(d,n)。此前最优结果为准多项式界，其指数仍含有较大的对数因子，作者希望借助对迭代Kalai–Kleitman递推的更精细编码进一步压低指数，并在不同n与d的比例区域给出更精确的界。

Method: 采用曲线路径编码（curved-path encoding）对迭代Kalai–Kleitman递推进行统一处理；结合定量化的d步归约（quantitative d-step reduction），对n≥d≥4一致地推导上界。对一般界取G_d=(4 ln2+o(1)) d/(ln d)^2（d→∞），并利用超额（excess, n−d）变量得到互补界，再在n−d=Θ(d)、n=d^{1/γ+o(1)}(0<γ<1)以及深尾区域(ln n)/d→∞等区域内分析指数行为。

Result: 得到Δ_u(d,n) ≤ (n−d)^{log_2 G_d}，G_d=(4 ln2+o(1)) d/(ln d)^2，较此前最佳拟多项式界在指数中额外降低一个对数因子；同时获得超额型界Δ_u(d,n) ≤ (n−d)^{(1/2)log_2(n−d)+O(1)}，其中常数绝对。在n−d=Θ(d)时该界渐近更强，并将指数主系数减半。对n=d^{1/γ+o(1)}(0<γ<1)给出更尖锐指数，并在(ln n)/d→∞的深尾区域得到几乎线性界。此外证明一般界的主项在此正路径计数框架内是紧的。

Conclusion: 曲线路径编码为迭代Kalai–Kleitman递推提供了统一且更优的直径上界工具，显著改进了拟多项式指数，并在超额尺度、幂尺度与深尾尺度上给出互补且更精确的估计；同时表明一般界主项在正路径计数框架内已达最优。该框架为后续研究指向多面体图直径的精细渐近行为提供了基准与限制。

Abstract: Let $Δ_u(d,n)$ be the maximum graph diameter of a pointed $d$-dimensional polyhedron with $n$ facets. Using a curved-path encoding of the iterated Kalai--Kleitman recurrence, we prove, uniformly over $n\geq d\geq 4$, \[
  Δ_u(d,n) \leq (n-d)^{\log_2 G_d},\qquad
  G_d = (4\ln 2+o(1))\frac{d}{(\ln d)^2}, \] where the asymptotic expression for $G_d$ is understood as $d\to\infty$, improving the exponent of the previous best quasi-polynomial bound by an additional logarithmic factor. With the quantitative $d$-step reduction, we also obtain the complementary excess-based bound \[
  Δ_u(d,n) \leq (n-d)^{\frac{1}{2}\log_2(n-d)+O(1)}, \] where the implied constant is absolute. In the regime $n - d = Θ(d)$, the latter bound is asymptotically stronger and halves the leading coefficient in the exponent. We further examine their behavior as $n$ grows relative to $d$, obtaining sharper exponents when $n = d^{1/γ+o(1)}$ for fixed $0<γ<1$ and an almost-linear bound in the deep-tail regime $(\ln n)/d\to\infty$. We also show that the leading term of the general bound is sharp within this positive path-counting framework.

</details>


### [164] [Binomial coefficients and the operadic butterfly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33480)
*Vladimir Dotsenko,Medet Jumadildayev*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明Laubie关于补全Loday算子蝴蝶图的另外两个operads各分量维数的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Loday的operadic butterfly diagram尚有两个operads的分量维数未被确定，Laubie就此提出猜想，本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 针对补全蝴蝶图的两个operads，分别计算其第n个分量的维数，并给出与二项式系数相关的精确表达式。

Result: 其中一个operad的第n分量维数对所有n≠3等于二项式系数C(2n, n-1)；另一个operad的第n分量维数对所有n≥5等于C(2n, n-1)+1。

Conclusion: Laubie的猜想成立，两个operads的分量维数得到完整刻画，从而补全了Loday算子蝴蝶图的维数信息。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Laubie on dimensions of components of two operads completing Loday's operadic butterfly diagram. For one of these operads, the dimension of the $n$-th component is equal to $\binom{2n}{n-1}$ for all $n\ne 3$, and for the other it is equal to $\binom{2n}{n-1}+1$ for all $n\ge 5$.

</details>


### [165] [Sunflowers of Reed--Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33512)
*Roni Con,Anina Gruica,Maria Montanucci,Ferdinando Zullo*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究 Reed–Solomon 向日葵（Reed–Solomon sunflowers），即两两交集都等于同一固定子空间的 Reed–Solomon 码族，给出基于广义 V-矩阵的代数判定准则，并通过计数与构造研究其规模；在维数 2 时证明所有不同 RS 码构成向日葵并确定其大小，在 k≥3、ℓ≥2k−1 时给出递归构造与贪心存在性下界，并将贪心论证用于 MDS 码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该概念位于极值子空间组合与编码理论的交叉点：可视为 Grassmannian 中太阳花问题的结构化版本，并能自然产生具有指定最小距离的常维子空间码，因此需要刻画 RS 码族何时形成向日葵并估计其规模。

Method: 以广义 V-矩阵为工具给出 RS 码族形成向日葵（中心为全一向量生成的一维子空间）的代数判定准则；通过计数方法研究维数 2 的情形，并用递归构造与贪心存在性论证研究固定维数 k≥3、长度 ℓ≥2k−1 的情形；进一步将贪心论证应用于 MDS 码族。

Result: 在维数 2 时，所有不同 Reed–Solomon 码构成向日葵，并通过按求值向量的仿射等价类计数确定其大小；对固定 k≥3 和 ℓ≥2k−1，给出含 Ω_{k,ℓ}(q^{⌊ℓ/(2k−1)⌋}) 个花瓣的显式递归构造，以及当 q→∞ 时含 Ω_{k,ℓ}(q^{ℓ−2k+2}) 个花瓣的贪心存在性论证；还得到规模为 Ω_{k,ℓ}(q^{2(ℓ−2k+2)}) 的 [ℓ,k]_q MDS 码族，其两两交集维数至多为 1 但不必相等。

Conclusion: Reed–Solomon 向日葵为常维子空间码与极值子空间组合提供结构化框架；其存在性与规模可由广义 V-矩阵刻画，并在维数 2、k≥3 的一般情形以及相关 MDS 码构造中获得定量下界。

Abstract: We introduce and study Reed--Solomon sunflowers, namely families of Reed--Solomon codes whose pairwise intersections are all equal to the same fixed subspace. This notion lies at the intersection of extremal subspace combinatorics and coding theory: it can be viewed as a structured version of the sunflower problem in the Grassmannian, and it naturally produces constant-dimension subspace codes with prescribed minimum distance. We focus mainly on the case in which the center is the one-dimensional space generated by the all-one vector. We give an algebraic criterion, expressed in terms of generalized $V$-matrices, ensuring that a family of Reed--Solomon codes forms such a sunflower. We then study the size of these families through counting and constructions. In dimension two, we show that all distinct Reed--Solomon codes form a sunflower and determine its size by counting Reed--Solomon codes up to affine equivalence of their evaluation vectors. For fixed dimension $k\geq3$ and length $\ell\geq2k-1$, we give an explicit recursive construction with $Ω_{k,\ell}(q^{\lfloor\ell/(2k-1)\rfloor})$ petals and a greedy existence argument with $Ω_{k,\ell}(q^{\ell-2k+2})$ petals as $q\to\infty$. We also apply the greedy argument to obtain families of $[\ell,k]_q$ MDS codes of size $Ω_{k,\ell}(q^{2(\ell-2k+2)})$, whose pairwise intersections have dimension at most one but need not be equal.

</details>


### [166] [Explicit Fractalizers: Prime-Order Paley Graphs and other Cayley Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33526)
*Aldo Kiem,Fan Wei*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了足够大的素数阶 Paley 图是 fractalizer，即对每个宿主阶数，最大化诱导拷贝数的极图恰为 H 的平衡迭代 blow-up；并构造了第二个显式的非平凡 Cayley fractalizer 无穷族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: inducibility 问题研究固定图在给定顶点数图中诱导拷贝的最大数量，但确定显式模式的全部极图（尤其要求在每个宿主阶数下都成立）仍很困难。已有概率结果表明随机图和随机交换 Cayley 图以趋于 1 的概率是 fractalizer，说明此类图大量存在，但未直接给出显式族；本文旨在解决显式构造问题。

Method: 通过极值图论与 flag algebra 相关工具，分析素数阶 Paley 图的递归极值结构，证明其满足 fractalizer 性质；同时构造另一显式非平凡 Cayley fractalizer 无穷族，并给出比 Paley 情形更简化的证明。

Result: 证明每个足够大的素数阶 Paley 图都是 fractalizer；并构造出第二类显式无限 Cayley fractalizer 族，其证明较 Paley 情形更简单。

Conclusion: Paley 图等经典算术模式在每个宿主阶数下精确决定极图的递归平衡迭代 blow-up 结构，且不依赖宿主图的代数假设；两项结果共同解决了 2025 年 AIM 研讨会“Flag Algebras and Extremal Combinatorics”中讨论的显式构造问题。

Abstract: The inducibility problem asks for the maximum number of induced copies of a fixed graph among all graphs with a prescribed number of vertices. Inducibility has been an active area of research in extremal combinatorics, but determining all extremal graphs for explicitly defined patterns remains challenging, particularly when the description is required to hold at every host order. A graph $H$ is called a fractalizer if, for every positive integer $n$, every $n$-vertex graph maximizing the number of induced copies of $H$ is a balanced iterated blow-up of $H$, obtained by recursively repeating the same pattern in parts whose sizes differ by at most one. Previous probabilistic results show that large random graphs and random abelian Cayley graphs are fractalizers with probability tending to one, establishing their abundance without directly providing explicit families.
  We prove that every sufficiently large prime-order Paley graph is a fractalizer. Thus these classical arithmetic patterns determine the exact recursive structure of every extremal host, at every host order and without any algebraic assumptions on the host. We also construct a second explicit infinite family of nontrivial Cayley fractalizers, for which the fractalizer property admits a simpler proof than in the Paley case. Together, these results resolve the explicit-construction question discussed at the 2025 American Institute of Mathematics workshop "Flag Algebras and Extremal Combinatorics."

</details>


### [167] [A Hopf Algebraic Theory of the Quantum Magnusian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33587)
*Li Guo,Joon-Hwi Kim,Jung-Wook Kim,Sungsoo Kim,Sangmin Lee,Jian-Rong Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文建立量子Magnus展开中图系数的Hopf代数理论，引入混合quiver的收缩Hopf代数，给出连通函数ω_c的普适闭公式与局部刻画，并在无环情形化简为下降数置换公式，同时得到量子Murua公式与Tutte、色多项式相关恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Magnus展开中的图系数缺少统一的代数框架：经典情形由有向树支配，量子情形则涉及环图、多重边与多种边数据，需要一种能容纳这些图并关于收缩封闭的Hopf代数结构，以刻画其组合与物理系数。

Method: 构造混合quiver的收缩Hopf代数，使其包含上述物理图并在收缩下封闭；在quiver张成的Hopf子代数上，由归一化线性扩张数据与蝌蚪规定定义特征e，取其卷积逆ω；对连通函数ω_c给出顶点集有序集分拆上的有限和闭公式，并给出由两点收缩恒等式、蝌蚪分解、非连通图消失与边界数据构成的局部刻画；对无环quiver化简为仅依赖下降数的置换公式，并通过算子乘积与Wick收缩从量子Magnus展开独立推导。

Result: 得到ω_c在所有有限quiver上的普适闭公式，无需无环或单边假设；局部刻画唯一确定ω_c；无环情形下公式化简为下降数置换公式，且该公式可由量子Magnus展开独立导出，表明物理图系数受收缩Hopf代数卷积结构支配；作为推论得到精化量子Murua公式及与Tutte、色多项式相关的定向和恒等式。

Conclusion: 收缩Hopf代数的卷积结构统一支配量子Magnus展开的图系数，闭公式与局部刻画适用于一般quiver，无环情形退化为下降数置换公式，并由此导出量子Murua公式与Tutte、色多项式相关恒等式。

Abstract: We develop a Hopf-algebraic theory of the graph coefficients arising in the quantum Magnus expansion. At the classical level, the graph expansion is governed by directed trees, whereas its quantum counterpart involves loop graphs, multiple edges, and different types of edge data. We introduce a contraction Hopf algebra of mixed quivers containing these physical diagrams and closed under contraction.
  On the Hopf subalgebra spanned by quivers, we define a character $e$ from normalized linear-extension data and a tadpole prescription, and let $ω$ be its convolution inverse. Our main result is a universal closed formula for the connected function $ω_c$ on every finite quiver. The formula is a finite sum over ordered set partitions of the vertex set and is valid without acyclicity or simplicity assumptions. We also give a local characterization: a two-vertex contraction identity, together with tadpole factorization, vanishing on disconnected graphs, and boundary data, determines $ω_c$ uniquely.
  For acyclic quivers, the general formula reduces to a permutation formula whose coefficients depend only on descent numbers. We derive the same formula independently from the quantum Magnus expansion via operator products and Wick contractions, showing that its physical graph coefficients are governed by the convolution structure of the contraction Hopf algebra. As further consequences, we obtain a refined quantum Murua formula and orientation-sum identities related to Tutte and chromatic polynomials.

</details>


### [168] [Polynomial Compressibility and Forbidden Oriented Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33600)
*Zhenhua Lyu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文否证了有界绝对/相对定向团数下的多项式τ界猜想，并刻画了哪些禁止图能给出多项式τ有界类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有向图H的τ(H)（H能同态到所有n阶锦标赛的最小n）与最长有向路径p(H)的关系，检验在绝对或相对定向团数有界条件下τ是否被p的多项式控制这一猜想。

Method: 对任意p≥3、g≥1构造连通无环定向图H：底层围长大于g、绝对与相对定向团数均为3，且p(H)=p、τ(H)=r_tr(p)（传递p阶锦标赛的锦标赛Ramsey数，为2^{Θ(p)}）；另给出最大入/出度为O(p^2)的构造；并对两棵四顶点树的所有定向分别证明多项式τ有界性。

Result: 构造反例推翻了多项式界猜想：τ(H)=r_tr(p)=2^{Θ(p)}，说明禁止图要产生多项式τ有界类，其底层图必须是森林。固定g时例子最小阶为p的多项式；p=4时最小阶为2^{Θ(g)}。对两棵四顶点树的定向给出τ界：纯爪为已知O(p^4)；混合爪与P_4的单转折定向在p≥2时为2p-2；P_4的有向与交替定向分别为4和3p-2；交替情形下当底层图无三角形时τ(H)=p(H)。

Conclusion: 在定向团数有界下τ不具备多项式上界，多项式τ有界类的禁止图底层必须是森林；对若干四顶点树的定向给出了具体多项式界，完善了τ有界性的结构刻画。

Abstract: For a nonempty acyclic oriented graph $H$, let $p(H)$ be the order of a longest directed path and let $τ(H)$ be the least positive integer $n$ such that $H$ admits a homomorphism to every tournament of order $n$. For all $p\ge3$ and $g\ge1$, we construct a connected acyclic oriented graph $H$ with underlying girth greater than $g$, absolute and relative oriented clique numbers equal to three, and \[
  p(H)=p,\qquad τ(H)=r_{\mathrm{tr}}(p), \] where $r_{\mathrm{tr}}(p)=2^{Θ(p)}$ is the tournament Ramsey number for a transitive $p$-vertex tournament. This disproves the conjectured polynomial bounds under bounded absolute or relative oriented clique number. It also shows that a forbidden graph can yield a polynomially $τ$-bounded class only if its underlying graph is a forest. For fixed $g$, the least order of these examples is bounded by a polynomial in $p$. A separate construction gives maximum in- and outdegree $O(p^2)$, uniformly in $g$. For $p=4$, the least order is $2^{Θ(g)}$. We also establish polynomial $τ$-boundedness for every orientation of the two four-vertex trees. The pure-claw case follows from the known $O(p^4)$ bound. We obtain the bound $2p-2$ for mixed claws and one-turn orientations of $P_4$ when $p\ge2$, and bounds $4$ and $3p-2$ for the directed and alternating orientations of $P_4$, respectively. In the alternating case, $τ(H)=p(H)$ when the underlying graph is triangle-free.

</details>


### [169] [AI's solution of Conjecture 9.1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33644)
*Jinyoung Park,Michel Talagrand*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解释了AI模型对Talagrand早先提出的若干大胆猜想之一所给出的一个非常优美的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Talagrand此前提出了若干大胆猜想，其中一个猜想一直缺乏证明。

Method: 借助AI模型给出的证明，作者对此证明进行解释与阐述。

Result: AI模型成功对Talagrand的其中一个猜想给出了非常优美的证明。

Conclusion: 该AI模型提供的证明被作者认为优美，并在本文中加以说明。

Abstract: In an earlier paper, M. Talagrand made a number of daring conjectures. An AI model provided a very beautiful proof of one of them which we explain here.

</details>


### [170] [Log-concavity of flat arrangement polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33651)
*Yuan Gao,Tianyu Yuan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了所有实平坦构型的行列式加权外部半活性多项式的对数凹性，强化了已知的梯形性质；通过混合体积表示与Alexandrov–Fenchel不等式，建立二次系数不等式并给出秩相关的幂凹性，并将结果应用于欧拉有向图的生成树多项式、正环流权重情形及特殊交错链环的Alexander多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果显示行列式加权外部半活性多项式具有梯形性，但其更强的对数凹性尚未解决；同时欧拉有向图生成树多项式的对数凹性仍是猜想。作者希望用统一方法证明并推广这些凹性现象。

Method: 引入系数的新混合体积表示，并结合Alexandrov–Fenchel不等式；进一步给出更一般的混合体积序列的因式分解与对数凹性公式，从而证明二次系数不等式，并推出秩至少为二时的幂凹性（指数显式依赖于秩）。

Result: 证明了实平坦构型行列式加权外部半活性多项式的对数凹性，并在秩至少为二时得到显式秩相关指数的幂凹性；获得更一般的混合体积序列因式分解与对数凹性；证明了欧拉有向图生成树多项式的对数凹性猜想，并推广至正环流权重，同时强化特殊交错链环Alexander多项式的系数不等式。

Conclusion: 混合体积表示与Alexandrov–Fenchel不等式为离散多项式凹性问题提供了有效统一框架，既解决了实平坦构型与欧拉有向图生成树多项式等对数凹性问题，也强化了链环Alexander多项式的系数不等式。

Abstract: We prove log-concavity for the determinant-weighted external semi-activity polynomials of all real flat arrangements, strengthening their known trapezoidality. In fact, we establish a quadratic coefficient inequality that, in rank at least two, implies power concavity with an explicit rank-dependent exponent. The proof uses a new mixed-volume representation of the coefficients and the Alexandrov--Fenchel inequality. A more general formula gives a factorization and log-concavity for related mixed-volume sequences. As applications, we establish the conjectured log-concavity for spanning-tree polynomials of Eulerian digraphs, extend it to positive circulation weights, and strengthen the coefficient inequalities for Alexander polynomials of special alternating links.

</details>


### [171] [Quantitative Merino--Welsh inequalities for joins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33680)
*Jungang Chen,Jiaxin Xie*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图论中比值 \(Q(G)=\frac{T(G;2,0)T(G;0,2)}{T(G;1,1)^2}\)，在图联（join）运算下给出定量下界，证明 \(Q(A\vee B)\) 有仅依赖阶数 \(a,b\) 的显式下界且严格大于1，并证明多因子联图及无割边完全多部图满足严格乘法 Merino–Welsh 不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merino–Welsh 猜想相关的不等式是图论与拟阵理论的核心问题，已有结果多为定性或特定图类。本文旨在对图的 join 运算给出定量、显式的下界估计，量化两因子内部边对 \(Q\) 的改进，并借此推广到更广的图类（多因子联图、无割边完全多部图），验证严格乘法 Merino–Welsh 不等式。

Method: 方法结合定向估计（orientation estimates）与生成树比较：利用有效电阻（effective resistance）比较生成树数量，并采用块消去（block elimination）技术进行精细分析，从而导出 \(Q(A\vee B)\) 的显式下界及 \(Q(K_2\vee F)\) 的定量估计。

Result: 对任意阶数 \(3\le a\le b\) 的简单图 \(A,B\)，\(Q(A\vee B)\) 存在仅依赖 \(a,b\) 的显式下界且严格大于1；量化了两因子内部边带来的改进。对任意简单图 \(F\)（\(n=|V(F)|+2\ge4\)），证明 \(Q(K_2\vee F)\ge \frac{27}{n^2}\left(\frac32\right)^{n-4}\)。由此推出任意至少三个非空因子的 join 及无割边完全多部图满足严格乘法 Merino–Welsh 不等式。

Conclusion: 图联运算能保证 \(Q\) 严格大于1，且随因子阶数增长下界以指数速率改善；所给结果覆盖多因子联图和无割边完全多部图等图类，验证了严格的乘法 Merino–Welsh 不等式，并展示定向估计、有效电阻与块消去相结合是处理此类问题的有效工具。

Abstract: For a connected graph \(G\), let \[ Q(G)=\frac{T(G;2,0)T(G;0,2)}{T(G;1,1)^2}. \] We obtain quantitative lower bounds for \(Q\) under the graph join operation. If \(A\) and \(B\) are arbitrary simple graphs of orders \(3\le a\le b\), then \(Q(A\vee B)\) admits an explicit lower bound depending only on \(a\) and \(b\), and this bound is strictly greater than \(1\). We further quantify the improvement produced by edges inside the two factors. For every simple graph \(F\), with \(n=|V(F)|+2\ge4\), we prove \[ Q(K_2\vee F)\ge \frac{27}{n^2}\left(\frac32\right)^{n-4}. \] Consequently, every join of at least three nonempty factors, and every complete multipartite graph with at least one edge and no cut edges, satisfies the strict multiplicative Merino--Welsh inequality. The proofs combine orientation estimates with spanning-tree comparisons based on effective resistance and block elimination.

</details>


### [172] [A note on three-quarters circulant digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33718)
*C. Dalfó,M. A. Fiol,M. A. Reyes*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入一类与循环群 Z_N 相关的“三扇区”循环有向图（three-quarters circulant），其距离函数不同于标准有向距离；借助平面格表示与周期镶嵌分析可达集增长，给出 Moore 型上界，并构造达到指定直径的无穷族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准循环有向图中的有向距离与阶/直径极值问题已有成熟理论，而将两个生成元 a、b 的容许组合限制在三个坐标扇区后会改变距离度量，产生新的几何与组合现象，值得研究其可达集增长与极值阶数问题。

Method: 采用平面格表示与周期镶嵌刻画受约束的生成元组合；分析可达集的增长并推导阶/直径问题的 Moore 型上界；给出基于格的方法判定容许生成元对，并分别针对偶直径与奇直径构造显式的三扇区循环结构无穷族；辅以中小阶计算实验。

Result: 得到不同于标准有向距离的距离型函数；建立 Moore 型上界；构造出具有指定直径的三扇区循环结构无穷族（偶、奇直径分别处理）；计算实验表明偶直径情形存在更优族，并启发最大可达阶的渐近猜想公式。

Conclusion: 受约束格表示、周期镶嵌与循环网络极值问题之间存在深刻联系；该框架为三扇区循环有向图的阶/直径问题提供了构造与上界工具，并为后续渐近最优性研究提出猜想。

Abstract: We introduce and study a new family of circulant digraphs associated with the cyclic group $\Z_N$, obtained by restricting admissible combinations of two generators $a$ and $b$ to three coordinate sectors. The resulting distance-like function differs from the standard directed distance in circulant digraphs and gives rise to new geometric and combinatorial phenomena. Using planar lattice representations and periodic tessellations, we analyze the growth of reachable sets and derive Moore-type upper bounds for the corresponding order/diameter problem.
  We construct explicit infinite families of three-quarters circulant structures with the prescribed diameter and provide lattice-based methods for determining admissible generator pairs. Separate constructions are obtained for even and odd diameters. In addition, computational experiments for small and moderate orders suggest improved families for even diameters and motivate a conjectural asymptotic formula for the maximum attainable order. The paper highlights the interplay between constrained lattice representations, periodic tilings, and extremal problems for circulant networks.

</details>


### [173] [Strong $q$-log-convexity of the $d$-Hoggatt polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33732)
*Shane Chern,Wenle Shi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究与 d-Hoggatt 数相关的三类 (q-)对数凸性/凹性，证明 d-Hoggatt 变换保持对数凸性，建立 d-Hoggatt 数 q-模拟的强 q-对数凹性，并基于 Schur 函数理论证明 d-Hoggatt 多项式序列具有强 q-对数凸性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数凸性/凹性是组合序列的重要性质，与组合序列的单峰性、无零点性等密切相关。d-Hoggatt 数是经典组合数列的自然推广，其 (q-)对数凸凹性尚待系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用组合变换分析证明 d-Hoggatt 变换保持序列的对数凸性；利用 q-模拟技术研究 d-Hoggatt 数的强 q-对数凹性；核心部分借助 Schur 函数理论证明 d-Hoggatt 多项式序列的强 q-对数凸性。

Result: d-Hoggatt 变换保持序列的对数凸性；d-Hoggatt 数的 q-模拟具有强 q-对数凹性；d-Hoggatt 多项式序列具有强 q-对数凸性。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了 d-Hoggatt 数及其 q-模拟、多项式形式的对数凸凹性质，表明这些序列具有良好的组合结构性质，Schur 函数方法是处理此类 q-对数凸性问题的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, three ($q$-)log-convexity or concavity properties related to the $d$-Hoggatt numbers are investigated. First, we show that the $d$-Hoggatt transformation preserves the log-convexity of a sequence. We then establish the strong $q$-log-concavity of a $q$-analog of $d$-Hoggatt numbers. Finally, we prove the core result of this work, namely, the sequence of $d$-Hoggatt polynomials is strongly $q$-log-convex, based on the theory of Schur functions.

</details>


### [174] [A quasipolynomial inverse theorem for the $\mathsf{U}^k(\mathbb{F}_p^n)$ norm in the high characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33733)
*Luka Milićević*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在 $p \geq k$ 情形下，对 Gowers 一致范数 $\mathsf{U}^k(\mathbb{F}_p^n)$ 证明了具有拟多项式（quasipolynomial）界的高阶逆定理，其核心是 Freiman 多重同态的拟多项式结构定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers 范数的逆定理是加性组合与高阶傅里叶分析的核心问题，但已有定量结果往往界较弱或证明繁琐。作者希望在 $p \geq k$ 的有限域向量空间情形下改进定量界，得到拟多项式级别的逆定理，并将问题归约为 Freiman 多重同态的结构刻画。

Method: 关键方法是证明 Freiman 多重同态（Freiman 同态向多变量映射的自然推广）的拟多项式结构定理。证明依赖三个主要工具：(1) 代数正则性方法（algebraic regularity method）；(2) 抽象 Balog-Szemerédi-Gowers 定理；(3) 定义于多重线性簇上的多重线性映射理论。其中第三个工具源自 Gowers 与作者的早期工作，本文对其作了显著扩展。建立该映射理论后，结构定理的证明变得相对简短。

Result: 在 $p \geq k$ 时，Gowers 一致范数 $\mathsf{U}^k(\mathbb{F}_p^n)$ 的逆定理成立，且具有拟多项式界；同时得到 Freiman 多重同态的拟多项式结构定理。

Conclusion: 作者通过 Freiman 多重同态结构定理建立了 $\mathsf{U}^k(\mathbb{F}_p^n)$ 的拟多项式逆定理，证明了多重线性簇上多重线性映射理论的有效性，使得证明较此前 Gowers 范数逆理论中的定量结果明显更短，显示了该理论框架在定量逆定理中的潜力。

Abstract: We prove an inverse theorem for the Gowers uniformity norm $\mathsf{U}^k(\mathbb{F}_p^n)$ with quasipolynomial bounds in the case when $p \geq k$. The inverse theorem follows from a quasipolynomial structure theorem for Freiman multihomomorphisms, which are a natural generalization of Freiman homomorphisms to maps of several variables.
  The proof of the structure theorem for Freiman multihomomorphisms is the central result of the paper and rests on three main ingredients: algebraic regularity method, abstract Balog-Szemerédi-Gowers theorem and the theory of multilinear maps defined on multilinear varieties. The last ingredient originates from an earlier work of Gowers and the author, and is significantly expanded in this paper. In particular, once the theory of such maps is in place, the proof of the structure theorem for Freiman multihomomorphisms is relatively short, especially compared to the previous quantitative results in the inverse theory of Gowers norms.

</details>


### [175] [The Fibonacci numbers are not 3-accessible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33744)
*William J. Wesley*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明斐波那契数集不是3-accessible的，从而解决了Landman与Robertson于2007年提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landman与Robertson在2007年提出关于D-diffsequence与r-accessible集合的公开问题，其中斐波那契数集是否为3-accessible尚未解决，本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 围绕D-diffsequence的定义（相邻项之差属于集合D），针对斐波那契数集构造性地研究正整数上的3-染色，证明不存在任意长的单色斐波那契diffsequence，即说明该集合不是3-accessible的。

Result: 证明了斐波那契数集不是3-accessible的，即存在一种正整数上的3-染色，使得其中不含任意长的单色斐波那契diffsequence。

Conclusion: 本文否定了斐波那契数集为3-accessible的可能性，解决了Landman与Robertson于2007年提出的公开问题。

Abstract: A $D$-diffsequence is a sequence of integers $x_1 < \dots < x_k$ such that $x_{i+1} -x_i \in D$ for $1 \le i \le k-1$. The set $D$ is called $r$-accessible if every $r$-coloring of the positive integers contains arbitrarily long monochromatic $D$-diffsequences. This note proves that the set of Fibonacci numbers is not 3-accessible, resolving an open problem of Landman and Robertson from 2007.

</details>


### [176] [Orthogonal and unitary signings of cube-like graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33819)
*Meirun Chen,Reza Naserasr*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入生成集S⊆Z₂ⁿ的Θ-性质，证明无零元且具Θ-性质的生成集给出可幺正签名的立方体类图Q_S，并给出显式局部签名公式；对Sidon集给出充要刻画及计数结果，同时解决相应极值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Alon与Zheng关于立方体类图正交与幺正签名工作的启发，研究哪些立方体类图Q_S允许幺正签名，并刻画其结构条件。

Method: 对生成集S⊆Z₂ⁿ引入Θ-性质（任意三个两两不交子集同和时至少两个大小为偶）；证明无零生成集满足Θ-性质可构造幺正签名Hermitian邻接矩阵M（M²=dI，非零元∈{±1,±i}），给出局部公式；将Θ-性质转化为依赖空间D上的模2恒等式|D₁∩D₂|≡|D₁||D₂|，即D↦C(|D|,2) mod 2线性；对Sidon集证明必要性并计数切换等价类；用二元自伴码构造极值例子。

Result: 无零且具Θ-性质的生成集给出可幺正签名的立方体类图Q_S，并具显式局部签名公式；Sidon情形下Θ-性质为充要条件，恰有2^{|S|-n}个切换等价类，并刻画何时可选为正交签名；极值问题中允许零元时得锐界|S|≤2n+1，对每个n≥3给出初等构造达到该界，附加Sidon条件时对每个n≥10用二元自伴码达到同一极值，并确定3≤n≤9的精确Sidon最大值。

Conclusion: Θ-性质给出了立方体类图存在幺正签名的统一代数刻画，并在Sidon集情形下完全刻画与计数；其依赖空间表述同时导出极值集问题的锐界与构造，连接了签名理论、Sidon集与二元自伴码。

Abstract: A unitary signing of a $d$-regular graph is a Hermitian adjacency matrix $M$ whose nonzero entries lie in $\{\pm1,\pm i\}$ and satisfies $M^2=dI$. Motivated by the work of Alon and Zheng on orthogonal and unitary signings of cube-like graphs, we introduce the $Θ$-property for a generating set $S\subseteq\mathbb Z_2^n$: whenever three pairwise disjoint subsets of $S$ have the same sum, at least two of them have even size.
  We prove that every zero-free generating set with the $Θ$-property gives a cube-like graph $Q_S$ admitting a unitary signing, and we give an explicit local formula for such a signing. For Sidon sets, the $Θ$-property is also necessary, yielding a characterization of the Sidon cube-like graphs that admit unitary signings. In this setting there are exactly $2^{|S|-n}$ switching-equivalence classes of unitary signings, and we characterize when a unitary signing can be chosen to be orthogonal.
  The $Θ$-property admits a linear-algebraic description in terms of the dependency space $\mathcal D$ of $S$:
  \[
  |D_1\cap D_2|
  \equiv
  |D_1||D_2|
  \pmod2
  \qquad
  (D_1,D_2\in\mathcal D).
  \]
  Equivalently, the map
  \[
  D\longmapsto \binom{|D|}{2}\pmod2
  \] is linear on $\mathcal D$. For the corresponding extremal set problem, in which zero is permitted, this formulation yields the sharp bound $|S|\leq 2n+1$ for generating sets with the $Θ$-property. We give elementary constructions attaining this bound for every $n\geq3$. Under the additional Sidon condition, the same extremal value is attained for every $n\geq10$ using binary self-dual codes. The exact Sidon maximum is also determined for $3\leq n\leq9$.

</details>


### [177] [Det-extremal cubic graphs and the total domatic number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33902)
*Myungho Choi,Hyemin Kwon,Boram Park*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明连通三次图是det-extremal当且仅当其全支配数为1，由此将McCuaig关于3连通det-extremal三次图的刻画推广到所有3连通三次图，并给出girth限制、非二分图最小阶数28及三角形无关3-构型必有阻塞集等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: det-extremal三次二分图与Pólya永久问题相关，McCuaig已刻画3连通的这类图；另一方面，全支配数为1的三次图刻画是长期未决问题。本文旨在建立二者的等价联系，从而用det-extremal的结构理论解决全支配数问题，并推广已有刻画。

Method: 证明连通三次图det-extremal与全支配数为1之间的等价关系；利用该对应关系，把McCuaig的3连通刻画从二分情形推广到所有3连通三次图；分析det-extremal三次图的girth可能取值；构造并证明非二分情形的最小顶点数下界；最后用构型语言翻译结果。

Result: 连通三次图det-extremal当且仅当全支配数为1；McCuaig刻画对所有3连通三次图成立；每个连通det-extremal三次图的girth为3、5或6；连通det-extremal三次非二分图至少有28个顶点，且该界可达；相应结论可转移到全支配数为1的三次图；任意无三角形3-构型存在阻塞集。

Conclusion: 通过建立det-extremal性与全支配数1的等价，本文解决了三次图全支配数1的刻画问题，推广了McCuaig定理，确定了girth与最小阶数结构，并在构型理论中给出阻塞集存在性。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is det-extremal if $|\operatorname{det} A|=\operatorname{per} A$ for its adjacency matrix $A$. Det-extremal cubic bipartite graphs arise in the study of Pólya's permanent problem, and McCuaig characterized the $3$-connected ones as vertex-sums of copies of the Heawood graph. The total domatic number of a graph is the largest number of pairwise disjoint total dominating sets. Characterization of the cubic graphs with total domatic number $1$ has been a long-standing open problem.
  In this paper, we prove that a connected cubic graph is det-extremal if and only if its total domatic number is $1$. We further show that McCuaig's characterization extends to all $3$-connected cubic graphs, and that every connected det-extremal cubic graph has girth $3$, $5$ or $6$. We also prove that a connected det-extremal cubic non-bipartite graph has at least $28$ vertices, and that this bound is best possible. Through this correspondence, these results carry over to cubic graphs with total domatic number $1$. In addition, in the language of configurations, our results imply that every triangle-free $3$-configuration has a blocking set.

</details>


### [178] [Uniform Factor-Balancedness in Arnoux-Rauzy Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33919)
*Halyna Bowley*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将Arnoux-Rauzy词的均匀因子平衡性刻画从三字母表推广到任意有限字母表，证明Arnoux-Rauzy词均匀因子平衡当且仅当其弱部分商有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Espinoza、Popoli与Stipulanti已在三字母表情形下研究了Arnoux-Rauzy词的均匀因子平衡性，本文希望将该结果推广至任意大小的有限字母表，从而给出更一般的刻画。

Method: 通过引入弱部分商（weak partial quotients）这一工具，对Arnoux-Rauzy词的因子平衡性质进行刻画，将三字母表情形的证明方法推广到任意有限字母表。

Result: 证明了Arnoux-Rauzy词x是均匀因子平衡的，当且仅当x具有有界的弱部分商。

Conclusion: 在任意有限字母表上给出了Arnoux-Rauzy词均匀因子平衡性的充要条件（弱部分商有界），推广了此前三字母表的结果。

Abstract: We characterize uniform factor-balancedness in Arnoux-Rauzy words, generalizing the work of Espinoza, Popoli, and Stipulanti on ternary alphabets to finite alphabets of any size. In particular, we prove that an Arnoux-Rauzy word $\mathbf{x}$ is uniformly factor-balanced if and only if $\mathbf{x}$ has bounded weak partial quotients.

</details>


### [179] [On the (independent) semitotal domination in subdivision, middle, and central graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33932)
*Abel Cabrera-Martínez,José Luis López-Carmona,Ismael Rios-Villamar,Alejandro Serrano-Díaz*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了半全控制集与独立半全控制集，并给出了细分图、中间图和中心图这三种图算子上的闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半全控制集要求集合中每个顶点与集合内另一顶点的距离不超过2，若同时为独立集则称独立半全控制集。该参数在图算子下的精确取值尚未有闭式结果，因此有必要研究细分、中间、中心图上的(独立)半全控制数。

Method: 针对由连通图定义的细分图(subdivision graph)、中间图(middle graph)和中心图(central graph)三种图算子，通过结构分析推导(独立)半全控制数的闭式公式。

Result: 获得了三种图算子(细分图、中间图、中心图)上(独立)半全控制数的闭式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了半全控制数与独立半全控制数在细分图、中间图和中心图中的精确计算公式，丰富了图算子下控制参数的理论结果。

Abstract: A dominating set $D$ of a nontrivial connected graph $G$ is called a semitotal dominating set of $G$ if every vertex in $D$ is at distance at most two from another vertex in $D$. If, in addition, $D$ is an independent set, then $D$ is called an independent semitotal dominating set of $G$. The (independent) semitotal domination number of $G$ is the minimum cardinality among all (independent) semitotal dominating sets of $G$. In this paper, we obtain closed formulas for these parameters in the following three well-known graph operators defined from a connected graph: the subdivision, middle, and central graphs.

</details>


### [180] [Strong edge coloring of graphs with maximum degree $6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33945)
*Runze Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了最大度不超过6的图的强色指数上界为57，改进了已知最好上界60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强边染色要求每个颜色类为诱导匹配，其强色指数的上界是图论中受关注的问题。对于Δ(G)≤6的情形，此前最好上界为60，仍有改进空间。

Method: 通过组合方法对最大度为6的图进行强边染色分析，构造染色方案并给出计数/结构论证，以证明可用57种颜色完成强边染色。

Result: 证明了任意满足Δ(G)≤6的图G均满足χ'_s(G)≤57，将此前最好上界60改进为57。

Conclusion: 对最大度至多为6的图，其强色指数不超过57，推进了该问题的上界研究。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph. Under a strong edge coloring of $G$, every color class is an induced matching. The strong chromatic index of $G$, denoted by $χ'_s(G)$, is the smallest integer $k$ such that $G$ admits a strong edge coloring with $k$ colors. Denote by $Δ(G)$ the maximum degree of $G$. In this paper, we prove that every graph $G$ with $Δ(G)\le 6$ satisfies $χ'_s(G)\le 57$, improving the best known upper bound $60$.

</details>


### [181] [Covering Families for DP-Coloring of Cartesian Products with Complete Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33966)
*Hemanshu Kaul,Jeffrey A. Mudrock,Emily A. Psyhogios,Gunjan Sharma,Illia Siutkin,Aparna Upadhyay*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入"覆盖族"概念，建立 μ(l)=κ(l) 的等价关系，证明了 μ(4)=12，并给出 μ(l) 的新下界与改进上界，同时改进了与完全二部图笛卡尔积相关的 DP-着色问题界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表着色中经典的民间结论表明列表色数与色数的差距可任意大，即 χ_ℓ(K_{l,t})=1+l 当且仅当 t≥l^l。Mudrock 于 2018 年研究其 DP-着色类似物，定义 μ(l) 为满足 χ_DP(K_{l,t})=1+l 的最小 t，并给出上下界。随后 Kaul、Mudrock 和 Sharma 将其推广至 χ_DP(G□K_{l,t})=k+l 的情形。本文旨在为这类 DP-着色问题提供新视角并改进相关界。

Method: 作者引入称为"覆盖族"（covering families）的新概念，用于刻画 [l]^l 的覆盖问题。通过建立 μ(l)=κ(l) 的等价关系（κ(l) 为 [l]^l 覆盖族的最小规模），将 DP-着色问题转化为组合覆盖问题进行分析，并利用该等价关系推导具体数值与一般性上下界。

Result: 证明了 μ(4)=12；获得了 μ(l) 的新的一般下界；证明了一个关于覆盖族最小规模的一般上界，由此得到 μ(l) 的改进上界，并改进了涉及与完全二部图笛卡尔积的相关 DP-着色问题的已知界。

Conclusion: 覆盖族概念为 DP-着色中关于完全二部图及其笛卡尔积的问题提供了统一而有效的框架，μ(l)=κ(l) 的等价关系不仅确定了 μ(4)，还系统性地改进了一般上下界，展示了该组合视角在此类问题中的价值。

Abstract: A famous folklore result in list coloring demonstrating that the gap between the list chromatic number and chromatic number of a graph can be arbitrarily large is: $χ_{\ell}(K_{l,t}) = 1+l$ if and only if $t \geq l^l$. DP-coloring (also called correspondence coloring) is a well-studied generalization of list coloring introduced in 2015. In 2018, Mudrock studied the DP analogue of the aforementioned folklore result. He proved that for $l \in\mathbb{N}$, if $μ(l)$ is the smallest integer $t$ such that $χ_{DP}(K_{l,t})=1+l$, then $\left\lceil l^l/l!\right\rceil \leq μ(l) \leq 1+l^l(\log(l!)+1)/l!$. Recently, Kaul, Mudrock, and Sharma studied a more general version of this problem by studying the smallest $t$ for which $χ_{DP}(G \square K_{l,t}) = k + l$, where $G$ satisfies certain criticality conditions and $G \square K_{l,t}$ denotes the Cartesian product of $G$ and $K_{l,t}$. In this paper, we introduce a notion we call covering families that gives a new perspective on these DP-coloring questions. In particular, if $κ(l)$ denotes the minimum size of a covering family of $[l]^l$, we show that $μ(l)=κ(l)$. We use this equivalence to prove $μ(4)=12$ and to obtain new general lower bounds on $μ(l)$. We also prove a general upper bound on the minimum size of covering families which yields an improved general upper bound on $μ(l)$ and gives improvements on known bounds for related DP-coloring questions involving Cartesian products with complete bipartite graphs.

</details>


### [182] [Edge-Defect Spectral Methods for Higher-Order Rankings of Spanning Tree Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33979)
*Shunya Tamura,José Luis Palacios*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了从完全图中删除固定数量边后所得图的生成树数目的高阶排序问题，通过边缺陷矩阵和交互数推导稳定性不等式，并结合删除图分类，确定了在特定参数范围内生成树数目前九大的删除图（同构意义下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成树计数是图论中的经典问题，但针对从完全图删除固定数量边所得图的生成树数目的高阶排序（即哪些删除方式对应最大生成树数）尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 引入边缺陷矩阵（由被删除边确定）和交互数（度量删除边之间的局部重叠程度），推导稳定性不等式以定量估计相对于匹配删除情形的生成树数目下降量；结合对低交互数删除图的分类，进行同构意义下的枚举与比较。

Result: 确定了在 $p\geq6$ 且 $n\geq2p$ 条件下，生成树数目前九大的删除图（同构意义下）；并通过归一化生成树数目的对数展开，阐明了交互数、删除图局部结构与生成树数目下降量之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了交互数与生成树数目下降之间的定量联系，为高阶生成树排序提供了系统工具，并揭示了删除图局部结构对生成树计数的影响机制。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the higher-order ranking, by spanning-tree count, of graphs obtained from a complete graph by deleting a fixed number of edges. Using the edge-defect matrix determined by the deleted edges and the interaction number measuring the local overlap among them, we derive a stability inequality that quantitatively estimates the decrease in the number of spanning trees from the matching-deletion case. Combining this stability estimate with a classification of deletion graphs having small interaction number, we determine, up to isomorphism, the nine deletion graphs with the largest spanning-tree counts for $p\geq6$ and $n\geq2p$. We also clarify the relation between the interaction number, the local structure of the deletion graph, and the decrease in the number of spanning trees through a logarithmic expansion of the normalized spanning-tree count.

</details>


### [183] [Strong Edge Colouring of Disk Graphs: A 6-Approximation and an Improved Unit-Disk Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34023)
*Sandip Das,Sk Samim Islam,Aashirwad Mohapatra,Sangita Saha,Saumya Sen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将单位圆图上的强边染色6-近似推广到盘图，并把单位圆图强色指数的渐近上界主系数从1.625改进到225/142≈1.5845。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强边染色是边染色中每色类为诱导匹配的经典问题。已知单位圆图有6-近似，而Erdős–Nešetřil猜想最大度为Δ时强色指数渐近不超过1.25Δ²，一般图上最好上界主系数为1.772，单位圆图上为1.625。作者希望把近似保证扩展到更一般的盘图，并降低单位圆图的上界主系数。

Method: 运用组合与概率方法分析盘图和单位圆图的强边染色及强列表染色，给出近似算法并证明渐近上界。

Result: 第一，将强边染色的因子6近似从单位圆图推广到盘图。第二，把单位圆图强色指数上界的主系数从1.625改进为225/142≈1.5845，并证明更强的χ'_{s,ℓ}(G)≤225/142·Δ²+O(Δ)。

Conclusion: 盘图上存在因子6的强边染色近似；单位圆图的强列表色指数满足χ'_{s,ℓ}(G)≤225/142·Δ²+O(Δ)，从而强色指数上界主系数改进为225/142。

Abstract: A strong edge colouring of a graph $G$ is an edge colouring in which every colour class is an induced matching. The minimum number of colours is the strong chromatic index $χ'_s(G)$. If each edge $e$ is assigned a list $L'(e)$ and its colour must belong to $L'(e)$, the corresponding parameter is the strong list chromatic index $χ'_{s,\ell}(G)$. From the definitions, $χ'_s(G)\leχ'_{s,\ell}(G)$.
  Barrett et al. gave an $8$-approximation for strong edge colouring on unit disk graphs and Grelier et al. improved the approximation factor to $6$. Our first result extends this factor-$6$ guarantee from unit disk graphs to the strictly larger class of disk graphs.
  In another direction, Erdős and Nešetřil conjectured that the strong chromatic index of a graph of maximum degree $Δ$ is asymptotically at most $1.25Δ^2$. The best published general asymptotic upper bound has leading coefficient $1.772$, due to Hurley et al. For unit disk graphs, Dębski et al. proved that $χ'_s(G) \leq 1.625 Δ^2$. Our second result improves this leading coefficient to $225/142 \approx 1.5845$. In fact, the proof establishes a stronger bound $χ'_{s,\ell}(G)\le\frac{225}{142} Δ^2+O(Δ)$ for unit disk graphs.

</details>


### [184] [Matching algebras and their Dunkl subalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34260)
*Darij Grinberg,Anatol Kirillov,Boris Shapiro*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入了匹配代数 $\mathcal M(G)$ 及其子代数 Dunkl 匹配代数 $\mathcal D(G)$，证明 $\mathcal D(G)$ 作为分次向量空间对偶于所有匹配多项式 $p_M$ 的张成空间，并研究了相关的 Specht 模饱和问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图的匹配结构与对称群表示论（Specht 模）之间的联系，量化匹配多项式何时能张成完整的两行 Specht 模，并刻画 Dunkl 匹配代数与完整匹配代数相等的情形。

Method: 构造多项式环对非相邻边乘积理想的商代数 $\mathcal M(G)$，在其中由带符号关联和 $\theta_v$ 生成子代数 $\mathcal D(G)$；通过分次对偶分析、Hilbert 级数计算以及图连通性（$k$-连通、$k$-linked）条件来研究饱和问题，并结合组合构造证明各种图类的性质。

Result: 证明 $\mathcal D(G)$ 与匹配多项式 $p_M$ 的张成空间分次对偶；对完全图 $K_n$ 该张成空间是两行 Specht 模的直和，Hilbert 级数为 Catalan 三角形，特征零下 $\mathcal D(K_n)$ 可由线性和二次关系表示；饱和性在度 $k$ 蕴含 $k$-连通，度 $2$ 饱和等价于 $2$-连通，$k$-linked 图在度 $k$ 饱和；补图是匹配时所有度均饱和；$\mathcal D(G)=\mathcal M(G)$ 当且仅当 $G$ 是森林，并给出单圈图的 Hilbert 级数公式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了匹配代数与 Dunkl 型算子代数的对偶框架，将匹配多项式的张成问题与图的连通性、Specht 模表示论联系起来，给出了饱和性的图论刻画及若干图类的精确 Hilbert 级数，为匹配组合学与代数表示论的交叉研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: For a finite simple graph $G = (V, E)$, we consider the quotient algebra $\mathcal M(G)$ of the polynomial ring in the variables $u_e$ (for $e \in E$) by the ideal generated by all products $u_e u_f$ for non-disjoint edges $e$ and $f$ (including all squares $u_e^2$). This quotient is called the matching algebra of $G$, since it has a basis indexed by the matchings of $G$. In this quotient, we define a subalgebra $\mathcal D(G)$ generated by the signed incidence sums $θ_v=\sum_{e=(v,w)}u_e-\sum_{e=(w,v)}u_e$ for all $v \in V$ (where all edges of $G$ are oriented arbitrarily); we call this the Dunkl matching algebra.
  We show that, as a graded vector space, $\mathcal D(G)$ is dual to the span of all polynomials $p_M = \prod_{(i,j) \in M} (x_i - x_j)$, where $M$ ranges over all matchings of $G$. For the complete graph $K_n$, the latter span is a direct sum of two-row Specht modules (one in each degree); thus its Hilbert series is that of the Catalan triangle, and, in characteristic zero, the Dunkl matching algebra can be presented by linear and quadratic relations. For arbitrary graphs, we formulate the saturation problem of deciding when the selected matching Specht generators $p_M$ in a given degree $k$ span the full two-row Specht module $S^{(n-k,k)}$. We show that saturation in degree $k$ forces $k$-connectivity, that saturation in degree $2$ is equivalent to $2$-connectivity, and that $k$-linked graphs are saturated in degree $k$. We also prove saturation in every possible degree whenever the complement of $G$ is a matching. We show that the Dunkl matching algebra equals the full matching algebra exactly for forests, and give an explicit Hilbert series formula for unicyclic graphs.

</details>


### [185] [Tight bounds on the Carathéodory and exchange numbers in $\triangle$-convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34441)
*Vishnu Kumar,Brahadeesh Sankarnarayanan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文指出Anand等人关于△-凸性空间中Carathéodory数与exchange数上界证明存在漏洞，通过新路径完整证明了c_△(G) ≤ t(G)+1与e_△(G) ≤ t(G)+2，并刻画了极值图，进而修正了块图的Carathéodory数和exchange数计算，且确定了k-树（k≥2）的exchange数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Anand等人此前证明了c_△(G) ≤ t(G)+1和e_△(G) ≤ t(G)+2并称界是紧的，但其第一个不等式证明存在漏洞，且该漏洞影响第二个不等式的证明。此外，紧性结果被误用为极值图的刻画，导致某些块图的Carathéodory数和exchange数计算错误。本文旨在修补这些漏洞并给出正确结果。

Method: 采用与Anand等人不同的新路径，给出c_△(G) ≤ t(G)+1的完整证明；结合Anand等人的论证补全第二个不等式e_△(G) ≤ t(G)+2的证明；并由此导出两个界的极值图刻画。利用该刻画重新计算块图的Carathéodory数和exchange数、识别极值块图，并确定k-树（k≥2）的exchange数。

Result: 给出了c_△(G) ≤ t(G)+1的完整证明，补全了e_△(G) ≤ t(G)+2的证明；得到了两个界的极值图刻画；修正了块图的Carathéodory数和exchange数计算；精确确定了所有k-树（k≥2）的exchange数e_△(G)。

Conclusion: 本文修补了Anand等人证明中的漏洞，并纠正了因误用紧性结果而导致的块图计算错误，同时给出了极值图刻画，并将结果推广到k-树的exchange数。

Abstract: The $\triangle$-convexity space on a finite, simple graph $G = (V,E)$ is the collection $\mathcal{C}$ of subsets $S \subseteq V(G)$ such that whenever $x \in V(G)$ forms a triangle with two vertices in $S$, we have $x \in S$. The members of $\mathcal{C}$ are called convex sets, and the convex hull of a set $S \subseteq V(G)$, denoted $\operatorname{Hull}(S)$, is the smallest member of $\mathcal{C}$ that contains $S$. The Carathéodory (resp., exchange) number, $c_{\triangle}(G)$ (resp., $e_{\triangle}(G)$), is the size of a largest Carathéodory (resp., exchange) independent subset of $V(G)$. It was shown by Anand et al. (JCMCC 126, 2025, 11--27) that $c_{\triangle}(G) \leq t(G)+1$ and $e_{\triangle}(G) \leq t(G) + 2$, where $t(G)$ is the number of triangles in $G$, and that these bounds are tight. They also computed $c_{\triangle}(G)$ and $e_{\triangle}(G)$ for a block graph $G$ in terms of the number and arrangement of non-$K_2$ blocks in $G$.
  In this paper, we point out a gap in the proof in Anand et al. of the first inequality, $c_{\triangle}(G) \leq t(G)+1$, which has consequences for the proof of the second inequality, $e_{\triangle}(G) \leq t(G) + 2$, as well. Moreover, the tightness results are inadvertently applied as characterizations of the extremal graphs, leading to incorrect computations of the Carathéodory and exchange numbers of block graphs in certain cases. We fix these gaps by giving a full proof of the first inequality via a different route from that in Anand et al. Together with the argument in Anand et al., this also completes the proof of the second inequality. Our proof also leads to a characterization of the extremal graphs for each bound, which we use to compute the Carathéodory and exchange numbers of block graphs and to identify the extremal block graphs. We also determine $e_{\triangle}(G)$ exactly for $k$-trees for every $k \geq 2$.

</details>


### [186] [Three Standard Deviations Suffice While One Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34471)
*Victor Reis,Zhao Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文改进了Spencer的"六倍标准差"定理的上界常数，从6√n降至约2.9992√n+4；同时首次突破了Olson和Spencer（1978）建立的√n下界，构造出使‖Ax‖∞>1.0000002√n的矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spencer (1985) 证明任意 A∈[-1,1]^{n×n} 存在符号向量 x 使 ‖Ax‖∞≤6√n，但上界常数6与已知下界√n之间存在较大间隙。作者旨在缩小这一差距：一方面改进上界常数，另一方面寻求下界的首次突破。下界自 Olson 和 Spencer (1978) 基于 Hadamard 矩阵的 √n 结果以来长期未获改进。

Method: 上界方面：直接对某一势函数的极小值点进行舍入（rounding），将其映射到立方体 {-1,1}^n 的顶点，从而得到 ‖Ax‖∞≤√(3·arsinh(10))·√n+4。下界方面：从 Hadamard 矩阵出发，将其 2^{-22} 比例的列替换为独立的随机符号向量，构造出 ‖Ax‖∞>1.0000002√n 的矩阵 A∈{-1,1}^{n×n}（n 为 2 的幂且 n≥2^50）。

Result: 上界：证明了 ‖Ax‖∞ ≤ √(3·arsinh(10))·√n + 4 < 2.9992√n + 4，改进了 Spencer 的常数6。下界：证明对每个 2 的幂 n≥2^50，存在 A∈{-1,1}^{n×n} 使得对任意符号向量 x∈{-1,1}^n 都有 ‖Ax‖∞>1.0000002√n，首次突破 Olson-Spencer (1978) 的 √n 下界。

Conclusion: 该工作同时收紧了Spencer定理的上下界：上界常数由6降至约2.9992，下界首次超过√n。所采用的方法——势函数极小值点的直接舍入与 Hadamard 矩阵列的随机扰动——为差异理论中这一经典问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Spencer's 1985 ``six standard deviations suffice'' theorem shows that every $A \in [-1,1]^{n \times n}$ has a sign vector $x \in \{-1,1\}^n$ with $\|Ax\|_\infty \le 6\sqrt{n}$. We show the upper bound $\sqrt{3\operatorname{arsinh}(10)}\sqrt{n}+4 < 2.9992 \sqrt{n} + 4$ by directly rounding the minimizer of a potential function to a vertex of the cube.
  We also show that, for every power of two $n \ge 2^{50}$, there exists a matrix $A \in \{-1,1\}^{n \times n}$ such that $\|Ax\|_\infty >1.0000002\sqrt{n}$ for every choice of signs $x \in \{-1,1\}^n$. The construction simply replaces a $2^{-22}$ fraction of the columns of a Hadamard matrix with independent random sign vectors. This is the first improvement over the $\sqrt{n}$ lower bound of Olson and Spencer (1978), which uses a Hadamard matrix.

</details>


### [187] [Interlacing on the Unit Circle via Coefficientwise Reciprocals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34551)
*Jianxi Mao,Lijie Wang,Sainan Zheng*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：若p、q分别是次数为n和n+1的仅含负实零点的回文多项式，则它们的系数倒数多项式零点都是单零点，且p#在单位圆上严格交错q#。方法基于有限Blaschke积与边界相位比较，推广到二项式、Eulerian、Narayana及Rogers–Szegő等多项式族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知仅含负实零点的回文多项式f，其系数倒数f#的全部零点位于单位圆上。本文希望进一步刻画不同次数此类多项式系数倒数之间的零点位置关系（交错性），并寻求可构造严格交错多项式族的统一框架。

Method: 将系数倒数多项式与有限Blaschke积相联系，通过比较其边界相位（boundary phases）来建立零点交错关系；并利用γ-系数的符号变化结合系数取倒数操作构造新的严格交错多项式族。

Result: 证明p#与q#的零点均为单零点，且p#在单位圆上严格交错q#；由此得到倒数二项式、倒数Eulerian多项式、倒数Narayana多项式的严格交错性；并构造了来自Rogers–Szegő、Poupard及Kreweras相关多项式的严格交错族。

Conclusion: 结果把“系数倒数多项式零点在单位圆上”的已知结论提升为跨次数的严格交错定理，并给出了构造严格交错多项式族的系统方法，统一覆盖多个经典组合多项式族。

Abstract: Let $f(z)=\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_kz^k$ be a polynomial with positive coefficients, and define its coefficientwise reciprocal by $f^{\#}(z)=\sum_{k=0}^{n}\frac{z^k}{a_k}.$ It is known that if $f$ is palindromic and has only negative real zeros, then all zeros of $f^{\#}$ lie on the unit circle. We prove that if $p$ and $q$ are palindromic polynomials of degrees $n$ and $n+1$, respectively, with only negative real zeros, then their coefficientwise reciprocals have only simple zeros, and $p^{\#}$ strictly interlaces $q^{\#}$ on the unit circle. Our proof is based on finite Blaschke products and the comparison of their boundary phases. As an immediate consequence, we obtain strict interlacing for the reciprocal binomial, reciprocal Eulerian, and reciprocal Narayana polynomials. By combining a sign change in the $γ$-coefficients with coefficientwise reciprocation, we further construct strictly interlacing families from Rogers--Szegő, Poupard, and Kreweras-related polynomials.

</details>


### [188] [A Turán-type extremal problem for the number of spanning trees in $C_4$-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34616)
*Shaohan Xu,Fengming Dong,Kexiang Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了在 $n=q^2+q+1$ 个顶点、边数不超过 $\frac12 q(q+1)^2$ 的 $C_4$-free 图中，生成树数目 $\tau(G)\le n^{(n-3)/2}$，且该上界在 $q\notin\{7,9,11,13\}$ 的素数幂情形恰好由正交极性图达到，从而证明了 London 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Turán 数 $\ex(n,C_4)$ 及其极值图（正交极性图等）已有经典结果，但关于极值图生成树数目 $\tau(G)$ 的刻画仍缺失。London 猜想希望确定达到最大生成树数目的 $C_4$-free 图，本文旨在解决该猜想。

Method: 通过证明一般性上界：任意 $n=q^2+q+1$ 个顶点、至多 $\frac12 q(q+1)^2$ 条边的 $C_4$-free 图满足 $\tau(G)\le n^{(n-3)/2}$，并逐项分析极值情形，确定等号成立的条件。

Result: 证明了 $\tau(G)\le n^{(n-3)/2}$；并证明当 $q\notin\{7,9,11,13\}$ 为素数幂时，等号仅由正交极性图取得，从而验证 London 猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将 $C_4$-free 图的边极值与生成树极值统一起来，对除少数小 $q$ 外的所有素数幂解决了 London 猜想，并为极值图的结构刻画提供了新工具。

Abstract: For a graph \(F\), the Turán number \(\ex(n,F)\) is the maximum number of edges in an \(F\)-free graph on \(n\) vertices. Let \(q\ge 2\) be an integer and set \(n=q^{2}+q+1\). Brown and Erdős, Rényi and Sós independently proved that $\ex(n,C_{4})\ge \frac12 q(q+1)^{2}$ for every prime power \(q\), and Füredi subsequently established the upper bound $\frac12 q(q+1)^{2}$ for $\ex(n,C_{4})$ whenever \(q\notin\{1, 7,9,11,13\}\). In this article, we prove that every \(C_{4}\)-free graph \(G\) on \(n\) vertices with at most \(\frac12 q(q+1)^{2}\) edges satisfies $τ(G)\le n^{(n-3)/2}$, where \(τ(G)\) denotes the number of spanning trees of \(G\). In particular, for every prime power $q\notin\{7,9,11,13\}$, the above upper bound on $τ(G)$ is attained precisely by the orthogonal polarity graphs, thereby proving London's conjecture for all such $q$.

</details>


### [189] [Recognizability of Being Point Determining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34775)
*M. H. Shirdareh Haghighi,A. Namazi,Z Rahimi,A. M. Ghazanfari*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明"点确定"性质（任意两顶点邻域不同）可由图的牌组(deck)重构识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点确定图是重构问题中一类具有特殊结构的图类，判定该性质是否属于可由牌组识别的图性质，有助于推进重构猜想相关研究。

Method: 采用图重构理论中的牌组(deck，即去掉单个顶点所得的所有生成子图之多重集)分析，证明可从牌组判定图是否为点确定图。

Result: 点确定性(point determining)性质是可识别的(recognizable)，即给定任意图的牌组即可判断原图是否具有该性质。

Conclusion: 点确定性质是可由牌组识别的图性质，为进一步刻画可重构/可识别性质提供了新的实例。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is point determining, if no two vertices have the same neighborhoods. In this paper, we show that this property is recognizable from the deck of cards of a graph.

</details>


### [190] [Optimization hierarchies for extremal geometry through complete positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34845)
*Bram Bekker*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本论文将完全正函数框架扩展至有限测度空间问题与球堆积问题，构建并证明了若干半定规划层级收敛到最优密度，并对Witsenhausen问题与t-几乎等角集问题进行了数值实现，其中后者在n=2,3且t≥0时给出解析解与最优构造枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全正函数是完全正矩阵的推广，已知可刻画极大球面码以及R^n和某些紧致度量空间中的最大密度避距子集。作者希望将这一框架推广到更广泛的问题类，包括有限测度空间中的相关问题与球堆积问题，并借助半定规划层级获得可计算的界。

Method: 基于完全正函数的逼近与图的Lovász theta数，构建并扩展收敛的半定规划界层级：将原有球面码层级推广到避距集问题与球堆积问题；对避距集引入矩层级等额外层级，并证明其强于完全正层级因而同样收敛；对紧致堆积问题研究相关层级。对球堆积情形，将最优堆积密度用Schwartz函数逼近。最后对Witsenhausen问题和t-几乎等角集问题进行数值实现与解析求解。

Result: 证明了所提出的半定规划层级收敛到最大密度；矩层级强于完全正层级并同样收敛；球堆积最优密度可由Schwartz函数逼近。数值实现给出Witsenhausen问题中最大比例α_n的界，以及t-几乎等角集最大规模α(n,t)的界；对t-几乎等角集问题的界进行解析求解，得到n=2和3且t≥0时最优构造的枚举。

Conclusion: 完全正函数框架可成功推广到有限测度空间与球堆积等更广问题，半定规划层级提供收敛且可计算的最优密度上界，并在具体极值几何问题中获得解析结果与最优构造刻画。

Abstract: Completely positive functions are an extension of completely positive matrices. They are known to characterize maximal spherical codes and maximum-density distance-avoiding subsets of $\mathbb{R}^n$ and certain compact metric spaces. This thesis expands this framework to related classes of problems in finite measure spaces and to the sphere-packing problem. For the latter, this is sharpened to show that the optimal sphere-packing density can be approximated using Schwartz functions.
  Converging hierarchies of semidefinite programming bounds on the size of optimal spherical codes are known, based on approximations of completely positive functions and the Lovász theta number of a graph. This thesis extends these hierarchies to distance-avoiding sets and similar problems and to the sphere-packing problem, and proves their convergence to the maximum density. For distance-avoiding sets, additional hierarchies, such as the moment hierarchy, are introduced and shown to be stronger than the completely positive hierarchy, hence they also converge. Related hierarchies for compact packing problems are also investigate.
  These bounds are implemented for Witsenhausen's problem, which asks for the maximum fraction $α_n$ of the $n$-dimensional unit sphere that is coverable by a set avoiding orthogonal pairs; and for the $t$-almost-equiangular-set problem: finding the maximum size $α(n,t)$ of a subset of the $n$-dimensional unit sphere in which every triple contains a pair with inner product $t \in [-1,1)$. An analytic solution to this bound yields an enumeration of optimal constructions for $n = 2$ and $3$ when $t \geq 0$.

</details>


### [191] [On rainbow saturated graphs with minimum number of edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34898)
*Yanzhe Qiu,Mei Lu,Yilin Pan,Yiduo Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究彩虹饱和数 rsat(n,F)，证明由孤立边支配的二分法：含孤立边时 rsat(n,F)=O(1)，不含孤立边时 rsat(n,F)=Θ(n)，并给出广义友谊图的渐近紧结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩虹饱和数 rsat(n,F) 是边染色图中介于饱和数与极值数之间的参数，现有结果多集中于特定图类，缺乏对一般图 F 的整体刻画。本文旨在为一般图 F 建立 rsat(n,F) 的渐近阶二分法，并刻画线性下界的系数。

Method: 1) 引入有向权重参数 η(F) 来刻画线性下界；2) 对含孤立边的情形，针对 F=H∪K_2 构造彩虹饱和图，得到常数上界；3) 对不含孤立边的情形，证明 Θ(n) 的线性下界，并改进 Cameron–Puleo 型系数；4) 将构造应用于广义友谊图 F_{t,p,q}=tK_p∨K_q。

Result: 证明二分法成立：F 含孤立边时 rsat(n,F)=O(1)（充分大 n）；F 不含孤立边时 rsat(n,F)=Θ(n)，线性下界由 η(F) 表出，且在若干情形改进了 Cameron–Puleo 型系数。对广义友谊图给出 rsat(n,F_{t,p,q})=(p+q-1)n+O(1)（固定 t≥2, p≥2, q≥1, n→∞）。

Conclusion: 孤立边的存在与否决定了 rsat(n,F) 的常数阶与线性阶二分行为；有向权重参数 η(F) 是刻画线性下界的关键工具，所提出的构造方法可推广应用于广义友谊图等目标，得到渐近紧的彩虹饱和数。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a fixed graph without isolated vertices. An edge-colored graph is $F$-rainbow saturated if it contains no rainbow copy of $F$, but the addition of any missing edge in any color creates a rainbow copy of $F$. The rainbow saturation number $rsat(n,F)$ is the minimum number of edges in such a graph on $n$ vertices. We prove a dichotomy governed by isolated edges: if $F$ contains an isolated edge, then $rsat(n,F)=O(1)$ for all sufficiently large $n$, while if $F$ has no isolated edge, then $rsat(n,F)=Θ(n)$. The linear lower bound is expressed in terms of a directed weight parameter $η(F)$ and establishes the linear half of the dichotomy; in several cases it also strengthens the Cameron--Puleo type coefficient. For the bounded half, we construct rainbow saturated graphs for targets of the form $H\cup K_2$.
