As an application of these constructions, we determine the asymptotically tight behavior for the rainbow saturation number of the generalized friendship graph $F_{t,p,q}=tK_p\vee K_q$, proving that $ rsat(n,F_{t,p,q})=(p+q-1)n+O(1)$ for fixed $t\geq 2$, $p\geq 2$ and $q\geq 1$ as $n\to\infty$.

</details>


### [192] [Polynomially superlinear growth of set-coloring Ramsey numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35079)
*Qizhong Lin,Lin Niu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了无穷多个三元组 (r,j,s)，使得集合染色 Ramsey 数 R(q+1;r,s)=Θ_q(r^{4/3})，其中 j~(q-1)^{-2/3}r^{1/3}, s=(1-1/q)(r-j)。对 q=3 回答了 Conlon、Fox、Pham、Zhao 的公开问题，并确定了相应 q 元码的最大规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究集合染色 Ramsey 数 R(k;r,s) 的增长阶，特别是超线性（多项式）增长的临界尺度问题。Conlon、Fox、Pham 和 Zhao 曾提出：对于 R(4;r,2(r-j)/3)，多项式超线性增长是否已在 j=Θ(r^{1/3}) 这一尺度上发生。本文旨在正面回答该问题，并将其推广到一般素数幂 q。

Method: 对每个固定素数幂 q，显式构造无穷多个正整数三元组 (r,j,s)，满足 j~(q-1)^{-2/3}r^{1/3} 与 s=(1-1/q)(r-j)。构造与有限域/代数几何或纠错码的编码理论相联系，通过对 q 元码长度 r、最小 Hamming 距离至少 s 的最大规模给出渐进估计，进而导出 R(q+1;r,s) 的上下界。

Result: 证明了 R(q+1;r,s)=Θ_q(r^{4/3})，沿所构造的序列成立。当 q=3 时，给出 R(4;r,2(r-j)/3) 在 j=Θ(r^{1/3}) 尺度上即出现多项式超线性增长的结论，正面回答 Conlon 等人的问题。此外，同一序列上 q 元码的最大规模为 (1+o(1))(q-1)^{4/3}r^{4/3}。

Conclusion: 集合染色 Ramsey 数可以早在较小的 j（即接近满染色 s 接近 r 的情形下，仅偏离 O(r^{1/3})）就呈现 r^{4/3} 阶的多项式超线性增长，而非仅在 j 线性尺度下出现；同时该结果与极值编码理论通过 q 元码的精确渐进规模紧密对应，统一并推进了 Ramsey 理论与编码理论中的相关极值问题。

Abstract: The set-coloring Ramsey number $R(k;r,s)$ is the least $N$ such that every assignment of an $s$-element subset of $[r]$ to each edge of $K_N$ yields a copy of $K_k$ whose edges share a common color. For every fixed prime power $q$, we construct infinitely many positive integer triples $(r,j,s)$ with $j\sim(q-1)^{-2/3}r^{1/3}$ and $s=(1-1/q)(r-j)$ such that $R(q+1;r,s)=Θ_q(r^{4/3})$. For $q=3$, this answers in the affirmative a question of Conlon, Fox, Pham and Zhao, showing that polynomially superlinear growth for $R(4;r,2(r-j)/3)$ already occurs at the scale \(j=Θ(r^{1/3})\). Moreover, along the same sequence, the maximum size of a $q$-ary code of length $r$ and minimum Hamming distance at least $s$ is $(1+o(1))(q-1)^{4/3}r^{4/3}$.

</details>


### [193] [Fractional Dichromatic Number and Domination in Tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35186)
*Paul Colinot,Alantha Newman*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出两个新证明，表明竞赛图的支配数可由其分数二色数界定：第一个证明归约到majority竞赛图支配数问题，得到与[BCT25]相同的指数界；第二个证明（借助AI获得）给出拟线性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bourneuf、Charbit和Thomassé [BCT25]证明竞赛图的支配数可由其分数二色数的函数界定，但所证函数为指数级，且工具基于VC维。本文旨在提供新的证明途径，并改进界的形式。

Method: 第一个证明：将问题归约到(1/2-ε)-majority竞赛图支配数的界定问题，该问题已有紧界[BCT25, CRW26]。第二个证明：受Ramakrishnan [Ram26]关于大小为5的Condorcet Winning Set存在性的书本证明启发，通过AI获得拟线性界。

Result: 第一个证明得到与[BCT25]相同的支配数指数界；第二个证明给出支配数关于分数二色数的拟线性界。

Conclusion: 本文为竞赛图支配数受分数二色数界定这一结论提供了两种新证明，其中一种将界从指数级改进为拟线性级。

Abstract: Bourneuf, Charbit and Thomassé [BCT25] showed that the domination number of a tournament can be bounded as a function of its fractional dichromatic number. The function proved was exponential and the tools were based on VC-dimension. In this paper, we present two new proofs of this theorem.
  The first proof is based on a reduction to the problem of bounding the domination number of a $(1/2-ε)$-majority tournament, for which [BCT25] and Charikar, Ramakrishnan and Wang [CRW26] gave tight bounds. This proof yields the same exponential bound on the domination number as in [BCT25]. The second proof gives a quasilinear bound for the domination in terms of the fractional dichromatic number. It was obtained via AI and was inspired by the recent book proof of the existence of a Condorcet Winning Set of size five due to Ramakrishnan [Ram26].

</details>


### [194] [Antimagicness of join graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35245)
*Grégoire Beaudoire,Cédric Bentz,Christophe Picouleau*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hartsfield和Ringel提出的著名猜想——除K₂外的所有连通图都是反魔图的——对于至少三个顶点的join图成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hartsfield和Ringel在1990年提出的反魔图猜想是图论中的一个著名公开问题，即除K₂外的所有连通图都是反魔图。该猜想至今未被完全解决，因此针对特定图类（如join图）验证该猜想具有重要价值。

Method: 论文采用反魔标号（antimagic labelling）的方法，即为图G=(V,E)的边分配1到|E|的双射，使得所有顶点的顶点和（关联边的标号之和）两两不同。作者针对至少三个顶点的join图构造了这样的反魔标号，从而证明其反魔性。

Result: 作者成功证明了对于至少三个顶点的join图，Hartsfield-Ringel反魔图猜想成立，即这些join图都是反魔图。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了反魔图猜想的验证工作，证明了一类重要的图（join图）满足该猜想，为最终解决这一著名猜想提供了新的进展。

Abstract: An antimagic labelling of a graph G = (V,E) is a bijection from E to {1,2,...,|E|}, such that all vertex-sums are pairwise distinct, where the vertex-sum of each vertex is the sum of labels over edges incident to this vertex. A graph is antimagic if it has an antimagic labelling. Hartsfield and Ringel in 1990 stated the celebrated conjecture: Every connected graph other than K_2 is antimagic. We prove the conjecture for join graphs with at least three vertices.

</details>


### [195] [Disproof of the dominating Hadwiger conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35361)
*Freddie Illingworth,Raphael Steiner*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出对支配Hadwiger猜想的反驳阐述，构造为基于Suzuki-Tits ovoid的伪随机无三角形图的补图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支配K_t-model是Hadwiger猜想的显著强化；Illingworth与Wood (2024) 提出是否每个色数至少为t的图都含有支配K_t-model，即支配Hadwiger猜想，本文旨在对此作出反驳。

Method: 对ChatGPT 6 Astra Ultra给出的反例进行重新阐述；构造方式为：取基于Suzuki-Tits ovoid的块几何图，随机子采样得到伪随机无三角形图，再取其补图。

Result: 该构造构成支配Hadwiger猜想的反例，说明色数至少为t并不蕴含支配K_t-model的存在。

Conclusion: 支配Hadwiger猜想不成立，文中提供了该反例的独立阐述。

Abstract: Hadwiger's conjecture (1943) states that every graph $G$ with chromatic number at least $t$ contains a $K_t$-model: a collection of $t$ vertex-disjoint connected subgraphs $T_1,\dots,T_t$ such that for all $1\le i<j\le t$ some vertex in $T_j$ has a neighbour in $T_i$. Replacing "some" in this definition by "every" gives rise to the significantly stronger notion of a dominating $K_t$-model introduced by Illingworth and Wood (2024). They raised the question whether every graph of chromatic number at least $t$ contains a dominating $K_t$-model. This statement is a significant strengthening of Hadwiger's conjecture and has come to be known as the dominating Hadwiger conjecture.
  We provide our own exposition of a disproof of this conjecture found by ChatGPT 6 Astra Ultra. The construction is the complement of a pseudorandom triangle-free graph that is obtained by randomly subsampling a block geometric graph based on the Suzuki-Tits ovoid.

</details>


### [196] [Maximizing the discrepancy between zero forcing parameters relative to graph order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35522)
*Andrew McKay,Allie Ray,Jonathan Valliere*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究零强迫过程中不同参数相对于图的阶的最大差异，给出了最小零强迫集的最大与最小基数之差的上界，并证明该界在一无限图族上可达；同时推导了最小零强迫集最大与最小传播时间之差的界，并证明其可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零强迫参数（如零强迫集的大小与传播时间）之间存在差异，量化这些差异相对于图阶的极值行为有助于理解零强迫过程的本质。本文旨在最大化不同零强迫参数之间的差异。

Method: 以图论中零强迫的变色规则为框架，研究最小零强迫集（不含真零强迫子集的零强迫集）的最大与最小基数之差；进一步分析最小零强迫集的最大与最小传播时间之差，并通过构造无限图族验证界的可达性。

Result: 得到最小零强迫集最大与最小基数之差的上界，并证明该上界对某无限图族是紧的；同时给出任意图的最小零强迫集最大与最小传播时间之差的上界，并证明该界对某无限图族也是紧的。

Conclusion: 零强迫参数之间的差异相对于图阶存在精确的上界，且这些界可由特定无限图族达到，从而完整刻画了此类差异的极值行为。

Abstract: Zero forcing is a process described by a color change rule on the vertices of a graph. In this paper, we maximize the discrepancy between various zero forcing parameters relative to graph order. First, we find an upper bound on the difference in cardinality between minimal zero forcing sets (sets containing no proper zero forcing subset) of maximum and minimum size, and we show that this bound is sharp for an infinite family of graphs. Furthermore, we derive an upper bound for the discrepancy between the maximum and minimum propagation times of the minimum zero forcing sets of any graph, showing this bound is sharp for an infinite family of graphs.

</details>


### [197] [Nonnegativity of the $g$-polynomial of split matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35550)
*Alice L. L. Gao,Matthew H. Y. Xie*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明所有 split matroid 的 g-多项式系数非负，验证了 Speyer 猜想的一个收缩封闭类（含 paving 与 copaving matroids），核心工具是一条删除—收缩恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Speyer 猜想断言所有 matroid 的 g-多项式系数非负，但一般情形未解。作者希望在一个性质良好、对取 minor 封闭的类上验证该猜想，从而扩展已知结果范围。

Method: 先证明每个简单、余简单、连通的 split matroid M 存在元素 e，使删除 M\e 与收缩 M/e 均连通。对一般连通 split matroid，构造辅助的连通 elementary split matroid N_{e,v}（秩 k-1，底集 E\{e,v}），得到恒等式 g_M = g_{M\e} + g_{M/e} + t·g_{N_{e,v}}。借助 E 上的固定全序规定 N_{e,v} 的真循环 flat 及其秩，并利用 Ferroni–Schröter 的余值公式、修正多项式的递推以及 Ferroni 关于 Schubert matroid 可容许 Delannoy 路径的计数完成证明。

Result: 建立了 split matroid 的删除—收缩恒等式，并由此对所有 split matroid 证明 g-多项式系数非负，从而在包含全部 paving 与 copaving matroids 的 minor 封闭类中证实 Speyer 猜想。

Conclusion: 恒等式右端三个 matroid 元素数均更少，因此该恒等式给出非负性归纳证明的递推步骤；该方法将 Speyer 猜想的验证推广到 split matroid 类。

Abstract: We prove that the $g$-polynomial of every split matroid has nonnegative coefficients, establishing Speyer's conjecture for a class closed under taking minors and containing all paving and copaving matroids. Our proof uses a deletion--contraction identity obtained by constructing an auxiliary split matroid. We first show that every simple, cosimple, connected split matroid $M$ has an element $e$ for which both the deletion $M\setminus e$ and the contraction $M/e$ are connected. More generally, let $M$ be any connected split matroid of rank $k$ on a ground set $E$ with $|E|\ge4$. Suppose that $e\in E$ is such that both $M\setminus e$ and $M/e$ are connected. For every $v\in E\setminus\{e\}$, we construct a connected elementary split matroid $N_{e,v}$ of rank $k-1$ on $E\setminus\{e,v\}$ satisfying \[
  g_M(t)=g_{M\setminus e}(t)+g_{M/e}(t)+t\,g_{N_{e,v}}(t). \] Using a fixed total order on $E$, we prescribe the proper cyclic flats of $N_{e,v}$ and their ranks. The identity follows from the covaluative formula of Ferroni and Schröter together with recurrences for its correction polynomials, derived from Ferroni's enumeration of admissible Delannoy paths for Schubert matroids. Since all three matroids on the right have fewer elements, the identity supplies the induction step in the proof of nonnegativity.

</details>


### [198] [The classification of maximum scattered linear sets of $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35617)
*Giovanni Longobardi,Valentina Pepe*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文完整分类了 $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$ 中的最大 scattered $\mathbb{F}_q$-线性集，证明其必为 pseudoregulus 型或 Lunardon-Polverino 型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大 scattered 线性集的分类是有限几何与有限域线性代数交叉领域的核心问题。此前 Lia、Longobardi 与 Zanella 已将 $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$ 情形约化，但尚余两族候选无法判定，本文旨在补全这一分类缺口。

Method: 在 Lia-Longobardi-Zanella 约化结果的基础上，将剩余的两族候选线性集化为两种标准形（normal forms），再通过研究相关代数簇上有理点的存在性来证明这些候选并非 scattered。

Result: 证明剩余两族候选均不包含 scattered 线性集，从而完成 $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$ 中最大 scattered $\mathbb{F}_q$-线性集的完整分类。

Conclusion: $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$ 中每个最大 scattered $\mathbb{F}_q$-线性集要么是 pseudoregulus 型，要么是 Lunardon-Polverino 型。

Abstract: We classify the maximum scattered $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear sets of $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$, proving that every such set is of pseudoregulus type or of Lunardon-Polverino type. Building on the reduction obtained by Lia, Longobardi and Zanella in [S. Lia, G. Longobardi and C. Zanella, Towards the classification of maximum scattered linear sets of $\mathrm{PG}(1,q^5)$, Algebraic Combinatorics 9 (2026), 327-355], we show that the two remaining candidate families contain no scattered linear sets. Our approach reduces these candidates to two normal forms and establishes their non-scatteredness through the existence of rational points on associated algebraic varieties.

</details>


### [199] [Parking with Frustrated Drivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35638)
*Joshua Hallam,Jenson Molebash,Chris Porter*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究"frustrated parking functions"的计数性质，证明其长度为 n 的数量为 (2n-1)!!，并通过与高度标号 Dyck 路径的对应关系刻画其 lucky cars 与 lucky spots 的分布，涉及 Stirling 数、Fubini 数与二阶 Euler 数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 parking functions 已有丰富的计数理论，而本文提出一种新的停车规则——当首选车位被占用时司机不再寻找附近空位而是开到最后一个空位，产生所谓 frustrated parking functions，需要一个系统的枚举刻画。

Method: 将 frustrated parking functions 与高度标号 Dyck 路径建立一一对应，并借助该双射分析 lucky cars/spots 的组合结构。

Result: 长度为 n 的 frustrated parking functions 数量为 (2n-1)!!；前 k 辆车（或前 k 个车位）均为 lucky 的情形计数为 k!S(n,k)（S 为第二类 Stirling 数）；一旦某辆车（或车位）不 lucky 则其余均不 lucky 的情形计数为第 n 个 Fubini 数；恰有 k 个 lucky cars（或 spots）的情形由二阶 Euler 数给出。

Conclusion: frustrated parking functions 具有良好的组合结构，其计数与双阶乘、Stirling 数、Fubini 数和二阶 Euler 数等经典序列紧密相关，lucky 性质的分布可被精确刻画。

Abstract: Imagine there are $n$ cars lined up along a one-way street containing $n$ spots. Each car contains a group of friends, including a reluctant driver. Each car has a preferred spot and cars enter one by one. The cars drive to their preferred spot and if it is empty park there. If it is not empty, a friend in the back yells out ``Hey! You should have driven faster!". Frustrated by this, the driver continues down the road until they find the last unoccupied spot (if one exists) and parks there. We say a sequence $(a_1,a_2,\dots, a_n)$ of preferred spots is a frustrated parking function if all cars can park under this rule.
  In this paper, we study the enumerative properties of frustrated parking functions. In particular, we show that the number of frustrated parking functions of length $n$ is $(2n-1)!!$. This is done by associating frustrated parking functions with height labeled Dyck paths. Using this association, we are then able to better understand the sets of lucky cars and lucky spots for frustrated parking functions. We show that the frustrated parking functions of length $n$ where the first $k$ cars (or first $k$ spots) are lucky is given by $k!S(n,k)$ where $S(n,k)$ is the Stirling number of the second kind. This in turn implies that the number of frustrated parking functions where once a car (or spot) is unlucky, the remaining cars (or spots) are unlucky is counted by the $n^{th}$ Fubini number. We also show that the number of frustrated parking functions with $k$ lucky cars (or spots) is given by the second order Eulerian number.

</details>


### [200] [Linearity bounds for APN functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35689)
*Christof Beierle*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进了几乎完美非线性(APN)函数线性度的上界，并给出了各阶Walsh系数的渐近上界及线性度与非平坦分量数q的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APN函数的线性度（最大非零分量Walsh系数的绝对值）是衡量其密码学性质的重要指标。此前已知的一般上界为偶数维N-6、奇数维N-4，本文旨在收紧这些界并刻画Walsh系数分布及与分量结构的关系。

Method: 通过Walsh谱分析，研究APN函数非零分量的最大、次大、第四大等各阶绝对Walsh系数，并引入非平坦(nonplateaued)非零分量数目q，建立线性度与q之间的定量关系。

Result: 证明线性度在偶数维至多N-10、奇数维至多N-6；对固定k，第k大绝对Walsh系数至多(1+O_k(2^{-n}))N/√k；第二、第四大系数分别至多2⌊N/3⌋和N/2；奇数维q&gt;0时L(F)^2≤N(1+√(q(N-1)))，偶数维q≤(4C-C^2-1)N/4+1时L(F)≤CN，特别q≤3N/16+2时L(F)≤N/2。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了APN函数线性度的上界，揭示了大维极限下Walsh系数的分布规律，并通过非平坦分量数q刻画线性度，给出L(F)/N→1蕴含q/N→1等结构结论。

Abstract: For $n\ge5$, let $F\colon\mathbb{F}_2^n\to \mathbb{F}_2^n$ be almost perfect nonlinear and write $N=2^n$. It is proven that the linearity $\mathcal{L}(F)$ of $F$, i.e., the largest absolute Walsh coefficient of a nonzero component, is at most $N-10$ in even dimension and at most $N-6$ in odd dimension. This improves the general upper bound of $N-6$ in even dimension and $N-4$ in odd dimension.
  It is further proven that, for each fixed $k$, the $k$-th largest absolute Walsh coefficient among nonzero components, counted with multiplicity, is at most $(1+O_k(2^{-n}))N/\sqrt{k}$ as $n\to\infty$. The second and fourth largest coefficients are at most $2\lfloor N/3\rfloor$ and $N/2$, respectively.
  Finally, a bound on the linearity in terms of the number $q$ of nonplateaued nonzero components is derived. In odd dimension, for $q>0$, we have $\mathcal{L}(F)^2\le N(1+\sqrt{q(N-1)})$, so that $\mathcal{L}(F)/N\to1$ implies $q/N\to1$. In even dimension, for every $1/2\le C<1$, the condition $q\le(4C-C^2-1)N/4+1$ implies $\mathcal{L}(F)\le CN$. In particular, $q\le3N/16+2$ implies $\mathcal{L}(F)\le N/2$.

</details>


### [201] [Hamiltonicity of mildly pseudorandom regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35766)
*Alp Müyesser*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了满足 λ≤(1-δ)d 且 d≫δ^{-6}(\log n)^3 的 (n,d,λ)-图是哈密顿图，改进了 Bradač–Janzer 的结果并给出更短、定量界更好的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的 (n,d,λ)-图哈密顿性结果（Bradač–Janzer）定量界较弱，本文希望用更简洁的方法获得更好的定量结果。

Method: 利用随机矩阵不等式证明随机采样适当比例顶点后谱间隙通常得以保持，从而推出伪随机图的典型平衡二分子图含完美匹配；再用排序网络方法的变体把一组完美匹配转化为哈密顿圈。

Result: 在 λ≤(1-δ)d 且 d≫δ^{-6}(\log n)^3 条件下，(n,d,λ)-图是哈密顿图。

Conclusion: 给出了该哈密顿性定理更短且定量更优的证明，结合了随机矩阵不等式与排序网络方法。

Abstract: We show that if an $(n,d,λ)$-graph satisfies $λ\leq (1-δ)d$ and $d\gg δ^{-6}(\log n)^{3}$ for some $δ>0$, then it is Hamiltonian. A qualitatively similar result was recently proven by Bradač and Janzer. Our proof here is shorter and gives better quantitative bounds. \par In our proof, as in earlier work of Ferber, Han, Mao, and Vershynin, we use a random matrix inequality to show that a mild spectral gap is typically preserved after randomly sampling an appropriate proportion of the vertices. This allows us to deduce that typical balanced bipartite subgraphs of pseudorandom graphs contain perfect matchings. To convert a collection of perfect matchings into a Hamilton cycle, we use a variant of the sorting network method.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Tamagawa numbers and torsion of elliptic curves over function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31910)
*David Kurniadi Angdinata,Mentzelos Melistas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究整体函数域上椭圆曲线的Tamagawa数 c(E) 与其挠子群 E(K)_tors 之间的整除关系，给出各挠族的完整刻画、例外情形及显式最优例子，并提出关于L函数首系数的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立Tamagawa数 c(E) 与椭圆曲线挠子群大小之间的联系，并探索Birch–Swinnerton-Dyer商整性所蕴含的L函数首系数性质。

Method: 在特征大于3的有限域 k 上的非同源平凡椭圆曲线 E/k(t) 情形，按挠子群族分类讨论 |E(k(t))_tors|^2 对 c(E) 的整除性；并在一般整体函数域 K 上，对 5 ≤ N ≤ 101 的素数阶有理点进行分析，给出 N^2 | c(E) 的证明。

Result: 证明了除四个例外挠族外，|E(k(t))_tors|^2 整除 c(E)；对每个挠族给出 c(E) 整除性的完整刻画，并构造显式例子说明结果最优；在一般整体函数域上证明 5 ≤ N ≤ 101 的素数阶有理点迫使 N^2 | c(E)。

Conclusion: Tamagawa数与挠子群大小之间存在深刻的整除关系，且该关系在少数例外族外普遍成立；基于Birch–Swinnerton-Dyer商整性，作者提出关于 E/K 的L函数首系数的猜想。

Abstract: We study the divisibilities of Tamagawa numbers $ c(E) $ of elliptic curves $ E $ over global function fields $ K $ in terms of their torsion subgroups $ E(K)_{\operatorname{tors}} $. In particular, for a non-isotrivial elliptic curve $ E / k(t) $, where $ k $ is a finite field of characteristic greater than $ 3 $, we prove that $ |E(k(t))_{\operatorname{tors}}|^2 $ divides $ c(E) $, except possibly in four exceptional torsion families. More specifically, we give a complete characterisation of divisibilities for $ c(E) $ in each torsion family, and provide explicit examples to prove that they are best possible. Over a general global function field, we also prove that a rational point of prime order $ 5 \le N \le 101 $ on $ E / K $ forces $ N^2 $ to divide $ c(E) $, which motivates our result. Finally, we formulate a conjecture on the leading coefficient of the $ L $-function of $ E / K $, motivated by the integrality of Birch--Swinnerton-Dyer quotients.

</details>


### [203] [Efficient Computation and Congruences for Colored Partition Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32033)
*Jean Carlos Villegas-Morales*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对整数α,证明了Rademacher型展开中双参数指数和A_k^(α)(n,m)的乘性及素幂约化公式,将其局部因子表示为经典或二次扭Kloosterman和;结合截断与精度界给出精确计算α-着色分拆数p_α(n)的高效算法,并计算了p_100(10^10)这一1,113,767位整数(耗时不到一小时),进而验证了新的Ramanujan型同余式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iskander–Jain–Talvola的Rademacher型展开对任意实α>0成立,但当α>24时包含多个极项与双参数指数和族A_k^(α)(n,m),直接计算代价高。作者希望为整数α建立这些和的数论结构(乘性与素幂约化),从而实现p_α(n)的精确高效计算,并用于验证Ramanujan型同余式。

Method: 针对整数α,证明A_k^(α)(n,m)关于指标的乘性,并给出素幂上的约化公式,将局部因子表达为经典Kloosterman和或二次扭Kloosterman和;同时建立显式截断界与精度界以控制展开误差,由此设计精确计算p_α(n)的算法,并用SageMath实现。

Result: 得到A_k^(α)(n,m)的乘性与素幂约化公式及局部因子的Kloosterman和表示;证明截断与精度界;算法在不到一小时内算出1,113,767位的整数p_100(10^10);并借此证明/验证了若干新的Ramanujan型同余式。

Conclusion: 整数α情形下,双参数指数和具有可由经典Kloosterman和刻画的乘性结构,使Rademacher型级数可被有效截断并精确求值,从而实现大规模p_α(n)计算的实用算法,并为发现与认证Ramanujan型同余式提供了新工具。

Abstract: For a positive integer $α$, let $p_α(n)$ denote the number of $α$-colored partitions of $n$. The Rademacher-type expansion of Iskander, Jain, and Talvola is valid for every real $α>0$, but when $α>24$ it involves several polar terms and a two-parameter family of exponential sums $A_k^{(α)}(n,m)$. For integral $α$, we prove multiplicativity and prime-power reduction formulas for these sums, expressing their local factors as classical or quadratically twisted Kloosterman sums. Together with explicit truncation and precision bounds, these formulas yield an efficient algorithm for computing $p_α(n)$ exactly. Our SageMath implementation computes the 1,113,767-digit integer $p_{100}(10^{10})$ in less than one hour. As an application, we use the algorithm to certify new Ramanujan-type congruences.

</details>


### [204] [Quasimodular Forms, Partitions, and Primes in Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32053)
*Kevin Allen,William Craig*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用素数检测的拟模形式，本文给出素数无穷性的证明思路，构造了基于分拆理论的分解算法，并将系数分布与区间内素数存在性联系起来，同时推广到等差数列中的素数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分拆理论、拟模形式、多重zeta值与q-多重zeta值的最新发展揭示了分拆与素数之间的联系，其中"素数检测拟模形式"（其非负系数恰在输入为素数时消失的q级数）是核心工具。作者希望系统利用这类拟模形式的性质，将其用于素数分布与分解等经典问题。

Method: 基于素数检测拟模形式的系数性质展开论证：借助这些q级数的系数消失条件来刻画素数，由此给出素数无穷性的证明，并构造一种新的、源自分拆理论的因式分解算法；进一步分析系数的分布规律，将之与区间内素数的存在性相联系，并把这些结果推广到等差数列中的素数情形。

Result: 给出了素数无穷性的（基于拟模形式的）证明；提出了一种新颖的分拆理论分解算法；证明了相关系数的分布与区间内素数存在性之间的关联；并建立了上述结果在等差数列素数情形的类似版本。

Conclusion: 素数检测拟模形式为连接分拆理论与素数分布提供了统一框架，可用于证明素数无穷性、设计分解算法并研究区间素数问题；作者还讨论了结果的潜在推广，包括Linnik问题。

Abstract: Recent developments in theories of partitions, quasimodular forms, multiple zeta values and $q$-multiple zeta values have led to connections between partitions and primes. Central to this progress are prime-detecting quasimodular forms, which are $q$-series whose non-negative coefficients vanish if and only if the input is prime. In this paper, we use properties of these quasimodular forms to motivate a proof of the infinitude of primes, to construct a novel partition theoretic factorization algorithm, and to show that the distribution of these coefficients relate to existence of primes in intervals. We also prove analogs of these results for primes in arithmetic progressions and discuss potential extensions of our results, including to Linnik's problem.

</details>


### [205] [Conductors and Quadratic base change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32104)
*Siddharth Ramakrishnan Cherukara*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在局部表示论框架下，精确刻画了全实二次扩域基变换下 Hilbert 尖点新形式水平（level）的变化，完整处理了 p=2 的野性情形（包括例外超尖表示），给出了基变换水平的显式公式及部分逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hilbert 尖点新形式 f 经全实二次扩域 K/F 基变换得到 f_K，其水平（level）如何由 f 的水平决定是一个自然问题。tame 情形（奇剩余特征）已由既有基变换理论解决，但 p=2 的 dyadic 情形涉及更精细的 ε-因子分析，尤其是例外超尖表示此前没有任何闭式基变换公式，文献中缺失，因此需要新的局部表示论处理。

Method: 纯局部表示论方法：对 F 的每个素位 𝔭，计算局部成分在二次基变换下导子（conductor）的变化。tame 情形沿用现有基变换理论；p=2 情形采用更精细的 ε-因子分析。系统处理 dyadic 非本原表示，并更细致地处理例外超尖表示。对 GL(2,Q_2) 上中心特征平凡、导子为 3 的每个例外超尖表示，用局部根数（local root numbers）确定其基变换。

Result: 给出了 f_K 水平的显式公式，文中特化到 F=Q 的情形，并证明了该公式的部分逆定理。特别地，首次确定了此前未知的例外超尖表示在二次基变换下的闭式描述（以局部根数表示）。

Conclusion: 通过完整的 dyadic 局部表示论分析（含 ε-因子与局部根数），成功确定了全实二次基变换下 Hilbert 尖点新形式水平的精确公式及部分逆命题，填补了 p=2 例外超尖表示基变换公式的文献空白。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a totally real field and $K/F$ a totally real quadratic extension. For a Hilbert cusp newform $f$ over $F$ with base change $f_K$ to $K,$ we determine the exact level of $f_K$ in terms of the level of $f,$ using only local representation theory: for each place $\mathfrak p$ of $F$ we compute how the conductor of the local component of $f$ changes under quadratic base change. The tame case (odd residue characteristic) follows from existing base change theory, but the case $p=2$ requires a finer $\varepsilon$-factor analysis that does not appear in the literature. We give a complete treatment of the dyadic imprimitive representations and, more delicately, the exceptional supercuspidal representations, for which no closed-form base change formula was previously known; in particular we determine, in terms of local root numbers, the base change of every exceptional supercuspidal representation of $GL(2,\mathbb Q_2)$ with trivial central character and conductor $3.$ This yields an explicit formula for the level of $f_K,$ specialized here to $F=\mathbb Q,$ along with a partial converse.

</details>


### [206] [Factorizations in algebraic monogenic semidomains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32138)
*Jiya Dani,Felix Gotti,Bryan Li,Arav Paladiya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究代数单生成半整环 $S_\alpha$ 的加法与乘法算术性质，建立其结构、单位群、有限因子分解性质及唯一因子分解提升等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数单生成半整环 $S_\alpha$ 介于环 $\mathbb{Z}[\alpha]$ 与加法幺半群之间，其算术性质（可除性、因子分解、单位群结构）尚缺乏系统刻画，作者希望统一研究其加法与乘法算术。

Method: 通过将 $S_\alpha$ 的结构与 $\alpha$ 的共轭元相联系，判定 $S_\alpha$ 何时为环、其加法幺半群何时自由；分析单位群 $S_\alpha^\times$ 并证明 Dirichlet 型定理；给出非原子性判别准则；利用有限因子分解域（FFD）理论及提升方法研究 $\mathbb{Z}[\alpha]$ 与 $S_\alpha$ 的有限因子分解性质；最后给出 $\mathbb{Z}[\beta]$ 中有理元的隶属判别准则与唯一因子分解的提升。

Result: 刻画了 $S_\alpha$ 的结构与其成为环、加法幺半群自由的条件；证明 $S_\alpha^\times$ 是有限循环单位根群与有限生成自由阿贝尔群的乘积，当 $\alpha>0$ 时单位群为自由阿贝尔群；给出产生非原子代数单生成半整环的准则；证明对任意代数数 $\alpha$，$\mathbb{Z}[\alpha]$ 是有限因子分解域，并给出 $S_\alpha$ 为有限因子分解半整环的判别准则，构造出无穷多个正三次生成元对应的非环有限因子分解半整环；给出任意代数 $\beta$ 下 $\mathbb{Z}[\beta]$ 中有理元的隶属判别准则；证明对代数整数 $\alpha$，唯一因子分解可从 $\mathbb{Z}[\alpha]$ 提升到 $\mathbb{Z}[\alpha/n]$（对任意 $n\in\mathbb{N}$）。

Conclusion: 代数单生成半整环的算术行为可借助生成元的共轭与单位群结构系统刻画；$\mathbb{Z}[\alpha]$ 恒为有限因子分解域，而 $S_\alpha$ 可同时在非环情形下具备有限因子分解性甚至唯一因子分解提升性质，从而拓广了经典代数数论中因子分解理论的研究范围。

Abstract: We study the additive and multiplicative arithmetic of algebraic monogenic semidomains of the form $S_α$. We first relate their structure to the conjugates of $α$, characterize when $S_α$ is a ring, and determine when its additive monoid is free. We then investigate units and prove a Dirichlet-type theorem showing that $S_α^\times$ is the product of a finite cyclic group of roots of unity and a finitely generated free abelian group; in particular, the unit group is free abelian when $α$ is positive. We also give a criterion that produces non-atomic algebraic monogenic semidomains. Turning to finiteness properties, we prove that $\mathbb{Z}[α]$ is a finite factorization domain for every algebraic number $α$, establish criteria ensuring that $S_α$ is a finite factorization semidomain, and construct infinitely many positive cubic generators yielding finite factorization semidomains that are not rings. Finally, we give a membership criterion for rational elements of $\mathbb{Z}[β]$ for arbitrary algebraic $β$, and prove that, for algebraic integers $α$, unique factorization ascends from $\mathbb{Z}[α]$ to $\mathbb{Z}[α/n]$ for every $n\in\mathbb{N}$.

</details>


### [207] [Unit fractions with semiprime denominators: an elementary proof of Erdős Problem #306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32140)
*Shisheng Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 给出Erdős问题#306的一个初等证明：任何分母为无平方因子数的正有理数都是两两不同素数乘积之倒数的有限和，证明已在Lean 4中形式化（除引用Ramanujan不等式外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős问题#306研究将分母无平方因子的正有理数表示为若干不同单位分数之和，且每个分母为两个不同素数之积。Tang的Lean形式化首次给出证明，但依赖锚点同步步骤，本文希望给出更初等、结构更清晰的构造并去除该步骤。

Method: 先归约到小目标；取区间(y^2,2y^2]内素数的完全二分图，并加入2与b的素因子，再取(y^8,y^9]内素数的一个调优初段；证明存在子图其倒数和模1同余于a/b，总质量很小从而迫使相等。将子图计数写成有限Fourier和，按二分图两侧索引的频率表分三类：小整数频率给出正主项，其余频率通过除数计数论证与无环绕形式的中国剩余定理证明可忽略。关于素数仅用Chebyshev型界。

Result: 该构造去除了Tang框架中的锚点同步步骤，并用初等方法（Chebyshev型素数界、除数计数、无环绕CRT）完成证明；除一处引用的Ramanujan不等式外，全部已在Lean 4中形式化。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Erdős问题#306的一个初等且可形式化的证明，简化了已有构造。工作为人机协作，AI工具在构造、实验和写作中有实质贡献。

Abstract: We give an elementary proof that every positive rational number $a/b$ with $b$ squarefree is a finite sum of distinct unit fractions $1/n$, where each $n$ is a product of two distinct primes (Erdős Problem #306). After a reduction to small targets, we take a single complete bipartite graph between the primes in $(y^2,2y^2]$, together with $2$ and the primes of $b$, and a tuned initial segment of the primes in $(y^8,y^9]$, and show that some subgraph has reciprocal sum congruent to $a/b$ modulo $1$; the small total mass then forces equality. Writing the number of such subgraphs as a finite Fourier sum, we sort the frequencies into three cases using a table indexed by the two sides of the graph. The small integer frequencies give a positive main term, and all other frequencies are negligible by a divisor-counting argument and a no-wrap-around form of the Chinese remainder theorem. The only inputs about primes are Chebyshev-type bounds. The circle-method framework comes from Tang's Lean development, which gave the first proof; our construction removes its anchor-synchronisation step. The proof has been formalised in Lean 4, apart from a cited inequality of Ramanujan. This work is a human-AI collaboration: AI tools contributed substantially to the construction, the experiments and the writing.

</details>


### [208] [On the sum of odd minimal excludants over overpartitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32207)
*Veena V S,S N Fathima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了overpartition的奇最小排除元求和函数，给出了其生成函数、无穷族同余式、以及Hardy-Ramanujan型渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andrews与Newman引入整数分拆的最小排除元mex并研究了求和函数，Baruah等人将其细化为奇、偶限制版本。本文旨在将这一研究方向推广到overpartition情形，定义奇最小排除元的求和函数。

Method: 推导精确生成函数并与Aricheta-Donato的overpartition最小排除元的双变量生成函数建立联系；运用初等q级数论证，结合经Gordon-Hughes-Newman-Ligozat判别法验证的φ(q)^2的权1 eta商恒等式，利用Wright圆法。

Result: 建立了无穷族同余式；得到当且仅当n为完全平方数时该函数模4为零（n≥1）；获得部分和与自卷积的同余式，特别是自卷积模8的无穷族同余式，以除数函数d_1(n)、d_3(n)表示；并建立Hardy-Ramanujan型渐近公式。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了overpartition奇最小排除元求和函数的生成函数、同余性质与渐近行为，推广了经典分拆的相关结果。

Abstract: Andrews and Newman introduced the minimal excludant $\mathrm{mex}(λ)$ of an integer partition $λ$ and studied the summatory function $σ\mathrm{mex}(n)$, and Baruah et al. refined this to the odd- and even-restricted functions $σ_o\mathrm{mex}(n)$ and $σ_e\mathrm{mex}(n)$. In this paper, we introduce and study the overpartition analogue $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(n)$, defined as the sum of odd minimal excludants over all overpartitions of $n$. We first derive the exact generating function for $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(n)$, and relate it to the bivariate generating function of Aricheta and Donato for the overpartition minimal excludant. Using elementary $q$-series arguments, together with a weight-one eta-quotient identity for $\varphi(q)^2$ verified via the Gordon--Hughes--Newman--Ligozat criterion, we establish an infinite family of congruences satisfied by $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(n)$. Consequently, we obtain that $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(0)=1$ and, for $n\ge1$, $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(n) \equiv 0 \pmod{4}$ if and only if $n$ is a perfect square. We further obtain congruences for the partial sums and self-convolution of $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(n)$; in particular, an infinite family of congruences modulo $8$ for the self-convolution, expressed in terms of the divisor functions $d_1(n)$ and $d_3(n)$. We conclude the paper by establishing a Hardy--Ramanujan-type asymptotic formula for $\overline{σ_o\mathrm{mex}}(n)$ via the Wright circle method.

</details>


### [209] [Matrix Solutions to the Diophantine equation $X^4+ Y^4=2Z^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32374)
*Swadhin Moharana,Pabitra Kumar Jena*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究丢番图方程 X^4+Y^4=2Z^4 在矩阵环与四次域扩张上的解，证明存在无穷多整数与有理矩阵解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Fermat 型四次丢番图方程 X^4+Y^4=2Z^4 的解结构长期受关注，但多局限于标量整数或有理数。将该方程推广到矩阵环及域扩张，可揭示非交换结构下解的存在性与多样性，拓展丢番图方程的研究范畴。

Method: 在矩阵环 M_2(Z)、M_3(Z)、M_4(Z) 上构造并求解该方程；进一步将系数域推广到四次域扩张 Q(⁴√l) 与 Q(⁴√l_1, ⁴√l_2)，利用有理数非完全四次幂且非完全平方的条件进行参数化构造。

Result: 在 M_2(Z)、M_3(Z)、M_4(Z) 中均存在无穷多整数矩阵解；在 M_4(Q(⁴√l)) 与 M_4(Q(⁴√l_1, ⁴√l_2)) 中亦存在无穷多矩阵解，前提是涉及的有理数不是完全四次幂且不是完全平方。

Conclusion: 该方程在适当矩阵环与四次域扩张上仍有丰富解集，表明非交换与非完全幂域结构可显著扩展经典丢番图方程的解空间。

Abstract: The present study explores integer and rational matrix solutions for the Diophantine equation $X^{4}+Y^{4}=2Z^{4}$, establishing that infinitely many solutions exist over different matrix rings, including $M_2(\mathbb{Z})$, $M_3(\mathbb{Z})$, $M_4(\mathbb{Z}).$ Furthermore, expanding the scope beyond the integer domain, the analysis demonstrates that an infinite number of matrix solutions can be found in degree-4 and degree-16 field extensions, specifically within $M_4(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt[4]{l}))$ and $M_4(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt[4]{l_1}, \sqrt[4]{l_2}))$ when the rational numbers involved lack perfect fourth powers and are not perfect squares.

</details>


### [210] [On the Ordering and Injectivity of Lagrange Constants Associated with Certain Substitutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32450)
*Shin-ichi Yasutomi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文通过参数化替换族 $\phi_{a,b}$（$0\mapsto aa$，$1\mapsto bb$，$b=a+1$）研究机械字生成的连分数之Lagrange常数，基于有理数对的三重算术分类确定其序关系，并在 $a\ge 2$ 时证明 Markoff 唯一性猜想的类似命题，同时提出关于边界 $b=a^2+2$ 处相变的一般猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Markoff 谱的机械字刻画以及 Markoff 唯一性猜想的启发，作者希望在替换生成的连分数框架下考察 Lagrange 常数的序关系，并寻找唯一性猜想的类比形式。

Method: 定义替换 $\phi_{a,b}: 0\mapsto aa,\ 1\mapsto bb$，取 $b=a+1$；对有理数对 $0\le x<y\le 1$ 作三重算术分类；研究机械字 $G(x)$ 对应的 Lagrange 常数 $\mathcal{L}([\phi_{a,a+1}(G(x))])$ 之间的序关系，并结合数值计算。

Result: 对第二类（type (2)）有理数对，当 $a\ge 1$ 时即建立不等式；对第一类和第三类（type (1)、type (3)）对，比较在 $a\ge 2$ 时成立。因此当 $a\ge 2$ 时三类序关系完全确定，映射 $x\mapsto\mathcal{L}([\phi_{a,a+1}(G(x))])$ 在 $\mathbb{Q}\cap[0,1]$ 上单射。数值计算支持对任意 $b>a$ 的序关系一般猜想，并揭示在 $b=a^2+2$ 边界处的急剧相变。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Markoff 唯一性猜想的一个类比：在 $a\ge 2$ 时 Lagrange 常数映射在有理点上单射；并提出一般 $b>a$ 情形的序关系猜想，指出 $b=a^2+2$ 处存在相变。

Abstract: Motivated by the modern characterization of the Markoff spectrum via mechanical words and the Markoff Uniqueness Conjecture, we study the Lagrange constants of continued fractions generated by a parameterized family of substitutions $φ_{a,b}$ $φ_{a,b}$
  defined by $0 \mapsto aa$ and $1 \mapsto bb$ with $b = a + 1$. Based on a three-fold arithmetic classification of pairs of rational numbers $0 \le x < y \le 1$, we investigate the order relations between the corresponding Lagrange constants $\mathcal{L}([φ_{a,a+1}(G(x))])$ associated with the mechanical words $G(x)$. While an inequality is established for pairs of the second type (type (2)) whenever $a \ge 1$, the comparisons for type (1) and type (3) pairs hold for $a \ge 2$. Consequently, for any integer $a \ge 2$, where the order relations across all three types are completely determined, we establish an analogue of the Markoff Uniqueness Conjecture: the map $x \mapsto \mathcal{L}([φ_{a,a+1}(G(x))])$ is injective on $\mathbb{Q} \cap [0, 1]$. Furthermore, supported by extensive numerical computations, we propose a general conjecture on the order relations for arbitrary $b > a$, highlighting a sharp phase transition in ordering behavior at the boundary $b = a^2 + 2$.

</details>


### [211] [On Fermat-type equations of signature $(r,r,p)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32509)
*Nuno Freitas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对每个不小于11的素数 r，存在无穷多个整数 C，使得 Fermat 型方程 x^r + y^r = C z^p 对所有充分大的素数指数 p 均无非平凡原始解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Fermat 型方程 x^r + y^r = C z^p 的非平凡原始解的存在性，将经典 Fermat 大定理的框架推广到带系数 C 的情形，考察对固定 r 能否找到无穷多个 C 使得该方程对所有充分大的 p 无解。

Method: 构造多个 Frey 曲线，迫使在 3 上方素点处产生同时的 Frobenius 迹相等关系；引入新的迹分离论证，证明这些迹相等关系蕴含 3 | x+y；随后再借助进一步的 Frey 曲线与 level lowering 得到与 Ramanujan 界的矛盾。

Result: 证明了对于任意素数 r ≥ 11，存在无穷多个整数 C，使得 x^r + y^r = C z^p 对所有充分大（依赖于 r 与 C）的素数指数 p 都没有非平凡原始解。

Conclusion: 通过多 Frey 曲线与 Frobenius 迹分离技术，结合 level lowering 与 Ramanujan 界的矛盾，给出了这类 Fermat 型方程在无穷多系数 C 下对大素数指数 p 无原始解的一般性结论。

Abstract: Let $r\geq11$ be a prime. We show that there are infinitely many integers $C$ for which the Fermat-type equation $$ x^r+y^r=Cz^p $$ has no non-trivial primitive solutions for all sufficiently large (in terms of $r$ and~$C$) prime exponents~$p$. The proof uses several Frey curves to force simultaneous Frobenius trace equalities at the primes above~$3$; a new trace separation argument shows that these equalities imply $3\mid x+y$, after which a further Frey curve and level lowering give a contradiction with the Ramanujan bound.

</details>


### [212] [Congruences for Overcolored Partition $k$-tuples Restricted by Parity of the Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32614)
*M. P. Thejitha,S. N. Fathima*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文为过染色分拆k元组函数 $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ 建立了研究奇素数模同余式的一致框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该函数推广了 Chacon 与 Sellers 研究的过立方分拆k元组，需要统一的同余式研究工具。

Method: 建立统一框架研究 $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ 模奇素数的同余性质。

Result: 证明了如 $n\ge 1$ 时 $\bar{b}_{4,2}^4(5n)\equiv 0\pmod{5}$ 等具体同余式。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了过染色分拆函数模奇素数的同余式结果。

Abstract: In recent work, authors defined $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ to be the number of overcolored partition $k$-tuples wherein both even and odd parts are colored with $r$ and $s$ colors, respectively. This function generalizes the overcubic partition $k$-tuples studied by Chacon and Sellers. The present work gives a uniform framework for studying congruences for $\bar{b}^k_{r,s}(n)$ modulo odd primes. For example, we prove that, for $n\ge 1$, \begin{align*} \bar{b}_{4,2}^4(5n)\equiv 0\pmod{5}. \end{align*}

</details>


### [213] [Lower Bounds for Moments of Automorphic L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32673)
*Deep Thakur*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出一种方法，对任意实数 k>0，在中心点处局部化地获得 L-函数取值 2k 阶矩的下界；具体处理了 GL_2(A_Q) 与 GL_3(A_Q) 上自对偶不可约酉尖点自守表示的二次扭转族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L-函数中心点取值的矩是解析数论的核心问题，其上下界刻画了中心值的大小分布及族的相关统计行为。对一般实数阶矩（而不仅是整数阶）给出下界，可补足矩方法适用范围，并推广到更高秩的自守表示族。

Method: 构造性地发展了一套方法，用于在中心点处局部化地推导 L-函数取值 2k 阶矩的下界，适用于任意实数 k&gt;0。

Result: 对 GL_2(A_Q) 和 GL_3(A_Q) 上自对偶不可约酉尖点自守表示的二次扭转族，获得了中心点处 2k 阶矩的下界，k 为任意正实数。

Conclusion: 该工作将中心点 L-函数矩下界的结果从 GL_2 情形推广至 GL_3，并覆盖任意实数阶，为相关族的矩问题提供了统一处理框架。

Abstract: In this article, we describe a method to obtain lower bounds for the 2kth moment of values of L-functions localised at the central point for all real k > 0. In particular, we handle the families of quadratic twists of self-dual irreducible unitary cuspidal automorphic representations of GL_2(A_Q) and GL_3(A_Q).

</details>


### [214] [The infinite sum of the cubes of reciprocal balancing numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32815)
*Subhasis Panda,Aditya Kumar Dash,Utkal Keshari Dutta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究平衡数倒数的三次方无穷级数∑_{k=n}^{∞}1/B_k³，给出该和式的显式上下界，并据此确定其倒数的整数部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡数（balancing numbers）的倒数幂级数是数论中的研究热点之一，此类级数的取值与显式估计对理解平衡数的渐近性质及整数部分问题具有意义。

Method: 利用平衡数的递推关系与指数增长性质，对尾部级数∑_{k=n}^{∞}1/B_k³进行放缩，构造显式的上界与下界。

Result: 得到了该无穷倒数立方和的显式上下界，并借助这些界确定了该和式倒数的整数部分。

Conclusion: 研究给出了平衡数倒数立方尾部和的显式估计，并成功刻画了其倒数的整数部分，为类似倒数和问题提供了可推广的估计方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the infinite reciprocal cubic sum $\sum_{k=n}^{\infty}\frac{1}{B_k^3}$ of balancing numbers. We obtain explicit bounds for this sum and use them to determine the integer part of its reciprocal.

</details>


### [215] [Pseudo-Effectivity in the Skolem-Mahler-Lech Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32901)
*Victor Shirandami*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Skolem–Mahler–Lech定理的（非有效性）零点有限性问题转化为“伪有效”定量理论，对阶3线性递推序列给出显式高度界，并在根的合适高度约束下推广到任意阶；核心是低高度乘性扰动下指数多项式方程的定量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Skolem–Mahler–Lech定理断言非退化线性递推序列只有有限多个零点，但由于缺乏一般性有效程序来确定这些零点，该定理是无效的（ineffective）。作者希望在不追求对每个序列都给出有效界的前提下，得到一种“伪有效”的替代方案：对某一自然等价类中除受控数量之外的、有界高度的所有序列，给出显式界。

Method: 引入伪有效方法：对具有界高度的序列，在自然等价意义下，显式界对所有但受控数量的序列成立。以阶3线性递推序列为主要研究对象，并通过对特征根施加合适的高度约束推广到更高阶。底层机制是关于低高度乘性扰动下指数多项式方程的定量理论；并证明当扰动高度以足够小的速率线性增长时，Skolem–Mahler–Lech定理所依赖的有限性现象仍然保持。

Result: 建立了阶3线性递推序列的伪有效显式界，并在特征根满足合适高度约束时推广到所有更高阶；证明了在高度以足够小速率线性增长的算术扰动下，Skolem–Mahler–Lech型有限性现象持续成立；并得到一个额外应用：线性轨道进入真线性子空间的扩张算术邻域时的移动目标有限性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并实现了伪有效框架，把Skolem–Mahler–Lech定理的非有效有限性在一定程度上量化为对“几乎所有”有界高度序列成立的显式界，且其有限性结论对低速率线性增长的算术扰动具有稳健性，并可用于线性轨道与扩张算术邻域相交的移动目标问题。

Abstract: The Skolem--Mahler--Lech Theorem asserts that a non-degenerate linear recurrence sequence (LRS) has only finitely many zeros, but is ineffective in the sense that no general procedure is known to determine them. In this paper a \emph{pseudo-effective} approach is introduced: rather than seeking effective bounds for every sequence, one obtains explicit bounds valid for all but a controlled number of sequences of bounded height, up to a natural equivalence. Such results are established for order $3$ LRS's, and extended to all higher orders subject to suitable height constraints on the characteristic roots. The underlying mechanism is a quantitative theory for exponential-polynomial equations subject to low-height multiplicative perturbations. In particular, it is shown that the finiteness phenomenon underlying the Skolem--Mahler--Lech Theorem persists under arithmetic perturbations whose height is permitted to grow linearly at a sufficiently small rate. As a further application, a moving-target finiteness result is established for linear orbits entering expanding arithmetic neighbourhoods of proper linear subspaces.

</details>


### [216] [Skew cyclic codes through Drinfeld modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32906)
*Giacomo Micheli,Mihran Papikian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用超奇异Drinfeld模$\phi_T=t+\tau^n$的挠元构造了秩度量下的斜循环码：挠模$\phi[\mathfrak{p}]$是$R_0=\kappa[\pi^m]$上的自由模，码为中心化子$R\cong\mathrm{Mat}_m(R_0)$的左理想，并证明了码与$R_0$-子模之间的格反同构及秩-BCH界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在秩度量框架下借助Drinfeld模的挠结构构造具有良好距离性质的代数编码方案，推广斜循环码的构造并建立可精确刻画码参数的理论工具。

Method: 在$\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$上取$\phi_T=t+\tau^n$，$t$为次数$r$且与$n$互素的单项不可约$\mathfrak{p}_0$的根；对任意$\mathfrak{p}\neq\mathfrak{p}_0$（次数$d$）及$n$的因子$m$，研究挠模$\phi[\mathfrak{p}]$作为$R_0=\kappa[\pi^m]$（$\pi=\tau^r$）上自由模的结构，取中心化子$R\cong\mathrm{Mat}_m(R_0)$的左理想作为码；建立码与$R_0$-子模之间的格反同构，并推导秩-BCH界。

Result: 证明了码与$\phi[\mathfrak{p}]$的$R_0$-子模之间存在格反同构；给出秩-BCH界，且其设计距离等于真实距离。

Conclusion: 该工作将Drinfeld模挠理论与秩度量编码相统一，得到结构清晰的斜循环码族及可精确确定最小距离的界，为构造和分析秩度量码提供了新途径。

Abstract: We construct skew cyclic codes in the rank metric from the torsion of the supersingular Drinfeld module $φ_T=t+τ^n$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$, where $t$ is a root of a monic irreducible $\mathfrak{p}_0\in\mathbb{F}_q[T]$ of degree $r$ coprime to $n$. For every prime $\mathfrak{p}\neq\mathfrak{p}_0$ of degree $d$ and every divisor $m$ of $n$, the torsion $φ[\mathfrak{p}]$ is a free module over the subring $R_0=κ[π^m]$ generated by a power of the Frobenius $π=τ^r$, $κ=\mathbb{F}_q[T]/\mathfrak{p}$, and the codes are the left ideals of the centralizer $R\cong\mathrm{Mat}_m(R_0)$ of $R_0$. We prove a lattice anti-isomorphism between codes and $R_0$-submodules of $φ[\mathfrak{p}]$, and a rank-BCH bound whose designed distance is the true distance.

</details>


### [217] [Adelic reduction of module lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32936)
*Henry Bambury,Seungki Kim,Changmin Lee,Phong Q. Nguyen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文将LLL算法严格推广到数域上的格约化，基于数域adele环上GL(n)的约化理论，给出无启发式、具严格质量与复杂度界的算法，并由此建立从module-(H)SVP到ideal-HSVP的归约层级，揭示结构格约化与数域上Diophantine逼近的紧密联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LLL算法及其变体多依赖启发式假设，缺乏严格的输出质量与复杂度保证；同时结构格（如module格）上的困难问题与理想格问题之间的关系尚不清晰。作者希望借助数域上GL(n)在adele环上的约化理论，给出一个严格的推广，并厘清结构格约化与数域Diophantine逼近之间的内在联系。

Method: 以数域adele环上一般线性群GL(n)的约化理论为基础，构造数域上的LLL型约化算法；算法不依赖启发式，并对输出质量与运行复杂度给出严格界。进而通过该约化框架建立从module-(H)SVP到ideal-HSVP的归约层级。

Result: 得到一个无启发式、输出质量与复杂度均有严格界的数域LLL推广算法；导出从module-(H)SVP到ideal-HSVP的一系列归约，其中存在运行时间与近似因子在域次数上均为次指数的实例；并发现结构格约化与数域上Diophantine逼近之间的密切联系。

Conclusion: 该工作严格推广了LLL算法至数域情形，为结构格问题提供了新的归约工具，揭示了结构格约化与数域Diophantine逼近的深层关联，为后续算法设计与密码分析提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We give a strict generalization of the LLL algorithm over number fields, based on the reduction theory of $\GL(n)$ over the adele ring of a number field. Our algorithm is free of heuristics, with rigorous bounds on output quality and complexity. As a consequence, we obtain a hierarchy of reductions from module-(H)SVP to ideal-HSVP, an example of which has runtime and approximation factors subexponential in the field degree. More importantly, we uncover a close connection between structured lattice reduction and a Diophantine approximation over number fields.

</details>


### [218] [A Unified Approach to Formal Arakawa-Kaneko Type Zeta Values via Iterated Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33019)
*Ende Pan,Ce Xu,Jianqiang Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入形式多重多对数函数（FMPF）与广义形式荒川-金子zeta值（GFAKZVs），利用迭代积分法建立其性质，证明对偶与求和公式，并统一导出若干已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通荒川-金子zeta值及金子-津村ψ值已有多种结果，但此前由不同方法分别获得，缺乏统一框架。作者希望借助代数拓扑中的迭代积分工具，构建更一般的形式化对象，以统一处理并推广这些结果。

Method: 引入形式多重多对数函数（FMPF）和广义形式荒川-金子zeta值（GFAKZVs）；采用迭代积分方法研究其性质，证明涉及FMPF与GFAKZVs的各种对偶公式和求和公式。

Result: 建立了FMPF与GFAKZVs的若干与普通荒川-金子zeta值类似的有用性质，证明了多类对偶与求和公式，并以统一方式重新导出关于荒川-金子zeta值和金子-津村ψ值的若干已知结果。

Conclusion: 迭代积分形式体系具有统一能力，可将此前由不同方法得到的荒川-金子zeta值与金子-津村ψ值结果纳入同一框架。

Abstract: We introduce a formal multiple polylogarithm function (FMPF) and general formal Arakawa-Kaneko zeta values (GFAKZVs), and establish several useful properties analogous to those of the ordinary Arakawa-Kaneko zeta values by employing the method of iterated integrals, a powerful tool originating in algebraic topology. In particular, we prove various duality and sum formulas involving FMPFs and GFAKZVs. This allows us to derive, in a uniform manner, a number of known results concerning Arakawa-Kaneko zeta values and Kaneko-Tsumura $ψ$-values that were previously obtained by different approaches, thereby highlighting the unifying power of the iterated integral formalism.

</details>


### [219] [Stacky Heights, Entropy, and Zeta Functions of Weighted Hypersurfaces over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33162)
*S. Salami,T. Shaska*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明加权超曲面的规范高度zeta函数存在有限扇区分解，从而统一解释其整数点特化（Hasse–Weil zeta、twist zeta、惯性栈质量）并给出函数方程，同时建立各向异性熵的Lang–Weil渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权射影空间中的加权超曲面往往具有非平凡的稳定子（惯性），其"栈"结构不能由粗空间刻画。作者希望构造一个能同时编码粗空间zeta函数、twist zeta函数与各惯性栈质量的规范高度zeta函数，并理解其解析/算术性质（函数方程、渐近），以统一处理这类带权几何中的计数问题。

Method: 对加权超曲面X及其栈𝔛，取𝐱∈X(F_{q^r})，令g_{𝐱}(r)=gcd(k_{S(𝐱)}, q^r−1)（k_S为权重的gcd），构造Θ_r(s)=∑_{𝐱}g_{𝐱}(r)^{1−s}及规范高度zeta函数Z_H^can(𝔛,s;t)=exp(∑_{r≥1}Θ_r(s)t^r/r)。利用闭各向同性扇区X^{(e)}、其定义域F_{q^{o_e}}（o_e=ord_e(q)，o_1=1）和Jordan totient函数J_{1−s}(e)，证明有限扇区分解。对度数D、p∤D的加权对角超曲面，构造有限Fermat覆盖以验证纯性与自对偶；并用Lang–Weil估计讨论各向异性熵的渐近，比较不同高度。

Result: 得到有限扇区分解Z_H^can(𝔛,s;t)=∏_{e∈E_𝐰} Z(X^{(e)}_{F_{q^{o_e}}}, t^{o_e})^{J_{1−s}(e)/o_e}。由此整数特化均为t的有理函数：s=1为粗空间的Hasse–Weil zeta函数，s=0为twist zeta函数，s=1−k为k重惯性栈的质量。当非空扇区自对偶且等维时得到函数方程；若再要求纯且几何不可约，则等维性对每个整数s≤0都是必要的。加权对角超曲面（p∤D）可由有限Fermat覆盖保证纯性与自对偶。各向异性熵满足Lang–Weil渐近，其主项周期由各向异性周期与Frobenius轨道控制；函数域高度与Ellenberg–Satriano–Zureick-Brown的稳定栈高度一致，而基于次数的高度仅为坐标复杂度统计量。

Conclusion: 该工作以规范高度zeta函数为核心工具，把加权超曲面栈的扇区结构、整数特化、函数方程与熵渐近纳入统一框架，并厘清了两种高度概念的差别：函数域高度对应稳定栈几何，次数高度只是坐标复杂度指标。

Abstract: Let \(X\subset \mathbb P^n_{\mathbf w}\) be a weighted hypersurface over \(\mathbb F_q\), with associated stack \(\mathfrak X\). For \(\mathbf x\in X(\mathbb F_{q^r})\), let \(g_{\mathbf x}(r)=\gcd(k_{S(\mathbf x)},q^r-1)\), where \(k_S=\gcd(w_i:i\in S)\), and define \(Θ_r(s)=\sum_{\mathbf x}g_{\mathbf x}(r)^{1-s}\) and \(Z_H^{\mathrm{can}}(\mathfrak X,s;t)=\exp(\sum_{r\ge1}Θ_r(s)t^r/r)\). We prove the finite sector factorization \[ Z_H^{\mathrm{can}}(\mathfrak X,s;t)=\prod_{e\in E_{\mathbf w}} Z(X^{(e)}_{\mathbb F_{q^{o_e}}},t^{o_e})^{J_{1-s}(e)/o_e}, \] where \(X^{(e)}\) is the closed isotropy sector, \(o_e=\operatorname{ord}_e(q)\) for \(e>1\) and \(o_1=1\), and \(J_{1-s}\) is the Jordan totient function. Hence the specializations \(s=1,0,-1,-2,\ldots\) are rational in \(t\): \(s=1\) gives the Hasse--Weil zeta function of the coarse space, \(s=0\) the twist zeta function, and \(s=1-k\) the masses of the \(k\)-fold inertia stack. The factorization yields a functional equation when the nonempty sectors are self-dual and equidimensional; if they are also pure and geometrically irreducible, equidimensionality is necessary for every integral \(s\le0\). For weighted diagonal hypersurfaces of degree \(D\) with \(p\nmid D\), a finite Fermat cover gives purity and self-duality. We also prove a Lang--Weil asymptotic for isotropy entropy, with periodic leading term governed by isotropy periods and Frobenius orbits, and identify the function-field height with the stable stack height of Ellenberg--Satriano--Zureick-Brown, while the degree-based height remains a coordinate-complexity statistic.

</details>


### [220] [The Beilinson--Bloch heights of generalized Ceresa cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33223)
*Kaiyuan Gu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文计算了阿贝尔簇上广义Ceresa循环的Beilinson–Bloch高度，将其表示为某个adelic线丛的算术交数，并证明该高度可由代数族上的adelic线丛给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ceresa循环与Beilinson–Bloch高度是算术代数几何中研究循环类非平凡性与高度猜想的核心对象，但广义Ceresa循环的高度缺乏明确的计算表达。作者希望给出其可计算的算术交数刻画。

Method: 对阿贝尔簇A上任意纯维数d的循环V，考虑广义Ceresa循环Ce(V)=½(V-[-1]V)，通过构造特定adelic线丛并计算其算术交数来刻画Beilinson–Bloch高度。

Result: 给出了广义Ceresa循环Beilinson–Bloch高度的显式表达，即等于某adelic线丛的算术交数；并证明该高度在代数族上可由一个adelic线丛实现。

Conclusion: 该结果将Ceresa循环的高度问题转化为adelic线丛的算术交数计算，并为高度的族性行为提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We compute the Beilinson--Bloch height of the generalized Ceresa cycle $\mathrm{Ce}(V)=\frac 1 2(V-[-1]V)$ for any cycle $V$ of pure dimension $d$ on an abelian variety $A$, in terms of an arithmetic intersection number of a certain adelic line bundle. As an application, we prove that this height is given by an adelic line bundle over algebraic families.

</details>


### [221] [Prime-Detecting Identities from Dirichlet Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33278)
*Zhichen Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完全确定了算术函数 F_k(n)=σ_k^{-1}(n)+μ(n)+J_k(n)+3 的零点集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由 σ_k 的 Dirichlet 逆、Möbius 函数 μ 与 Jordan  totient 函数 J_k 组合而成的函数 F_k 的零点分布，并给出与之相伴的 σ_k^{-1}(n)+n^k=-1 的素数刻画。

Method: 利用卷积恒等式 σ_k=1*I_k 与 J_k=μ*I_k（其中 I_k(n)=n^k），结合 σ_k^{-1} 在素数幂上的显式公式，并按素数指数模式进行分类；同时借助 Lambert 级数系数恒等式与精确的中间因子关系。

Result: 证明了 F_1(n)=0 当且仅当 n 为素数或 n=18；而对任意 k≥2，F_k(n)=0 当且仅当 n 为素数。并证明对 k≥1 与 n>1，σ_k^{-1}(n)+n^k=-1 当且仅当 n 为素数。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 F_k 零点集的完整分类，并通过中间因子关系解释了两个刻画之间的联系以及例外值 18 的来源。

Abstract: For each integer $k\geq1$, let $σ_k(n)=\sum_{d\mid n}d^k$, let $σ_k^{-1}$ denote its Dirichlet inverse, and let $J_k$ denote the $k$th Jordan totient function. For $n\in\mathbb{N}$, define $F_k(n)=σ_k^{-1}(n)+μ(n)+J_k(n)+3$. We determine the zero set of $F_k$ completely. We prove that $F_1(n)=0$ if and only if $n$ is prime or $n=18$, whereas for every $k\geq2$, $F_k(n)=0$ if and only if $n$ is prime. The proof uses the convolution identities $σ_k=\mathbf{1}*I_k$ and $J_k=μ*I_k$, where $I_k(n)=n^k$, together with an explicit prime-power formula for $σ_k^{-1}$ and a classification by prime-exponent patterns. We also prove the companion characterization $σ_k^{-1}(n)+n^k=-1$ if and only if $n$ is prime, for $k\geq1$ and $n>1$. Lambert-series coefficient identities recover the three constituent functions, while an exact intermediate-divisor relation explains the connection between the two characterizations and the exceptional value $18$.

</details>


### [222] [On the distribution of the zeros of the Euler double zeta-function $ζ_2(s,s)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33324)
*Kohji Matsumoto,Tomokazu Onozuka,Isao Wakabayashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文严谨地研究了欧拉二重zeta函数在 $s_1=s_2=s$ 的截面 $\zeta_2(s,s)$ 的零点分布，并给出了此前由数值实验观察到的结论的理论证明与改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前第一作者与 M. Shōji 的论文中关于 $\zeta_2(s,s)$ 零点分布的结论仅依赖数值计算观察，缺乏严格证明，本文旨在为这些观察提供坚实的理论支撑并加以改进。

Method: 将欧拉二重zeta函数 $\zeta_2(s_1,s_2)$ 限制在 $s_1=s_2=s$ 的对角线情形，得到单变量函数 $\zeta_2(s,s)$，并对其解析性质与零点进行研究，给出严格的理论证明。

Result: 建立了关于 $\zeta_2(s,s)$ 零点分布的定理，严格证明了先前数值观察的正确性，并对相关结论进行了改进（refinements）。

Conclusion: $\zeta_2(s,s)$ 的零点分布可由严格理论方法刻画，数值观察可被数学证明所支撑，从而推动二重zeta函数零点理论的完善。

Abstract: We investigate the distribution of zeros of the function $ζ_2(s,s)$, namely the function defined by putting $s_1=s_2=s$ in the Euler double zeta-function $ζ_2(s_1,s_2)$. Our theorems provide refinements of observations, obtained numerically in a previous paper by the first author and M. Shōji, with rigorous theoretical proofs.

</details>


### [223] [$k$-Fibonacci and $k$-Lucas numbers with the Hölder inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33573)
*Herbert Batte,Prosper Kaggwa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 将Dujella-Jakšetić-Pečarić建立的幂和不等式链统一推广到Falcón-Plaza的k-Fibonacci与k-Lucas数列，并利用交叉恒等式得到连接两族的Cauchy-Schwarz型不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文的不等式链仅针对经典Fibonacci数列，借助两个特定求和恒等式。作者希望证明该机制可统一适用于单参数族k-Fibonacci与k-Lucas数，并寻找Fibonacci-only框架下不存在的跨族不等式。

Method: 基于Hölder与Cauchy不等式及其逆向形式，配合k-Fibonacci、k-Lucas数的广义求和恒等式（平方和、相邻项乘积和），以及交叉恒等式F_{k,i}L_{k,i}=F_{k,2i}，构造并推导不等式链。

Result: 给出了k-Fibonacci与k-Lucas平方和及相邻项积和的显式公式（k=1时退化到经典恒等式）；得到一个连接两族的Cauchy-Schwarz型不等式，该不等式在纯Fibonacci情形无对应；k=1时化为普通Fibonacci与Lucas数之间的显式初等不等式，并给出Lucas数的Hölder与Cauchy转换精细形式。

Conclusion: Dujella-Jakšetić-Pečarić的不等式机制可统一推广到k-Fibonacci与k-Lucas数列，且交叉恒等式带来新的跨族不等式，拓展了经典Fibonacci不等式理论。

Abstract: Dujella, Jak\v setić and Pe\v carić recently established in \cite{DJP}, a chain of power-sum inequalities, built from Hölder's and Cauchy's inequalities and their converse forms, and applied it to the Fibonacci sequence using the identities $\sum_{i=1}^nF_i^2=F_nF_{n+1}$ and $\sum_{i=1}^nF_iF_{i+1}=F_{n+1}^2-\frac{1+(-1)^n}{2}$. We show that this machinery applies uniformly across the one-parameter family of $k$-Fibonacci and $k$-Lucas numbers of Falcón and Plaza, via the generalized identities
  \begin{align*}
  \sum_{i=1}^nL_{k,i}^2=\frac{L_{k,n}L_{k,n+1}-2k}{k},\qquad
  \sum_{i=1}^nF_{k,i}^2=\frac{F_{k,n}F_{k,n+1}}{k},
  \end{align*}
  and
  \begin{align*}
  \sum_{i=1}^nL_{k,i}L_{k,i+1}=\frac{L_{k,n+1}^2}{k}-k+\bigl((-1)^n-1\bigr)\left(\frac{2}{k}+\frac{k}{2}\right), \end{align*} which recover the classical Fibonacci and Lucas identities at $k=1$. We further exploit the cross-identity $F_{k,i}L_{k,i}=F_{k,2i}$, valid for every $k\ge1$, to obtain a Cauchy-Schwarz-type inequality linking the two families that has no counterpart in the Fibonacci-only setting. At $k=1$, this specializes to a fully explicit elementary inequality between ordinary Fibonacci and Lucas numbers, alongside the corresponding Hölder and Cauchy-conversion refinements we obtain for ordinary Lucas numbers.

</details>


### [224] [Biases in the distribution of primes in short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33692)
*Tristan Freiberg*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在Hardy-Littlewood素数元组假设下，给出短区间内素数个数的二阶渐近，主项为Poisson，但算术修正项不同于Cramér独立模型的二项修正，并预测更强的向均值附近的偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究长度与平均素数间距相当的短区间中素数个数分布，检验并修正Cramér独立模型的预测。

Method: 结合容斥原理、Montgomery-Soundararajan的奇异级数估计，以及控制交错和随截断阶数增长的有限筛法论证；并建立改进的随机模型。

Result: 得到二阶渐近：主项符合Poisson分布，算术修正与Cramér模型的二项修正不同，并预测对均值附近计数的更强偏置；数值比较支持该预测。

Conclusion: 短区间素数计数的真实算术修正偏离独立模型，改进随机模型可复现该修正。

Abstract: Assuming a suitably uniform Hardy--Littlewood prime tuples hypothesis, we obtain a second-order asymptotic for the proportion of short intervals containing a prescribed number of primes, when the interval length is comparable to the average prime spacing. The leading term is Poisson, but the arithmetic correction differs from the binomial correction in Cramér's independent model and predicts a stronger bias toward counts near the mean. The proof combines inclusion--exclusion with singular-series estimates of Montgomery and Soundararajan and a finite-sieve argument that controls the required alternating sums as the truncation order grows. We also give a refined random model that reproduces the correction and numerical comparisons that support the prediction.

</details>


### [225] [Cyclotomic factors and irreducibility of the denominators of $q$-deformed rational numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33703)
*Kengo Miyamoto*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究 q-变形模群在单位根处的性质，刻画了分式 r/s 使 d-阶分圆多项式整除 q-变形有理数分母的条件，并验证了相关猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 d-阶分圆多项式何时整除 q-变形有理数 [r/s]_q 的分母 S_{r/s}(q)，并证明 Byakuno-Ren-Yanagawa 及 Kogiso 等人的两个猜想。

Method: 利用 q-变形模群在 d 次本原单位根处特殊化为 (2,3,d) 型三角群；通过 PSL(2,Z) 中平移 z↦z+d 的正规闭包作用下 ∞ 的轨道刻画满足整除条件的 r/s；对 a∈{2,3,4,6} 且 n>5a² 与 a 互素，证明 S_{a/n}(q) 在分圆因子意义下不可约，并结合小素数计算机验证。

Result: 满足条件的 r/s 恰为平移 z↦z+d 在 PSL(2,Z) 正规闭包下 ∞ 的轨道；同余式 s≡0 与 r≡±1 (mod d) 仅当 d≤5 时精确刻画整除；对素数 p 及与 p 互素的 r（满足 r≡±a 或 ar≡±1 mod p）验证了猜想。

Conclusion: 证明了 Byakuno-Ren-Yanagawa 猜想，并确认了 Kogiso、Ren、Wakui、Yanagawa 及作者的猜想对所有相应素数 p 与 r 成立。

Abstract: For $d\ge2$ the $q$-deformed modular group specialized at a primitive $d$-th root of unity is the triangle group of type $(2,3,d)$. Using this we determine the fractions $r/s$ for which the $d$-th cyclotomic polynomial divides the denominator $S_{r/s}(q)$ of the $q$-deformed rational number $[r/s]_q$. They form the orbit of $\infty$ under the normal closure of the translation $z\mapsto z+d$ in $\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$. This proves a conjecture of Byakuno, Ren and Yanagawa, and shows that the congruences $s\equiv0$ and $r\equiv\pm1$ modulo $d$ characterize the divisibility exactly for $d\le5$. For $a\in\{2,3,4,6\}$ and $n>5a^2$ prime to $a$ we show that $S_{a/n}(q)$ is irreducible up to cyclotomic factors. Together with a computer check for small primes, this confirms a conjecture of Kogiso, Ren, Wakui, Yanagawa and the author for every prime $p$ and every $r$ prime to $p$ with $r\equiv\pm a$ or $ar\equiv\pm1\pmod p$.

</details>


### [226] [Necessary and sufficient condition for reciprocal polynomials to be monogenic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33766)
*Anuj Narode*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了倒数多项式为单基因（monogenic）的充要条件，并借此给出了分圆域极大实子域为单基因的一个更简单的替代证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数域K=Q(θ)、θ为整系数首一不可约多项式f(x)的根、整数环Z_K的背景下，f(x)的单基因性（即Z_K=Z[θ]）是代数数论中的核心问题；而倒数多项式（f(x)=x^{°(f)}f(1/x)）的单基因性此前缺乏系统刻画，且分圆域极大实子域的单基因性已有证明较为复杂，故需要建立倒数多项式单基因性的充要条件并简化已有结论的证明。

Method: 针对倒数多项式，建立其单基因性的必要且充分条件；并将该条件应用于分圆域的极大实子域。

Result: 得到了倒数多项式单基因性的充要条件；作为应用，给出了分圆域极大实子域为单基因的另一种且大为简化的证明。

Conclusion: 倒数多项式的单基因性可由所建立的充要条件刻画，该理论可有效应用于经典数域、特别是分圆域极大实子域的单基因性问题，并显著简化原有证明。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{Z}_K$ denote the ring of integers of the number field $K=\mathbb{Q}(θ)$, where $θ$ is a root of a monic irreducible polynomial $f(x)\in\mathbb{Z}[x]$. We say that $f(x)$ is monogenic if $\mathbb{Z}_K=\mathbb{Z}[θ]$. A polynomial $f(x)\in\mathbb{Z}[x]$ is called reciprocal if $f(x)=x^{°(f)}f(1/x)$.
  In this article, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the monogeneity of reciprocal polynomials. As an application, we obtain an alternative and much simpler proof that the maximal real subfields of cyclotomic fields are monogenic.

</details>


### [227] [The two-block odd partition function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33770)
*Mircea Merca*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究带符号划分函数 $a(n)$（将 $n$ 分成恰好两种互异部分大小、每种出现奇数次的带符号划分数）的算术与组合性质，证明 $a(n)\ge 0$ 并给出模 $3$ 的整除性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该函数源自双 Lambert 级数，属于受奇数重数与受限支撑约束的带符号划分函数族，其符号行为与算术规律尚不明确。

Method: 通过生成函数（双 Lambert 级数）与组合分析研究 $a(n)$ 的符号性与模算术性质，结合模 $3$ 的整除性分析。

Result: 证明对所有 $n$ 有 $a(n)\ge 0$，即存在正性现象；并证明 $a(n)$ 满足模 $3$ 的非平凡整除性质。

Conclusion: 在奇数重数约束与受限支撑定义的一类划分函数中发现了意外的算术规律性。

Abstract: We investigate arithmetic and combinatorial properties of the function $a(n)$, which is the signed number of partitions of $n$ into exactly two distinct part sizes, each occurring an odd number of times. We prove that $a(n)\ge 0$ for all $n$, establishing a positivity phenomenon for a signed partition function arising from a double Lambert series. Furthermore, we show that $a(n)$ satisfies nontrivial divisibility properties modulo $3$. The results reveal unexpected arithmetic regularity in a family of partition functions defined by odd multiplicity constraints and restricted support.

</details>


### [228] [Rank stability of elliptic curves over $\mathbb{F}_q(t)$ in residue classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33792)
*Steve Fan,Sun Woo Park*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在全局函数域上，固定素数 ℓ，证明了非等平凡椭圆曲线的秩在正比例的一类 ℤ/ℓℤ 扩张中保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究椭圆曲线在数域/函数域塔中的秩稳定性是算术几何的核心问题之一，本文关注秩在特定 ℤ/ℓℤ 扩张族中的行为。

Method: 结合解析与概率技巧，研究由多项式 f 构造的辅助阿贝尔簇的相关素数 Selmer 群的分布。

Result: 对满足 gcd(q,6)=1 且 q≡1 (mod ℓ) 的函数域 K=𝔽_q(t) 上的非等平凡椭圆曲线 E，在温和条件下，E 的秩相对于正比例的 ℤ/ℓℤ 扩张 K(ℓ√f)/K 保持不变，其中 f 遍历满足 f≡A (mod Q) 的首一多项式。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了函数域上椭圆曲线秩在特定扩张族中的稳定性，说明秩不变的现象具有正密度。

Abstract: Fix a prime $\ell$. Let $K = \mathbb{F}_q(t)$ be a global function field such that $\gcd(q,6) = 1$ and $q \equiv 1 \pmod \ell$. Let $E$ be a non-isotrivial elliptic curve over $K$. Given a fixed monic polynomial $Q$ over $\mathbb{F}_q$, and assuming some mild conditions on $E$, we show that the rank of $E$ does not change with respect to a positive proportion of $\mathbb{Z}/\ell \mathbb{Z}$ extensions $K(\sqrt[\ell]{f})/K$, as $f$ varies over the set of monic polynomials over $\mathbb{F}_q$ such that $f \equiv A \pmod Q$ for any given polynomial $A$. We obtain this result by combining analytic and probabilistic techniques to study the distribution of certain prime Selmer groups of auxiliary abelian varieties constructed from these polynomials $f$.

</details>


### [229] [Higher-order colossally abundant numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33794)
*Oleg R. Musin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从“极大丰数(colossally abundant numbers)”的凸包支撑线几何描述出发，通过坐标变换构造出“高阶极大丰数”嵌套子集，并在其上研究与Robin不等式、黎曼假设相关的性质、增长速率与整除链结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极大丰数可由平面凸包支撑线几何刻画，其与Robin不等式及黎曼假设的联系是数论中的重要议题。作者希望推广这一几何视角，构造新的嵌套数集族，从而在不直接依赖黎曼假设的前提下，给出Robin不等式成立的等价条件，并探究这些集合的增长与整除结构。

Method: 以极大丰数的凸包支撑线描述为起点，进行坐标变换，得到称为“高阶极大丰数”的嵌套子集；其定义不依赖黎曼假设。在此基础上给出使Robin不等式在某个子集上成立当且仅当黎曼假设成立的条件；并对两类坐标类别分析最小成员的增长速度、整除链性质与最小上极限增长率；同时引入凹性假设、幂横坐标等情形，并结合Ramanujan–Nicolas界解释终止链及数值示例。

Result: 构造出若干高阶极大丰数族：有些族在每个层级都是无限集但交集为空；对两类坐标类别确定了最小成员的增长速度；证明每个固定正整数都能整除足够高阶的全部成员；在凹性假设下，最小成员构成整除链，对幂横坐标情形相继商的无界素因子数具有显式limsup增长率；第二类族保留归一化除数和的所有全局最大值，且其最小成员趋于无穷当且仅当黎曼假设成立；并给出数值示例，用Ramanujan–Nicolas界说明终止链。

Conclusion: 通过凸包坐标变换推广极大丰数，得到了与黎曼假设等价刻画Robin不等式的新框架，并揭示了高阶族在增长、整除链及极限行为上的丰富结构，为相关数论问题提供了几何与分析相结合的研究途径。

Abstract: Colossally abundant numbers have large sums of divisors relative to their size. They also admit a geometric description using supporting lines of a planar convex hull. Starting from this description, we change coordinates to obtain nested subsets of these numbers, which we call colossally abundant numbers of higher order. Their definition does not depend on the Riemann hypothesis. We give conditions under which Robin's inequality holds on any one of these subsets if and only if the Riemann hypothesis is true. Some of the resulting families have infinite sets at every level but an empty intersection. For two classes of coordinates, we determine how fast the least members grow. We also prove that every fixed positive integer divides all members of sufficiently high order. Under a concavity assumption, the least members form a divisibility chain; for power abscissas, successive quotients have unbounded numbers of prime factors, with an explicit limsup growth rate. In a second class of families, every global maximum of the normalized divisor sum is retained, and the least members tend to infinity if and only if the Riemann hypothesis is true. We give numerical examples and explain how the Ramanujan--Nicolas bounds produce terminating chains.

</details>


### [230] [Omega bound for the Piltz divisor problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33820)
*Nilmoni Karak,Kamalakshya Mahatab*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了数域上Piltz除数问题误差项的Ω界，通过结合Lamzouri共振论证与非负乘性函数计数定理，去掉了先前界中涉及三阶迭代对数的因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数域上Piltz除数问题误差项的下界（Ω界）此前已有效果，但其中含有与三阶迭代对数相关的多余因子，需要进一步改进以获得更精确的振荡估计。

Method: 将Lamzouri的共振论证（resonance argument）与一个针对非负乘性函数（nonnegative multiplicative functions）的计数定理相结合；计数定理为数域上除数系数提供所需估计，从而消除三阶迭代对数因子。

Result: 得到了数域上Piltz除数问题误差项改进的Ω界，去掉了先前界中涉及三阶迭代对数的因子。

Conclusion: 结合共振论证与乘性函数计数方法可有效改进数域上除数问题误差项的Ω估计，移除三阶迭代对数因子。

Abstract: We obtain improved omega bounds for the error term in the Piltz divisor problem over number fields. Our proof combines Lamzouri's resonance argument with a counting theorem for nonnegative multiplicative functions. The counting theorem gives the estimates needed for the divisor coefficients over a number field and allows us to remove the factor involving the third iterated logarithm from the earlier bounds.

</details>


### [231] [Square root cancellation in the hyperbolic lattice counting problem over cocompact groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33829)
*Dimitrios Chatzakos,Panagiotis Dimakis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进余紧Fuchsian群双曲格点计数问题误差项估计，几乎处处达到 O(X^{1/2+ε}) 的最优上界，并推广到高维H^n，同时研究二阶矩并否定Phillips-Rudnick猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Selberg关于格点计数函数误差项的经典界并非最优，作者希望改进该误差项上界，并在几乎处处意义下逼近最优估计，同时澄清Phillips与Rudnick关于误差项二阶矩的猜想。

Method: 针对余紧Fuchsian群Γ≤PSL(2,R)，作者分析双曲格点计数函数误差项 E(X;z,w)，对几乎每一对点z,w给出估计，并将其方法推广到n维实双曲空间H^n上的余紧格；在二维情形进一步研究误差项的二阶矩。

Result: 对几乎每一对z,w得到 E(X;z,w)=O(X^{1/2+ε}) 的本质最优上界；该逐点结果推广至H^n；在二维情形改进了Chamizo与Cherubini对几乎所有的z,w的二阶矩上界，但否定了Phillips-Rudnick猜想在余紧Fuchsian群情形下的成立性。

Conclusion: 作者改进了Selberg界，在几乎处处意义下达到最优误差估计并推广到高维，同时修正了此前关于二阶矩的猜想，表明Phillips-Rudnick猜想在余紧Fuchsian群情形下不成立。

Abstract: For cocompact Fuchsian groups $Γ\leq \hbox{PSL}(2, \mathbb{R})$ we improve Selberg's bound for the error term of the counting function in the hyperbolic lattice counting problem, achieving an essentially optimal upper bound for the error term \begin{equation*}
  E(X;z, w) = O(X^{1/2+\varepsilon}) \end{equation*} for almost every pair of points $z,w$. We extend this pointwise result for cocompact lattices acting on the $n$-dimensional real hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^n$. Moreover, in the $2$-dimensional case we study the second moment of the error term of the counting function. We improve the upper bounds of Chamizo and Cherubini for almost all pairs $z,w$, but we disprove a conjecture of Phillips and Rudnick for the case of cocompact Fuchsian groups.

</details>


### [232] [An improved high-low method for the parabola](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33949)
*Zane Kun Li,Po-Lam Yung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过对Guth-Maldague-Wang高-低方法的改进，本文给出抛物线的离散限制常数的尖锐估计，并获得抛物线上有理点三重加性能量的无直径界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原高-低方法在给出解耦定理时，难以直接得到抛物线离散限制常数的尖锐估计，以及抛物线和圆上有理点三重加性能量的无直径界。作者希望改进该方法以统一并强化这些结果。

Method: 对Guth-Maldague-Wang的高-低方法进行修改：引入F^3的正交展开，并构造一种新的平方函数，使其在剪枝后仍保留被丢弃的波包，从而得到新的、更高效的高引理与低引理。

Result: 所得解耦定理给出抛物线的离散限制常数的尖锐估计，同时给出抛物线和圆上有理点三重加性能量的无直径界；这两类结果分别与Deng-Fan-Guo-Guo-Luo和Cushman-Demeter-Wu近期工作一致。

Conclusion: 改进后的高-低方法能够统一导出多个近期重要结果，说明正交展开与保留剪枝波包的新平方函数是提升该类解耦与限制估计效率的关键技术。

Abstract: We demonstrate a modification of the high-low method of Guth-Maldague-Wang that yields a decoupling theorem which gives the sharp estimate for the discrete restriction constant for the parabola and also a diameter-free bound for the three-fold additive energy of rational points on the parabola and circle. These results were recently obtained by Deng-Fan-Guo-Guo-Luo and Cushman-Demeter-Wu, respectively. The novel features in this argument are an orthogonal expansion of $F^3$ and a new square function that also keeps the wavepackets discarded after pruning, resulting in new and more efficient high and low lemmas.

</details>


### [233] [Sums of von Mangoldt convolutions via trace-sum averages over $U(N)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33953)
*Ayesha Irfan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究 von Mangoldt 卷积 Λ^{*k} 的短区间和方差渐近式中出现的分段多项式函数 δ_k(c)，将其识别为 Kuperberg–Lalín 定理中矩阵积分的领头阶系数，并给出显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: von Mangoldt 卷积 Λ^{*k} 的短区间和方差存在猜想渐近式，其中出现分段多项式函数 δ_k(c)，但其显式形式尚未确定。作者希望通过函数域类比和矩阵积分来刻画该函数。

Method: 基于 Kuperberg 和 Lalín 的定理，将涉及迹乘积之和的酉矩阵积分与该方差函数域类比的大 q 极限联系起来；令总迹度数与矩阵尺寸按固定比例 c 增长，对矩阵积分的两种格点表示进行渐近分析。

Result: 将 δ_k(c) 识别为该矩阵积分在总迹度数与矩阵尺寸按固定比例 c 增长时的领头阶系数，并获得 δ_k(c) 的显式形式；同时得到有限样条展开，以及将 δ_k(c) 表示为双权块 Hankel 行列式的逆 Laplace 变换的围道积分表示。

Conclusion: δ_k(c) 可由矩阵积分的领头阶渐近完全刻画，并具有样条展开与基于块 Hankel 行列式的围道积分表示，为相关方差猜想提供了显式结构。

Abstract: We study piecewise polynomial functions $δ_k(c)$ that appear in the conjectured asymptotics for the variance of short-interval sums of von Mangoldt convolutions $Λ^{*k}$. By a theorem of Kuperberg and Lalín, a unitary matrix integral involving a sum of trace products governs the large-$q$ limit of the function-field analogue of this variance. We identify $δ_k(c)$ as the leading-order coefficient of this integral when the total trace degree and matrix size grow with a fixed ratio $c$. Through an asymptotic analysis of two lattice-point representations for the matrix integral, we obtain explicit forms of $δ_k(c)$. A finite spline expansion and a contour-integral representation expressing $δ_k(c)$ as the inverse Laplace transform of a two-weight block Hankel determinant are also obtained.

</details>


### [234] [An elementary treatment of Ramanujan's elementary continued fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33529)
*Gaurav Bhatnagar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Ramanujan的初等连分数，这些条目是其超几何与q-超几何连分数的前身，见于Ramanujan第二本笔记第12章前三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ramanujan笔记中的初等连分数是其后期超几何及q-超几何连分数的重要基础，但相对缺乏系统研究，需要加以整理与理解。

Method: 以Ramanujan第二本笔记第12章前三分之一的相关条目为对象，对其中记载的初等连分数进行整理、分类与数学分析。

Result: 识别并刻画了这些连分数条目，说明它们在超几何与q-超几何连分数发展中的先驱地位。

Conclusion: 这些初等连分数构成Ramanujan连分数理论的重要起点，为其后续更复杂结果提供了基础。

Abstract: We study the elementary continued fractions of Ramanujan. The entries considered are precursors of his hypergeometric and $q$-hypergeometric continued fractions. They appear in the first third of Chapter 12 of Ramanujan's second notebook.

</details>


### [235] [Coxeter Symmetry in Hypergeometric Functions and Elliptic Integral Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34016)
*Dianbin Bao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文围绕Mishev完成的Saalschütz型L函数及其\(W(D_5)\)对称性，构建了椭圆积分矩的超几何框架，统一推导了互补模矩族、四Bessel矩\(s_{4,0}\)的调和级数表示，以及涉及Riemann zeta函数与Bessel周期的特殊值恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆积分矩、调和超几何和与特殊值恒等式通常分散在不同理论框架中研究。作者希望借助Mishev的Saalschütz型L函数及其\(W(D_5)\)对称性，将互补椭圆积分矩与调和超几何和放入同一参数空间，揭示它们之间的共同结构来源。

Method: 采用Bailey--Mishev非常良平衡\({}_7F_6(1)\)表示作为统一参数框架；通过碰撞法（collision-based）超几何推导给出涉及\(K'^2\)、\(E'K'\)、\(E'^2\)的三类互补模矩族；并利用与椭圆积分微分系统对应的Barnes-contiguous关系独立恢复\(E'^2\)恒等式；随后推导四Bessel矩\(s_{4,0}\)调和级数表示的超几何求值；在对称参数点利用Coxeter对称性迫使对称适应路径上的Taylor系数消失，生成调和与特殊值恒等式。

Result: 给出了三类互补模椭圆积分矩\(K'^2\)、\(E'K'\)、\(E'^2\)的碰撞型超几何推导；独立由Barnes-contiguous关系恢复\(E'^2\)恒等式；得到四Bessel矩\(s_{4,0}\)调和级数表示的超几何求值；在对称参数点获得一系列调和恒等式与特殊值恒等式，涉及Riemann zeta函数值及同一Bessel周期。

Conclusion: 椭圆积分矩、调和和与特殊值恒等式并非孤立现象，而是源自广义超几何函数共同的\(W(D_5)\)对称性；该超几何框架为统一处理这些恒等式提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We develop a hypergeometric framework for elliptic integral moments organized around Mishev's completed Saalschützian $L$-function and its $W(D_5)$ symmetry. The Bailey--Mishev very-well-poised ${}_7F_6(1)$ representation places complementary elliptic integral moments and harmonic hypergeometric sums in a common parameter space. We first give collision-based hypergeometric derivations of the three complementary-modulus moment families involving $K'^2$, $E'K'$, and $E'^2$. The $E'^2$ identity is also recovered independently from a Barnes-contiguous relation mirroring the differential system of the elliptic integrals. We then derive a hypergeometric evaluation of the harmonic-series representation of the four-Bessel moment $s_{4,0}$. At symmetric parameter points, Coxeter symmetry forces selected Taylor coefficients to vanish along symmetry-adapted paths, producing families of harmonic and special-value identities involving values of the Riemann zeta function and the same Bessel period. The resulting picture shows that these elliptic-moment, harmonic-sum, and special-value identities arise from a common $W(D_5)$ symmetry of generalized hypergeometric functions.

</details>


### [236] [Density of $μ$-ordinary Primes for K3 Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34093)
*Trung Can,Yuxin Lin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Bogomolov–Zarhin关于K3曲面普通约化结论的基础上，对超越Hodge结构自同态域$F$在$\mathbb{Q}$上交换的非CM K3曲面，证明了$\mu$-普通约化的素数密度为1，并给出普通约化素数密度为$1/[FL:L]$的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bogomolov与Zarhin已证明K3曲面在数域上经过有限扩张后，在密度1的素数集上具有普通约化。作者希望细化该结果，刻画非CM K3曲面在不作域扩张时普通约化（及$\mu$-普通约化）的素数密度，并将其与超越Hodge结构的自同态域$F$及$l$-adic单值群的连通分支联系起来。

Method: 设$X$为$L$上的非CM K3曲面，其超越Hodge结构的自同态域$F$在$\mathbb{Q}$上交换。假设关于$X$的$l$-adic单值群连通分支的某个条件成立；当$F$为全实域时，额外假设超越格在$F$上秩的奇偶性条件。在这些假设下，分析$\mu$-普通约化发生的素数集合的密度。

Result: 在上述假设下，$L$中使$X$具有$\mu$-普通约化的素数集合密度为1。作为推论，$L$中使$X$具有普通约化的素数集合密度为$1/[FL:L]$。文中还给出了满足这些条件的K3曲面的具体例子。

Conclusion: 本文细化了Bogomolov–Zarhin关于K3曲面普通约化密度1的结果：通过引入超越Hodge结构自同态域$F$、$l$-adic单值群连通分支条件以及（$F$全实时）秩的奇偶性条件，得到$\mu$-普通约化密度1及普通约化密度$1/[FL:L]$的精确结论，并提供了具体实例验证条件的可实现性。

Abstract: By a result of Bogomolov and Zarhin, a K3 surface $X$ over a number field $L$ has ordinary reduction at a density 1 set of primes after passing to a finite extension of $L$. In this paper, we refine this result for non-CM K3 surfaces whose transcendental Hodge structure has endomorphism field $F$ abelian over $\mathbb{Q}$. We further assume a condition on the connected components of the $l$-adic monodromy group of $X$, and, when $F$ is totally real, a parity condition on the rank of the transcendental lattice over $F$. Under these assumptions, we prove that the set of primes of $L$ at which $X$ has $μ$-ordinary reduction has density $1$. As a corollary, the set of primes of $L$ at which $X$ has ordinary reduction has density $1/[FL:L]$. We include explicit examples of K3 surfaces satisfying these conditions.

</details>


### [237] [Exceptional poles of archimedean Asai $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34165)
*Akash Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文分类了 $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 不可约通用表示 $\pi$ 的 Asai $L$-函数 $L(s,\pi,\mathrm{As})$ 的例外极点，并给出其表示论刻画；当 $\pi$ 处于一般位置时，进一步用 $\pi$ 的导数中不可约成分对应的例外 $L$-因子表示该 Asai $L$-函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Asai $L$-函数及其例外极点是自守 $L$-函数理论中的重要研究对象，其极点位置与表示论性质密切相关。已有工作尚未对 $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 上不可约通用表示的 Asai $L$-函数例外极点给出完整的分类与表示论解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设 $\pi$ 为 $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ 的不可约通用表示，研究其 Asai $L$-函数 $L(s,\pi,\mathrm{As})$ 的例外极点，给出分类并进行表示论刻画；对处于一般位置的 $\pi$，利用 $\pi$ 的导数中出现的不可约成分所附带的例外 $L$-因子来表达 $L(s,\pi,\mathrm{As})$。

Result: 分类了 $L(s,\pi,\mathrm{As})$ 的例外极点，并给出其表示论刻画；当 $\pi$ 处于一般位置时，将 $L(s,\pi,\mathrm{As})$ 表示为 $\pi$ 的导数中各不可约成分对应的例外 $L$-因子的组合。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Asai $L$-函数例外极点的分类与表示论解释，并在一般位置情形下给出了 Asai $L$-函数与导数中不可约成分的例外 $L$-因子之间的精确关系。

Abstract: Let $π$ be an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$. We classify the exceptional poles of the associated Asai $L$-function $L(s,π,\mathrm{As})$ and give a representation-theoretic characterization of them. When $π$ is in general position, we further express $L(s,π,\mathrm{As})$ in terms of the exceptional $L$-factors attached to the irreducible constituents occurring in the derivatives of $π$.

</details>


### [238] [Plectic Lie Algebra Action on Hilbert modular varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34465)
*Yuanyang Jiang,Lue Pan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Hilbert模簇的局部解析p进完备上同调上构造了Nekovář-Scholl plectic猜想所预言的部分plectic Galois作用，并由此证明了中阶以下的完备上同调消没结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nekovář-Scholl提出了plectic猜想，预言Hilbert模簇（及更一般的Shimura簇）的上同调上存在比通常Galois作用更大的plectic Galois作用。该猜想此前缺乏显式构造，而构造这一作用需要新的工具来处理p进完备上同调上的算子结构。

Method: 在Hilbert模簇的局部解析p进完备上同调上，利用此前通过几何方法构造的部分Sen算子，显式构造plectic Galois作用的一部分。

Result: 构造出了plectic Galois作用的一部分；作为副产品，证明了Hilbert模簇完备上同调在中阶（middle degree）以下消没。

Conclusion: 部分验证了Nekovář-Scholl的plectic猜想，并表明几何方法构造的部分Sen算子是研究plectic作用与完备上同调消没性的有效工具。

Abstract: We construct some part of the plectic Galois action on the locally analytic $p$-adically completed cohomology of Hilbert modular varieties, predicted by the plectic conjecture of Nekovář-Scholl. As a byproduct, we prove a vanishing result for the completed cohomology of Hilbert modular varieties below middle degree. To do this, we make use of the partial Sen operators constructed earlier by a geometric method.

</details>


### [239] [Structure of derived Hecke algebras with coefficients in torsion-free profinite rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34589)
*Shuta Kataoka*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究以无挠投射有限环为系数的导出Hecke代数，将其与有限系数导出Hecke代数的逐次逆极限及其"一致有限双陪集支撑"子代数进行比较，在系数同调比较假设下刻画了自然映射的像与核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无挠投射有限环作为离散环时，系数拓扑与支撑条件会对导出Hecke代数产生不同影响，而此前缺乏对有限系数逆极限、一致有限支撑子代数与整系数代数之间关系的系统比较。

Method: 将无挠投射有限系数导出Hecke代数与有限环系数的逐次逆极限及具有一致有限双陪集支撑的子代数进行对比；在系数同调比较假设下分析自然映射，用连续上同调描述其核，并验证与Yoneda积的相容性及对挠Ext类的作用。

Result: 自然映射到子代数的像由零次部分与正次挠部分给出；核为可除平方零理想，被所有正次元素零化，且可用连续上同调描述。比较与Yoneda积相容，对挠Ext类的作用经由该像分解。计算了加法群Q_p的情形，并给出p>3时(GL_2(Q_p),GL_2(Z_p))以Z_p为系数的底层分次阿贝尔群。

Conclusion: 系数拓扑与支撑条件对导出Hecke代数产生彼此不同的效应，所建立的比较框架统一刻画了逆极限、有限支撑子代数与整系数代数间的关系。

Abstract: In this article, we study derived Hecke algebras with coefficients in torsion-free profinite rings regarded as discrete rings. We compare each such algebra with the degreewise inverse limit of the derived Hecke algebras with coefficients in finite rings and with its subalgebra consisting of elements with uniformly finite double-coset support. Under cohomological comparison hypotheses, the natural map to this subalgebra has image given by its degree-zero part and its positive-degree torsion. We describe the kernel in terms of continuous cohomology and prove that it is a divisible square-zero ideal annihilated by every positive-degree element. These comparisons are compatible with Yoneda products, and the action on torsion Ext classes factors through the image. We compute the resulting algebras for the additive group $\mathbb{Q}_p$ and determine the underlying graded abelian groups for $(\operatorname{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q}_p),\operatorname{GL}_2(\mathbb{Z}_p))$ with coefficients in $\mathbb{Z}_p$, for $p>3$. The examples exhibit distinct effects of the coefficient topology and the support condition.

</details>


### [240] [A new proof of two-color partition regularity of Pythagorean triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34676)
*James Leng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 给出一个关于“二色染色自然数必存在单色勾股三元组”的新证明，该证明由ChatGPT Astra发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原证明（如基于van der Waerden定理等的方法）较为复杂或不够直观，寻求一个更简洁、自包含的新证明路径。

Method: 对自然数进行两色染色，通过构造性/组合论证（由AI模型ChatGPT Astra生成）证明单色勾股三元组的必然存在。

Result: 成功给出一个新的证明，验证了任意二色染色下必存在单色勾股三元组这一经典结论。

Conclusion: 该结论成立，且新证明的来源为ChatGPT Astra，突出了AI在数学证明发现中的潜在作用。

Abstract: We give a new proof that if the natural numbers are colored in two colors, then there exists a monochromatic Pythagorean triple. The proof was found by ChatGPT Astra.

</details>


### [241] [Hausdorff Dimension of Weighted Singular Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35009)
*Bohan Yang,Tianru Zhu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明了加权丢番图逼近奇集 $\operatorname{Sing}(\mathbf w)$ 的 Hausdorff 维数精确等于 $s_*=d-(1+w_1)^{-1}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定加权丢番图逼近奇集（即对每个权重方向都被同时很好逼近的点集）的 Hausdorff 维数，此前仅有 Kim--Park 给出的下界，上界缺失，故维数的精确公式尚未确定。

Method: 对逼近集 $\operatorname{DI}_{\mathbf w}(\varepsilon)$ 给出维数上界估计：证明存在常数 $C_{d,\mathbf w}>0$ 与 $\varepsilon_0>0$，使得对任意 $0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_0$ 有 $\dim_H\operatorname{DI}_{\mathbf w}(\varepsilon)\le s_*+C_{d,\mathbf w}\sqrt\varepsilon$，其中 $s_*=d-(1+w_1)^{-1}$；再令 $\varepsilon\to0$ 并与已有下界结合。

Result: 得到 $\dim_H\operatorname{DI}_{\mathbf w}(\varepsilon)\le s_*+C_{d,\mathbf w}\sqrt\varepsilon$，结合 Kim--Park 的下界，证明 $\dim_H\operatorname{Sing}(\mathbf w)=s_*$。

Conclusion: 加权丢番图逼近奇集的 Hausdorff 维数被完全确定，为 $s_*=d-(1+w_1)^{-1}$，从而解决了该问题并给出精确公式。

Abstract: Let $d\ge2$ and let $\mathbf w=(w_1,\ldots,w_d)$ satisfy $w_1\ge\cdots\ge w_d>0$ and $\sum_i w_i=1$. Set $s_*=d-(1+w_1)^{-1}$. We prove that there exist constants $C_{d,\mathbf w}>0$ and $\varepsilon_0=\varepsilon_0(d,\mathbf w)>0$ such that for all $0<\varepsilon<\varepsilon_0$, $$
  \dim_H\operatorname{DI}_{\mathbf w}(\varepsilon)\le s_*+C_{d,\mathbf w}\sqrt\varepsilon. $$ Together with the lower bound of Kim--Park, this gives the exact formula $$
