  \dim_H\operatorname{Sing}(\mathbf w)=s_*. $$

</details>


### [242] [Moments of random multiplicative functions with polynomial coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35187)
*Xinyu Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究了Steinhaus随机乘法函数与多项式相位指数和的矩，证明了一个定量二分现象：每个偶数阶矩要么接近高斯矩，要么多项式系数可被小分母有理数逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画随机乘法函数在多项式相位下的指数和的分布行为，特别是其矩与高斯分布的关系，并给出判别系数"随机性/结构"的Diophantine条件。

Method: 设S_N = N^{-1/2} Σ_{n≤N} f(n) e(g(n))，f为Steinhaus随机乘法函数，g为d次多项式。对每个整数s≥2分析偶数阶矩E|S_N|^{2s}，结合有理逼近（小分母）进行二分刻画；提供Lean 4形式化代码用于验证。

Result: 对每个整数s≥2，证明定量二分：E|S_N|^{2s}要么接近高斯矩s!，要么g的系数可被小分母有理数逼近。特别地，若g的系数满足Diophantine条件，则S_N依分布收敛到均值为0、方差为1的复正态分布。

Conclusion: 在Diophantine条件下，随机乘法函数与多项式相位的指数和呈现高斯极限行为；矩的高斯性与系数的有理逼近结构形成互补二分，Lean 4代码支持证明验证。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a Steinhaus random multiplicative function and let $g$ be a polynomial of degree $d$. Write $S_N=N^{-1/2}\sum_{n\le N}f(n)\,e(g(n))$. We prove a quantitative dichotomy for the moments of $S_N$: for each integer $s\ge 2$, either $\mathbb{E}|S_N|^{2s}$ is close to the Gaussian moment $s!$, or the coefficients of $g$ can be approximated by rationals with a small denominator. In particular, if the coefficients of $g$ satisfy a Diophantine condition, then $S_N$ converges in law to a complex normal distribution with mean $0$ and variance $1$. Lean 4 code for the proofs is provided, for convenience of verification.

</details>


### [243] [Weak Hall's conjecture, approximation gains, and continued fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35252)
*R. Laniewski,K. Müller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 将丢番图方程 $y^2=x^3+k$ 的质量分解为逼近增益 $G_a$ 与幂增益 $G_p$，证明 Hall 猜想的弱形式等价于 $G_a\le 1$ 的渐近界，并推广到 $G_a\le\kappa$ 情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $abc$ 猜想与 Hall 猜想之间的联系。Müller、Taktikos 和 de Weger 曾将三次-平方三元组 $(x^3,|k|,y^2)$ 的质量通过乘积 $P=xy|k|$ 分解为逼近增益与幂增益，本文希望在此框架下精确刻画质量与 Hall 型下界之间的对应关系。

Method: 对整数解 $y^2=x^3+k$（$x,y\ge1$，$k\ne0$），定义逼近增益 $G_a=\log\max(x^3,y^2)/\log P$ 与幂增益 $G_p=\log P/\log\operatorname{rad}(xy|k|)$，其中 $P=xy|k|$。在 $6/11\le\kappa<6/5$ 范围内研究渐近界 $G_a\le\kappa$ 与 $|k|$ 下界之间的推导与等价性。同时利用连分数工具，将 $|k|$ 的小值与 $\sqrt{x}$ 的大偏商联系起来。

Result: Hall 猜想的弱形式（对任意 $\delta>0$，除有限多个解外 $|k|>x^{1/2-\delta}$）等价于渐近界 $G_a\le 1$。更一般地，对 $6/11\le\kappa<6/5$，$G_a\le\kappa$ 推出 $|k|>x^{3/\kappa-5/2-\delta}$（除有限多个解外）；对 $3/4\le\kappa<6/5$ 两命题等价。由于逼近增益不超过质量，指数为 $1\le\kappa<6/5$ 的 $abc$ 不等式对原始解给出相同下界；$\kappa=1$ 情形可推广到所有解，恢复 $abc$ 猜想推出弱 Hall 的经典结论。

Conclusion: 逼近增益 $G_a$ 是连接 $abc$ 猜想与 Hall 猜想的关键量：其渐近上界直接等价于 Hall 弱形式的指数下界，推广形式给出统一的 $\kappa$ 参数刻画。此外，$|k|$ 的小值与 $\sqrt{x}$ 的大偏商存在关联，为后续研究提供了连分数视角。

Abstract: For an integer solution of $y^2=x^3+k$ with $x,y\ge 1$ and $k\ne 0$, the integers $x^3$, $|k|$ and $y^2$ form a triple in which one term is the sum of the other two. When $\gcd(x,y)=1$ this triple is coprime, and the $abc$ conjecture predicts that its quality is asymptotically at most $1$. Following Müller, Taktikos and de Weger, we factor this quality through the product $P=xy|k|$ into the approximation gain $G_a=\log\max(x^3,y^2)/\log P$ and the power gain $G_p=\log P/\log\rad(xy|k|)$, which is at least $1$. We show that the weak form of Hall's conjecture, which asks that $|k|>x^{1/2-δ}$ for every $δ>0$ outside finitely many solutions, is equivalent to the asymptotic bound $G_a\le 1$. More generally, for $6/11\leκ<6/5$, an asymptotic bound $G_a\leκ$ gives $|k|>x^{3/κ-5/2-δ}$ for every $δ>0$ outside finitely many solutions, and for $3/4\leκ<6/5$ the two statements are equivalent. Since the approximation gain never exceeds the quality, an $abc$ inequality with exponent $1\leκ<6/5$ gives the same bound for primitive solutions. The case $κ=1$ extends to all solutions and recovers the classical implication from the $abc$ conjecture to weak Hall. We also relate small values of $|k|$ to large partial quotients of $\sqrt x$.

</details>


### [244] [Singular intersections in split semiabelian varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35307)
*Francesco Ballini,Laura Capuano,Nicola Ottolini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了分裂半阿贝尔簇中不包含于真代数子群的不可约曲线上，与代数子群切触的点集是有限的，推广了Marché-Maurin关于二维环面曲线的结果，并应用于几何整除序列的无平方性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marché和Maurin此前证明了二维环面中曲线与代数子群切触点的有限性。本文旨在将该结果推广到更高维的分裂半阿贝尔簇，并寻找其数论应用。

Method: 设$\mathcal G$为维数$n\geq 2$的分裂半阿贝尔簇，$\mathcal C\subset\mathcal G$为定义在$\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$上的不可约曲线，且不包含于$\mathcal G$的任何真代数子群中。证明满足在点$P$处与曲线切触的代数子群$H$中所含曲线点$P$的集合有限。

Result: 证明了在自然非退化假设下，曲线上落于在某点与其切触的代数子群中的点集有限，成功将Marché-Maurin的结果从二维环面推广至任意维分裂半阿贝尔簇。

Conclusion: 该切触有限性定理为几何整除序列的无平方性提供了新的应用，拓展了挠点与曲线相交问题的研究范围。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal G$ be a split semiabelian variety of dimension $n \ge 2$ and let $\mathcal C \subset \mathcal G$ an irreducible curve, where everything is defined over $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$. We prove that, under the natural hypothesis that $\mathcal C$ is not contained in any proper algebraic subgroup of $\mathcal G$, the set of points $P$ of $\mathcal C$ lying in an algebraic subgroup $H$ which intersects the curve tangentially at $P$ is finite. This generalizes a result for curves in 2-dimensional tori by Marché and Maurin. We apply these results to square-freeness of certain geometric divisibility sequences.

</details>


### [245] [On a generating function of the basis of weakly holomorphic functions on an elliptic curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35382)
*Joshua S. Friedman,Jay Jorgenson,Lejla Smajlović*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究余有限Fuchs群（genus one、单尖点）情形，构造弱全纯模函数典则基，并给出Kloosterman zeta函数特殊值生成函数与权2调和Maass形式的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对genus one、单尖点的余有限Fuchs群，希望理解其弱全纯模函数空间的结构（寻找典则基），并借助生成函数方法建立Kloosterman zeta函数特殊值与权2调和Maass形式之间的联系，推广经典结果。

Method: 将商空间光滑紧化为椭圆曲线Xg，取在尖点处阶为2、3的典则生成元x,y，满足广义Weierstrass关系；取权2尖点形式f，使对应全纯微分ω=dx/(2y+a1x+a3)。由此定义生成函数((y(τ)+y(z)+a1x(z)+a3)f(z))/(x(z)−x(τ))=Σ_{m≥0}Φ_m(τ)q_z^m，得到弱全纯模函数族{Φ_m}，并证明{Φ_0}∪{Φ_m}_{m≥2}构成M_{0,Γ}^{!,∞}的典则基。再将该生成函数与Niebur–Poincaré级数的生成函数比较。

Result: 证明{Φ_0}∪{Φ_m}_{m≥2}是弱全纯模函数空间M_{0,Γ}^{!,∞}（极点仅支撑于尖点）的典则基；通过生成函数比较，证明Kloosterman zeta函数在1处特殊值的生成函数在相差一个加性常数意义下是某权2调和Maass形式的全纯部分。

Conclusion: 在genus one、单尖点的余有限Fuchs群情形，生成函数方法给出弱全纯模函数的典则基，并揭示Kloosterman zeta函数在1处特殊值的生成函数是权2调和Maass形式的全纯部分（至多相差常数）。

Abstract: Let $\Gz$ be a cofinite Fuchsian group whose quotient $\Gz\backslash\HH$ has genus one and a single cusp, normalized to be at $\infty$ with width one. Let $\Xg$ be its smooth compactification, which is an elliptic curve over $\CC$. Let $x,y$ be canonical generators of the function field on $\Xg$, which have poles of order $2,3$ respectively at the cusp and which {satisfy} a generalized Weierstrass relation $y^2+(a_1x+a_3)y=x^3+a_2x^2+a_4x+a_6$. Let $f$ be the unique (up to scale) weight two cusp form for $\Gz$, normalized so that the associated holomorphic differential is $ω=dx/(2y+a_1x+a_3)$. With all this, we prove that the generating function identity \[ \frac{\bigl(y(τ)+y(z)+a_1x(z)+a_3\bigr)f(z)}{x(z)-x(τ)}=\sum_{m\ge0}Φ_m(τ)q_z^m \] defines a family $\{Φ_m\}_{m\ge0}$ of weakly holomorphic modular functions on $\Xg$ such that the set $\{Φ_0\}\cup\{Φ_m\}_{m\ge2}$ is a canonical basis of the space $M_{0,\Gz}^{!,\infty}$ of weakly holomorphic modular functions on $\Xg$ with poles supported only at the cusp. As an application, by comparing the generating function of the family $\{Φ_m\}_{m\ge0}$ with the generating function of the Niebur--Poincaré series, we show that the generating function of the special values of the Kloosterman zeta function at $1$ is the holomorphic part of a certain weight two harmonic Maass form up to an additive constant.

</details>


### [246] [Complete classification of $7$-adic Galois images for non-CM elliptic curves over $\Q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35460)
*Tho Nguyen Xuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 解决了Furio与Lombardo提出的猜想1.6，从而完成了$\Q$上非CM椭圆曲线$7$-adic Galois像的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是解决Furio与Lombardo (2026) 提出的猜想1.6，以补全$\Q$上非CM椭圆曲线$7$-adic Galois表示的像的分类。

Method: 作者在2026年6月至9月期间经过长期"AI-training"（自主训练/思考）独立完成该证明，未与他人合作。

Result: 成功证明了猜想1.6，作为推论给出了$\Q$上非CM椭圆曲线$7$-adic Galois像的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该工作终结了$7$-adic情形下的分类问题，均由作者个人独立完成。

Abstract: We solve Conjecture 1.6 of Furio and Lombardo [On $7$-adic Galois representations for elliptic curves over $\Q$, Proc.\ London Math.\ Soc.\ (3) \textbf{133} (2026), e70193.]. As a consequence, we complete the classification of $7$-adic Galois images for non-CM elliptic curves over $\Q$. The proof is the result of a long AI-training period by the author from June 2026 to September 2026. The author has been working on this project alone without the help of any other.

</details>


### [247] [A note on large clusters of $E_2$-numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35548)
*Genheng Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过 Sone 筛法中的配方技巧并求解一维变分问题，无条件证明了相邻半素数（两个不同素数之积）在固定偏移 ρ 下的最小间隔上界为 exp((π+ε)√ρ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半素数（两个不同素数之积）序列在给定偏移 ρ 下的间距上界问题，希望无条件（不依赖未证明的假设）地给出 q_{n+ρ} - q_n 的 liminf 的指数型上界，并改进此类结果的常数。

Method: 采用 Sone 筛法，在主项中完成平方配方，将筛法问题转化为一维变分问题并求解该变分极值，从而无需任何未证明假设即可导出间距估计。

Result: 对任意 ε>0 及充分大的整数 ρ，无条件成立 liminf_{n→∞}(q_{n+ρ}-q_n) ≤ exp((π+ε)√ρ)。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了半素数序列存在无穷多对小间隔的定量无条件上界，指数常数达到 π+ε，进一步推进了半素数分布的小间隔研究。

Abstract: Let $q_n$ denote the $n$th product of two distinct primes. By completing a square in Sono's sieve and solving the resulting one-dimensional variational problem, we prove unconditionally that, for every $\varepsilon>0$ and all sufficiently large integers $ρ$, \[
  \liminf_{n\to\infty}(q_{n+ρ}-q_n)
  \leq \exp\left((π+\varepsilon)\sqrtρ\right). \]

</details>


### [248] [On the largest prime factors of $p-1$ and $p+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35610)
*Genheng Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明不等式 P⁺(p+1)>P⁺(p-1) 与 P⁺(p-1)>P⁺(p+1) 各对正比例的素数成立，即 p±1 的最大素因子在两个方向上均无一致偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对素数 p 的相邻整数 p−1 与 p+1 的最大素因子 P⁺ 的相对大小，此前缺乏无条件的同阶比较结果；本文欲给出两个方向均成立的正比例命题，排除单一方向占优的可能。

Method: 将对数平衡恒等式与在稀疏余因子带（thin cofactor strip）上的一致 Selberg 筛法相结合；该迁移机制对两个平移 p−1、p+1 对称适用，把单侧的大素因子"储备"转化为双向的无条件排序结论。

Result: 对正比例的素数 p，P⁺(p+1)>P⁺(p−1) 成立；同样对正比例的素数 p，P⁺(p−1)>P⁺(p+1) 成立。

Conclusion: 关于 p±1 最大素因子的两个相反不等式均以正密度成立，表明该量在素数上不存在单向的普遍偏序；所建立的对数平衡—筛法迁移框架对两个移位通用，可用于同类比较型问题。

Abstract: We prove that each of the inequalities $P^{+}(p+1)>P^{+}(p-1)$ and $P^{+}(p-1)>P^{+}(p+1)$ holds for a positive proportion of primes. The argument combines a logarithmic balance identity with a uniform Selberg sieve over a thin cofactor strip. The same transfer mechanism applies to either shift and converts a one-sided large-prime-factor reservoir into an unconditional ordering result in both directions.

</details>


### [249] [A difference formula of $p$-adic height pairings via the Bloch-Kato logarithm map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35626)
*Taiga Adachi,Yu Katagiri,Ryota Shii*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究几何p进表示上p进高度配对对Hodge滤过分裂的依赖性，对二维辛自对偶表示给出了两个由Frobenius确定的分裂所对应高度配对之差的局部Bloch–Kato对数表达式，并证明了分圆p进高度配对的非平凡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p进高度配对的构造依赖于整体p进对数以及p上方各素处的Hodge滤过分裂，因此需要理解高度配对对这些分裂选择的依赖程度，尤其是不同自然分裂所导致的高度配对差异及其算术含义。

Method: 针对数域绝对Galois群的几何p进表示，选取合适的二维辛自对偶表示（包括非普通素处、不整除水平的偶权新形式所附表示的自对偶扭），比较由Frobenius确定的两种分裂所对应的高度配对，并用局部Bloch–Kato对数显式刻画其差。

Result: 显式给出了两种分裂对应的高度配对之差等于局部Bloch–Kato对数的表达式；在Q上，附加Frobenius特征值在p处互异以及Bloch–Kato Selmer群在p处的局部化映射非零的条件下，证明了两个分圆p进高度配对中至少有一个非平凡。

Conclusion: 高度配对对Hodge滤过分裂的依赖可由局部Bloch–Kato对数精确控制，并由此在适当假设下得到分圆p进高度配对的非平凡性结论。

Abstract: The construction of a $p$-adic height pairing for a geometric $p$-adic representation of the absolute Galois group of a number field depends on a global $p$-adic logarithm and on local splittings of the Hodge filtrations at the primes above $p$. We study the dependence on these splittings for suitable two-dimensional symplectic self-dual representations, including self-dual twists of representations attached to even-weight newforms at non-ordinary primes not dividing the level. We express the difference between the height pairings associated with the two splittings determined by Frobenius explicitly in terms of local Bloch--Kato logarithms. As an application over $\mathbb{Q}$, we prove that at least one of the two cyclotomic $p$-adic height pairings is non-trivial under the additional assumptions that the Frobenius eigenvalues at $p$ are distinct and the localization map at $p$ from the Bloch--Kato Selmer group is non-zero.

</details>


### [250] [Heavenly Elliptic Curves over Cubic Number Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35642)
*Suzanne O'Hara*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三次数域上的heavenly椭圆曲线，类比McLeman与Rasmussen在二次域的工作，发现三次情形下平衡椭圆曲线迹的可能行为更丰富，引入"平衡"与"全平衡"的区分，证明全平衡椭圆曲线在ℓ-挠上的表示迹非满射，给出使任意heavenly椭圆曲线必须平衡的素数ℓ的上界，并与CM椭圆曲线的迹行为作比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源自Ihara的一个问题：当椭圆曲线E/K在ℓ处是heavenly时，扩张K(E[ℓ^∞])/K(μ_ℓ^∞)是pro-ℓ且在ℓ外非分歧，这与K上射影直线挖去三点后pro-ℓ étale覆盖核所对应固定域的算术条件相同。作者希望在三次数域情形下推广并细化对heavenly椭圆曲线的理解。

Method: 沿用McLeman与Rasmussen在二次域的研究框架，将分析推广到三次数域上的heavenly椭圆曲线；针对平衡椭圆曲线的迹，进一步区分"平衡"与"全平衡"两类曲线，并据此展开理论分析；同时计算素数ℓ的界，并对照CM椭圆曲线的迹行为进行比较。

Result: 在三次情形下，平衡椭圆曲线迹的可能行为比二次情形更宽泛；对全平衡椭圆曲线，其在ℓ-挠上的表示迹是非满射的；给出了一个素数ℓ的上界，超过该上界后三次数域上的任意heavenly椭圆曲线必为平衡的；并比较了全平衡椭圆曲线与CM椭圆曲线的迹行为。

Conclusion: 三次数域上heavenly椭圆曲线的迹行为较二次情形更为复杂，通过引入全平衡概念可得到迹非满射的结论，并获得迫使曲线平衡的素数上界；全平衡椭圆曲线与CM椭圆曲线在迹行为上存在可比性，为后续研究提供了新的分类视角。

Abstract: The study of heavenly abelian varieties is motivated by a question of Ihara. When an elliptic curve $E/K$ is heavenly at $\ell$, the extension $K(E[\ell^\infty])/K(μ_\ell^\infty)$ is pro-$\ell$ and unramified away from $\ell$. These are the same arithmetic conditions as the fixed field of the kernel attached to pro-$\ell$ étale covers of the projective line over $K$ minus three points. In this paper we study heavenly elliptic curves defined over cubic number fields. Following the work on McLeman and Rasmussen in the quadratic case, we find that there is a wider range of possible behaviors for the trace of a balanced elliptic curve in the cubic case. In this setting, we introduce a further distinction between balanced and totally balanced curves. With this, we show that trace of the representation on the $\ell$-torsion for totally balanced elliptic curves is non-surjective. We also compute a bound on primes $\ell$ after which any heavenly elliptic curve defined over a cubic number field must be balanced. Finally we compare the trace behavior of totally balanced elliptic curves with CM elliptic curves.

</details>


### [251] [A Padovan-automatic description of a nested recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33421)
*Benoit Cloitre,Haobo Ma,Wenlin Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究递归序列 A076502，将其刻画为贪心 Padovan 数系中的二位移位并带有限状态修正，证明其有界偏差、精确偏移集、26-字母 morphic 表示、最小平衡常数 4，并在 Lean 中形式化相关结果，辅以精确计算细化数值区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该序列由嵌套递归定义，行为不直观。作者希望揭示其与 Padovan 数系的结构联系，并给出偏差、偏移、morphic 表示与平衡性质的严格、可形式化验证的描述。

Method: 构造基于精确整数进位不变量的加法自动机，通过有限语言包含认证完备性；用同步自动机验证嵌套递归；建立偏差有界性并确定偏移集；构造 26 字母非擦除 morphic 表示；给出包围全局偏差极值的有效算法；在 Lean 中形式化主要结论。

Result: 序列对应贪心 Padovan 数系中的二位移位加有限状态修正；相对斜率 c（c³−c²+2c−1=0 的根）的直线有有界偏差；对 ⌊cn⌋ 的精确偏移集为 {−1,0,1,2}；给出首差词的 26 字母非擦除 morphic 表示；其最小平衡常数为 4；六位小数偏差界与极值包围算法成立，并经精确计算细化。

Conclusion: 该工作把递归序列 A076502 纳入自动机与数系框架，得到偏移集、morphic 表示、平衡常数和偏差界等精确结论，并通过 Lean 形式化与精确计算加以验证，为嵌套递归序列的系统分析提供了方法范式。

Abstract: We study the sequence $a(0)=0$, $a(1)=1$ and $a(n)=n-a(n-a(n-a(n-1)))$ for $n\ge 2$, listed as A076502 in the On-Line Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences. We identify $a(n)$ as a two-position shift in the greedy Padovan numeration system, with a finite-state correction. The proof constructs an addition automaton from an exact integer-carry invariant and certifies its completeness by finite-language inclusion; a synchronized automaton then verifies the nested recurrence. We establish bounded discrepancy from the line of slope $c$, where $c^3-c^2+2c-1=0$, and show that the exact set of offsets from $\lfloor cn\rfloor$ is $\{-1,0,1,2\}$. We construct an explicit 26-letter non-erasing morphic presentation of the first-difference word, prove that its least balance constant is 4, and give an effective procedure for enclosing the global discrepancy extrema to arbitrary accuracy. We formalize the recurrence identification, six-decimal discrepancy bound, exact offset set, concrete morphic identity, least balance constant, and an effective extrema algorithm in Lean. Separate exact computations refine the numerical enclosures.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Genus, Morse Index, and Area of Minimal Surfaces in Three-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32001)
*Riccardo Caniato*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明闭三维黎曼流形中任意闭嵌入极小曲面满足第一贝蒂数与指标、面积之和的线性上界，无需可定向或双侧假设，并对正Ricci曲率情形给出显式亏格界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Song猜想闭三维流形中极小曲面的拓扑复杂度（亏格/第一贝蒂数）可由其Morse指标与面积共同控制，此前结果多依赖可定向性或双侧性假设，缺乏一般性刻画。

Method: 通过极小曲面的Morse指标理论与稳定性分析，结合面积估计，对非可定向、单侧嵌入曲面建立统一的贝蒂数上界；并在截面曲率下界及正Ricci曲率条件下推导显式亏格界。

Result: 证明了 b1(Σ;Z2) ≤ C(Ind(Σ)+Area(Σ))，对任意闭嵌入极小曲面成立且无需可定向/双侧假设；对闭连通可定向双侧极小浸入给出曲率下界下的显式亏格界；在正Ricci曲率下得到普遍不等式 γ(Σ) ≤ 8Ind(Σ)。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了Song的加性亏格—指标—面积猜想，并给出曲率约束情形下的定量拓扑控制，统一并推广了相关极小曲面拓扑有限性结果。

Abstract: We prove that, in every closed Riemannian three-manifold $(M^3,\bar g)$, there exists a constant $C>0$ such that every closed smoothly embedded minimal surface $Σ\subset M$ satisfies $b_1(Σ;\mathbb{Z}_2)\le C\bigl(\operatorname{Ind}(Σ)+\operatorname{Area}(Σ)\bigr)$. The estimate holds without orientability or two-sidedness assumptions and establishes the additive genus-index-area estimate conjectured by Song. For closed connected orientable two-sided minimal immersions, we also obtain explicit genus bounds under a lower bound on the ambient sectional curvature. Under positive ambient Ricci curvature, we prove the universal inequality $γ(Σ)\le 8\operatorname{Ind}(Σ)$.

</details>


### [253] [Polynomial area growth for special Kähler metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32087)
*Yiqian Shi,Ke Wang,Bin Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 仿射特殊 Kähler 度量在 puncture 处的面积多项式增长可推出全纯三次微分在 puncture 处亚纯，并给出极点阶数的严格上界；本质奇点则排除任何多项式面积上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射特殊 Kähler 度量与其关联全纯三次微分在 puncture 附近的分析性质，刻画面积增长速率如何控制三次微分的极点行为。

Method: 对 (g, Ξ) 构造曲率为 -1 的共形伪度量，应用 Ahlfors--Schwarz 引理，再结合全纯函数的次均值不等式进行估计。

Result: 若同心 punctured 邻域的 g-面积至多多项式增长，则 q 在 puncture 处亚纯；面积上界 eps^{-N} 迫使 q 的每个极点阶数严格小于 N+3；三次微分的本质奇点排除所有多项式面积上界。

Conclusion: 建立了面积增长与三次微分极点阶数之间的定量联系，并讨论了恒为零的三次微分及导致严格极点阶数界定的临界对数增长情形。

Abstract: Let $g=w|dz|^2$ be an affine special Kähler metric on a punctured disc and let $Ξ=q(z)\,dz^3$ be its associated holomorphic cubic differential. We prove that if the $g$-area of concentric punctured neighbourhoods grows at most polynomially, then $q$ is meromorphic at the puncture. More precisely, an area bound of order $\eps^{-N}$ forces every pole of $q$ to have order strictly less than $N+3$. Hence an essential singularity of the cubic differential rules out every polynomial area upper bound. The proof associates to $(g,Ξ)$ a curvature $-1$ conformal pseudometric and applies the Ahlfors--Schwarz lemma, followed by the submean inequality for holomorphic functions. We also discuss the identically zero cubic differential and the borderline logarithmic growth responsible for the strict pole-order bound.

</details>


### [254] [Spiral Harmonic Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32191)
*Hai Zhang,Yongsheng Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究由两条球面特征映射通过S³中的轮廓曲线耦合得到的调和映射，源空间配备双挠曲积度量，且源挠曲与轮廓幅值无关。该框架导出两类互补问题：由给定轮廓或源数据重构相容源度量，以及在源度量固定时寻找闭合轮廓。对任意给定挠曲，约化拉格朗日量的均质化给出强凸锥Finsler度量，其有向图测地线恰为调和轮廓。在单位速度逆问题中，相位动量将重构化为标量幅值方程，得到全局重构及闭合轮廓稠密的周期族。在固定度量问题中，时间变换给出自治Neumann系统，一个固定源度量承载连续族闭合轮廓与无穷多闭合图测地线；对固定偶数p,q≥2，S¹×S^p×S^q上Neumann类中的每个固定度量都支持度为4m的调和映射。匹配常挠曲情形下退化为显式特征映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文受螺旋极小积的系统研究启发，旨在通过双挠曲积度量框架统一考察球面调和映射的构造。核心动机是揭示调和映射的轮廓曲线可由锥Finsler几何刻画，并借此将调和映射问题转化为逆问题（由轮廓/源数据重构度量）与正问题（固定度量下寻找闭合轮廓），从而得到全局重构结果与闭合轮廓的稠密性/连续性结论。

Method: 在源空间S¹×S^p×S^q上采用双挠曲积度量，源挠曲函数与轮廓幅值无关，将两条球面特征映射通过S³中的轮廓曲线耦合。对约化拉格朗日量进行均质化，构造强凸锥Finsler度量，建立其有向图测地线与调和轮廓的对应关系。单位速度逆问题中利用相位动量把重构约化为标量幅值方程；固定度量问题中通过时间变换化为自治Neumann系统。

Result: 逆问题方面，由给定幅值、单个源挠曲或单调体积因子得到全局重构，并构造出闭合轮廓在有限覆盖上稠密的周期族。固定度量方面，一个固定源度量承载连续族闭合轮廓与无穷多闭合图测地线。对固定偶数p,q≥2，S¹×S^p×S^q上Neumann类中每个固定度量都支持度为4m（m≥1）的调和映射到S^{p+q+1}；匹配常挠曲情形退化为显式特征映射。

Conclusion: 双挠曲积框架下，调和轮廓可等价刻画为锥Finsler度量的有向图测地线，使得重构问题与闭合轮廓存在问题得到统一处理。所得全局重构、周期族稠密性、连续族闭合轮廓以及度为4m的调和映射存在性，推广并统一了既有结果；匹配常挠曲时的显式特征映射提供了可验证的基准情形。

Abstract: Motivated by the systematic study of spiral minimal products, we consider harmonic maps obtained by coupling two spherical eigenmaps through a profile curve in $\mathbb S^3$, with a doubly warped product metric on the source. The source warps are independent of the profile magnitudes. This gives two complementary problems: reconstructing a compatible source metric from prescribed profile or source data, and finding closed profiles when the source metric is fixed. For arbitrary prescribed warps, homogenization of the reduced Lagrangian gives a strongly convex conic Finsler metric whose oriented graph geodesics are exactly the harmonic profiles. In the unit-speed inverse problem, the phase momenta reduce reconstruction to a scalar magnitude equation. We obtain global reconstructions from a prescribed magnitude, one source warp, or a monotone volume factor, together with periodic families in which profiles closing on finite covers are dense. In the fixed-metric problem, a change of time gives an autonomous Neumann system. One fixed source metric then carries continuous families of closed profiles and infinitely many closed graph geodesics. For fixed even $p,q\geq 2$, every fixed metric in this Neumann class on $\mathbb S^1\times\mathbb S^p\times\mathbb S^q$ supports harmonic maps into $\mathbb S^{p+q+1}$ of degree $4m$ for every $m\geq 1$. In the matched constant-warp case, these maps reduce to explicit eigenmaps.

</details>


### [255] [Gravitational instantons and hyperbolic space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32243)
*Nobuhiro Honda*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了A_odd型环面引力瞬子的Hitchin非标准minitwistor空间到简单双曲orbifold的minitwistor空间的退化，并证明相应的minitwistor线族在此退化下收敛，从而非标准Einstein–Weyl空间收敛到该双曲orbifold。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究引力瞬子（gravitational instantons）与非标准Einstein–Weyl几何之间的联系，理解环面引力瞬子所对应的minitwistor空间在极限情形下的几何行为。

Method: 构造与A_odd型环面引力瞬子相关联的Hitchin非标准minitwistor空间的退化族，令其退化为简单双曲orbifold的minitwistor空间，并分析相应minitwistor线族的收敛性。

Result: 所构造的退化使非标准minitwistor空间收敛到简单双曲orbifold的minitwistor空间，对应的minitwistor线族在此退化下也收敛。

Conclusion: 由此推出相关的非标准Einstein–Weyl空间收敛到该双曲orbifold，给出了引力瞬子与非标准Einstein–Weyl几何之间的一种极限对应关系。

Abstract: We construct a degeneration of Hitchin's non-standard minitwistor spaces associated with toric gravitational instantons of type A$_{\rm odd}$ to the minitwistor space of a simple hyperbolic orbifold. We show that the corresponding families of minitwistor lines also converge under this degeneration. Consequently, the associated non-standard Einstein--Weyl spaces converge to the hyperbolic orbifold.

</details>


### [256] [Isoperimetric bounds for the weighted Neumann and Steklov eigenvalues in dimensions three through six](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33555)
*Daguang Chen,Hao Liu,Chengxi Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文确定了维度 3≤n≤6 时质量归一化第一加权 Neumann 特征值在欧氏有界域上的尖锐等周上界，并给出了相应的尖锐严格 Steklov 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究质量归一化第一加权 Neumann 特征值的最优等周上界，并回答 Vinokurov 提出的 Question 1.12。

Method: 利用正则旋转调和映射剖面的第一驻定半径角 ϑ_n，对有限非负非原子测度导出等周型特征值不等式，并分析球域上由光滑正径向密度达到极值；同时对具有有限边界测度的容许域证明严格 Steklov 不等式，并用 C^1 穿孔域逼近其常数。

Result: 对 3≤n≤6 得到 \overlineλ_1^N(Ω,μ) ≤ n(n-1)/(n-2) ω_n sin^2ϑ_n (|Ω|/ω_n)^{(n-2)/n}，等号由球上光滑正径向密度达到；并证明 σ_1(Ω)|∂Ω||Ω|^{(2-n)/n} < n(n-1)/(n-2) ω_n^{2/n} sin^2ϑ_n，常数由 C^1 穿孔域逼近。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了质量归一化第一加权 Neumann 特征值的尖锐等周界，肯定回答了 Vinokurov 的 Question 1.12，并建立了对应的尖锐严格 Steklov 界，其最优常数可由穿孔域逼近。

Abstract: For $3\le n\le6$, we determine the sharp isoperimetric bound for the mass-normalized first weighted Neumann eigenvalue on bounded Euclidean domains. For every finite nonnegative nonatomic measure $μ$ on $\overlineΩ$,
  \begin{equation*}
  \overlineλ_1^N(Ω,μ)\le\frac{n(n-1)}{n-2}ω_n\sin^2\vartheta_n\left(\frac{|Ω|}{ω_n}\right)^{(n-2)/n},
  \end{equation*}
  where $ω_n$ is the volume of the unit ball and $\vartheta_n\in(π/2,π)$ is the angle at the first stationary radius of the regular rotational harmonic-map profile. The bound is attained on balls by a smooth positive radial density. In fact, we affirmatively resolve Question~1.12 posed by Vinokurov \cite{Vinokurov2026}. Furthermore, for admissible domains with finite boundary measure, we also prove the sharp strict Steklov bound
  \begin{equation*}
  σ_1(Ω)|\partialΩ|\,|Ω|^{(2-n)/n}<\frac{n(n-1)}{n-2}ω_n^{2/n}\sin^2\vartheta_n,
  \end{equation*}
  whose constant is approached by $C^1$ perforated domains.

</details>


### [257] [Equivariant isometric immersions of surfaces in hyperbolic space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32300)
*Jean-Marc Schlenker*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了双曲三维空间中具有负曲率度量的曲面存在唯一的等变等距浸入，使得无穷远共形结构由高斯映射拉回，并给出其在反调和曲面情形下的对偶结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲三维空间中曲面的等距嵌入与无穷远共形结构的对应关系，并寻求与grafting映射及圆模式理论已知或猜想结论的平滑对应版本。

Method: 利用双曲高斯映射与Gauss映射的拉回构造共形结构，通过双曲端(hyperbolic end)表述等价问题，建立从度量与共形结构到等变等距浸入的唯一对应。

Result: 对紧致有向亏格≥2曲面S上曲率K∈(-1,0]的光滑度量h以及共形结构c，存在唯一的等变等距浸入(S,h)→H³，使双曲高斯映射拉回无穷远共形结构为c；对偶地，曲率为负的第三基本形式h*情形也成立唯一等变浸入。等价地，存在唯一的双曲端E与等距嵌入对(E,u)或(E,u*)。

Conclusion: 该结论给出grafting映射与圆模式相关已知或猜想命题的平滑对应，表明负曲率曲面在H³中的等距嵌入与无穷远共形结构之间存在唯一的对应关系。

Abstract: Let $S$ be a closed, oriented surface of genus at least $2$, let $h$ be a smooth Riemannian metric on $S$ with curvature $K\in (-1,0]$, and let $c\in \cT_S$ be a conformal structure on $S$. There exists a unique equivariant isometric immersion of $(S,h)$ in $\HH^3$ such that the pull-back by the hyperbolic Gauss map of the conformal structure at infinity is $c$. Dually, if $h^*$ is a smooth metric on $S$ with curvature $K^*\in (-\infty, 0)$ and if $c\in \cT_S$, there exists a unique equivariant immersion of $S$ into $\HH^3$ with third fundamental form $h^*$ and such that the pull-back of the conformal class at infinity by the Gauss map is $c$.
  Equivalently, given $h$ and $c\in \cT_S$, there is a unique pair $(E,u)$ where $E$ is a hyperbolic end with conformal structure at infinity $c$ and $u$ is an isometric embedding of $(S,h)$ in $E$. Given $h^*$ and $c$, there exists a unique pair $(E,u^*)$, where $E$ is a hyperbolic end with conformal structure at infinity $c$, and $u^*:S\to E$ is an embedding inducing the third fundamental form $h^*$.
  Those statements can be considered as a smooth counterpart of known or conjectural statements on the grafting map and on circle patterns.

</details>


### [258] [A decomposition theorem for elliptic structures of minimal rank on compact Lie groups and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32335)
*Guido Drei,Max Reinhold Jahnke*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明奇数维紧李群上最小秩的左不变椭圆结构存在代数分解，并据此将该结构的上同调计算约化到极大环面上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复情形下Pittie、以及Charbonnel与Khalgui对超曲面型左不变CR结构已获得代数分解结果，本文旨在将这一思路推广到更一般的椭圆结构并简化其上同调计算。

Method: 对奇数维紧李群上的最小秩左不变椭圆结构构造与Pittie及Charbonnel–Khalgui类似的代数分解，并利用该分解进行上同调约化。

Result: 证明了任意最小秩左不变椭圆结构均容许此类代数分解，并据此证明相关上同调可约化为在适当极大环面上的计算。

Conclusion: 该代数分解是复情形与CR情形已知结果的统一推广，为上同调计算提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We show that every left-invariant elliptic structure of minimal rank on an odd-dimensional compact Lie group admits an algebraic decomposition analogous to those obtained by Pittie in the complex case and by Charbonnel and Khalgui for left-invariant CR structures of hypersurface type. We then use this decomposition to prove that the cohomology associated with such an elliptic structure can be reduced to a computation on a suitable maximal torus.

</details>


### [259] [Strict lifts and cohomological obstructions in symplectic Lie--Rinehart--Jacobi prequantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32370)
*Basile Guy Richard Bossoto,Servais Cyr Gatsé,Olivier Mabiala Mikanou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究辛Lie--Rinehart--Jacobi代数中典范1-形式的严格提升问题，刻画其严格对称性为形如$X_f+fe$的元素，并把Jacobi Lie代数$(A,\{\,,\})$与其同构；对局部Hamilton元素，提升障碍为同调类$[i_x\omega]\in H^1_\rho(\G,A)$，由此得到正合列，并给出共形平凡性判据及闭曲面上的非平凡结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究辛Lie--Rinehart--Jacobi(LRJ)代数结构下，$(G,\rho,\omega)$标准扩张的典范1-形式的严格提升问题，理解其严格对称性、提升障碍及与局部Hamilton、辛curl、共形结构的关系。

Method: 在交换代数$A$上定义辛LRJ代数$(\G,\rho,\omega)$，等价于带1-余循环$\chi$和关于扭曲微分$d_\rho=d_{\bar\rho}+\chi\wedge$闭的非退化2-形式；由2-余循环$\omega$作标准扩张，研究典范1-形式$\eta$的严格提升，分析严格对称性并构造从Jacobi Lie代数到严格对称Lie代数的映射$f\mapsto X_f+fe$；用$H^1_\rho(\G,A)$中的类$[i_x\omega]$刻画局部Hamilton元素的提升障碍，并联系Okassa辛curl与$d\eta$的变分。

Result: 证明$\eta$的严格对称性恰为形如$X_f+fe$的元素，且$f\mapsto X_f+fe$给出Jacobi Lie代数$(A,\{\,,\})$与该严格对称Lie代数的同构；对局部Hamilton元素，严格提升的障碍为$[i_x\omega]\in H^1_\rho(\G,A)$，得到正合列$0\to\ham\to\hamloc\to H^1_\rho(\G,A)\to0$；将Okassa辛curl与$d\eta$的变分联系起来并建立共形平凡性判据；光滑情形退化为局部共形辛几何，在亏格$g\geq2$的闭曲面上给出非共形平凡结构且障碍空间维数为$2g-2$。

Conclusion: 辛LRJ代数的标准扩张中，典范1-形式的严格对称性由Jacobi Lie代数同构刻画，局部Hamilton提升的障碍由$H^1_\rho(\G,A)$分类；所得框架统一了局部共形辛几何，并在高亏格闭曲面上产生维数$2g-2$的障碍空间与非共形平凡结构。

Abstract: Let $(\G,ρ,ω)$ be a symplectic Lie--Rinehart--Jacobi algebra over a commutative algebra $A$, equivalently a Lie--Rinehart algebra $(\G,\barρ)$ endowed with a $1$-cocycle $χ$ and a nondegenerate $2$-form closed for the twisted differential $d_ρ=d_{\barρ}+χ\wedge$. Starting from the standard extension by the $2$-cocycle $ω$, we study the strict lifting problem for its canonical $1$-form $η$ in the LRJ setting. We prove that the strict symmetries of $η$ are precisely the elements $X_f+fe$ and that $f\mapsto X_f+fe$ identifies the Jacobi Lie algebra $(A,\{\,,\})$ with this Lie algebra of strict symmetries. For locally Hamiltonian elements, the obstruction to a strict lift is the class $[i_xω]\in H^1_ρ(\G,A)$, yielding the exact sequence $0\to\ham\to\hamloc\to H^1_ρ(\G,A)\to0$. We also relate Okassa's symplectic curl to the variation of $dη$ and establish a conformal triviality criterion. In the smooth case the framework reduces to locally conformally symplectic geometry, and on a closed surface of genus $g\geq2$ we obtain structures that are not conformally trivial and whose obstruction space has dimension $2g-2$.

</details>


### [260] [A Note on the Schouten--Nijenhuis Bracket of Lie--Rinehart Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32371)
*Servais Cyr Gatsé \and Basile Guy Richard Bossoto \and Irmely Gladesh Mabanza Nsiloulou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文从分次导子的视角重新审视Lie-Rinehart代数$(G,\rho)$上外代数$\bigwedge_A G$所携带的Gerstenhaber结构，证明Schouten-Nijenhuis扩张在系数代数$A$上的良定义性，并给出Gerstenhaber括号与Lie-Rinehart结构一一对应的完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lie-Rinehart代数上，Schouten-Nijenhuis（SN）括号能否从$G$良定义地下降到$A$-平衡外代数$\bigwedge_A G$上需要细致的验证。既有文献多将该结论视为显然或证明存在循环论证，本文旨在以分次导子为工具给出无循环的严格处理，并补全反向构造的完整证明。

Method: 以$A$上外代数$\bigwedge_A G$为分次代数，利用分次导子框架研究SN扩张。关键点是：Lie-Rinehart恒等式恰好提供使括号下降到$A$-平衡外代数所需的修正项；随后在无循环论证的前提下依次验证分次反对称性、Leibniz法则与分次Jacobi恒等式，并考察内分次导子$\adSN(P)=[P,\cdot]_{SN}$。

Result: 证明了SN括号在$A$-平衡外代数上的良定义性，其中Lie-Rinehart恒等式提供的修正项起到决定作用；建立了分次反对称性、Leibniz法则与分次Jacobi恒等式；证明了内分次导子的同态性质$[\adSN(P),\adSN(Q)]=\adSN([P,Q]_{SN})$；并完整证明了反向构造，即$\bigwedge_A G$上的Gerstenhaber括号与$G$上的Lie-Rinehart结构一一对应。

Conclusion: Lie-Rinehart代数的外代数上的Gerstenhaber结构与Lie-Rinehart结构互为等价描述，二者之间存在一一对应；该结果以分次导子语言给出了无循环的严格证明。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a commutative associative unital algebra over a field $K$ of characteristic zero, and let $(G,ρ)$ be a Lie--Rinehart algebra over $A$. We revisit the Gerstenhaber structure carried by the exterior algebra $\bigwedge_A G$ from the point of view of graded derivations. Particular attention is paid to the well-definedness of the Schouten--Nijenhuis extension over the coefficient algebra $A$: the Lie--Rinehart identity is shown to provide exactly the correction terms needed for the bracket to descend to the $A$-balanced exterior algebra. After establishing graded antisymmetry, the Leibniz rule and the graded Jacobi identity without circularity, we study the inner graded derivations $\adSN(P)=[P,\cdot]_{SN}$ and prove \[ [\adSN(P),\adSN(Q)]=\adSN([P,Q]_{SN}). \] Finally, we give a complete proof of the converse construction: Gerstenhaber brackets on $\bigwedge_A G$ are in one-to-one correspondence with Lie--Rinehart structures on $G$.

</details>


### [261] [A tentacle example for the stability of the positive mass theorem for Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32383)
*Romy Marie Merkel*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 C^2 上构造了一列具有非负且可积标量曲率的 AE Kähler 度量，其 ADM 质量趋于零，但在适当的基点选取下不能以指向 Gromov-Hausdorff 意义收敛到欧氏空间。这表明 Klemmensen 关于 Kähler 流形正质量定理稳定性结果中的切除（excision）条件不可或缺，与三维黎曼情形一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 Kähler 流形正质量定理稳定性结果中切除条件的必要性。作者希望弄清：若去掉额外假设，质量趋于零的 AE Kähler 度量序列是否必然收敛到欧氏空间；并验证该现象是否与已知的三维黎曼情形相符。

Method: 通过显式构造一列 C^2 上的渐近欧氏（AE）Kähler 度量，使其标量曲率非负且可积，ADM 质量趋于零，同时选择合适的基点，考察其在指向 Gromov-Hausdorff 拓扑下的收敛行为。

Result: 所构造的度量序列 ADM 质量趋于零，但在适当选取基点后并不指向 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛到欧氏空间，从而给出反例。

Conclusion: 在没有附加假设的情况下，Klemmensen 正质量定理稳定性结果中的切除（excision）条件是本质性的、不可省略，这一结论与三维黎曼几何中的已有发现相吻合。

Abstract: We construct a sequence of AE Kähler metrics on C^2 with nonnegative and integrable scalar curvature whose ADM mass tends to zero but which, for a suitable choice of basepoints, fails to converge to Euclidean space in the pointed Gromov-Hausdorff sense. This shows that, without any additional assumptions, the excision in Klemmensen's stability result for the positive mass theorem for Kähler manifolds is crucial, which aligns with the findings in the (3-dimensional) Riemannian case.

</details>


### [262] [Biharmonic Lagrangian $H$-umbilical submanifolds in complex space forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32506)
*Toru Sasahara*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了复空间形式中的proper biharmonic Lagrangian H-umbilical子流形，证明其仅存在于复射影空间中，且局部同余于由Hopf纤维化给出的显式模型；在复欧氏空间中的不存在性部分支持了Chen猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究proper biharmonic子流形在复空间形式中的分类问题，并针对Chen猜想（关于bi harmonic子流形在有非正截面曲率的空间形式中不存在）在复欧氏空间情形下给出部分验证。

Method: 对复空间形式中的proper biharmonic Lagrangian H-umbilical子流形进行完整分类，利用Hopf纤维化构造显式模型并判定其存在空间与局部同余关系。

Result: 此类子流形仅存在于复射影空间中，并局部同余于由Hopf纤维化得到的显式模型；在复欧氏空间中不存在proper biharmonic Lagrangian H-umbilical子流形。

Conclusion: 该分类结果刻画了复空间形式中proper biharmonic Lagrangian H-umbilical子流形的存在性，其在复欧氏空间的不存在性为Chen猜想提供了部分肯定回答。

Abstract: We give a complete classification of proper biharmonic Lagrangian $H$-umbilical submanifolds in complex space forms. Such submanifolds exist only in complex projective space and are locally congruent to an explicit model obtained via the Hopf fibration. In particular, their nonexistence in complex Euclidean space provides a partial affirmative answer to Chen's conjecture.

</details>


### [263] [Morphisms of double Lie groupoids: a simplicial approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32553)
*Daniel Álvarez,Kalin Krishna,Stefano Ronchi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文从单纯观点研究双李群胚的态射，证明余对角函子 W̄ 可从对象延拓到水平/垂直主双丛及方形 (1,1)-态射，并给出非交换 gerbe 模型等价与 Courant 代数胚 Manin 三元组积分的两个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受广义 Kähler 几何近期发展的启发，需要在单纯框架下理解双李群胚的态射结构，并为非交换 gerbe 的模型等价、Courant 代数胚的积分等构造提供统一而严格的范畴论基础。

Method: 采用单纯观点研究双李群胚态射；将余对角函子 W̄ 从对象延拓到水平与垂直主双丛以及方形 (1,1)-态射；后者给出李 2-群胚反函子之间方形与球状形态的二维态射。

Result: 第一，重新审视非交换 gerbe 的两类模型（群胚丛 gerbe 与主 2-丛），证明主 2-丛及其关联的群胚丛 gerbe 确定从流形到相应结构李 2-群胚的等价反函子。第二，精化传递 Courant 代数胚中 Manin 三元组的积分，证明同一 Courant 代数胚中不同横截 Manin 三元组对应的积分之间由辛 Morita 等价联系。

Conclusion: 由此，背景 Courant 代数胚的积分在辛 Morita 等价意义下是良定义的。

Abstract: Motivated by recent developments in generalized Kähler geometry, we study morphisms of double Lie groupoids from a simplicial viewpoint. We show that the codiagonal functor $\Wbar$ extends from objects to horizontal and vertical principal bibundles and to square-shaped $(1,1)$-morphisms. The latter determine 2-dimensional morphisms of square and globular shape between anafunctors of Lie 2-groupoids. We illustrate these constructions through two applications:
  First, we revisit the equivalence between two models of nonabelian gerbes: groupoid bundle gerbes and principal 2-bundles. We show that a principal 2-bundle and its associated groupoid bundle gerbe determine equivalent anafunctors from a manifold to the corresponding structure Lie 2-group.
  Second, we refine the integration of Manin triples in transitive Courant algebroids. We show that integrations associated with different choices of suitably transverse Manin triples in the same Courant algebroid are related by symplectic Morita equivalences. Consequently, the resulting integration of the background Courant algebroid is well defined up to symplectic Morita equivalence.

</details>


### [264] [The Kähler-Einstein metric on the third Del Pezzo surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32655)
*Timothy Buttsworth,William Hadden,Elli Heyes,Daniel Platt,Toby Wiseman*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文结合解析与计算机辅助方法，为紧致四维流形 $\mathbb{CP}_2\# 3\overline{\mathbb{CP}_2}$ 上的环面 Kähler-Einstein 度量构造显式近似爱因斯坦度量 $g$，并证明真实度量 $g_{\text{KE}}$ 与之接近，进而给出第一不变特征值界并证明该度量不具有处处正的全纯截面曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbb{CP}_2\# 3\overline{\mathbb{CP}_2}$ 已知容许环面 Kähler-Einstein 度量 $g_{\text{KE}}$，但该度量无闭式表达，导致难以研究其几何性质。因此需要一种可显式描述的近似度量来间接推断真实度量的几何特征。

Method: 采用解析方法与计算机辅助技术相结合的方式，构造显式的近似爱因斯坦度量 $g$，并证明真实 Kähler-Einstein 度量 $g_{\text{KE}}$ 与 $g$ 接近，其中接近程度与拓扑均有显式描述。

Result: 给出了显式的近似爱因斯坦度量 $g$；证明了 $g_{\text{KE}}$ 与 $g$ 的接近性（含显式的接近程度与拓扑描述）；作为应用，得到 Laplace-Beltrami 算子第一不变特征值的界，并证明该 Kähler-Einstein 度量并非处处具有正的全纯截面曲率。

Conclusion: 对无闭式的 Kähler-Einstein 度量，可通过显式近似度量定量刻画其几何，进而获得特征值估计及全纯截面曲率符号的否定性结论。

Abstract: The compact four-dimensional manifold $\mathbb{CP}_2\# 3\overline{\mathbb{CP}_2}$ is known to admit a toric Kähler-Einstein metric $g_{\text{KE}}$, but the metric is not known in closed form, which makes it difficult to draw conclusions about its geometry. In this article, we use a combination of analytic and computer-assisted techniques to produce an approximate Einstein metric $g$ described explicitly herein, and also prove that the true Einstein metric $g_{\text{KE}}$ is close to $g$, where both the closeness and the topology are described explicitly. As an application, we prove bounds on the first invariant eigenvalue of the Laplace-Beltrami operator, and prove that this Kähler-Einstein metric does not have positive holomorphic sectional curvature everywhere.

</details>


### [265] [Positive sectional curvature and non-isometric circle actions on a family of eleven-spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32680)
*Shaoqiang Deng,Zhiguang Hu,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文通过非等距圆周作用构造正截面曲率度量，在一族同伦十一维球面上，以四元数Hopf丛的规范变换描述球面，构造相容度量并磨光逆度量，使三个有效圆周作用在包括中间度量与最终度量在内的各偏度量下均非等距，得到显式正下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正曲率度量的构造，特别是利用非等距圆周作用（而非传统等距对称）来生成正截面曲率，拓展正曲率度量的构造手段并给出显式曲率下界。

Method: 针对Eells–Kuiper不变量构成Z/992偶数子群的一族同伦十一维球面，先用四元数Hopf丛的规范变换描述球面；再在两个圆盘上构造相容度量，在保持作用公式的前提下磨光其逆度量；最后通过局部共轭使圆周作用变为非等距。

Result: 对每个成员构造出光滑背景度量q与三个有效圆周作用生成元W1,W2,W3，使得g^{-1}=q^{-1}+Σ W_a⊗W_a对应的度量具有正截面曲率；每个作用对每个偏度量（含起始度量与最终度量）均非等距，并得到显式正下界2^{-54}(1+M_{0,k}+M_{1,k})^{-28}，其中M_{0,k}、M_{1,k}为固定联络的曲率及其一阶协变导数的范数，下界可依赖于族中成员。

Conclusion: 该工作在同伦十一维球面上实现了由非等距圆周作用生成正截面曲率度量，并给出依赖成员的显式正曲率下界，为通过非等距对称构造正曲率度量提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study the construction of positively curved Riemannian metrics by non-isometric circle actions. For a family of homotopy eleven-spheres whose Eells--Kuiper invariants form the even subgroup of $\mathbb{Z}/992$, we construct, on each member, a smooth background metric $q$ and three effective circle actions with generators $W_1,W_2,W_3$ such that the metric determined by $g^{-1}=q^{-1}+\sum_{a=1}^3W_a\otimes W_a$ has positive sectional curvature. Each action is non-isometric for every partial metric, including its incoming metric and the final metric. We first describe the sphere by gauge transformations of the quaternionic Hopf bundle. We then construct compatible metrics on two disks and smooth their inverse metrics while preserving the action formula. A local conjugation makes the circle actions non-isometric. For each fixed member, we obtain an explicit positive lower bound $2^{-54}(1+M_{0,k}+M_{1,k})^{-28}$, where $M_{0,k}$ and $M_{1,k}$ are norms of the curvature and its first covariant derivative for its fixed connection. The bound may depend on the member of the family.

</details>


### [266] [The relative isoperimetric profile of a unit square with a central square removed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32946)
*Kyle Byassee,Hunter Johnson,Brandon Jones,Ezra Nance*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文计算了"单位正方形挖去中心小正方形"这一带孔区域上的精确相对等周剖面，即给出一个函数，对任意给定面积返回在该区域内所能达到的最小相对周长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对等周问题研究在给定区域内部，固定面积集合的最小相对周长（即与区域边界的交界长度）。对于带孔（含洞）区域，边界条件更为复杂，中心挖空的单位正方形是典型且具有代表性的几何模型。此前对这类具体区域的精确解缺乏系统性结果，而这类精确剖面在几何测度论和变分问题中具有基础意义。

Method: 采用几何分析与变分方法，将问题视为在带孔区域内求固定面积的相对周长泛函的最小化问题。通过分析相对周长最小集合的几何结构（例如与边界正交的圆弧或直线段构成的接触集），对区域进行分情形讨论，并据此推导各面积区间上最小相对周长的精确表达式。

Result: 得到了相对等周剖面的精确闭式表达：对中心小正方形固定尺寸的情形，给出了一个函数，其输入为区域内任意可测集合的面积，输出为该面积下最小可能的相对周长，从而完整刻画了该区域的等周性质。

Conclusion: 带孔正方形区域的相对等周问题可获得精确解析解，其剖面函数依面积大小呈现分段结构。该结果补充了相对等周剖面的相变现象研究，并为带障碍或带洞区域中周长最小化问题提供了可参照的显式例子。

Abstract: We calculate the exact values of the relative isoperimetric profile for a unit square with a central square removed. In other words, given a removed central square of a fixed size, we provide a function which produces the smallest relative perimeter which bounds any possible area within this shape.

</details>


### [267] [Alternative proof of the gradient estimate for complex Hessian equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33091)
*Jianchun Chu,Yaxiong Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对紧Kähler流形上的复Hessian方程给出梯度估计的一个新证明，避免使用爆破（blow-up）论证，并明确了梯度估计对右端项的依赖关系；同时将类似方法推广到带边Kähler流形的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的复Hessian方程梯度估计通常依赖爆破（blow-up）论证，且估计对右端项的依赖关系不够清晰。本文旨在提供不依赖爆破的替代证明，并阐明这种依赖关系，同时探讨带边情形。

Method: 采用不依赖爆破论证的替代方法，对紧Kähler流形上的复Hessian方程进行梯度估计；并将类似方法应用于带边Kähler流形。

Result: 给出了紧Kähler流形上复Hessian方程梯度估计的一个替代证明，明确刻画了估计对右端项的依赖；对带边Kähler流形相应情形也得到结果。

Conclusion: 复Hessian方程的梯度估计可在不借助爆破论证的情况下得到，且其与右端项的关系可被清晰刻画；该方法可推广至带边Kähler流形。

Abstract: For complex Hessian equations on compact Kähler manifolds, we give an alternative proof of the gradient estimate without using blow-up argument, and clarify its dependence on the right-hand side. In addition, using the similar approach, the case of Kähler manifolds with boundary is also investigated.

</details>


### [268] [Hermitian connections with parallel torsion and constant holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33374)
*Shuwen Chen,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究与满足 \(\nabla T=0\) 的 Hermitian 联络相关的常全纯截面曲率问题，证明非零常数曲率强制挠率 \(T=0\)，从而度量是 Kähler 且局部为复空间形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非 Kähler 几何中长期存在一个猜想：具有常 Chern 全纯截面曲率的紧 Hermitian 流形，若常数为非零则应为 Kähler，若常数为零则应为 Chern 平坦。本文旨在研究更一般的 Hermitian 联络 \(\nabla\)（满足 \(\nabla T=0\)）情形下的对应问题，以推进对这一猜想的理解。

Method: 首先证明若 \(\nabla\)-全纯截面曲率是常数，则曲率的 \((1,1)\)-部分为 \(\nabla\)-平行。主要结果的证明是逐点的（pointwise），不要求紧性或完备性。

Result: 证明了非零常数 \(\nabla\)-全纯截面曲率强制挠率 \(T=0\)；由此度量是 Kähler 的，且局部是复空间形式。此外，将结果应用于 Chern 联络和 Bismut 联络。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了非 Kähler 几何中关于常全纯截面曲率猜想的相应结论，表明在 \(\nabla T=0\) 的 Hermitian 联络框架下，非零常数曲率必然导致 Kähler 与局部复空间形式，并给出了对 Chern 与 Bismut 联络的应用。

Abstract: A long-standing conjecture in non-Kähler geometry states that a compact Hermitian manifold with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature should be Kähler when the constant is nonzero and Chern flat when the constant is zero. In this article, we study the corresponding problem for a Hermitian connection $\nabla$ with $\nabla T=0$. We first show that if the $\nabla$-holomorphic sectional curvature is constant, then the $(1,1)$-part of the curvature is $\nabla$-parallel. Our main result states that a nonzero constant forces $T=0$; consequently, the metric is Kähler and locally a complex space form. The proof is pointwise and requires neither compactness nor completeness. We also obtain applications to the Chern and Bismut connections.

</details>


### [269] [Timelike surfaces of constant mean and constant Gaussian curvature in anti-de Sitter 3-space via loop groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33504)
*Jorge Bravo-Gadea*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在反德西特3维空间 $\mathbb{H}^3_1\cong SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ 中，用时滞曲面与环群方法统一构造常平均曲率曲面（Gauss映射关于第一基本形式的共形结构调和）与常高斯曲率曲面（$K>-1, K\neq0$，Gauss映射关于第二基本形式调和），给出调和性刻画、Sym-Bobenko型延拓框架恢复公式，并用广义DPW方法求解几何Cauchy问题；最后证明两类构造仅相差一个规范化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作用环群描述Gauss映射为Lorentz调和的曲面：平坦Lorentz 3维空间中的常平均曲率时滞曲面，以及3维球面中的常高斯曲率曲面。作者希望将这一构造推广到反德西特3维空间 $\mathbb{H}^3_1\cong SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ 中的时滞曲面，并澄清常平均曲率与常高斯曲率两类构造之间的关系。

Method: 对 $\mathbb{H}^3_1$ 中的时滞曲面分两类处理：（1）常平均曲率曲面，其Gauss映射关于第一基本形式的共形结构调和；（2）常高斯曲率 $K>-1, K\neq0$ 曲面，其Gauss映射为浸入且关于第二基本形式调和。证明两类曲面的调和性刻画，用Sym-Bobenko型公式从延拓框架恢复曲面；借助广义DPW方法求解几何Cauchy问题，势函数由数据显式给出且不对Gauss映射作规范化。进而通过 $\lambda=\zeta^2$ 替换与常数规范变换，建立两类构造的联系。

Result: 证明了两类曲面的调和性刻画；用Sym-Bobenko型公式由延拓框架恢复曲面；用广义DPW方法求解几何Cauchy问题，势显式表达且无需规范化Gauss映射。证明两类构造仅差一个规范化：在 $\lambda=\zeta^2$ 和常数规范下，常平均曲率延拓框架 $\hat F$ 是其Gauss映射的延拓框架，且 $\hat F|_{\lambda=e^{4\theta}}\exp(\theta e_3)\hat F|_{\lambda=1}^{-1}$ 与 $e_3\hat F|_{\lambda=e^{4\theta}}e_3\exp(\theta e_3)\hat F|_{\lambda=1}^{-1}$（$e_3=\mathrm{diag}(-1,1)$）在常平均曲率曲面非平坦处分别为常高斯曲率 $1/\sinh^2\theta$ 与 $-1/\cosh^2\theta$ 的时滞曲面；前者是经典平行曲面，后者是其极曲面。给出显式族，含闭形式的延拓框架与曲面。

Conclusion: 该文将环群构造成功推广到 $\mathbb{H}^3_1\cong SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ 中的时滞曲面，统一了常平均曲率与常高斯曲率两类曲面：二者仅相差一个规范化，并分别对应经典平行曲面与极曲面。显式例子验证了构造，扩展了可积系统方法在Lorentz几何中的应用。

Abstract: The loop group description of surfaces whose Gauss map is Lorentz harmonic is known for timelike surfaces of constant mean curvature in the flat Lorentzian 3-space, and for surfaces of constant Gaussian curvature in the 3-sphere. We carry out the corresponding construction for timelike surfaces in anti-de Sitter 3-space $\mathbb{H}^3_1\cong SL(2,\mathbb{R})$: those of constant mean curvature, whose Gauss map is harmonic for the conformal structure of the first fundamental form, and those of constant Gaussian curvature $K>-1$, $K\neq0$, whose Gauss map is then an immersion and is harmonic for the second fundamental form. For both classes we prove the harmonicity characterization, recover the surfaces from extended frames by Sym-Bobenko-type formulas, and solve the geometric Cauchy problem by the generalized DPW method, with potentials written explicitly in terms of the data and no normalization of the Gauss map. We then show that the two constructions differ only by a normalization: after the substitution $λ=ζ^2$ and a constant gauge, a constant mean curvature extended frame $\hat F$ is an extended frame of its Gauss map, and the maps $\hat F|_{λ=e^{4θ}}\exp(θe_3)\hat F|_{λ=1}^{-1}$ and $e_3\hat F|_{λ=e^{4θ}}e_3\exp(θe_3)\hat F|_{λ=1}^{-1}$, with $e_3=\mathrm{diag}(-1,1)$, are timelike surfaces of constant Gaussian curvature $1/\sinh^2θ$ and $-1/\cosh^2θ$ wherever the constant mean curvature surface is not flat. The first is the classical parallel surface, the second its polar surface. Explicit families, with extended frames and surfaces in closed form, illustrate the constructions.

</details>


### [270] [A short proof of Bernstein's theorem via the Omori--Yau maximum principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33643)
*Luis J. Alias,Miguel A. Meroño*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出Bernstein定理（R³中每个完备极小图都是平面）的一个简短、自洽的证明。唯一使用的整体工具是Omori-Yau极大值原理，并仅对一个显式函数F应用一次即可完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bernstein定理的证明通常依赖复杂的整体分析工具或较重的偏微分方程理论。本文旨在提供一个更为简洁、自洽的证明，减少对外部工具的依赖，使证明结构更加清晰易懂。

Method: 以Omori-Yau极大值原理为唯一整体工具，该原理在赋予诱导度量的完备极小图上成立。构造一个由曲面角度函数与主曲率组成的显式函数F，对F建立逐点微分不等式，并对其精确应用一次Omori-Yau原理，从而迫使F处处取到最小值。

Result: 函数F在其定义域上处处达到最小值，由此推出曲面为平面，进而得到Bernstein定理成立。

Conclusion: 该证明表明，仅借助Omori-Yau极大值原理和单一显式函数的逐点微分不等式，即可简洁地证明R³中的Bernstein定理，体现了几何分析中整体极大值原理的有效性与简洁性。

Abstract: We give a short, self-contained proof of Bernstein's theorem: every entire minimal graph in $R^3$ is a plane. The only global tool is the Omori-Yau maximum principle, which holds on entire minimal graphs with their induced metric. We apply it exactly once to a single explicit function $F$ built from the surface's angle function and principal curvatures. A pointwise differential inequality for $F$, combined with the Omori-Yau principle, forces it to attain its minimum value everywhere, which yields the planarity of the surface and hence Bernstein's theorem.

</details>


### [271] [RC-positivity of complex surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33656)
*You-Cheng Chou,Kuang-Ru Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 紧复曲面若具非平坦Ricci-flat Kähler度量，则其全纯切丛可赋RC-正Hermitian度量，并由此给出RC-正性的一系列反例性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RC-正性（RC-positivity）这一正性概念的性质与边界，特别是它与更严格的uniform RC-positivity之间的关系，以及它在张量、外积、对称幂运算下的保持性，并检验其是否蕴含底流形的有理连通性。

Method: 构造紧复曲面上的非平坦Ricci-flat Kähler度量；利用通过Weyl算子反自对偶部分对RC-正性的刻画；应用四维Einstein流形上的Weitzenböck公式；对Ricci-flat度量作共形扰动。

Result: 证明了此类曲面的全纯切丛上存在RC-正Hermitian度量；进一步证明RC-正性在取张量积、外积或对称幂后不被保持；RC-正性严格弱于uniform RC-positivity；全纯切丛的RC-正性不一定蕴含底流形的有理连通性。

Conclusion: RC-正性是一种比uniform RC-positivity更弱的正性概念，且不具备运算封闭性与对有理连通性的蕴含关系，说明其作为正性条件的局限性与反例来源。

Abstract: Given a compact complex surface with a nonflat Ricci-flat Kähler metric, we show that its holomorphic tangent bundle admits an RC-positive Hermitian metric. The proof relies on a characterization of RC-positivity through the anti-self-dual part of the Weyl operator, the Weitzenböck formula on the four dimensional Einstein manifold, and a conformal perturbation on the Ricci-flat metric.
  As a consequence, we prove that RC-positivity is not preserved after taking tensor, exterior, or symmetric power. Moreover, we show that RC-positivity is a strictly weaker notion than uniform RC-positivity, and that RC-positivity of holomorphic tangent bundle does not necessarily imply rational connectedness of the base manifold.

</details>


### [272] [Instability and Morse Index of Exponentially Subelliptic Harmonic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33789)
*Xin Huang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究指数亚椭圆调和映射的Morse指数，证明其有限性，并通过外在与内在两类检验空间给出指数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在括号生成分布（次黎曼几何）背景下，调和映射的Morse指数理论尚不完善，需建立指数有限性并寻找可计算的估计方法。

Method: 在闭流形M上，对指数形式分别采用两种检验空间：外在方法对投影的环境平行标架取平均，将估计化归为含四次指数项的外在标量的迹恒等式；内在方法使用拉回丛的平行截面，使指数形式下降为耦合二次型，当存在整体平行标架时该二次型为精确形式。

Result: 建立了指数的有限性，得到外在迹恒等式与内在耦合二次型两种估计；结果适用于圆球、球面乘积及凸超曲面；在闭Heisenberg幂零流形上，内在约化给出指数的谱公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为指数亚椭圆调和映射的Morse指数提供了统一的有限性与估计框架，并在典型几何（球面、凸超曲面、Heisenberg幂零流形）上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a closed manifold with a bracket-generating distribution $H$. We study the Morse index of exponentially subelliptic harmonic maps. After establishing finiteness, we estimate the index through two test spaces. The extrinsic one averages the index form over projected ambient parallel frames, reducing the estimate to a trace identity involving an extrinsic scalar with a quartic exponential term. The intrinsic one uses parallel sections of the pullback bundle, on which the index form descends to a coupled quadratic form that is exact when a global parallel frame exists. The bounds apply to round spheres, products of spheres, and convex hypersurfaces. On a closed Heisenberg nilmanifold, the intrinsic reduction yields a spectral formula for the index.

</details>


### [273] [Yet Another Family of Complex Structures on $S^6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33826)
*Wenfei Liu,Sönke Rollenske*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文类比近期 $S^6$ 复结构构造，构造出一类新的复结构族，且与已知的复结构族互不相交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $S^6$ 上构造复结构是一个长期未决的经典问题，近期已出现相关构造；本文旨在沿类似思路给出新的例子，并厘清其与既有构造的关系。

Method: 沿用近期构造 $S^6$ 复结构的类似路径/框架，进行推广以得到新的复结构族。

Result: 成功构造出另一族 $S^6$ 上的复结构。

Conclusion: 新构造的复结构族与已知的复结构族不相交，说明其为本质上不同的新构造。

Abstract: Following a similar path as the recent construction of a complex structure on $S^6$ we construct another such family, disjoint from the known ones.

</details>


### [274] [Heintze--Karcher-Type Inequalities for the $p$-Laplacian and Serrin-Type Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34252)
*Matheus Nunes Soares,Weiller F. Chaves Barboza*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为$p$-Laplacian非线性Dirichlet问题正解建立了参数化Heintze--Karcher型不等式，并研究等号情形下的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heintze--Karcher型不等式是Riemann几何中描述流形体积增长与曲率关系的经典结果，但将其推广到$p$-Laplacian非线性Dirichlet问题正解的情形尚缺乏参数化的统一框架。作者希望借助Reilly型恒等式与加权Hessian不等式，在Ricci曲率下界与边界平均曲率条件下获得此类估计，并与已有结果比较，进而刻画等号对应的几何结构。

Method: 在有界Riemann区域上，对$p\geq2$的$p$-Laplacian非线性Dirichlet问题正解，在Ricci曲率下界、正边界平均曲率及适当的结构性和逼近假设下，利用正则化的Reilly型恒等式与加权Hessian不等式推导参数化估计。对$p=2$情形，通过精确的亏量（deficit）恒等式，将逐点条件$f'\leq nk$替换为加权积分条件。

Result: 建立了参数化Heintze--Karcher型不等式，并与先前估计进行了比较；刻画了等号所对应的几何性质。对$p=2$给出了精确亏量恒等式及更弱的加权积分条件。

Conclusion: 作为应用，得到Heintze--Karcher型与Soap Bubble型刚性结果：等号成立时，区域必为度量球，且解为径向解。

Abstract: We establish a parameterized Heintze--Karcher-type inequality for positive solutions of nonlinear Dirichlet problems involving the $p$-Laplacian, with $p\geq2$, on bounded Riemannian domains. Under a Ricci curvature lower bound, positive boundary mean curvature, and suitable structural and approximation assumptions, the estimate follows from a regularized Reilly-type identity and a weighted Hessian inequality. We compare the resulting bound with previous estimates and investigate the geometry associated with equality. For $p=2$, an exact deficit identity allows the pointwise condition $f'\leq nk$ to be replaced by a weighted integral condition. As applications, we obtain Heintze--Karcher and Soap Bubble-type rigidity results, with equality forcing the domain to be a metric ball and the solution to be radial.

</details>


### [275] [Stable Constant Mean Curvature Hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{H}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34283)
*Filippo Gaia,Rafe Mazzeo,Ivan Miranda*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在双曲空间 $\mathbb H^n$ ($n\ge4$) 中，对每个接近 $n-1$ 的平均曲率 $H$，存在关于 Schottky 群不变的、具有无穷多个端、真嵌入且强稳定的 CMC 超曲面，从而表明该曲率范围内稳定的 Bernstein 型刚性失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲空间中常平均曲率（CMC）超曲面的刚性现象。已知道当 $H$ 较大时存在 Bernstein 型刚性，作者希望探究当平均曲率 $H$ 接近临界值 $n-1$ 时是否仍保持刚性，从而界定刚性成立的阈值范围。

Method: 构造性方法：利用双曲空间中的 Schottky 群作用，结合几何分析技术（如极小/CMC 曲面的存在性与稳定性分析），构造在群作用下不变、真嵌入的强稳定 CMC 超曲面，并证明其具有无穷多个端。

Result: 对每个 $n\ge4$ 及靠近 $n-1$ 的 $H$，成功构造出平均曲率为 $H$、具有无穷多个端且强稳定的真嵌入 CMC 超曲面——这是高余维数下非平凡的拓扑结构示例。

Conclusion: 在 $H$ 接近 $n-1$ 的范围内，稳定的 Bernstein 型刚性定理不再成立，说明双曲空间中 CMC 超曲面的刚性存在一个与 $H$ 相关的临界区间。

Abstract: For every $n\geq 4$ and every $H$ in a neighborhood of $n-1$ (depending on $n$), we prove the existence of a properly embedded strongly stable CMC hypersurface in $\mathbb H^n$ with mean curvature $H$ and infinitely many ends, invariant under the action of a Schottky group. In particular, stable Bernstein-type rigidity fails in this range.

</details>


### [276] [Counterexamples to preservation of flat normal bundles under mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34291)
*Keita Kunikawa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中一个嵌入环面与一个整体图，其法丛初始平坦但在平均曲率流下瞬间失去该性质；并给出例子表明平行主法向条件在平均曲率流下不被保持，即便法丛沿流保持平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察平均曲率流对子流形法丛几何性质（平坦性、平行主法向条件）的影响，厘清这些性质在流下的保持性。

Method: 通过在 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中构造嵌入环面和整体图作为具体例子，分析其法丛的平坦性随时间演化的情况。

Result: 所构造的例子中法丛初始平坦，但在平均曲率流下瞬间失去平坦性；另有一例显示平行主法向条件不被平均曲率流保持，而法丛在该流下仍保持平坦。

Conclusion: 法丛平坦性与平行主法向条件均非平均曲率流的不变量，二者在流下的保持性相互独立。

Abstract: We construct an embedded torus and an entire graph in $\mathbb{R}^4$ whose normal bundles are initially flat but lose this property instantaneously under mean curvature flow. We also give an example showing that the parallel principal normal condition is not preserved under mean curvature flow, even though the normal bundle remains flat along the flow.

</details>


### [277] [Determination of all diffeomorphism invariant tensor fields on the space of smooth positive densities on a compact manifold with corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34601)
*Peter W. Michor*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文将"闭流形上唯一不变弱黎曼度量（Fisher–Rao度量的倍数）"这一结果推广到带角紧流形，并完整刻画了光滑正概率密度空间上的所有微分同胚不变张量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结论仅在闭流形（维数大于1）上成立：密度空间上微分同胚群不变的弱黎曼度量必为Fisher–Rao度量的倍数。作者希望突破闭流形这一几何限制，把结论推广到带角紧流形，并把"度量"这一层提升到"所有不变张量场"这一更一般的分类问题。

Method: 将原证明框架从无边闭流形推广到带角紧流形：在光滑正概率密度空间上考虑微分同胚群的作用，利用不变性约束，对不变张量场进行整体分类，其中弱黎曼度量情形作为特例给出。

Result: 在带角紧流形上证明了：微分同胚不变的弱黎曼度量仍为Fisher–Rao度量的倍数；并确定了光滑正概率密度空间上的全部微分同胚不变张量场。

Conclusion: Fisher–Rao度量的唯一性（在不变弱黎曼度量意义下）不依赖于闭流形假设，可推广至带角紧流形；不变张量场的完整分类则给出了该唯一性结果的更一般形式。

Abstract: It was proved in [3] that on a closed manifold of dimension greater than one, every smooth weak Riemannian metric on the space of smooth positive probability densities, that is invariant under the action of the diffeomorphism group, is a multiple of the Fisher-Rao metric. Here we extend this result to compact manifolds with corners, and we determine all diffeomorphism invariant tensor fields on the space of smooth positive probability densities.

</details>


### [278] [The Asymptotic Plateau Problem for $p$-Convex Hypersurfaces in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34751)
*Shujun Shi,Zhenan Sui*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在双曲空间中解决了由主曲率p重和的正规化几何平均所给出曲率的渐近Plateau问题，证明了对每个$n\ge3$、$2\le p\le n-1$、$\sigma\in(0,1)$，任意具有光滑平均凸边界的$\mathbb{R}^n$中有界域$\Omega$都存在唯一完备容许竖直图，具有指定曲率$\sigma$和渐近边界$\partial\Omega\times\{0\}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已知的$(n,p)=(3,2)$和$(4,3)$情形推广到$n\ge3$、$2\le p\le n-1$的一般范围，推进双曲空间中渐近Plateau问题在更一般曲率设定下的研究。

Method: 通过逼近的Dirichlet问题求解，关键在于获得关于正边界高度一致（均匀）的曲率估计；证明中为线性化约束下的完整三阶导数二次型建立了两个下界：系数为$2/p$的尖锐通用下界，以及当相关主曲率落在特定范围内时更强的方向依赖下界。

Result: 对每个$n\ge3$、$2\le p\le n-1$、$\sigma\in(0,1)$，每个具有光滑平均凸边界的$\mathbb{R}^n$中有界域$\Omega$都恰有一个完备容许竖直图，具有指定曲率$\sigma$和渐近边界$\partial\Omega\times\{0\}$；并将已知的$(3,2)$和$(4,3)$情形扩展到了所述范围。

Conclusion: 该工作将双曲空间渐近Plateau问题的唯一可解性推广到由主曲率p重和的正规化几何平均定义的更一般曲率族，核心工具是关于边界高度一致的三阶导数二次型下界估计。

Abstract: We solve the asymptotic Plateau problem in hyperbolic space for the curvature given by the normalized geometric mean of the $p$-fold sums of the principal curvatures. For every $n\ge3$, $2\le p\le n-1$, and $σ\in(0,1)$, each bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ with smooth mean-convex boundary admits a unique complete admissible vertical graph with prescribed curvature $σ$ and asymptotic boundary $\partialΩ\times\{0\}$. The result extends the known cases $(n,p)=(3,2)$ and $(4,3)$ to the stated range. The key curvature estimate is uniform with respect to the positive boundary height in the approximating Dirichlet problems. The proof establishes two lower bounds for the full third-derivative quadratic form under the linearized constraints: a sharp universal bound with coefficient $2/p$ and a stronger direction-dependent bound when the relevant principal curvature lies in a specified range.

</details>


### [279] [Rigidity of Lagrangian submanifolds with conformal Maslov form and Legendrian capillary boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34787)
*Dong Gao,Yong Luo,Hui Ma,Jiabin Yin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对复单位球中满足共形Maslov形式与Legendrian毛细边界的浸入Lagrange n-球（n≥2）进行分类，证明其像或为赤道Lagrange盘，或包含于以原点为中心的Whitney球中；二维情形证实了Li-Wang-Weng的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于Lagrange子流形的分类研究，特别是具有特殊变分性质（共形Maslov形式）与特定边界条件（Legendrian毛细边界）的浸入球的刻画，并旨在验证Li、Wang、Weng提出的一个猜想。

Method: 对复单位球内光滑浸入的Lagrange n-球施加共形Maslov形式与Legendrian毛细边界条件，通过几何分析手段（如变分、极大值原理等）进行分类刻画。

Result: 任何此类浸入的像要么是赤道Lagrange盘，要么包含于以原点为中心的Whitney球中；在二维情形下，确认了Li、Wang、Weng的猜想。

Conclusion: 该分类定理完整刻画了满足上述条件的浸入Lagrange n-球的可能几何形态，并作为推论验证了二维情形下的Li-Wang-Weng猜想。

Abstract: We classify smoothly immersed Lagrangian $n$-balls, $n\geq 2$, in the unit ball of $\mathbb{C}^n$ with conformal Maslov form and Legendrian capillary boundary. The image of every such immersion is either an equatorial Lagrangian disk or is contained in a Whitney sphere centered at the origin. In dimension two, this confirms a conjecture of Li, Wang and Weng [Sci. China Math. 2021].

</details>


### [280] [Obata theorem of $σ_k$-flat and constant associated $k$th boundary curvature equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34835)
*Xuezhang Chen,Wei Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在上半球面上，利用Obata-Escobar论证，为σ_k-平坦且具有正常数边界B_k-曲率的方程建立了Obata定理，推广了Case-Wang的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Case-Wang [Adv. Math. 337 (2018), 83-106] 开创了σ_k-平坦与边界B_k-曲率相关方程的研究，本文旨在推进这一研究方向，为该类方程建立Obata型刚性定理。

Method: 采用Obata-Escobar论证方法（Obata-Escobar argument），在上半球面上分析σ_k-平坦且边界B_k-曲率为正常数的方程。

Result: 建立了针对σ_k-平坦且正常数边界B_k-曲率方程的Obata定理，给出了该方程解的刚性刻画。

Conclusion: 在上半球面情形下，σ_k-平坦且边界B_k-曲率为正常数的方程满足Obata型刚性结论，扩展了Case-Wang开创的相关理论。

Abstract: On the upper hemisphere, we use the Obata-Escobar argument to establish an Obata theorem for $σ_k$-flat and positive constant boundary $\mathcal B_k$-curvature equation, which was initiated by Case-Wang [Adv. Math. 337 (2018), 83-106].

</details>


### [281] [Classification of hypersurfaces with constant principal curvatures in $\mathbb{H}^m\times \mathbb{H}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34876)
*Haizhong Li,Renhao Tan,Zeke Yao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{H}^m\times\mathbb{H}^n$ 中具有常主曲率的超曲面：分类 $g\leq2$ 的情形，证明常主曲率且常乘积角函数的超曲面满足 $g\leq3$ 并给出完全分类，进而分类该类空间中的等参超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积空间 $\mathbb{H}^m\times\mathbb{H}^n$ 中常主曲率超曲面及等参超曲面的分类尚不完整，需要刻画不同主曲率个数 $g$ 下的结构。本文旨在系统分类此类超曲面，推进乘积空间等参理论。

Method: 设 $g$ 为互异常主曲率的个数。首先对 $g\leq2$ 的超曲面进行整体分类；其次在附加"常乘积角函数"条件下证明 $g\le3$；结合乘积角函数与主曲率结构完成完全分类；最后通过特例化导出等参超曲面的分类。

Result: 完成了 $g\leq2$ 情形下常主曲率超曲面的分类；证明常主曲率且常乘积角函数的超曲面必有 $g\le3$ 并给出完全分类；作为推论，分类了 $\mathbb{H}^m\times\mathbb{H}^n$ ($m\geq3, n\geq2$) 中具有常主曲率的等参超曲面。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{H}^m\times\mathbb{H}^n$ ($m\geq3, n\geq2$) 中常主曲率超曲面在 $g\leq2$ 及"常乘积角函数"条件下均可完全分类，且由此得到该类空间中常主曲率等参超曲面的完整分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{H}^m\times\mathbb{H}^n$ ($m\geq3, n\geq2$) with constant principal curvatures. Let $g$ be the number of distinct constant principal curvatures. First, we classify all such hypersurfaces with $g\leq2$. Then, we prove that a hypersurface with constant principal curvatures and constant product angle function has $g\le3$, and we obtain a complete classification of these hypersurfaces. As a corollary, we classify the isoparametric hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{H}^m\times \mathbb{H}^n$ ($m\geq3, n\geq2$) with constant principal curvatures.

</details>


### [282] [Lagrangian mean curvature flow in the Kummer K3 surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34923)
*Carlos Alberto Ochoa Flores*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在Kummer K3曲面中构造了收敛到特殊Lagrangian球面的新型Lagrangian平均曲率流，将构造归结为可用不动点论证求解的标量扰动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何分析中的核心难题之一是理解Lagrangian平均曲率流的行为，而现有全时存在并收敛到极小Lagrangian的显式流实例极少，本文旨在补充此类实例。

Method: 在Kummer K3曲面上构造Lagrangian平均曲率流，将其约化为标量扰动问题，并运用不动点论证求解。

Result: 构造出Kummer K3曲面中收敛到特殊Lagrangian球面的新型Lagrangian平均曲率流实例。

Conclusion: 该工作为Lagrangian平均曲率流提供了新的显式例子，并通过标量扰动与不动点方法为相关问题提供了可行途径。

Abstract: A major problem in geometric analysis is to understand the behaviour of the Lagrangian mean curvature flow. This has proved to be a challenging problem, in particular, not many explicit examples of flows that exist for all time and converge to a minimal Lagrangian are known. In this paper we construct new examples of Lagrangian mean curvature flows in the Kummer K3 surface that converge to special Lagrangian spheres. We reduce the construction to a scalar perturbation problem that can be solved by a fixed point argument.

</details>


### [283] [Another sharp criterion for Dirac zero modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34958)
*Guofang Wang,Mingwei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Frank–Loss提出的公开问题：在球面 $\mathbb{S}^n$ 上证明Dirac零模对应的向量场 $\mathrm{d}A^\flat$ 的 $L^{n/2}$ 范数满足尖锐下界，并刻画等号成立情形，其中 $n\ge5$ 情形归结为带重心约束的改进Sobolev不等式并给出通用估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frank–Loss提出的公开问题关注球面上Dirac零模与向量场 $A$ 的定量关系。作者希望证明 $\|\mathrm{d}A^\flat\|_{n/2}$ 的尖锐下界，并确定等号可达条件；此前仅在 $n=3$ 情形用不同方法证明，其余维数仍需处理。

Method: 论文对剩余情形分三类处理：i) $n\ge5$ 奇数；ii) $n\ge5$ 偶数；iii) $n=4$，各情形采用不同方法。对 $n\ge5$，论证关键归结为 $\mathbb{S}^n$ 上带重心约束 $\int x|u|^{2n/(n-2)}=0$ 的改进Sobolev不等式，定义最佳常数 $\mathfrak{a}_n$ 并证明其大于 $\frac{n(n-2)}{4(n^2-3n+1)}$。

Result: 作者证明了尖锐下界：$\|\mathrm{d}A^\flat\|_{n/2}\ge 2\lfloor n/2\rfloor^{-1/2}\frac{n-1}{n-2}S_n=\lfloor n/2\rfloor^{-1/2}\frac{n(n-1)}{2}\omega_n^{2/n}$。等号可达当且仅当 $n$ 为奇数；此时在共形与规范变换意义下，$\varphi$ 为Killing旋量，$A$ 为与 $\varphi$ 相关的Reeb场的实倍数。此外，在 $n\ge5$ 情形获得重心约束下Sobolev不等式的通用估计 $\mathfrak{a}_n>\frac{n(n-2)}{4(n^2-3n+1)}$。

Conclusion: 论文完整解决了Frank–Loss公开问题，建立尖锐下界并刻画奇数维等号情形。 $n\ge5$ 的证明依赖带重心约束的改进Sobolev不等式及其估计；精确确定最佳常数 $\mathfrak{a}_n$ 的值仍为未解决问题。

Abstract: We resolve an open problem posed by Frank--Loss. Let $n\ge 3$ and let $\varphi\in L^p(\mathbb{S}^n)$, with $\frac{n}{n-1}<p<\infty$, be a nontrivial Dirac zero mode on $\mathbb{S}^n$, i.e. a nonzero spinor satisfying \begin{equation*}
  D\varphi = iA\cdot\varphi, \end{equation*} where $D$ is the Dirac operator and $A$ is a vector field with $\mathrm{d} A^\flat\in L^{n/2}$. We prove the sharp lower bound \begin{equation*}
  \|\mathrm{d} A^\flat\|_{\frac{n}{2}} \ge 2\left[\frac{n}{2}\right]^{-\frac12}\frac{n-1}{n-2}S_n
  = \left[\frac{n}{2}\right]^{-\frac12}\frac{n(n-1)}{2}ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}. \end{equation*} Equality is attainable if and only if $n$ is odd; in that case, modulo conformal and gauge transformations, $\varphi$ is a Killing spinor and $A$ is a real multiple of the Reeb field associated with $\varphi$. The case $n=3$ was proved in our recent paper using a different method. In the paper we divide the remaining cases into 3 cases: i) $n\ge 5$ is odd, ii) $n\ge 5$ is even and iii) $n=4$. All these cases need to use different methods.
  For $n\ge 5$, the argument crucially reduces to an improved Sobolev inequality on $\mathbb{S}^n$ under a barycenter constraint. Specifically, for $u\in W^{1,2}(\mathbb{S}^n)$ we consider \begin{equation*}
  \mathfrak{a}_n := \inf\Bigg\{ \frac{ \int \Big(|\nabla u|^2 + \frac{n(n-2)}{4}u^2\Big) - \frac{n(n-2)}{4}ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}\|u\|_{\frac{2n}{n-2}}^2 }{ ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}\|u\|_{\frac{2n}{n-2}}^2 - \int u^2 } \,\Bigg|\, \int x|u|^{\frac{2n}{n-2}}=0,\ ω_n^{\frac{2}{n}}\|u\|_{\frac{2n}{n-2}}^2 - \int u^2>0 \Bigg\}, \end{equation*} and we obtain the following universal estimate \begin{equation*}
  \mathfrak{a}_n > \frac{n(n-2)}{4(n^2-3n+1)}, \end{equation*} which is enough for our aim. Determining the exact value of $\mathfrak{a}_n$ remains open.

</details>


### [284] [Submanifolds of higher rank with curvature normals of constant length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35007)
*Santiago Castañeda-Montoya,Guillermo Lobos,Carlos Olmos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究欧氏子流形（秩≥2、曲率法向量等长/适应第三基本形式特征值恒定）的局部几何，证明在正则点附近M可由一类"平行流形"生成，并给出曲率法向量内积恒定时特征分布的结构定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Di Scala与第三作者）完整解决了完备情形，证明了此类完备子流形具有常主曲率；但一般（非完备、局部）情形下的几何结构尚不清楚，本文希望刻画正则点附近这类子流形的局部生成方式，并推广曲率齐性（curvature-homogeneity）相关结果。

Method: 在正则点局部展开研究：(1) 利用isoparametric rank定理排除非脐点（非全脐）因子；(2) 用平行流形（parallel manifolds）的方法，将M表示为由超曲面N生成，并要求N的外在因子含于球面中、且当维数≥2时秩为1；(3) 反向通过组装这些因子构造M的例子。

Result: 证明正则点附近M可由平行流形由超曲面N生成，其外在因子落在球面内，维数≥2时秩为1，且这些因子可反拼装出M的实例；isoparametric rank定理用于排除非脐因子。进一步，当曲率法向量的内积为常数时，恰有两个特征分布，其中一个是1维且自平行（autoparallel）。

Conclusion: 在任意余维、法丛平坦的情形下推广了Tsukada的曲率齐性结果，以及Bryant、Florit、Ziller关于超曲面的近期结果；刻画了此类子流形局部由球面内秩≤1外在因子经平行流形生成的结构，并给出特征分布为两个（其一1维自平行）的精细结论。

Abstract: We study the local geometry of Euclidean submanifolds of rank at least two whose curvature normals with respect to the flat part of the normal bundle have constant length, equivalently, whose adapted third fundamental form has constant eigenvalues. The complete case was fully settled by Di Scala and the third author, who showed that such a complete submanifold has constant principal curvatures. Around a regular point, we show that $M$ is generated, via parallel manifolds, by a hypersurface $N$ whose extrinsic factors are contained in spheres and, when of dimension at least two, have rank one; conversely, such factors can be assembled to construct examples of $M$. The proof relies on the isoparametric rank theorem to rule out non-umbilical factors. As a further application, when the inner products of the curvature normals are constant, we show that there are exactly two eigendistributions, one of which is one-dimensional and autoparallel, extending, for arbitrary codimension when the normal bundle is flat, curvature-homogeneity results of Tsukada and the recent ones of Bryant, Florit, and Ziller for hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [285] [Derived deformation theory of heterotic $G_2$ systems near the standard embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35071)
*Bram Brongers*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在自旋七维流形上的固定弦 Courant 代数胚中，研究异弦 $G_2$ 结构在无挠标准嵌入附近的一阶 $\alpha'$ 与全形式阶形变，构造超势变分临界理论与物理理论的相容性扩张，证明二者局部形式同构，并通过循环转移与混合阶椭圆性给出有效势与相对上同调的阿贝尔性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究异弦 $G_2$ 结构在 Courant 代数胚框架下的形变理论，旨在统一物理超势与变分临界理论，解决小通量扇区 ${H^{[0]}=0}$ 下理论不自洽（需附加相容性恒等式）的问题，从而构建一个"已消解"的物理理论并刻画其与变分理论的局部形式关系。

Method: 采用可容许度量、旋量线与密度确定正则广义 Dirac 泛函，其在物理分裂下的表达式即异弦超势；在小通量扇区附加条件及相容性恒等式，构造物理理论与变分临界理论的相容性扩张；利用移位余切 Hamilton 构造使变分临界理论循环；借助混合阶椭圆性与同伦转移得到有限维极小模型，并做循环转移得到有效势；最后计算相容性比较的相对上同调。

Result: 两个消解在实局部形式 $Q$-理论意义下局部形式同构。当 $Y$ 闭且 gerbe 与反常扇区固定时，超势临界场与每个集中于零次的局部实 Artin 代数上的 BPS 场重合。循环转移在形式坐标变换后给出零次 Artin 代数上普通形式物理解轨迹的有效势 $W_{\mathrm{eff}}$。相对上同调可由 dg-Artin 代数探测，被变分临界理论遗忘；标准嵌入处的带点相对纤维是同伦阿贝尔的。

Conclusion: 异弦 $G_2$ 结构的物理理论与变分临界理论在局部形式意义下等价，超势临界场与 BPS 场一致，且通过循环同伦转移获得有效势；相对上同调在 dg-Artin 代数上可探测但对变分理论不可见，标准嵌入点的相对纤维同伦阿贝尔。这为小通量扇区下异弦 $G_2$ 结构的形变提供了严格的代数与同伦刻画。

Abstract: We study heterotic $G_2$ structures on a fixed string Courant algebroid on a spin seven-manifold $Y$, near a torsion-free standard embedding, to first order in $α'$ and to all formal orders in the deformations. The admissible metric, spinor line and density determine a canonical generalized Dirac functional whose expression in a physical splitting is the heterotic superpotential. We work in the small-flux sector $H^{[0]}=0$. We adjoin this condition and its compatibility identities to obtain a resolved physical theory, and construct a corresponding compatibility extension of the variational critical theory. The two resolutions are locally formally isomorphic as real local formal $Q$-theories.
  When $Y$ is closed and the gerbe and anomaly sector is fixed, the critical fields of the superpotential coincide with the BPS fields over every local real Artin algebra concentrated in degree zero. The shifted-cotangent Hamiltonian construction makes the variational critical theory cyclic. Mixed-order ellipticity and homotopy transfer give finite-dimensional minimal models, and cyclic transfer yields an effective potential $W_{\mathrm{eff}}$ for the ordinary formal physical solution locus over these degree-zero Artin algebras, after a formal coordinate change. We compute the relative cohomology of this compatibility comparison, which is detected on dg-Artin algebras and forgotten by the variational critical theory, and prove that the pointed relative fibre at the standard embedding is homotopy abelian.

</details>


### [286] [Supercritical dual k-Minkowski flows with general prescribed data: a priori estimates and asymptotic convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35241)
*Hongyi Sheng,Weimin Sheng,Jiazhuo Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究归一化流 X_t = -f(ν)|X|^α σ_k(κ)ν + βX（β=C_n^k）在超临界范围 α>k+1 下的长期行为，证明从任意光滑严格凸初始超曲面出发流存在且以显式指数率 β(α-k-1) 指数收敛到静止方程的唯一严格凸解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Bryan-Ivaki-Scheuer 仅通过从障碍出发的扩张型流证明了超临界范围 α>k+1 下静止方程严格凸解的存在性（对任意正角向数据），但未给出从任意初始数据出发的收敛性、收敛速率及极限唯一性。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 考虑单位球面上正光滑函数 f 所定义的归一化收缩流，其中含 |X|^α σ_k(κ)ν 项与线性项 βX（β=C_n^k）。核心工具是相对残差 p=(log u)_t=β-f(x)r^α σ_k(κ)/u，证明其 L^∞ 范数单调不增并以显式指数 β(α-k-1) 衰减；残差估计的证明独立于且先于曲率估计。

Result: 在超临界范围 α>k+1 下，对每个光滑严格凸且包围原点的初始超曲面，该流对所有时间 t 存在，并在 C^∞ 中指数收敛（速率为 β(α-k-1)）到 f(x)r^α σ_k(κ)=βu 的唯一光滑严格凸解。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于证明了归一化收缩流从任意初始数据的收敛性，给出显式指数收敛速率，并建立了极限的唯一性，从而补充并强化了已有关于静止方程严格凸解存在性的结果。

Abstract: Let k be an integer from 1 to n, let f be a positive smooth function on the unit sphere, and consider the normalized flow X_t = -f({nu})|X|^{alpha} sigma_k({kappa}) {nu} + beta X, beta = C_n^k. In the supercritical range alpha > k+1, we prove the flow exists for all time and converges exponentially in C^{infty}, from every smooth strictly convex initial hypersurface enclosing the origin, to the unique smooth strictly convex solution of f(x)r^{alpha} sigma_k({kappa}) = beta u. The convergence is driven by the relative residual p = (log u)_t = beta - f(x)r^{alpha} {sigma}_k({kappa})/u, whose L^{infty} norm is nonincreasing and decays with the explicit exponent beta(alpha-k-1); the residual estimates are proved before, and independently of, the curvature estimates. Existence of a strictly convex solution of the stationary equation in this range, for arbitrary positive angular data, was previously obtained by Bryan-Ivaki-Scheuer through an expanding-type flow started from a barrier; the contribution here is the convergence of the normalized contracting flow from arbitrary initial data, with an explicit exponential rate, together with uniqueness of the limit.

</details>


### [287] [Pointwise energy monotonicity for stable Higgs bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35278)
*Tianzhi Hu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Qiongling Li关于稳定SL(n,C) Higgs丛的能量密度沿C*-流逐点单调性的猜想；证明该猜想在秩2成立，但对每个秩n≥3构造反例，表明该现象本质依赖于秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qiongling Li提出一个关于能量密度沿稳定SL(n,C) Higgs丛的C*-流逐点单调性的猜想，其成立与否关系到该单调性是否为普遍现象。作者旨在验证该猜想并探讨其是否依赖于秩。

Method: 通过分析稳定SL(n,C) Higgs丛沿C*-流的能量密度行为：对秩2情形证明能量密度逐点非递减；对秩n≥3，构造使能量密度沿C*-流失去单调性的稳定SL(n,C) Higgs丛。

Result: 猜想在秩2下成立：任意稳定SL(2,C) Higgs丛的能量密度沿C*-轨道逐点非递减。相反，对每个秩n≥3，均存在稳定SL(n,C) Higgs丛，其能量密度沿C*-流不单调。

Conclusion: 能量密度沿C*-流的逐点单调性并非普遍成立，而是本质依赖于秩：秩2时猜想成立，秩≥3时失败。

Abstract: We study a conjecture of Qiongling Li concerning the pointwise monotonicity of the energy density along the $\mathbb C^*$-flow of stable $\mathrm{SL}(n,\mathbb C)$ Higgs bundles. We prove that the conjecture holds in rank two: for every stable $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb C)$ Higgs bundle, the energy density is pointwise nondecreasing along the $\mathbb C^*$-orbit. In contrast, we show that this phenomenon is genuinely rank-dependent. For every rank $n\geq 3$, we construct stable $\mathrm{SL}(n,\mathbb C)$ Higgs bundles for which the energy density fails to be monotone along the $\mathbb C^*$-flow.

</details>


### [288] [Pluriclosed deformations of Bismut flat metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35313)
*Giuseppe Barbaro*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 通过形变紧致单连通Bismut平坦流形的复结构，构造出仅具pluriclosed度量而不具Bismut平坦度量的新复流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在pluriclosed（强Kähler挠率）几何与Bismut平坦几何的交界处，构造真正新的pluriclosed流形实例，并深化对pluriclosed度量形变理论的理解。

Method: 对紧致单连通Bismut平坦流形的复结构进行形变。

Result: 得到一批新的复流形：它们承认pluriclosed度量，但不承认Bismut平坦度量，从而给出真正新的pluriclosed流形例子。

Conclusion: 主要结果有助于在任意维数下搜寻pluriclosed孤子与非平凡Bismut Hermitian Einstein度量。

Abstract: We deform the complex structure of compact simply-connected Bismut flat manifolds, obtaining new complex manifolds that admit pluriclosed metrics but do not admit Bismut flat metrics. This produces genuinely new examples of pluriclosed manifolds while enriching our understanding of the deformation theory of pluriclosed metrics. Moreover, our main result is relevant for the search of pluriclosed solitons and non-trivial Bismut Hermitian Einstein metrics in all dimensions.

</details>


### [289] [Harmonic maps from spheres with lowest possible index and their properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35589)
*Surkov Egor*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明 n>2 时从 S^n 到 CP^m 的指标为 n+1 的调和映射必为伪水平弱共形，且此时 n 必须为奇数；偶数 n 时任何非平凡调和映射指标至少为 n+2。并给出 S^4 到 Gr_C(2,4) 的指标为 5 的非伪水平弱共形调和映射，说明该结论不能推广到 Grassmann 流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面到复射影空间的调和映射指标与其几何性质（伪水平弱共形）的关系，并探讨该结论是否能推广到更一般的 Grassmann 流形。

Method: 通过度量调和映射的 Morse 指标，结合伪水平弱共形（PHWC）判据进行理论证明；并构造具体反例。

Result: 对 n>2，若 S^n→CP^m 的调和映射指标为 n+1，则其为伪水平弱共形且 n 为奇数；偶数 n 时非平凡调和映射指标不小于 n+2。存在 S^4→Gr_C(2,4) 指标为 5 且非伪水平弱共形的调和映射。

Conclusion: 调和映射指标与伪水平弱共形性质在 CP^m 情形下有强联系，但该结论不适用于一般 Grassmann 流形。

Abstract: In this note, we prove that for n > 2 a harmonic map $\mathbb{S}^n \to \mathbb{CP}^m$, of index n+1 is pseudo horizontally weakly conformal, that $n$ must be odd. In particular, for even n every non-constant harmonic map $\mathbb{S}^n \to \mathbb{CP}^m$ has index at least n+2. We also present a harmonic map from $\mathbb{S}^4$ to $\mathrm{Gr}_{\mathbb{C}}(2,4)$ of index 5 that is not pseudo horizontally weakly conformal, so the statement does not extend to Grassmannians.

</details>


### [290] [Deformations of generalized complex structures on $\mathcal{G}$-flat transitive Courant algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35640)
*Vicente Cortés,Paula Naomi Pilatus*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将Gualtieri关于精确Courant代数胚上广义复结构形变定理推广到$\mathcal{G}$-flat传递Courant代数胚，借助tame Fréchet空间与Nash-Moser隐函数定理，并赋予自等价群tame Fréchet Lie群结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gualtieri的形变定理原仅限于精确Courant代数胚，适用范围较窄；希望能将该形变理论推广到更一般的$\mathcal{G}$-flat传递Courant代数胚这一框架下。

Method: 采用tame Fréchet空间的理论框架与Hamilton的Nash-Moser（隐函数）定理。作为证明的关键步骤，为$\mathcal{G}$-flat传递Courant代数胚的自等价群赋予tame Fréchet Lie群结构，并证明存在由内导数作用的tame精确自等价子群。

Result: 成功将Gualtieri形变定理推广到$\mathcal{G}$-flat传递Courant代数胚；建立了自等价群的tame Fréchet Lie群结构，并给出其tame精确自等价子群的存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作把广义复结构的形变理论从精确Courant代数胚拓展到$\mathcal{G}$-flat传递Courant代数胚，并为后续研究提供了自等价群的tame Fréchet Lie群工具。

Abstract: We extend Gualtieri's deformation theorem for generalized complex structures on exact Courant algebroids to the more general class of $\mathcal{G}$-flat transitive Courant algebroids. Our approach relies on the framework of tame Fréchet spaces and Hamilton's Nash-Moser (implicit function) theorem. As an important step of the proof of our deformation theorem, we endow the group of autoequivalences of a $\mathcal{G}$-flat transitive Courant algebroid with a tame Fréchet Lie group structure and show that there exists a tame subgroup of exact autoequivalences, whose infinitesimal action is by inner derivations.

</details>


### [291] [Partial Regularity of Stable Stationary Harmonic Maps into Compact Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35731)
*Jacob Krantz*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了从紧黎曼流形到紧李群（带双不变度量）的稳定驻定调和映射，其奇异集的Hausdorff余维数至少为4，且该估计是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射的奇异集正则性是其理论核心问题。对于稳定驻定调和映射，以往结果给出的奇异集维数估计往往不够精细，而目标流形为群流形时其特殊几何结构（双不变度量）有望给出更强的维数下界。本文旨在确定这一最优余维数界限。

Method: 在M为紧黎曼流形、G为带双不变度量的紧李群的设定下，研究稳定驻定调和映射u: M → G，结合稳定性和驻定性条件，通过单调性公式、能量估计及奇点附近blow-up分析，对奇异集的Hausdorff维数进行估计。

Result: 证明了任意稳定驻定调和映射u: M → G的奇异集Hausdorff余维数至少为4，并构造例子说明该界是尖锐的（不可改进）。

Conclusion: 稳定驻定调和映射的奇异集正则性由Hausdorff余维数4这一最优界刻画，推广并改进了已有结果。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a compact Riemannian manifold, and let $G$ be a compact Lie group with bi-invariant metric. We show that the singular set of any stable stationary harmonic map $u : M \to G$ has Hausdorff codimension at least four. This is sharp.

</details>


### [292] [On the degenerate Weyl problem on isometric immersions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28905)
*Siran Li,Xiangxiang Su*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究退化Weyl问题，即二维球面到三维欧氏空间或一般三维流形的等距嵌入/浸入，在Gauss曲率仅非负（而非严格正）情形下取得两项结果，并同时解决了一般环境流形中的非退化Weyl问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Weyl问题要求Gauss曲率严格为正，但实际中常出现曲率在有限点退化或仅非负的度量。作者希望将Weyl问题的存在性理论推广到退化情形，并给出仅依赖内蕴几何条件的判据，同时把结果从欧氏空间推广到一般单连通三维环境流形。

Method: 通过研究退化Weyl问题，在曲率非负条件下构造等距嵌入或浸入：一是对球面上光滑度量，在K_g除有限点外严格正且这些点处K_g的Hessian正定的条件下，构造全局C^{2,1}等距嵌入；二是对一般单连通三维流形，在K_g ≥ K_0 ≥ sec_g、 (K_g-K_0)^{-1/2} ∈ L^p（p ≥ 2）以及近似非退化等距浸入满足一致pinching条件时，构造W^{3,p}等距浸入。并利用相应方法处理非退化Weyl问题（K_g>0），覆盖g, g ∈ C^{2,1}的一般环境流形情形。
