Result: 第一，对球面上光滑度量g，若K_g除有限点外严格正且在这些点处Hessian(K_g)正定，则g可全局C^{2,1}等距嵌入到R^3；这是退化Weyl问题中首个纯内蕴条件的结果。第二，对一般单连通三维流形(M, g)，若K_g ≥ K_0 ≥ sec_g、 (K_g-K_0)^{-1/2} ∈ L^p（p ≥ 2）且近似非退化等距浸入满足一致pinching条件，则存在W^{3,p}等距浸入。此外，还解决了g, g ∈ C^{2,1}时到一般单连通三维流形的非退化Weyl问题（K_g>0）。

Conclusion: 本文将Weyl问题的等距嵌入/浸入存在性从严格正Gauss曲率推广到曲率仅非负的退化情形，给出了首个纯内蕴的退化Weyl问题结果，并把结论推广到一般单连通三维环境流形，同时解决了相应非退化情形。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the Weyl problem, \emph{i.e.}, the existence of isometric immersions or embeddings of two-spheres into the three-dimensional Euclidean space or general ambient three-manifolds. We establish two results on the degenerate Weyl problem, namely when the Gaussian curvature $K_g$ is only nonnegative rather than strictly positive. First, for a smooth metric $g$ on the two-sphere, if $K_g$ is strictly positive except at finitely many points where the Hessian of $K_g$ is positive definite, then $g$ admits a global $C^{2,1}$-isometric embedding into $\mathbb{R}^3$. It appears to be the first result on the degenerate Weyl problem with purely intrinsic conditions on $g$. Second, for a general simply-connected ambient three-manifold $(\mathcal{M},{\overline{g}})$, if $K_g \geq K_0 \geq {\rm sec}_{\overline{g}}$ for some constant $K_0$, $(K_g-K_0)^{-1/2} \in L^p$ with $p \geq 2$, and a certain uniform pinching condition holds for approximate nondegenerate isometric immersions, then there exists a $W^{3,p}$-isometric immersion. Alongside we also resolve the nondegenerate Weyl problem (\emph{i.e.}, when $K_g>0$) into general simply-connected ambient three-manifolds for $g$, ${\overline{g}} \in C^{2,1}$.

</details>


### [293] [Energy convexity and uniformity of the $H$-surface flow in $\mathbb{R}^{3}$ with Dirichlet boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30740)
*Da Rong Cheng,Longzhi Lin,Xin Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中给定平均曲率曲面的能量泛函及其 $H$-曲面流的凸性估计，并证明小能量初值下解的唯一性与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画给定平均曲率（$H$-surface）问题在小 Dirichlet 能量下的能量泛函凸性，并借此建立 $H$-曲面流长时间行为与稳态解唯一性的定量理解。

Method: 首先在 $H\cdot\mathrm{vol}_{\mathbb{R}^3}$ 具有满足一定界估计的原函数时，证明限定在单位 $2$-圆盘上、具有小 Dirichlet 能量与固定边界值的映射，其能量泛函具有凸性。随后在 $H:\mathbb{R}^3\to\mathbb{R}$ 的更弱假设下，对 $H$-曲面流证明类似的凸性估计，条件是初始 Dirichlet 能量充分小；先处理经典解，再借助改自调和映射热流的定量唯一性结果，通过逼近推广到弱解。

Result: 得到了能量泛函的凸性估计以及沿 $H$-曲面流的相应估计，并将其由经典解推广到弱解。

Conclusion: 作为凸性估计的推论，单位 $2$-圆盘上小能量、属于 $C^0\cap W^{1,2}$ 的初始映射所对应的 $H$-曲面流在无穷时间一致收敛到唯一极限，该极限以相同边界值求解相应的稳态问题（$H$-曲面系统）。

Abstract: We prove that the energy functional associated with surfaces of prescribed mean curvature in $\mathbb{R}^3$ exhibits a convexity property when restricted to maps from the unit $2$-disk having small Dirichlet energy and a fixed boundary value, provided that the $3$-form $H \cdot \text{vol}_{\mathbb{R}^3}$ has a primitive satisfying certain bounds.
  Under milder assumptions on $H:\mathbb{R}^3 \to \mathbb{R}$, we show that an analogous convexity estimate holds along the heat flow of the functional (the $H$-surface flow) when the initial Dirichlet energy is sufficiently small. This is done first for classical solutions, and then extended by approximation to weak solutions using a quantitative uniqueness result adapted from previous work on the harmonic map heat flow. As a consequence of the convexity estimate, we show that the $H$-surface flow with small-energy initial map of class $C^0 \cap W^{1, 2}$ on the unit $2$-disk converges uniformly to a unique limit at infinite time, which solves the corresponding stationary problem (the $H$-surface system) with the same boundary value.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [294] [Orbit-breaking in Deaconu-Renault groupoids and models for UCT Kirchberg algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31883)
*Robin J. Deeley,Ian F. Putnam,Karen R. Strung*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 将轨道断裂(orbit-breaking)概念从同胚推广到满射局部同胚，由此构造Deaconu–Renault群胚的开子群胚；在局部扩张等假设下得到含单位的UCT Kirchberg代数，并给出嵌入O_2及实现所有稳定UCT Kirchberg代数的方法，以及由K群同态诱导^*-同态的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道断裂原是研究同胚动力系统C^*-代数的重要技术，但既有理论局限于同胚情形。作者希望将其推广到更一般的满射局部同胚(Deaconu–Renault系统)，以统一刻画UCG Kirchberg代数并显式构造其嵌入与同态。

Method: 1) 定义满射局部同胚的轨道断裂，产生Deaconu–Renault群胚的开子群胚；2) 在局部扩张等自然假设下证明所得群胚C^*-代数为含单位的UCT Kirchberg代数；3) 构造一类特定的Deaconu–Renault系统模拟Cuntz代数O_2，并给出轨道断裂代数的显式嵌入；4) 引入"去掉不动点后再断裂"的方法，实现所有稳定UCT Kirchberg代数及其到O_2⊗K的嵌入；5) 由群胚包含及可数分次阿贝尔群同态构造相应的^*-同态。

Result: 轨道断裂产生的群胚C^*-代数为含单位UCT Kirchberg代数；构造出模拟O_2的Deaconu–Renault系统并得到显式嵌入；通过"去不动点轨道断裂"可实现所有稳定UCT Kirchberg代数并嵌入O_2⊗K；任意可数分次阿贝尔群同态均可在轨道断裂代数间实现为^*-同态，从而由K群同态给出稳定UCT Kirchberg代数间的显式^*-同态。

Conclusion: 该工作将轨道断裂推广至满射局部同胚框架，为UCT Kirchberg代数提供了统一的群胚模型，并给出了嵌入与K理论同态的显式实现，深化了动力系统、群胚C^*-代数与分类理论之间的联系。

Abstract: We generalize the concept of orbit-breaking from the case of a homeomorphism to the case of a surjective local homeomorphism. This method creates open subgroupoids of the Deaconu--Renualt groupoid associated to the surjective local homeomorphism. When the map satisfies natural assumptions (including being locally expanding) the resulting groupoid $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebras are unital UCT Kirchberg algebras. A specific class of Deaconu--Renault systems are constructed, each modelling the Cuntz algebra $\mathcal{O}_2$. The $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebras obtained from orbit-breaking for such systems come with explicit embeddings into $\mathcal{O}_2$. We also introduce a method for orbit-breaking after removing a fixed point. Using this method, we can realize all stable UCT Kirchberg algebras with each coming with an embeddings into $\mathcal O_2\otimes\mathcal K$. Moreover, we show that, given a homomorphism between countable graded abelian groups, there is a $^*$-homomorphism between orbit-breaking algebras obtained from a groupoid inclusion. In particular, we obtain explicit $^*$-homomorphisms between stable UCT Kirchberg algebras from any group homomorphism between their $K$-groups.

</details>


### [295] [On generators of $\mathrm{C}_r^*(\mathbb{F}_2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32058)
*George A. Elliott,Chun Guang Li,Zhuang Niu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明约化群C*-代数 C_r*(F_2) 是单生成的，并给出其关于所构造生成元的拓扑自由熵维数为2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自由群约化群C*-代数的生成性质及其非交换维数不变量，明确 C_r*(F_2) 的单生成性与拓扑自由熵维数之间的关系。

Method: 显式构造 C_r*(F_2) 的一个单生成元，并针对该生成元计算拓扑自由熵维数。

Result: C_r*(F_2) 是单生成的；关于文中所构造的生成元，其拓扑自由熵维数为2。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了 C_r*(F_2) 尽管可由单个元素生成，其拓扑自由熵维数仍为2，说明单生成性并不限制其非交换维数，为自由熵维数与生成元选择的相关性提供了例证。

Abstract: It is shown that the reduced group C*-algebra $\mathrm{C}_r^*(\mathbb{F}_2)$ is singly generated, where $\mathbb F_2$ is the free group on two generators. With respect to the generator constructed in the paper, the topological free entropy dimension of $\mathrm{C}_r^*(\mathbb{F}_2)$ is shown to be $2$.

</details>


### [296] [Selfless nonnuclear crossed products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34011)
*Jamie Bell*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 论文证明了Bell-Geften-Kerr引入的Cantor作用类$\mathrm{A}^*(F_d,X)$的约化交叉积C*-代数是C*-无私的，并由此推出其具有稳定秩一、严格比较、唯一拟迹态以及实秩零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Cantor系统所生成约化交叉积C*-代数的结构性质，尤其针对Bell、Geffen与Kerr定义的$\mathrm{A}^*(F_d,X)$作用类，希望借助C*-无私性统一推导其正则性特征。

Method: 对属于$\mathrm{A}^*(F_d,X)$类的Cantor作用，证明其约化交叉积C*-代数满足C*-无私性；进而由该性质导出相关结构与分类不变量。

Result: 所讨论的约化交叉积C*-代数具有稳定秩一、严格比较、唯一拟迹态以及实秩零。

Conclusion: C*-无私性为该类Cantor作用交叉积代数的正则性提供了有效判据，并系统性地给出了其稳定秩、比较理论、拟迹态与实秩方面的结论。

Abstract: We prove C*-selflessness for the reduced crossed product C*-algebras of Cantor actions belonging to the class $\mathrm{A}^*(F_d,X)$ introduced by Bell, Geffen, and Kerr. We deduce that they have stable rank one, strict comparison, a unique quasitracial state, and real rank zero.

</details>


### [297] [Distances Between von Neumann Subalgebras: Spin Models, Commuting Squares, and Free Group Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34856)
*Indrajit Ghosh,Sumit Kumar*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 通过 Mashood-Taylor 距离、Kadison-Kastler 距离及内角研究 von Neumann 子代数的相对位置，得到 spin 模型子因子的距离公式，刻画了交换方阵与极大距离的关系，并区分了内角正交与 Popa 正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 von Neumann 子代数之间相对位置的不同度量方式（d_MT、d_KK 与内角）及其相互关系，澄清不同正交性概念（Popa 正交、内角正交、极大距离）之间的区别与联系。

Method: 对超有限 II_1 因子的 2×2 spin 模型子因子连续族计算 d_MT；利用交换方阵与指标条件分析距离；在自由群因子 L(F_2) 中计算 d_MT 的具体表达式，并构造不来自子群的极大距离 masas。

Result: 对 spin 模型子因子族有 d_MT(R_{H_α}, R_{H_β}) = |sin(α−β)|；两子因子在其交集上构成交换方阵当且仅当极大距离 (d_MT=1)；自然指标条件下交换方阵的 II_1 因子强制极大距离，使 d_KK=1=d_MT；Popa 正交的扩散子代数极大距离；族中任两个成员均非 Popa 正交，但极大距离时内角为 π/2；在 L(F_2) 中 d_MT(L(⟨a⟩), uL(⟨a⟩)u*) = √(1−|τ(u)|^4)；构造出 L(F_2) 中不来自 F_2 子群的极大距离 masas。

Conclusion: 极大距离与交换方阵密切关联，且内角意义下的正交区别于 Popa 正交；在自由群因子中给出了显式距离公式并构造了非子群来源的极大距离 masas。

Abstract: We investigate the relative position of von Neumann subalgebras through their Mashood-Taylor ($\mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{MT}}$) and Kadison-Kastler ($\mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{KK}}$) distances and their interior angle. For the continuous family $(\mathscr{R}_{\mathsf{H}_α})_{α\in[0,π)}$ of $2\times2$ spin model subfactors of the hyperfinite $\mathrm{II}_1$-factor $\mathscr{R}$, we establish \[ \mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{MT}}(\mathscr{R}_{\mathsf{H}_α},\mathscr{R}_{\mathsf{H}_β}) =|\sin(α-β)|. \] We prove that two such subfactors form a commuting square over their intersection if and only if they are maximally distant ($\mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{MT}}=1$). More generally, we show that commuting squares of $\mathrm{II}_1$-factors, under natural index conditions, force maximal distance, yielding $\mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{KK}}=1=\mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{MT}}$. We also prove that diffuse subalgebras orthogonal in the sense of Popa are maximally distant. In contrast, no two members of $(\mathscr{R}_{\mathsf{H}_α})_{α\in[0,π)}$ are Popa-orthogonal. Nevertheless, whenever two are maximally distant, their interior angle over their intersection is $π/2$, demonstrating that orthogonality via interior angle differs from Popa orthogonality. Finally, in the free group factor $L(\mathbb{F}_2)=L(\langle a,b\rangle)$, for $u\in L(\langle b\rangle)$, we establish \[ \mathrm{d}_{\mathrm{MT}}(L(\langle a\rangle),uL(\langle a\rangle)u^*) =\sqrt{1-|τ(u)|^4}. \] Finally, we construct maximally distant masas in $L(\mathbb{F}_2)$ that do not arise from subgroups of $\mathbb{F}_2$.

</details>


### [298] [Noncommutative Maximal Averages over Submanifolds and Variable Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34906)
*Xudong Lai,Siyu Liu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在半有限von Neumann代数上的非交换L^p空间建立算子值函数几何平均的极大不等式，覆盖固定有限型光滑子流形、多项式参数化及变超曲面情形，并给出保迹R^n作用的非交换极大遍历不等式与双边几乎一致收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典调和分析与遍历理论中的几何极大算子估计推广到非交换L^p空间，特别是针对有限型子流形、多项式参数化和满足一致旋转曲率条件的变超曲面，填补非交换极大遍历理论中几何平均情形的空白。

Method: 核心工具是适配非各向同性膨胀的正则化辅助族的弱型(1,1)不等式，其证明基于Cuculescu投影的非交换Calderón-Zygmund分解；再与基于Fourier的L^2估计插值，恢复原平均的极大不等式。变超曲面情形采用振荡L^2估计与局部化的独立论证。

Result: 对参数原点处固定有限型光滑子流形的平均，证明所有1<p≤∞的局部极大界；对多项式参数化，在无有限型假设下得到所有正尺度上的界，常数仅依赖次数与维度；对R^n (n≥3)中满足一致旋转曲率条件的变超曲面，在p>n/(n-1)范围证明局部极大不等式。

Conclusion: 作为应用，得到保迹R^n作用（对应该几何平均）的非交换极大遍历不等式，以及归一化遍历平均的双边几乎一致收敛。

Abstract: We establish maximal inequalities for geometric averages of operator-valued functions in noncommutative \(L^p\)-spaces associated with semifinite von Neumann algebras. For averages over a fixed smooth submanifold of finite type at the parameter origin, we prove local maximal bounds for every \(1<p\leq\infty\). For polynomial parametrizations, we obtain bounds over all positive scales without a finite-type assumption, with constants only depending on the degree and dimensions. We also prove local maximal inequalities for variable hypersurfaces in \(\mathbb R^n\), \(n\geq3\), satisfying a uniform rotational curvature condition, in the range \(p>n/(n-1)\). The finite-type and polynomial estimates rely on a weak type \((1,1)\) inequality for a regularized auxiliary family adapted to non-isotropic dilations. Its proof uses a noncommutative Calderón-Zygmund decomposition based on Cuculescu projections. Interpolation with Fourier-based \(L^2\) bounds recovers the maximal inequalities for the original averages. The variable-hypersurface result uses a separate argument based on oscillatory \(L^2\) estimates and localization. As applications, we obtain some noncommutative maximal ergodic inequalities for trace-preserving actions of \(\mathbb R^n\) (corresponding to the geometric averages considered above) and bilateral almost uniform convergence for normalized ergodic averages.

</details>


### [299] [Unique equivariant pseudo expectation and generalized Powers averaging for crossed product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35567)
*Tattwamasi Amruram,Fatemeh Khosravi,Zahra Naghavi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明：对可数离散群 $\Gamma$ 与单位 $\Gamma$-单的 $\Gamma$-$C^*$-代数 $A$，$A\rtimes_r\Gamma$ 具有广义 Powers 平均性质当且仅当标准映射 $A\rtimes_r\Gamma\to I_\Gamma(A)$ 是唯一的 $\Gamma$-等变伪期望；该平均性质可提升到 $I_\Gamma(A)\rtimes_r\Gamma$，且平均系数仍属于 $A$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究群 $C^*$-代数（约化交叉积）中的平均性质与伪期望的唯一性，刻画广义 Powers 平均性质的等价条件，并探讨其从 $A\rtimes_r\Gamma$ 到 $I_\Gamma(A)\rtimes_r\Gamma$ 的提升问题。该问题与 $\Gamma$-单性、$C^*$-单性及正则迹的刻画密切相关。

Method: 以可数离散群 $\Gamma$ 及其 $\Gamma$-单的 $\Gamma$-$C^*$-代数 $A$ 为框架，考察约化交叉积 $A\rtimes_r\Gamma$ 上的 $\Gamma$-等变伪期望。通过唯一性条件（标准映射 $A\rtimes_r\Gamma\to I_\Gamma(A)$ 是唯一 $\Gamma$-等变伪期望）来等价刻画广义 Powers 平均性质；并利用提升（lifting）技术将该性质推广至 $I_\Gamma(A)\rtimes_r\Gamma$，控制平均系数的取值范围。

Result: 证明了如下等价性：$A\rtimes_r\Gamma$ 具有广义 Powers 平均性质，当且仅当标准映射 $A\rtimes_r\Gamma\to I_\Gamma(A)$ 是唯一的 $\Gamma$-等变伪期望。进一步证明该平均性质可提升至 $I_\Gamma(A)\rtimes_r\Gamma$，且提升后的平均系数仍取自 $A$。

Conclusion: 广义 Powers 平均性质可由 $\Gamma$-等变伪期望的唯一性完全刻画，并且该性质在向 $I_\Gamma(A)$ 的交叉积提升时保持，说明 $A$ 与 $I_\Gamma(A)$ 的交叉积在平均性质上具有紧密的相容性。

Abstract: Let $Γ$ be a countable discrete group and let $A$ be a unital $Γ$-simple $Γ$-$C^*$-algebra. We prove that $A\rtimes_rΓ$ has the generalized Powers averaging property if and only if the canonical map $A\rtimes_rΓ\to I_Γ(A)$ is the unique $Γ$-equivariant pseudo-expectation. We also show that this averaging property lifts to $I_Γ(A)\rtimes_rΓ$, with the averaging coefficients still belonging to $A$.

</details>
